Chapter 801: Wait for People to Come

Zong Ge asked Zi Di, “Now that things have come to this, what do you think we should do next?”

The current situation was that the Imperial Secret Spy Agency had taken control of the Wisteria shop. The shop's flying messaging unit was definitely unusable now.

Not only that, but it would be best to avoid using the Wisteria flying messaging units around the periphery of Ice Sculpture Island as well.

The Imperial Secret Spy Agency was vast in scale and likely had arrangements in place elsewhere as well.

Zi Di said, "The Bai Zhen Clan posted a hefty bounty through the Imperial Bounty Hunter Guild. I've considered that we might pose as bounty hunters and contact the Bai Zhen Clan through the guild."

“Unfortunately, there are no branches of the Bounty Hunter Guild within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

Zong Ge nodded slightly. “There used to be one, but over forty years ago, a rebellion led the Ice Sculpture Kingdom to completely ban all Imperial Bounty Hunter Guild branches.”

Zi Di was well aware of this fact. When they first arrived on Ice Sculpture Island, they had purchased a large amount of intelligence.

That rebellion over forty years ago was a major focus in the intelligence reports. It was also a crucial piece of information for outsiders to understand the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Zi Di continued, “Therefore, to leave Ice Sculpture Island, we must sail a considerable distance to find a Bounty Hunter Guild branch on another island.”

“Alternatively, we could seek aid from the Imperial Navy.”

“The Bai Zhen Clan once mobilized their connections to have the Imperial Navy search for Zhen Jin and me.”

“But relying on the Imperial Navy isn't safe.”

“The Bai Zhen Clan's current situation is precarious. If news of our contact with the navy reaches their political enemies first, it could bring immense trouble upon us.”

At this point, Zi Di paused, her gaze sharpening as she stared at the alchemy communication device before her. She ventured cautiously, “Ultimately, what we lack most is influence.”

“If we could find a pivotal figure capable of communicating with the Empire's high command, we could bypass the Bai Zhen Clan entirely.”

“But none of us possess connections reaching that level.”

Such influence was no ordinary matter.

It required someone with influence within the Sheng Ming Empire, someone possessing sufficient power and resources to protect the survivors' group.

Before the Wisteria Merchant Alliance was reorganized, Zi Di's father had barely managed to find such a person.

But after her father's death, though Zi Di took over, the merchant alliance she inherited lay in utter ruin, reduced to less than one percent of its former size.

Zi Di's words were a probe into Zong Ge.

From previous clues, Zong Ge's bloodline seemed extraordinary, and his background appeared exceptional.

A merchant's daughter, raised in such circles, could judge a person's social standing and commercial value.

Zi Di concluded: Though a half beastman, among the survivors, Zong Ge was most likely to have access to the Empire's upper echelons.

Hearing Zi Di's words, Zong Ge fell into a prolonged silence.

Seeing no response from the other end of the communication device for an extended period, Zi Di continued, “Beyond these two avenues, there is a third option, one I personally believe best suits our current circumstances.”

“We can await the Ice Sculpture National Ceremony.”

“The Ice Sculpture Kingdom must have issued invitations in advance. During the ceremony, the Empire will undoubtedly dispatch high-ranking officials.”

“It's highly likely naval commanders will be among them.”

“We can attempt to make contact with them.”

Although Zi Di had nearly been ambushed by the Imperial Secret Spy Agency, firstly, the spies hadn't yet connected the necromancer with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Second, the Empire itself was rife with factions and complex internal politics.

Zi Di: “Though the business plan succeeded, it only completes the first phase.”

“What follows remains crucial, it hinges on people.”

"This is an excellent opportunity. Elsewhere, we wouldn't draw attention."

"But here, dueling culture is rampant. Commander previously slew Teng Dong Lang, making us unrivaled in prestige. Our alliance with the Li Jian Clan and ties to Snow Bird Harbor City Lord bolster our standing in negotiations with the Empire's high command."

“We can stir even greater waves in the market, demonstrate our strength, and build leverage for future talks with the Empire's leaders.”

Previously, the survivors primarily sought to contact the Bai Zhen Clan. But with events unfolding this way, Zi Di adapted her strategy to the situation.

Since reaching out externally proved troublesome and costly, it would be wiser to wait for the Empire's high command to approach them.

Zong Ge pondered for a moment: “This approach is sound.”

“The Empire and the Ice Sculpture Kingdom have always placed great importance on maintaining their connection.”

"The Emperor even entrusted the young Thirteenth Prince to the Ice Sculpture Royal Family for upbringing. For a powerful nation to voluntarily offer a hostage prince to a weaker one is an exceedingly rare occurrence."

"It was only a few years ago that the Thirteenth Prince formally returned to the Empire for the first time to pay his respects to his father, the Emperor."

"This National Ceremony marks the Thirteenth Prince's return, and he must be accompanied by high-ranking officials from the Empire."

“Reflecting now, the Emperor's entrusting of the hostage to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom was likely preparation for the current campaign on the Wilderness Continent.”

After Zong Ge's reflection, he formally reached an agreement with Zi Di.

They chose to remain in the Ice Sculpture Capital, awaiting the national ceremony and the arrival of the Empire's high-ranking officials.

The core plan was set; now came the details.

Zong Ge: “Given that, I've decided to resume the mercenaries' military training.”

“Especially naval training, it's crucial.”

“The vast ocean separates the Sheng Ming Continent from the Wilderness Continent. Naval warfare will undoubtedly be a major theme in future conflicts.”

Zi Di frowned: "Funding is no longer an issue. But is this safe?"

At Snow Bird Harbor's docks, the mercenaries had protection from the mage tower and others. But leaving port for training might give the enemy an opening.

Zong Ge stood firm on his suggestion: “We need a fallback, Miss Zi Di.”

“We must plan for future retreats.”

“Especially for us, facing two hostile factions, it's extremely dangerous.”

"The Deep Sea Monster Fish was taken by the commander and hasn't returned yet. If we ever need to escape Ice Sculpture Island, our main reliance will be our sea vessels and subordinates."

“Of course, this is only one escape route.”

“We could follow Da Dou's approach and build some secret teleportation devices.”

Zi Di opened her mouth to speak, but Zong Ge cut her off: “I understand your point.”

“True, their military training will inevitably take them far from the dock.”

“Should the enemy strike, this entire group could be wiped out.”

“But we must proceed regardless.”

“Because this is the right thing to do!”

“We cannot halt our path of self-strengthening merely because danger looms!”

A bone-chilling cold seeped from Zong Ge's voice.

“Moreover, based on my understanding of the enemy, it's unlikely they'll target the branch directly.”

Chapter 802: The Higher-Ups' Callousness

Zong Ge judged that the mercenaries at Snow Bird Harbor Branch were unlikely to face enemy attacks, leaving Zi Di puzzled.

She pressed him directly for the reasoning and logic behind this assessment.

Zong Ge replied, “Our primary enemy right now is that mysterious force lurking in the shadows of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

“We've clashed with them several times already.”

“It's like two armies facing off. After multiple engagements, you can discern the opposing commander's temperament, thought patterns, and behavioral habits.”

Zi Di nodded slightly. “So?”

Zong Ge continued, “Based on the impression I've formed of this enemy group, their commander is exceptionally strong.”

“The assault plans he/she devises are both steady and incisive.”

“The steadiness lies in the fact that after I counter-killed three gold level operatives, we went a long stretch without any attacks.”

“The sharpness manifests in how their strikes consistently target our vulnerabilities.”

"For instance, when I intimidated the enemy, they didn't falter, they immediately shifted their assault positions. They annihilated our supply convoy. Remember that?"

Zi Di: “Of course I remember.”

Zong Ge: “This was their tactic, striking our vulnerabilities to force us into disarray, or at the very least, to reveal more of our cards.”

"Our countermeasure was to secretly transport goods through the minor merchant alliance of Snow Bird Harbor City Lord. For a time, supplies continued uninterrupted, and the enemy couldn't discern the outcome. Under these circumstances, they immediately attacked Snow Bird Harbor's alchemy workshop."

Zong Ge's gaze sharpened as he continued his analysis: “In my view, the enemy's attack plan was exceptionally well-crafted.”

"In terms of troop strength, the enemy must have conducted precise calculations. They deployed only transcendents, several silver levels. There was only one gold level."

“This force was neither too few nor too many; it successfully destroyed our four alchemy workshops.”

“Achieving this alone was no small feat. On the battlefield, deploying precisely calibrated forces is no ordinary task, it's exceptionally difficult to execute.”

"The unexpected support from Shuang Lian caught the enemy off guard. However, they quickly made up for it shortly afterward. Now Shuang Lian, Bu Quan, and even the Honey Snow Tower have vanished. Such losses are truly devastating!"

Zi Di nodded in strong agreement yet also felt profound relief: “Had we not deepened our collaboration with the Li Jian Clan in time, relying on their strength for alchemy, distribution, and sales, our business plan would have certainly failed by now!”

Zong Ge continued: “The enemy is well aware of the Li Jian Clan's partnership with us. Given their vast hidden influence, couldn't they have destroyed the Li Jian Clan's alchemy workshops?”

“Of course not.”

“Yet to this day, not a single workshop of the Li Jian Clan has been attacked.”

“That's because these alchemy workshops are no longer our weak point.”

“The Li Jian Clan's business is vast. Destroying countless alchemy workshops, or even entire factories, would barely scratch their surface.”

As the analysis unfolded, a sharp gleam flashed in Zi Di's eyes.

She grasped the logic behind Zong Ge's earlier assessment: “So you mean even if the branch is destroyed, even if our entire force is wiped out, it won't cripple us!”

“Exactly.” Zong Ge replied without hesitation. “The big guy has already been seized. The greatest loss would be the magic ship itself.”

Zi Di drew a sharp breath. "You seem to have forgotten that there are other surviving comrades at the branch. There's Chi Lai, Men Shi, and other silver level fighters. Plus the two squads you painstakingly trained..."

Zong Ge cut her off: “Even if all of them were wiped out, our core strength would still remain intact, wouldn't it?”

“Chi Lai and Men Shi have exhausted their potential. Though they're skilled silver level fighters, they're not irreplaceable.”

“As for the two teams I trained, they've never been fully developed, still in their infancy.”

"As for the likes of Mu Ban and Bai Ya, they're not even transcendents. After leaving Mystifying Monster Island, their usefulness has dwindled to near-negligibility."

“Their greatest value now might be as bait.”

Zi Di snapped coldly, “Do you realize what you're saying?”

Zong Ge met her glare without flinching or remorse. “Of course I do.”

“This is precisely what I've been trying to say!”

“Our Commander is far too merciful and lenient.”

“Expanding our ranks, recruiting new members, operating under the corps' banner, I support all of that.”

“But if we cherish them too much, these men cease to be our strength, they become our burden!”

“Do you think we recruit them, lead them, only to shield them from every storm in the end?”

“This isn't a game of house.”

“We seek an opportunity to atone to the Empire. We fight for our very…survival!”

Zong Ge continued, “Given our current situation, we need these men to rapidly improve their military skills.”

“They could become our escape route off this island.”

“Of course, their primary role is as bait.”

"We can leverage the Li Jian Clan, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, and others to set up ambushes. If the enemy takes the bait, they'll be thoroughly impressed."

“Though it's naval training, the Li Jian Clan's spatial technology can easily conceal ambush forces.”

"Personally, I believe this bait ambush plan has low odds of success. But there's no harm in trying."

Zi Di fell into a long silence.

She knew Zong Ge's words made sense.

Among the survivors, within the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, Zi Di was a transcendent, a superior figure.

It made no sense for a superior like her to risk her life protecting inferiors. It would only let the inferiors reap the rewards without effort.

“From Zong Ge's perspective, this reasoning is perfectly logical.”

“But in my view, it's not that simple.”

This was because Zi Di knew of the Bloodcore!

“The Bloodcore can alter a person's innate potential.”

“If potential can be changed, then the value of the other survivors becomes unmeasurable.”

"The most crucial factor is loyalty. They are in the same boat as us, possessing innate loyalty and the bond of comradeship forged through shared life-and-death experiences."

"I've already tested most of their bloodlines, and the Bloodline Purification Formation is complete. The hardest part is behind us, Lan Zao has already benefited..."

Sacrificing these survivors at this stage would be a tremendous waste.

Zi Di was a merchant.

Merchants thrived on weighing pros and cons, calculating gains and losses.

In this regard, Zi Di and Zong Ge were actually quite similar.

Therefore, Zi Di understood Zong Ge.

But she couldn't reveal any information about the Bloodcore to him just yet.

“Zong Ge is already a gold level, while the commander isn't.”

“What would he do if he learned about the Bloodcore?"

Zi Di couldn't guarantee Zong Ge's subsequent reaction. She couldn't handle the potential disastrous consequences.

After a moment of deep thought, Zi Di finally spoke: “We'll do as you say, but I must summon Fei She.”

Zong Ge immediately agreed: "Of course. He's a veteran of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance and can refine the level zero secret order. This will aid us in unearthing the Wisteria Cache."

“What about Tripleblade?” Zi Di inquired.

Zong Ge hesitated not a moment: “He must remain there, of course.”

Chapter 803: Follow with Your Life

The secret correspondence from Zi Di and Zong Ge swiftly reached Tripleblade and Fei She.

Since deepening cooperation with the Li Jian Clan, communication efficiency within the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had skyrocketed.

Inside the cabin, Tripleblade incinerated the decrypted letter.

Knock. Knock. Knock.

A knock sounded at the door.

Tripleblade knew exactly who it was: “Come in.”

The next moment, Fei She pushed the door open and entered.

Fei She's brow furrowed, his face heavy with concern. “Are we really... really leaving port to restart military training?”

“It's too dangerous. We could be wiped out entirely.”

The flickering light from the burning paper illuminated Tripleblade's face as he turned to look at Fei She. “Didn't Miss Zi Di mention this in her secret letter?”

“This is both military training and a tactical operation to lure the enemy into an ambush.”

Zong Ge and Zi Di had each sent back secret letters, delivered through the Li Jian Clan's channels to Tripleblade and Fei She.

In the letters, they outlined the branch's upcoming plans.

Fei She sighed. “The Lord Pres, President told me that we must serve as bait, and coordinate with the Li Jian Clan's ambush forces.”

“This is far too dangerous!”

"The enemy's strength is too gre, great, gold level fighters appear endlessly. Even our alchemy workshop in Snow Bird Harbor was destroyed by them."

"Those sacrificial warriors are terrifying. They'd sacrifice their lives just to destroy the alchemy workshop."

“Magicians Bu Quan, Shuang Lian, and the Honey Snow Tower remain missing, unlikely to be alive...”

“Enough, Fei She," Tripleblade cut him off, “this is Lord Zong Ge's order. We only need to carry it out.”

Fei She shook his head, utterly baffled. “We'll die!”

“We barely escaped Mystifying Monster Island after enduring such peril. Why would we willingly risk our lives now?”

Tripleblade snorted coldly. "This decoy operation doesn't concern you, Fei She. Miss Zi Di has already made arrangements for your departure."

Fei She fell silent. He stared at Tripleblade, noting the latter's unwavering resolve, and could only say, “As instructed by the President, my mission is to pose as an inspector and observe the actual conditions of shops across the region. Therefore, I won't be traveling alone, I require a group to serve as my subordinates.”

“Oh?” Tripleblade was taken aback. This detail hadn't appeared in the secret letter Zong Ge had given him.

Fei She produced his own secret letter and its translated version, handing them to Tripleblade. “These are the President's instructions. You may verify them.”

Tripleblade was inclined to believe him—after all, the letters were easy to authenticate. Still, he took the documents and scanned them quickly.

Tripleblade instantly grasped Zi Di's meaning.

He snapped his head up, fixing Fei She with a stare. “You intend to take others with you?”

The “others” here referred to the other survivors.

Fei She shrugged. "I can't leave the mercenary corps alone; it'd be too conspicuous. So, I need a legitimate mission to carry out."

“Inspecting the specific operations and ledgers of local shops is both excellent and perfectly natural.”

“But I can't handle this alone, I'll need helpers, of course.”

Tripleblade fully grasped the meaning.

Zong Ge's letter was concise, but Tripleblade possessed extensive experience leading a mercenary group.

When he noticed discrepancies between Zong Ge and Zi Di's letters, he realized a rift had emerged between them.

“They failed to reach an agreement, hence the separate correspondence.”

“Previously, there was always just one letter.”

“Zi Di wants to rescue the other survivors, after all, this diversionary tactic is extremely dangerous.”

“And since Lord Zong Ge didn't specifically mention this in his letter, his intentions are quite clear!”

At this thought, Tripleblade shook his head. “You cannot take the other survivors with you.”

“Because they each have their assigned roles. If they're pulled out and gathered together, it will arouse great suspicion.”

"In fact, the same applies to you. Your role aboard the ship is as a cook. Being assigned to this inspection team is already an exceptional arrangement."

"Externally, it could be portrayed as: you bribed higher-ups and schemed your way in. But it's impossible for everyone else to have schemed their way in too. That would make the higher-ups of our Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps look utterly incompetent, it defies common sense."

“Under normal circumstances, the inspection team must include transcendents. Having an iron level leader is most appropriate.”

"Silver level combat power is extremely valuable to the branch. Therefore, neither Men Shi nor Chi Lai can leave."

“Bronze level is too low; Iron level is the most suitable.”

“Tripleblade!” Fei She glared, "What the hell are you trying to do? They're my, our comrades!"

Bam!

Tripleblade slammed his fist on the table, leaping to his feet. “You're the one who's blind!”

“Don't you see the situation?”

“If we redeploy personnel as you, no, as Zi Di suggests, it'll be a massive vulnerability!”

“The enemy will grow suspicious!”

"Then, not only will our bait ambush plan fail, but your inspection team will be in grave danger too. The enemy will take a keen interest in you."

Fei She fell silent.

Tripleblade took several deep breaths before slowly sitting down. “I can't let these minor issues jeopardize the greater mission.”

“I'll personally select the specific personnel for the operation. You only need to participate.”

“Lord Zong Ge's arrangement is most appropriate. Miss Zi Di isn't a soldier after all. I understand, but I won't carry it out.”

Fei She stared at Tripleblade: “At the very least, our comrades, our companions, deserve to know, right?”

Without hesitation, Tripleblade shook his head: "No need to inform them. Once they know, their words and actions will become telltale signs, giving us away to the enemy."

“Even though we've been holed up at Snow Bird Harbor's docks, the enemy's eyes are surely upon us.”

“Fei She, deep down you know this is the safest, most prudent course, don't you?”

“Don't let irrelevant emotions cloud your judgment!”

Fei She gritted his teeth, making one final effort: “So... we just disregard the, their lives?”

Tripleblade snorted coldly: "Isn't that the nature of military operations? How can one be so hesitant? Even in your business dealings, is every transaction guaranteed profit? Though I know little of commerce, I understand that greater risks often yield greater rewards!"

"Besides, I will remain stationed here, steadfastly executing Lord Zong Ge's orders. If they sacrifice themselves, I fear I will too."

Tripleblade lowered his gaze to the secret letter on the table, now reduced to ashes. "Lord Zong Ge faces two deadly dangers, yet he has decided to accept external challenges. He aims to continue showcasing his prowess at the Ice Sculpture National Ceremony, raising the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' banner high!"

"With our commander leading thus, how could his subordinates shirk danger?!"

Fei She was deeply moved.

In that moment, he sensed Tripleblade's unshakable loyalty to Zong Ge.

“This guy is truly risking his life to follow Zong Ge...” Fei She marveled inwardly.

He was merely a mortal; he couldn't sway Tripleblade. With no other option, he could only withdraw.

Before closing the door, Tripleblade deliberately added, “Fei She, consider carefully the predicament your President faces.”

“I believe she's been too deeply influenced by our commander, becoming overly idealistic.”

“Everything must be grounded in reality.”

“We must not be overly optimistic, nor underestimate our enemies!”

"One misstep, and we risk losing everything. What does it matter if we sacrifice our lives? The crucial point is Lord Zong Ge, and your President..."

“The laughable part is, our commander is nowhere to be found even now. He might have met with misfortune, or he might still be alive, but at least he won't be implicated.”

"Fei She, don't be so naive. If you want to save others, you must save yourself first."

“Do you want this incident to create a flaw that ultimately leads to your President's death?”

“Oh, she's already dead.”

“What I mean is, do you want her undead status exposed, purged completely by the enemy, leaving no chance for resurrection?”

Fei She's body trembled violently. With vacant eyes, he slowly closed the hatch.

Tripleblade's final warning struck deep into Fei She's heart.

He walked with head bowed, lost in thought: “Tripleblade is right.”

“If I carry out the President's orders like this, it will be a weakness.”

“It could easily be detected by the enemy, and it could endanger the President.”

This was the last thing Fei She wanted to see happen.

He was a founding member of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance, having built it from nothing alongside Zi Di's father, climbing from humble beginnings to prominence.

Through the arduous expansion of their enterprise, he and Zi Di's father had developed a deep bond and seamless partnership.

He had practically watched Zi Di grow up.

Fei She himself had no descendants.

After Zi Di's father passed away, a profound sense of responsibility naturally arose within Fei She. He sought to take his place, safeguarding Zi Di's safety.

Zi Di, disregarding the peril, became the new president of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance. Fei She was strongly opposed, but he was but a mortal, unable to sway the will of the magician Zi Di.

He could only accompany her, doing his utmost to assist Zi Di in steering the restructured Wisteria Merchant Alliance.

He could only stand by her side as they journeyed across the Wilderness Continent, to Mystifying Monster Island, Snake Rat Island, Two Eyes Island, Ice Sculpture Island...

When he discovered Zi Di had fallen for the dragonman youngster, his feelings were complex. He had tried to dissuade the dragonman youngster, but in the end, he could only offer silent blessings.

When Zi Di died, Fei She was utterly heartbroken, wishing he could have taken her place. When Zi Di became a necromancer, Fei She felt no aversion, standing steadfastly behind her. Resurrection was not only Zi Di's wish, but his as well.

And when he witnessed the astonishing business acumen Zi Di displayed, he felt immense pride. At such moments, memories often flooded back, immersing him in the passionate days when he and Zi Di's father had stood shoulder to shoulder, expanding their commercial empire.

Dark corridors always had an end.

As Fei She traversed the corridor, climbed the stairs, and stepped onto the bright deck, he emerged from his reverie.

The deck bustled with activity.

Dogmen sparred against the water pigmen.

Among the spectators, Xu Ma secretly whispered with others, seemingly placing clandestine wagers.

Bai Ya watched with rapt attention, projecting himself onto the combatants, absorbing their battle experience.

Just then, Mu Ban dragged his weary body aboard from the gangplank, he’d just completed a trivial mercenary job: repairing furniture.

He spotted Fei She at the staircase entrance, gave a slight nod, and offered a subtle greeting. After all, on the surface, their relationship wasn’t particularly close. But bonds among survivors were always incredibly tight.

Fei She returned the nod.

Warm sunlight bathed him, yet his gaze grew cold. He thought to himself: “Even if these people are companions... they're not worth putting Miss Zi Di in danger.”

Chapter 804: Icebound Pirates

Blue skies and white clouds.

Sea Birds perched on the ship's ropes as the Mermaid gently rocked with the waves.

As captain of the Mermaid and commander of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's First Navy, Mei Lin was handling official duties.

She studied a piece of intelligence in her hands, a thoughtful expression on her face.

This intelligence concerned the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

“Yesterday, Long Fu accepted a silver level challenge. He chose a private duel format and directly slew the silver level challenger.”

“An interesting approach...”

There were many forms of duels.

Some allowed spectators, while others were strictly private.

“Those who challenge Long Fu seek fame and glory, but by choosing a private duel, even if victorious, the challenger gains far less renown.”

“By killing the challenger outright, Long Fu sought to demonstrate ruthless ferocity and intimidate others.”

“Of course, this also serves to protect his intelligence, concealing further tactics from public view.”

Beyond this intelligence, Mei Lin also kept tabs on Yao Ma (Zi Di).

“Has she still not registered for this Warm Snow Cup?”

The Snow Bird Harbor branch has resumed operations, departing the port to conduct military training at sea.

“Interesting.”

"Oh, and another squad has set out from Snow Bird Harbor. They will inspect and audit all the Dragon Lion shops along the route. They will ultimately enter the royal capital to report to the commander, Long Fu, and Magician Yao Ma."

Upon reading this, Mei Lin was suddenly seized by the urge to dispatch troops to ambush them.

But the next moment, she shook her head slightly, dismissing the notion of military action.

“Even if we killed every last one of them, we couldn't destroy the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

Their highest-tier magic ship is merely silver level. A single gold level cannon and a feathered serpent sail are their most advanced alchemy components.

“Given the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' current earning capacity, they could easily afford all of these.”

“Is that precisely why they're continuing their military training?”

“No. Perhaps they also intend to lure me into action!”

“The Li Jian Clan has deep ties with them, and the former possesses formidable spatial technology. They could easily set an ambush even in the vast ocean.”

Mei Lin's primary focus was drawn to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps detachment led by Tripleblade, paying little heed to Fei She and his group.

Intelligence indicated this iron level squad's objective was clear: to audit and verify the operational status of local merchants.

Mei Lin couldn't be bothered to intervene. She even hoped they might uncover some shady dealings, potentially driving a wedge between the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Li Jian Clan.

After some deliberation, she concluded that the Li Jian Clan had likely set an ambush. Such a significant risk further diminished her desire to launch an attack and wipe out the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps branch.

Zong Ge's analysis was correct; Mei Lin's personality shaped her approach to warfare. She preferred targeted strikes, precise calculations, and meticulously deploying every ounce of strength, rather than spreading her forces thin in an all-out effort to eradicate the enemy's support network.

With a soft sigh, Mei Lin decided against moving against the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' branch.

She set aside the relevant intelligence and retrieved another report to examine.

This one had nothing to do with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Instead, it concerned pirates.

“With the National Ceremony approaching, the seas have indeed begun to stir.”

The intelligence in Mei Lin's hands pertained to the Spray Pirates.

Their captain was a gold level alchemist, exceptionally skilled in battlefield healing.

According to the report, the Spray Pirates had been invited by a coalition of other pirate crews. Nearly all these crews were gold level forces.

Mei Lin put down the report in her hand and inspected the rest of the other documents.

Eventually, she fixed her gaze on one report.

“The Icebound Pirates!”

“Pirate Captain Da Han, a saint level sorcerer and the fourth-in-command of the Fire Beard Pirates.”

“The reason so many pirate crews have flocked here is because Da Han is leading the Icebound Pirates straight toward our nation.”

“Of course, this is merely the stated reason. Behind the scenes, there may be a mastermind pulling the strings.”

The Fire Beard Pirates were one of the three strongest pirate crews in the Main Plane, with Fire Beard himself a legend level powerhouse. He commanded several saint level individuals, Da Han being one of them.

Da Han alone was reason enough for the Ice Sculpture Kingdom to take extreme caution.

After all, the nation's strongest experts numbered only three saint level individuals.

Not to mention Da Han's crew brimmed with talent, their fleet boasted numerous magic ships, and though their flagship was merely gold level, the pirate crew raised a saint level Ice Crystal Jellyfish.

“This Ice Crystal Jellyfish possesses aerial flight capabilities and formidable regenerative powers—a truly troublesome foe.”

"In contrast, our navy has just completed its rearmament. While equipped with new warships, they haven't undergone proper combat integration. Even the Li Jian Clan's previous combat tests against pirate fleets yielded poor results."

Mei Lin sighed inwardly, the weight of pressure bearing heavily on her heart.

The Icebound Pirates alone compelled the Ice Sculpture Kingdom to take notice. Preliminary estimates suggested over twenty gold level transcendents among the other pirate fleets. Like sharks sensing blood, they swarmed in.

All because of the Ice Sculpture National Ceremony.

During this period, envoys from various factions, merchant alliance ships, and countless important figures would be invited to attend.

If they couldn't seize merchant convoys, pirates could capture these VIPs as hostages to extort their factions.

Pirates frequently engaged in this hostage-taking extortion.

It offered greater advantages than standard cargo plundering.

Much of the seized merchandise was useless to pirates, forcing them to sell it at a discount, a waste of time and manpower. Extortion, however, allowed them to demand direct payment, equipment, arms, or even ship repair services.

As a commander in the Kingdom's First Navy, Mei Lin bore heavy patrol duties during the national ceremony.

Her primary focus had to be on this task, not the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

In truth, this applied not only to her but to the other duelists as well.

For instance, Xue Tuan Zi was participating in this year's Warm Snow Cup, while Mo Gui Ji, Yun Zhong, and others were competing in the Kingdom Grand Duel.

Others like Zhu Gan had no interest in dueling, much less following Mei Lin's orders to risk confronting Zong Ge and his crew.

“For now, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps must be set aside.”

“But they absolutely cannot be allowed to continue operating!”

Mei Lin's resolve to crush the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was unwavering, especially after reviewing intelligence estimating their profits from trafficking dragon rider potions.

The sheer scale of their gains sent a chill down Mei Lin's spine.

“Money can solve many problems.”

“After the National Ceremony, we must eliminate them swiftly. The longer we delay, the harder it will become.”

Chapter 805: Tranquil Hill Divine Domain

Tranquil Hill Divine Domain.

Honey Snow Tower, control room.

Shuang Lian sat cross-legged mid-air, eyes tightly closed. Countless magic runes drifted around her body, weaving chains of arcane symbols.

Nearly a hundred magic rune chains connected one end to Shuang Lian's mind, while the other ends sank into the surrounding walls, linking to the tower spirit residing in the middle layer.

Waves of magic fluctuations continuously radiated outward from Shuang Lian's body, forming ceaseless ripples of energy.

Suddenly, Shuang Lian snapped her eyes open, her gaze blazing with dazzling brilliance.

In an instant, the fiery intensity in her eyes vanished.

The intense magic fluctuations died down as well.

Shuang Lian drifted slowly downward until her feet touched the ground.

The magic runes vanished into thin air, severing the link between Shuang Lian and the tower spirit.

Her hair fell wildly about her shoulders. The moment her feet touched the ground, an overwhelming wave of exhaustion washed over her entire body.

She longed to collapse onto a bed and sleep soundly right then and there!

Gritting her teeth, she forced herself to endure, reflecting on the results of this extreme calculation.

“The tower spirit has reached its absolute limit.”

“It was insufficient to calculate the flaw in this divine domain, unable to locate the spatial weak point.”

"But it did once again validate my conjecture, this divine domain truly is ownerless. The reason the Old King's Crowbar proved ineffective lies in that hill on the horizon!"

Honey Snow Tower was built from nothing by Shuang Lian herself.

The tower spirit within was also conjured by Shuang Lian herself through magic, existing as a kind of personal avatar. For this, she paid a heavy price, her mental capacity was permanently and drastically reduced, never to be restored.

Yet this sacrifice brought immense benefits.

For instance, the tower spirit could flawlessly control and govern every aspect of the Honey Snow Tower, far more profoundly and thoroughly than Shuang Lian herself could ever achieve.

Moreover, the tower spirit's computational abilities surpassed Shuang Lian's own. When Shuang Lian and the tower spirit were tightly linked, forming a unified mind, their combined computational power surged severalfold, far exceeding Shuang Lian's limits before the tower spirit's creation.

This was an extreme calculation.

Such calculations could not be sustained for long. While Shuang Lian herself still had reserves, the tower spirit could not endure.

Before the tower spirit was burnt out, Shuang Lian proactively halted the extreme computation.

“We must investigate that hill.”

Forcing herself to rise despite her exhaustion, Shuang Lian opened the control level doors and descended the stairs.

Soon, she gathered the students within the mage tower to hear their reports.

“Teacher, while you were performing the extreme calculations, we transported 3.5 tons of honey snow from the demi-plane.”

“As per your instructions, we maintained the public magic pool at full capacity, keeping the magic power reserve line consistently above 80%.”

"Tower Master, during this period, the enemy launched three waves of attacks. Though different gold level forces engaged us, none managed to fully breach our defenses. Repair work has been ongoing, and the tower structure remains near intact."

“Teacher, the lightning defense formation you ordered us to construct is now operational.”

“Teacher, we've continuously transmitted messages outward, yet we've received no response—not a single one.”

…

After hearing all the reports, Shuang Lian declared, “Cease all external communications.”

The students exchanged bewildered glances.

Ever since the fall of the Honey Snow Tower, they had been sending distress signals outward. Of course, these signals were encrypted, only the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's Alchemy Guild possessed the means to decode them.

Shuang Lian feared that revealing the location of the ownerless divine domain would attract more rivals. Outwardly, she stated, “Through this calculation, I've identified a vulnerability in this place.”

With a flick of her finger, a magic projection materialized, revealing a hazy image of Tranquil Hill.

“This is the core location.”

“By controlling this place, no, by exploring it and gathering sufficient intelligence, we can break free.”

“Now, I need to assemble an exploration team. Those interested step forward. I will reward you handsomely.”

Shuang Lian spoke truthfully.

This was primarily because she possessed an artifact of legend level—the Old King's Crowbar.

The more information gathered, the greater the crowbar's power would become, enabling her to pry open a crack in the Tranquil Hill Divine Domain and escape successfully.

Of course, Shuang Lian's ambitions reached far beyond mere escape.

She sought to pry open a larger share, to lay claim to, and ultimately control, this wondrous land!

Her students fell silent.

None showed eagerness at the call for volunteers. When Shuang Lian's gaze swept over them, their eyes darted away.

Her expression darkened.

Bu Quan, watching from the crowd, couldn't bear it. Remembering Shuang Lian's kindness to her, she was about to step forward when Shuang Lian shot her a fierce glare.

Bu Quan flinched, her foot retreating the instant it lifted.

Only then did Shuang Lian soften her expression. “Since none of you wish to volunteer, then so be it. I won't force you.”

Indeed, these magicians and apprentices were mostly scholarly or research-oriented types, with few skilled in combat. Even those possessing some combat ability, after witnessing the earlier fierce clash, dared not risk themselves by leaving the mage tower to explore this space.

Shuang Lian had anticipated this outcome. She proceeded with her arrangements: “Then we shall draw snow elements here.”

Despite their fall into the Tranquil Hill Divine Domain, the connection between the Honey Snow Tower and the demi-plane remained intact.

A coordinate anchor from the demi-plane was anchored upon the Honey Snow Tower.

The Old King's Crowbar had pried open a crack, preserving the link between the mage tower and the demi-plane, preventing it from being severed by the divine domain.

Within Shuang Lian's demi-plane, vast swathes of honey snow coexisted with numerous magic creatures. The most formidable among them were two gold level snow elementals.

When Yi Jiu had fallen into the demi-plane, these two snow elementals had pursued and hunted him, posing significant threats.

These two snow elementals were Shuang Lian's own magic pets.

Shuang Lian was wealthy, in fact, she was extremely wealthy!

Such wealth translated directly into corresponding power for a magician.

“Using these two magic pets as the main force for the exploration team, supplemented by some alchemy puppets for reconnaissance, is feasible,” Shuang Lian mused inwardly.

Outside the tower.

The duelists had changed shifts.

Gu Dong replaced Bao Li Gen, once again standing outside the mage tower.

Gazing at the tower, he sighed inwardly: “The Ice Sculpture National Ceremony has drawn all the duelists' attention away. We'll have to leave the Honey Snow Tower unattended for now.”

“Hope nothing unexpected happens during this time.”

Gu Dong crushed the snow beneath his foot with a stomp, launching himself toward the mage tower like a cannonball.

At the foot of Tranquil Hill.

A valley cabin.

Spring Boxer and Bing Xiao were engaged in a confidential discussion.

“That mage tower that was breached hasn't been destroyed yet?” Spring Boxer expressed surprise.

Shuang Lian could see Tranquil Hill on the horizon. From Spring Boxer's vantage point, the tower silhouette of the Honey Snow Tower, now miniature, could also be seen.

Mei Lin had also relayed relevant intelligence to Bing Xiao.

Thus, both Bing Xiao and Spring Boxer were aware of the situation.

Bing Xiao shook his head slightly. “The mage tower is not our concern. For us, the most crucial task is to uncover the traitor lurking among us!”

Spring Boxer frowned. “Does our Lord intend to stand idly by?”

Bing Xiao's expression grew solemn as he rebuked Spring Boxer. “How could we presume to fathom the thoughts of the deity?”

"In my view, this is a challenge our Lord has deliberately bestowed upon us, a trial. Not only does it test our mettle, but it also helps us grow stronger."

Spring Boxer hastily acknowledged the lesson and returned to the matter of the traitor.

Bing Xiao sighed: "All this time, we've speculated and investigated everyone, yet we still can't be certain. Everyone is a suspect, yet no one is truly suspect."

Spring Boxer pondered, “If that's the case, perhaps we should shift our approach.”

“Lord Bing Xiao, your previous approach was actually quite sound.”

“My previous approach?” Bing Xiao froze in surprise. “You mean relying on faith?”

He shook his head immediately. “Faith alone can't identify the traitor.”

"But it can create constraints. Once the traitor betrays us, they'll face punishment. From that perspective, all we need to do is deepen their faith as much as possible."

“You're absolutely right!” Bing Xiao pondered for a moment, his gaze toward Spring Boxer now tinged with a hint of relief. In that fleeting moment, he saw Teng Dong Lang in Spring Boxer.

Spring Boxer had been Teng Dong Lang's coffin bearer. With Bing Xiao's aid, he had successfully laid Teng Dong Lang's remains to rest in Tranquil Hill.

Thus, in the days that followed, he gradually absorbed Teng Dong Lang's memories and experiences from before his death.

Now, Spring Boxer has fully assimilated them.

The innocence and youthfulness that once graced his face had faded, leaving almost no trace behind.

Teng Dong Lang's legacy profoundly shaped him, forging a more exceptional and formidable Spring Boxer in remarkably short time.

Spring Boxer's behavioral habits, operational style, and even thought processes had advanced significantly.

He continued, “As long as everyone remains deeply devoted to our Lord, even if a traitor betrays us, our losses won't be excessive.”

“Until we can identify the traitor, this is our only strategy.”

Bing Xiao agreed, then analyzed, “Xiong Ju's devotion has reached the level of a fanatic.”

“The focus is on the others.”

“Let us ‘assist’ them a little. What do you think?”

In the past, Spring Boxer wouldn't have understood, but now he grasped Bing Xiao's implied meaning instantly.

Spring Boxer nodded. “Let me speak with them individually.”

Not long after.

Lan Zao was summoned and entered the wooden hut alone, meeting Spring Boxer.

“He really has changed,” Lan Zao thought inwardly.

Were it not for Spring Boxer retaining his former appearance, Lan Zao would have almost mistaken him for someone else.

After exchanging pleasantries, Spring Boxer got straight to the point: “When we first arrived, to maintain stability, we didn't reveal the truth...”

After hearing Spring Boxer's words, cold sweat beaded on Lan Zao's forehead.

His eyes widened in disbelief as he confirmed, “If my faith doesn't reach the devout level, I'll... die?”

Spring Boxer nodded, his expression grave.

He stated he absolutely did not want to see this happen, but he was powerless to prevent it.

“Pray fervently.”

“Devote yourself wholeheartedly to our Lord, that is your only sliver of hope.”

“This applies not just to you, but to everyone else as well.”

“You've already experienced the blessings of faith in our Lord, haven't you?”

"This is our Lord's divine domain. Only true believers may enter freely; outsiders cannot come and go as they please. Do you understand?"

Lan Zao was intimidated, his face grim. He nodded slightly, his voice hoarse: “I understand.”

Just like the Charming Blue Archbishop Zhou Zhang had done, to spread doctrine and faith, the Spring Boxer also employed death threats to coerce the other members of the Bayonet Gang into actively converting, deepening their devotion to the Dueling God.

Chapter 806: Magnet Archipelago

A fleet of three sea vessels gradually approached the Magnet Archipelago.

On the flagship, the captain respectfully knocked on the captain's cabin door and announced to the occupant inside, “Lord Da Dou, we have reached our destination.”

“Oh? Already?” came Da Dou's voice from within the captain's cabin.

Moments later, the cabin door swung open.

Da Dou bent low to emerge.

He strode onto the deck and walked to the ship's edge, causing the vessel to tilt immediately.

The captain hurriedly called out, “Lord Da Dou, please hold your step! My ship is only iron level, constructed entirely from driftwood. It's extremely light, and any significant shift in weight distribution could easily capsize it!”

Da Dou halted instantly. “My apologies.”

A giant, he straightened up, his head nearly level with the ship's tallest main mast.

He raised his palm to shade his brow like a sun hat and scanned the horizon. Sure enough, a cluster of dark shapes emerged above the sea.

Employing his reconnaissance battle skill, his vision sharpened, revealing the distinctive landscape of the Magnet Archipelago.

“Magnet Archipelago, indeed. Ben Liu, your fleet's speed is truly impressive!” Da Dou exclaimed in admiration.

After narrowly escaping Shuang Lian's grasp, he had pushed forward relentlessly, exhausting the remaining uses of his Tunnel Staff.

He had also traversed the vast sea using divine magic, his path marked by constant flashes of firelight.

Da Dou possessed no aerial battle skill, making divine magic nearly his only option for long-distance travel across the ocean.

However, divine magic consumed divine power. To replenish it, Da Dou needed to pray or perform rituals.

In the vast expanse of the sea, conducting a ritual to the Fire God totem proved extremely troublesome and difficult to accomplish. The energy and time expended were better spent directly on prayer.

Da Dou was fortunate.

While swimming across the sea during one of his journeys, he encountered this small fleet.

The fleet consisted of only three ships, all iron level magic ships. The captain was named Ben Liu, a seahorseman. His crew were also seahorsemen.

They were neither pirates nor mercenaries, but transport ships.

Their primary task was transporting people.

Da Dou revealed his identity and background from Eruption Island to Ben Liu, earning his trust and securing passage aboard his vessel.

Though Ben Liu's ships were only iron level, their sails, oars, and other alchemy components formed a cohesive system designed to complement each other. Their sole purpose was to maximize speed.

During heavy seas, Ben Liu would personally lead his crew into the ocean, pulling the ship forward.

The seahorsemen's unique racial ability allowed them to run on waves and accelerate against currents, making the fleet faster when moving against the tide.

Da Dou had expected another three days to reach the Magnet Archipelago, but they arrived sooner than anticipated.

Ben Liu beamed, "Lord Da Dou, I welcome you aboard my vessel again next time. If possible, please spread the word about my Seahorse Transport Service."

Da Dou reiterated his proposal: “If you could deliver me directly to Eruption Island, I can leverage my connections to help you establish operations there.”

Eruption Island was home to a large population of giants who were rather xenophobic, with few other races living there.

Da Dou's suggestion stirred something in Ben Liu, but he quickly regained his composure and shook his head. “Lord Da Dou, making a living around Eruption Island would be too difficult.”

“My target customer base would become giants.”

"As you know, my ship isn't large or spacious. Your current stay aboard was only possible after we urgently cleared space and converted the captain's cabin on deck."

“My fleet simply isn't suited for business around Eruption Island. Thank you for your kind offer.”

Da Dou scratched his head. After Ben Liu's analysis, he realized he'd misjudged the price and quickly revised his offer, proposing a generous reward.

But Ben Liu still shook his head. “Lord, transporting people around the Magnet Archipelago is Seahorse Transport Service's primary business.”

“This is the busiest time of the year.”

“Honestly, the price you're offering wouldn't even cover what I earn in just a few weeks.”

“And!”

“This period is particularly crucial for the Seahorse Transport Service, a truly rare opportunity.”

"In maritime shipping, we've long been overshadowed by the Drip Fleet. We simply can't match them in either personnel or vessels."

"But the Ice Sculpture National Ceremony has drawn immense attention. Leaders and envoys from major and minor factions have been invited, drawing a large number of pirates as well."

“The maritime situation has become highly complex and dangerous. Considering the risks involved, the Drip Fleet has temporarily withdrawn from these waters.”

“This is precisely our moment to seize the initiative and capture market share for our Seahorse Transport Service.”

"I'm also considering using this chance to sign long-term transport agreements with nearby islands. Such opportunities are rare indeed."

Da Dou inquired curiously, "Aren't you afraid of pirates? Didn't you also receive intelligence that Da Han, the fourth-in-command of the Fire Beard Pirates, is heading to Ice Sculpture Island this time?"

A resolute expression crossed Ben Liu's face. “To make money, you have to take risks.”

“There's no other way.”

“My fleet may be small, but don't forget, we're all seahorsemen.”

“If danger strikes, we can always jump ship and swim to safety.”

“True enough...” Da Dou nodded. Once he fully understood Ben Liu's resolve, he stopped pressuring him.

Da Dou wasn't inherently unreasonable.

His earlier aggressive demeanor in Snow Bird Harbor had been directed solely at his enemy, Ci Hai Shen. Later, seizing the big guy had stemmed from tribal necessity, the stakes were too high to pass up, forcing him into the role of pirate, which didn't align with his nature or preferences.

Soon after, the Seahorse Transport Service's ships officially arrived at the Magnet Archipelago.

Da Dou and the other passengers disembarked, and the fleet remained anchored for over an hour.

Ben Liu's crew not only transported passengers but also cargo.

After unloading, they picked up new passengers from the Magnet Archipelago before departing in a flurry of activity.

Magnet Archipelago was a desolate landscape, covered in pitch-black rock with almost no vegetation. The terrain was rugged and steep, with bizarre peaks and hills, and the air felt noticeably hotter than at sea.

Yet Magnet Archipelago was far from lifeless.

They teemed with people.

Humans, dwarves, elves, almost every race could be seen here.

Most came seeking treasure, while a few saw business opportunities, setting up shops, pharmacies, and even temporary docks.

Da Dou made no effort to conceal his gold level aura, drawing considerable attention.

After inquiring about potential ships bound for the Magnet Archipelago, he learned the next departure wouldn't be for three days, and it wasn't heading back toward Eruption Island.

“Guess I'll have to wait.”

“First, find a secluded spot to cook.”

“The little one must be hungry!”

Chapter 807: Bathing in the Big Guy’s Blood

Da Dou held the iron wok, deftly shaking his wrist.

The silver level iron wok was filled to the brim with beef.

As he continuously tossed the ingredients, an intense golden aroma quickly spread throughout the kitchen.

Sweat dripping down his face, Da Dou pulled a bundle of gold level green onions from a packing bag.

His gold battle qi coalesced into several small blade-like forms, chopping the scallions into fine mince.

He tossed the garnish directly into the iron wok, then cranked up the heat. After several minutes of vigorous stir-frying, the dish was finally complete.

He retrieved a silver level serving plate and loaded it with the stir-fried beef.

“Lunch is finally ready.”

Da Dou approached a stone slab with a flat top and placed the stir-fried beef upon it.

After setting it down, the stone table now held four plates.

Three were dishes, and one was soup.

He had spared no expense, using copious amounts of gold level ingredients and laboring over the stove for over an hour to produce this much.

Then...

Battle skill - Food Compression!

The gold level aura of the three dishes and soup was instantly suppressed and condensed, ultimately downgraded to silver level.

“This is the perfect transcendent meal for the little guy.”

Da Dou's extravagant act had only one purpose: to ensure the giant ate better and digested faster.

The giant magic chef wiped sweat from his brow and checked his own condition.

His battle qi was nearly depleted, and his spirit felt somewhat sluggish, but his divine power remained abundant. This was all thanks to his relentless prayers aboard the swift sea vessel, day and night.

“Feed him first, then refine the battle qi.” Having made his decision, Da Dou released the giant from the packing bag.

The big guy lay with eyes tightly shut, still in a deep slumber.

Though Da Dou's packing bag could contain living beings, he feared the giant might cause trouble inside. That's why he'd always kept him in a state of unconsciousness.

Several minutes later, Da Dou successfully roused the giant.

Before the giant could fully focus his blurry eyes and react, Da Dou spoke first: Hey, listen up, kid. You must be starving. Let's eat first, then we'll talk properly. Okay? Don't make a move..."

Hearing Da Dou's voice, the big guy abruptly recalled his situation, his gaze instantly sharpening.

He growled lowly, immediately standing up and swinging a punch at Da Dou.

Da Dou scrambled back several steps, quickly creating distance. His words came fast, still trying to reason with the big guy.

But the big guy wouldn't listen.

Da Dou had no choice but to fight.

Compared to the big guy, Da Dou's strength was overwhelming, a crushing level advantage.

Compounded by the big guy's complete lack of technique, relying solely on brute force and reckless charges, he was easily read by Da Dou, who swiftly exploited his weaknesses.

Seconds later, with a thud, the big guy collapsed to the ground, knocked unconscious once more by Da Dou.

Da Dou crouched beside him, pried open his jaws, and began shoveling the painstakingly prepared magic dishes into the big guy's mouth, one after another.

Though unconscious, the giant's primal instincts kicked in, causing him to chew involuntarily.

Once he chewed, the food slid down his throat. His body, more eager than his own consciousness, craved the nutrients from the outside world.

“Eat up, little guy.”

A big smile spread across Da Dou's face as he watched the giant unconsciously activate his battle qi technique in his sleep, accelerating digestion. Half of him felt relieved, the other half was puzzled.

“Why is this kid so utterly determined?”

“What kind of offer did the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps give to make him so utterly devoted?”

Da Dou hadn't intended to repeatedly force his will upon the big guy. His original plan was merely to recruit the big guy; he hadn't wanted to treat him so harshly.

According to Da Dou's original plan: first, he would seize the big guy. Then, by demonstrating his formidable strength, he could intimidate him.

Next, the magic delicacies he would repeatedly prepare would make the big guy keenly feel the stark difference in treatment.

Finally, through persuasion, Da Dou believed the generous rewards of joining Eruption Island would be enough to sway the big guy, compelling him to make a new life choice.

Truth be told, this carrot-and-stick approach, though straightforward, held a high probability of success.

But the big guy's situation proved far more unique than Da Dou had imagined.

After putting his plan into action, the big guy displayed an intense hatred and unwavering resolve. This rendered all communication between Da Dou and the big guy utterly futile.

The big guy's approach to Da Dou was either fight or flee.

Though Da Dou repeatedly fed him, sparing no expense to craft gold level magic dishes only to deliberately downgrade them, the big guy remained unconscious each time he consumed these meals. He couldn't possibly appreciate Da Dou's kindness.

This made Da Dou feel as though the big guy was merely freeloading off him.

Yet Da Dou cherished it: “Even if it is freeloading, I'm still happy.”

“You little rascal, someday you'll understand my heartfelt intentions.”

“Joining Eruption Island is infinitely better than joining that worthless mercenary group of yours.”

“We are a legend level force here on Eruption Island.”

“Once you grow stronger, you'll become one of our pillars.”

“We're all giants. Out here on the vast ocean, we should live together.”

After finishing his meal, the big guy resumed his battle qi cultivation.

Da Dou cleared the plates and bowls, then carefully wiped the giant's mouth.

Only after finishing these tasks did he plop down on the ground, pulling out some dry rations from his pack to munch on.

As he ate, his gaze drifted toward the distant hills.

Perched atop one hill stood a silver level transcendent.

She was a human woman of ordinary appearance, holding a crystal ball in her hands.

The sphere was filled with an unknown transparent liquid. At its center floated a delicate, diamond-shaped pointer.

The crystal ball itself was iron level, while the pointer was silver level. Combined, this alchemy artifact was known as the treasure hunting pointer.

It was through this treasure hunting pointer that the human woman had detected the hidden “treasure” here, prompting her swift relocation to the vicinity.

Eventually, she climbed the hill and witnessed the entire process of Da Dou cooking the dish and feeding the giant.

“A gold level magic chef preparing a magic dish, no wonder the treasure hunting pointer detected it.”

“Isn't that little giant he knocked out and fed a member of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?”

“I couldn't have seen wrong, could I?”

The woman was named Hong Zhu. During her time on Two Eyes Island, she had frequent interactions with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

When Two Eyes Island shifted, it triggered a tidal wave of sea beasts. At that time, Hong Zhu had boarded the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' ship, fighting alongside them to resist the beast tide.

Therefore, Hong Zhu recognized the big guy's appearance. After all, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had only one giant, the big guy, making him quite conspicuous.

Noticing Da Dou's gaze fixed on her, Hong Zhu quickly ducked her head to avoid his stare.

Her heart pounded as she sprinted down the hill, fleeing from Da Dou.

Only when she was sure he wasn't pursuing her did she breathe a sigh of relief and steady her breath.

“He spotted me just now, but he didn't attack.”

“He isn't here to capture me.”

Ever since leaving Two Eyes Island, Hong Zhu had been deeply worried she'd be hunted down by the island's pursuers.

This all stemmed from the fishmen's assault.

At the time, Hong Zhu had intended to leave Two Eyes Island alongside Cang Xu (Wisteria elder), partly as a gesture of goodwill. As a treasure hunter, Hong Zhu particularly favored wealthy buyers like Cang Xu.

But when Cang Xu proactively unleashed undead magic, Hong Zhu knew she was in deep trouble.

She had fought alongside Cang Xu, only to discover he was a necromancer. With everyone watching, no amount of explanation could clear her name.

Compounding her predicament, she had already looted numerous treasures from the island's graveyard. Fearing prolonged exposure, escape became her sole priority.

As a mere silver level, navigating the open seas alone was nearly impossible.

Fortunately, Hong Zhu had secured a homeward dolphin at the auction. Mounting the homeward dolphin, she finally managed to slip away.

Though the homeward dolphins couldn't travel long distances, they could carry Hong Zhu from one deserted island to another.

And so, Hong Zhu left Two Eyes Island.

After several twists and turns, she reached Orange Soda Island, where she rested and recuperated. Then, she resupplied in Chatter Sea Market.

Her passion and curiosity for treasure hunting reignited, ultimately leading her to the Magnet Archipelago.

Hong Zhu had been searching for treasure in the Magnet Archipelago for some time now, with almost nothing to show for it.

But she refused to give up.

After leaving Da Dou behind, Hong Zhu relied on her treasure hunting pointer to enter a subterranean cave.

Moments later, she emerged, looking utterly disheveled.

Trailing behind her were three earth elementals.

Though all were merely iron level, the silver level Hong Zhu had no desire to engage in battle and instead fled relentlessly.

Earth elementals possessed formidable defenses, and their magic crystals could freely shift within their bodies, making them extremely difficult to deal with.

Hong Zhu had once spent nearly half a day grinding down an earth elemental of the same level, only to gain a single earth elemental essence as her reward.

What a horrid loss!

From then on, Hong Zhu's approach to dealing with earth elementals was simple: run.

Once she'd put enough distance between them and they could no longer sense her, she quietly slipped back into the underground cavern.

Their speed was abysmal, allowing Hong Zhu to easily outpace them.

Earth elementals possessed ranged stone-throwing attacks to compensate for their sluggishness.

Yet these three did not hurl stones. Instead, they followed their leader's orders, prioritizing the defense of their sentry post.

Deep underground.

The earth elemental leader was praying.

They stood within a vast magic formation. Before him lay heaps of earth elemental essence and earth elemental crystals.

The formation activated, consuming the essence and crystals to manifest a phantom statue.

The colossal figure radiated an aura as majestic and weighty as mountains and earth itself—none other than one of the Four Elemental Main Gods: the Earth Elemental God!

“My Lord, my Heavenly Father, the Great God of Earth!”

“You are the ruler of the Earth Elemental Plane, the supreme being of the land.”

"You tower like mountains, stretch as wide as vast plains. You are the wellspring of life, the guardian of the land."

“With each breath You exhale, the earth stirs with vitality, blossoming in vibrant splendor, thriving and flourishing.”

“With a gentle touch of Your hand, You effortlessly shape the land, raising mountains and carving deep valleys.”

“Here, Your believers offer sacrifices, beseeching Your mercy to break through life's limitations and ascend to greater heights.”

The ethereal statue of the Earth Elemental God did not even glance at the Earth Elemental Chieftain before swiftly vanishing without a trace.

Along with it, all the offerings at the center of the magic circle disappeared entirely.

Yet the Earth Elemental Chieftain felt no disappointment, only wild elation.

For in that very instant, he had received a divine revelation.

“Divine revelation!”

“That unconscious giant being fed is my opportunity to break through my life's bottleneck.”

“Capture him, kill him, bathe in his blood. and I shall transcend the limits of the gold level!”

Chapter 808: Divine Emissary

White Hair Trench.

Cang Xu surveyed the horde of undead before him.

Skeleton archers, skeleton warriors, various fish-like ghosts, a few zombies, the largest form was a gold level ghost ship.

Whatever they were, they were all sealed by Zhou Zhang's divine magic, motionless as statues.

Necromancy - Undead Bloodline Detection!

Cang Xu cast spells continuously, his gaze sharp.

He patiently cross-referenced the bloodline chart, identifying targets among these undead entities related to the Hanging Corpse Tree bloodline.

“Though they're all lower-tier branches of the Hanging Corpse Tree, most are far removed from its core bloodline. Still, small gains accumulate, they can still bolster me.”

“The Hanging Corpse Tree bloodline truly lives up to its saint level potential. Even a 1% increase, no, even 0.1%, brings noticeable change to me.”

Though Cang Xu showed no outward transformation, his essence had undergone a dramatic leap forward.

Not only had his magician cultivation reached silver level, but his bloodline now soared to saint level, though its concentration remained insufficient.

Yet this alone filled Cang Xu with satisfaction and delight, his life had entered a new realm.

He meticulously selected undead creatures suitable for enhancing his bloodline, stacking them at the right exit of this undersea valley.

This seabed valley lay not far from the gathering place of the Charming Blue believers, making it ideal for concealing troops.

Zhou Zhang had stationed numerous transcendents here as guards, who also served as jailers overseeing these undead creatures.

Watching Cang Xu continuously cast undead spells to identify undead beings, the guards wore complex expressions.

There was apprehension, disgust, and rejection toward the undead, yet also warmth toward their own kind, and reverence for Cang Xu, this unique missionary.

Cang Xu's decision to detain Xiao Wang Zi and Ku Feng as prisoners proved remarkably wise.

With no other undead converting to his faith, he stood alone among the Charming Blue believers.

The series of unique divine spells he wielded proved exceptionally effective for proselytizing. Most crucially, even Archbishop Zhou Zhang lacked these divine spells!

Cang Xu possessed exceptional political acumen. After emerging from the Ocean Mother Nest, he not only meditated but actively spread his teachings far and wide.

His missionary work yielded tangible results, earning him widespread public acclaim.

His repeated survival through teleportation blood sacrifices conclusively proved his “unwavering” and “unquestionable” faith. Thus, Cang Xu's reputation and standing continued to rise, showing no signs of peaking.

The conversion of necromancers to the Charming Blue faith also caused some to waver in their beliefs.

At this critical juncture, Zhou Zhang demonstrated her skill and capability, proving herself worthy of her position as at the level of Archbishop.

She selected eloquent clergy to continuously deliver speeches and public explanations, guiding mainstream opinion.

"Whatever our Lord does is right. Since our Lord has granted necromancers a new identity, what more is there to say?"

“The goddess must have profound intentions, beyond the comprehension of us mere mortals.”

“Consider this: even necromancers now worship our Lord, proof of Her greatness!”

This vigorous propaganda proved highly effective. Thus, Zhou Zhang gradually quelled dissent against Cang Xu.

Of course, a few stubborn believers remained, but they were few and far between.

They weren't executed outright either but instead dispatched on hunting missions. Then, various accidents occurred, and they never returned.

The Charming Blue believers were numerous, gathered here, with prayer being their primary activity.

Therefore, some had to be assigned to hunt for food.

The beast tide erupting from the White Hair Trench brought benefits beyond abundant sacrificial offerings. For the believers, it meant hordes of magic beasts everywhere, ready to be hunted for food.

But these rampaging beasts also preyed upon the believers themselves. Thus, the deaths of these hunters were hardly surprising.

Zhou Zhang's methods were ruthless and decisive, swiftly silencing all dissent.

Cang Xu blended in seamlessly, fostering harmony among the faithful and creating an excellent atmosphere.

No wonder they said this was a world of the strong.

If words fail to persuade, then force will. If problems could not be solved, then eliminate those who created them.

Zhou Zhang's actions made Cang Xu the greatest beneficiary.

Necromancy - Undead Bloodline Detection!

Necromancy - Undead Bloodline Detection!

…

Cang Xu cast the spell repeatedly, targeting the final object—the ghost ship.

A ghost ship of gold level, surpassing his own silver level cultivation.

But it didn't matter. After casting Undead Bloodline Detection over a dozen times, the accumulated data finally revealed the ghost ship's bloodline.

“It's unrelated to the Hanging Corpse Tree's bloodline,” Cang Xu lamented.

Such a gold level entity, if it met the criteria, sent to be absorbed by the Bloodcore, would undoubtedly bring a significant boost to Cang Xu's Hanging Corpse Tree bloodline.

The ghost ship remained utterly motionless, its spirit consciousness feeble. Even as Cang Xu cast spells upon it, it remained utterly oblivious to having narrowly escaped the brink of life and death.

“Keep a close watch on these undead beings. They are all sacrifices for what comes next!” Cang Xu instructed the guards before departing the valley.

The guards hurriedly agreed, their expressions solemn.

Cang Xu had long secured Zhou Zhang's acquiescence. Claiming divine mandate, he had successfully persuaded Zhou Zhang to use undead lives as the primary sacrifices for the upcoming blood rites.

Returning to the believers' encampment, Cang Xu hadn't even reached his crude hut when a messenger arrived, Jian Gui sought an audience.

Jian Gui had been fabricating memories, preparing to perform a blasphemous prayer upon teleporting to the Mother Nest and slip through undetected.

He was indeed resourceful. Leveraging the regenerative advantage of necromancy, he plotted secretly under Zhou Zhang's nose, steadily advancing his self-rescue plan without Zhou Zhang ever suspecting a thing.

However, consecutive blood sacrifices during teleportations had captured both Xiao Wang Zi and Ku Feng, leaving them in a state of “deceived death.”

This drastically reduced Jian Gui's memory fabrication efficiency, but his earlier accumulation was sufficient. If his calculations held, he would barely manage to obtain a complete fabricated memory before his own turn arrived.

“This means Qi Ling has no choice but to take a risk.”

Cang Xu surmised that Jian Gui's reason for seeking him out was likely tied to Qi Ling.

Upon meeting, it proved to be precisely that matter!

Jian Gui requested Cang Xu's assistance in guiding Qi Ling's faith.

The conflict between Qi Ling and Cang Xu ran deep. Even under the crushing pressure of impending death, Qi Ling found it difficult to speak up and actively beg Cang Xu for mercy.

At this critical juncture, Jian Gui proactively stepped in as mediator.

He needed conflict between his subordinates to solidify his authority.

Yet simultaneously, he required his subordinates to “conflict without damage”, preventing their rivalry from escalating to a point that threatened his personal interests.

Thus, Jian Gui sought out Cang Xu for negotiation.

Cang Xu hastily declared: Jian Gui had done him a favor; such formality was unnecessary. Though he clashed with Qi Ling, since Jian Gui had spoken, it was no longer an issue.

His words carried genuine sincerity. He seemed to have completely forgotten the secret, that Jian Gui had tampered with him, setting a deadly trap!

Jian Gui was thoroughly satisfied with Cang Xu's attitude.

Cang Xu had embraced the faith of the Charming Blue Goddess, a necromancer and a believer, effectively straddling two sides. He'd been thriving during this period.

But Jian Gui remained convinced this was merely a facade. As long as he held the fatal switch, no matter how Cang Xu maneuvered, he could never escape his grasp. After all, Cang Xu's very life hung on his whim, what else could Jian Gui fear?

As Cang Xu guided Qi Ling in her faith, the fishman youngster composed himself and entered the prayer chamber.

This period of rest had allowed him to fully assimilate the divine blessings from his previous prayers.

The concentration of the Surging Wave fishman bloodline has risen from 79% to 86%. It's only a few steps away from the legend level cap.

The increase in bloodline concentration brought the fishman youngster comprehensive benefits.

Not only did it help him digest the divine blessings faster, but it also allowed him to absorb more divine content at once.

“As for divine favor, I already have over 20,000 points.”

Truth be told, ever since the fishman youngster gained divine favor, it had only grown, never diminishing.

The duelists on Ice Sculpture Island used their divine favor sparingly, counting every drop. But the fishman youngster possessed so much divine favor he couldn't spend it all.

His precise, targeted prayers required no expenditure of divine favor to obtain what he desired.

For the fishman youngster, the divine favor of the Charming Blue Goddess gradually became a mere number.

“Charming Blue Goddess, my Lord, I pay homage to You.”

“You are the master of deception, making the sea Your home. You hide truth within endless mist, moving effortlessly through illusions and enchantments, as fluid and mysterious as the tides.”

“You are the embodiment of lies and deceit, bewildering all beings in the world.”

“Your believer prays here, beseeching You to grant me extraordinary enhancement in battle qi and magic power. Let the silver level become a thing of the past, enabling me to embrace the arrival of the gold level.”

“Charming Blue Goddess, my Lord, may Your wisdom forever guide me, making me a part of Your grand endeavor.”

"Thank You for Your constant protection and care, oh Charming Blue Goddess. Let me break free from my cocoon and transform into a butterfly under Your power..."

Boom!

Before the fishman youngster could finish his prayer, a colossal divine power manifested above his head.

The Charming Blue divine power coalesced into a pillar of light, its volume now one-fifth larger than before.

The pillar washed over the fishman youngster from head to toe.

Gritting his teeth, the youngster was forced to halt his prayer momentarily, channeling all his focus to receive the divine blessing.

He had planned his blasphemous prayer in advance, mapping out three distinct paths. After careful deliberation, he opted for stability, prioritizing the advancement of both his fighter and magician levels to gold level.

His current life level was gold level, limiting the scale of divine power within him.

Possessing gold level fighter and magician levels would provide an effective counterbalance to the divine power. Additionally, he could utilize his Bloodcore to transform into his dragonman form, further resisting the subtle alterations the divine power wrought upon his body, mind, and soul.

The fishman youngster had once considered prioritizing his clerical position. But recently, his dreams had become more frequent. Each time, he glimpsed a shadow as vast as a mountain at the bottom of the great whirlpool, drawing ever closer.

After consulting with Cang Xu, he altered his plan, opting for stability first.

The pillar of divine light lasted longer than before, extending by roughly three minutes.

After the light completely dissipated, the fishman youngster kneeled on the ground, utterly drained. He struggled to his feet.

“This time, I hadn't even finished my prayer before receiving the divine blessing.”

“Charming Blue Goddess... is so eager?”

Surprise overshadowed joy on the fishman youngster's face.

This divine blessing hadn't granted him a qualitative leap in battle qi or magic power—instead, it bestowed a clerical position.

“I clearly prayed for a battle qi upgrade yet received a clerical position instead!”

“Divine emissary!”

“Did I skip straight past the level of sacred son?”

Chapter 809: Divine Spell – Believer Detection

The fishman youngster and Cang Xu had once discussed and estimated the likelihood of a clerical position promotion.

Their conclusion was: most likely sacred son, a small chance of Cardinal, and a minuscule possibility of Pope.

During his last prayer, the fishman youngster had already ascended to Archbishop, equivalent to Zhou Zhang.

To draw an analogy: an Archbishop was akin to a local noble or a powerful feudal lord. A Cardinal was an imperial high official, overseeing the daily operations of the church and able to command Archbishops across the lands to a certain extent.

The Pope was analogous to a king.

Yet, the outcome was...

"Divine emissary, acting in the name of the god, walking the world and exercising divine authority. How does this feel like a position even more powerful than the Pope?"

The fishman youngster muttered to himself.

“Regardless, I've finally obtained the divine spell, Divine Domain Descent!”

The fishman youngster's primary goal in advancing his clerical position was to acquire higher-level divine spells, enabling him to teleport away from the Ocean Mother Nest.

This time, instead of granting him promotion to gold level fighter, the divine blessing elevated his clerical position and bestowed numerous divine spells.

Foremost among them was the divine spell [Imbue Divine Might].

This passive divine spell had already been bestowed upon him during the prayer, imbued by a pillar of divine light.

With this divine spell, he could now wield a measure of divine might.

Divine spell - Divine Authority Execution.

This too was a passive divine spell.

Upon successful imbuement, the fishman youngster automatically received certain information.

He learned all the authorities of the Charming Blue Goddess. The primary authority was deception, the secondary was fishman, while the third and fourth were water and fishing, respectively.

The deception authority was already familiar to the fishman youngster.
