The fishman authority was a classic racial divine authority. Simply caring for the fishman, ensuring the growth and prosperity of their race, and ideally becoming their primary deity of worship, would bring benefits to the Charming Blue Goddess.

The water divine authority primarily concerned the transformations of water elements. This was a specialized branch within the Water God's duties. Generally, aquatic lifeforms ascending to divinity almost invariably possessed this specialized authority.

The fishing authority, as the name implied, pertains to the harvest of fish. This was a subcategory of the harvest authority.

After receiving this divine spell, the fishman youngster would gain benefits whenever he engaged in deception, protected fishmen, facilitated changes in water elements, or fished.

But exactly how much? The youngster had no idea. Unlike last time, this divine blessing didn't grant him experience, leaving him on the novice stage, still quite clueless.

Divine spell - Divine Perception.

Still a passive divine spell, it granted the fishman youngster a special perceptual ability.

With its blessing, he could sense divine presences within a certain range. This perception became exceptionally potent when detecting divinity related to divine authorities.

Only through personal experimentation would the fishman youngster gain deeper understanding.

Divine spell - Divine Domain Descent.

This was an active divine spell.

Its effect invoked a portion of the Charming Blue Divine Domain to descend near the believer.

“The space within the Ocean Mother Nest is a fragment of the Charming Blue Divine Domain.”

This truth had been uncovered by Cang Xu through inquiries with Zhou Zhang, and the fishman youngster now knew it too.

“But how do I use this spell to get out?”

Further experimentation was needed.

Divine spell - Divine Artifact Mastery!

An active divine spell.

Its effect was powerful yet unadorned: mastery over divine artifacts.

Divine artifacts were not for just anyone to wield.

Forged using the power of deities, their effects were peerless, yet this very power made them difficult for others to control.

Only the deity itself could truly unleash the full potential of a divine artifact.

The Jade Gold Coffin wielded by the survivors and the Mermaid Fairy Tale were both divine artifacts.

Even a legend level alchemist of the War Merchant's caliber, straining every ounce of strength and ingenuity, could only unlock a fraction of the Mermaid Fairy Tale's true power.

The fishman youngster was eager to unleash this divine spell, hoping to touch upon the Mermaid Fairy Tale and see what he could gain.

Divine spell - Divine Script Mastery.

Magic runes were tools that harnessed magic power. By analogy, divine scripts were akin to runes that utilized the corresponding divine power.

Each deity was unique, and their divine power was equally singular, thus the divine script derived from them were all different.

Of course, similar divine script did exist. But no matter how alike they appeared, their essence remained fundamentally different.

This was a passive divine spell.

Upon its activation, the fishman youngster instantly mastered the Charming Blue divine script with extraordinary proficiency.

“Now, I can effortlessly decipher the Charming Blue divine script within the great magic formation surrounding the Ocean Mother Nest.”

Beyond these, there were divine spells like [Divine Blessing], [Divine Revelation], [Divine Oracle], [Divine Descent], and more.

The total exceeded twenty.

This wasn't the largest number of Charming Blue divine spells the fishman youngster had ever received, but he was certain it was the highest quality set he'd ever obtained!

After studying them for a moment, the fishman youngster focused his attention on two specific divine spells.

The first divine spell - Divine Power Channel.

It allowed the caster to forge temporary conduits between certain faith coordinates.

These faith coordinates could be believers, saint beasts, or even divine statues, altars, sanctuaries, temples, and the like.

The second divine spell - Believer Perception.

Believers of Charming Blue Goddess could be sensed by the caster.

“First use the divine spell [Believer Perception], then employ [Divine Power Channel] to forge a temporary conduit. Finally, utilize spells like [Divine Blessing], [Divine Revelation], or [Divine Oracle] to bestow blessings upon the faithful.”

During his analysis, the fishman youngster realized he could seemingly impersonate Charming Blue Goddess to grant divine blessings to other believers of Charming Blue Goddess.

Theoretically, it was entirely feasible.

The fishman youngster began experimenting with divine spells.

As a divine emissary, his clerical position surpassed even that of the Pope in certain respects.

Thus, he possessed the authority to freely wield all Charming Blue divine spells he had mastered.

The youngster resolved to proceed from simplest to most complex, testing every divine spell in turn.

The easiest was Believer Perception.

After casting the divine spell, the divine power within the fishman youngster began to deplete.

His spiritual energy underwent an instantaneous transformation, radiating outward from his body like lightning. Its speed was astonishingly fast, like light itself. In the next moment, it had spread to a distance beyond the youngster's estimation.

Expanding, ceaselessly expanding.

The fishman youngster's perception seemed boundless, its scope terrifyingly vast.

"Maintaining the divine spell consumes less divine power. Though my mental energy undergoes a qualitative change under the technique, I can feel it draining rapidly. Overall, I can sustain this for about twenty minutes."

This was a unique dimension of perception.

Immersed in this perception, the fishman youngster felt as though he stood within a vast, dark void.

The size of this space expanded ceaselessly with his mental energy, spreading outward in all directions.

Within this dark expanse, every Charming Blue believer radiated a faint glow.

Some emitted a negligible flicker, others a white light akin to candles, while a few shone as brightly as alchemy lamps.

The area closest to the fishman youngster was empty. Beyond that, however, lay a vast expanse dotted with countless points of light, densely packed.

“I am now within the Ocean Mother Nest. Beyond the nest lies the army of Charming Blue believers. These points of light represent them.”

Among all this radiance, one light stood out most prominently, bright as a bulb.

That was Zhou Zhang.

The Charming Blue Archbishop!

The fishman youngster's focus sharpened on this point, his perception suddenly becoming tenfold clearer.

He could even sense that Zhou Zhang was handling affairs, listening to subordinates' reports. He could perceive the voices of those reporting, including mention of Cang Xu. They said that after returning to the gathering place, Cang Xu had rushed to Jian Gui's quarters, where he performed divine magic to guide Qi Ling's faith.

Using divine magic for the first time, the fishman youngster felt uncertain and dared not act rashly, fearing Zhou Zhang might detect his persistent observation.

He cautiously withdrew his attention. The next moment, he felt as though he had returned to that previous dark space.

He selected other points of light and continued his observation.

After a short while, the fishman youngster gained considerable insight.

“After focusing my attention, I can perceive the believers' actions at that moment and ‘hear’ their conversations.”

"Building on that, by concentrating further, I can sense the believers' psychological states. Not necessarily their inner thoughts, but mostly their moods and feelings."

"Concentrating even deeper allows me to perceive the deeper information within the believers. This information isn't actively revealed, yet I can still sense it. Primarily the believer's name, identity, attributes, transcendent level, their current vital signs, transcendent status, faith level, and so on."

“How marvelous.”

“Is this the perspective of a deity?”

He concentrated his attention once more, but nothing changed.

After observing more samples, the fishman youngster discovered three patterns.

"First, concentrating on extracting information intensifies the consumption of divine power and mental energy. "

"Second, when probing information about fishman believers, one can perceive their bloodline details. But examining octopusmen, sea elves, and others reveals no such data. Could this be tied to the Fishman God authority?"

"Third, the radiance emitted by believers results from multiple factors, not solely faith intensity. Some fanatical believers with weak transcendent strength emit only a faint white glow. Yet a saint level Archbishop like Zhou Zhang, whose faith level is merely pious, shines as brightly as a light bulb."

After gaining proficiency, the fishman youngster felt confident and observed Zhou Zhang again.

Zhou Zhang remained completely unaware.

The fishman youngster made an intriguing discovery—the mighty Archbishop's faith level was merely pious!

But he quickly understood.

“Above piousness lies fanaticism.”

"Fanatics possess unwavering faith, driven to reckless devotion. If the god commands death, they would commit suicide instantly, without hesitation."

“This is a complete loss of reason.”

“And should anyone slight the deity, fanatics often transform into violent mobs, seeking vengeance and retribution at all costs.”

“They're essentially potential criminals!”

“How could a fanatic possibly serve as Archbishop, handling daily affairs, representing the Charming Blue Church in dealings with other forces?”

“A fanatic as Archbishop would be a catastrophe for the Charming Blue Church.”

Thus, pious level faith was precisely the right balance.

Of course, above fanaticism was sainthood.

Believers at this level possess both fervent devotion and sound reason, the most valuable treasures among the faithful.

“However, if Zhou Zhang were sainthood level, coupled with her saint level power, she would never settle for a mere Archbishop position.”

After becoming a divine emissary, the position of Archbishop seemed nothing more than “mere” to the fishman youngster.

Chapter 810: Xiao Guai, Father has Come to See You!

Merely by sensing this divine spell through the believers, the fishman youngster gained profound insights into both the believers and the deity upon his first use.

He focused his attention and observed Cang Xu again, discovering that he was guiding faith for Qi Ling.

“Qi Ling holds no faith in the Charming Blue Goddess. Within the range of my divine art's perception, there is not a single speck of light.”

“Under normal circumstances, I wouldn't be able to observe her.”

“Only now, with Cang Xu in close contact, can I detect her presence.”

“Looking at it this way, establishing a bond of faith with a deity makes one perceptible!”

“Wait, could I use this opportunity, with Cang Xu as the bridge, to influence Qi Ling?”

Theoretically, it was feasible.

The divine spells Cang Xu wielded could guide Qi Ling to channel her spiritual energy, delivering it to the Charming Blue Goddess.

Thus, the fishman youngster's divine emissary status allowed him to communicate with Qi Ling.

“Qi Ling isn't a believer, but Cang Xu serves as the crucial bridge, enabling me to exert direct influence.”

At that moment, the fishman youngster suddenly realized just how vital Cang Xu's role was at this juncture!

“Cang Xu's unique divine spells can selectively alter the faith of certain sentient beings.”

“Used effectively, this holds immense potential for expanding the entire Charming Blue Church!”

“No wonder Zhou Zhang holds Cang Xu in such high regard, clearing the way for him. Even Cang Xu's demand to sacrifice vast numbers of undead creatures was swiftly approved by Zhou Zhang.”

In that instant, the fishman youngster truly grasped Cang Xu's unique value.

After some thought, he ultimately refrained from seizing the opportunity to influence Qi Ling.

On one hand, he feared that displaying divine miracles might alarm Jian Gui, prompting him to react violently.

The current situation was ideal, its continued development would greatly benefit both the fishman youngster and Cang Xu.

On the other hand, he didn't want Qi Ling to believe she had changed her faith, gained Charming Blue Goddess' approval, and could survive the blood sacrifice.

Qi Ling's fate had already been decided by the fishman youngster and Cang Xu—she wouldn't be killed by them, but would become a captive, deceived into thinking she was dead, sharing the same fate as Qing Xin, Ku Feng, Xiao Wang Zi, and Lady Sea Snake.

With that, the Deep Sea Monster Fish now held four silver level captives.

The fishman youngster continued observing Cang Xu, focusing his attention on the third layer.

He had also extracted the deeper information embedded within Cang Xu.

“No trace of the Hanging Corpse Tree bloodline, even though I already know the answer.”

“Cang Xu has been imbued with the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, yet the information remains unaltered and undisguised.”

“Perhaps because I am already privy to the truth?”

“Or could it be that this spell combination cannot deceive the Believer Perception divine spell?”

“If so, is it that Charming Blue Goddess is unique, or do other deities possess such abilities?”

The fishman youngster pondered for a moment, concluding that the combination of the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell should be capable of resisting the Believer Perception.

Otherwise, their tracks would have been discovered long ago.

Since nearly all survivors were believers, they could be perceived by the deities they worshipped. If deities could extract deep information from their believers, they should be able to uncover the survivors' secrets, exposing the two divine artifacts on Mystifying Monster Island.

Yet, to this day, the survivors lived well, free from joint manhunts by major and minor deities or religious sects. Inverse reasoning suggests the artifacts remain undiscovered. The combination of the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell effectively masked their presence, countering the Believer Perception divine spell.

The fishman youngster surmised: Charming Blue Goddess must be unique. After all, the core of this spell combination was the Deceptive Disguise spell, and its source originated from Charming Blue Goddess' divine artifact.

“Thankfully, Charming Blue Goddess remains dormant, trapped in slumber.”

“Otherwise, the day I arrived at the Ocean Mother Nest would have been my last!”

"For us survivors, the Charming Blue Goddess poses an immense threat. Before Her, the Deceptive Disguise spell proves utterly useless."

This revelation held no surprise for the fishman youngster. Before this, he and Cang Xu had already suspected as much and confirmed it once.

The fishman youngster refocused his attention, observing the other Charming Blue believers.

He paid particular attention to the stronger ones among them.

For instance, the two known as Jiu Hua Bi and Zhang Yu Wan.

After continuously gathering intelligence, the fishman youngster concluded these two were the strongest Charming Blue believers aside from Zhou Zhang.

“Truly worthy of being Zhou Zhang's right-hand men.”

“Both are fighters possessing formidable aquatic prowess. Their battle skills and battle qi complement each other perfectly, making them exceptionally skilled in aquatic combat.”

“Their Charming Blue faith is at the pious level.”

“But I still can't detect their bloodline. It's likely because they aren't fishmen, but octopusmen.”

The fishman youngster kept trying, guessing and speculating throughout the process.

“Next time I pray, I should ask for relevant usage experience.”

After scanning the believers around him, the fishman youngster began attempting to “see” into the distance.

“Just how far can this perception reach?” the fishman youngster questioned inwardly.

As he observed figures like Zhou Zhang and Cang Xu, his sensory range kept expanding without pause, not for a single second.

It spread and spread, seemingly boundless.

But the fishman youngster knew full well there was a limit. At least his own mental energy was one constraint.

Once his mental energy depleted. no, even reaching a certain low point, it would leave him dizzy and drowsy, drifting into sleep. Pushing further would trigger his body's self-preservation instinct, plunging the fishman youngster into a deep slumber.

The fishman youngster “gazed” toward the farthest reaches.

Beyond the Ocean Mother Nest, near-total darkness prevailed, with only rare specks of light appearing.

“Charming Blue Goddess has slumbered for ages, and the Charming Blue Church faces relentless suppression. The situation is dire...”

Through the unique perception granted by divine magic, the fishman youngster gained clearer insight into the current state of the Charming Blue Church.

This had nothing to do with his immediate surroundings.

In fact, the oceanic environment was remarkably conducive to spreading the Charming Blue Goddess' faith.

The sea teemed with intelligent beings, such as sea elves, nagas, sea giants, lobstermen, and more. The most numerous and largest in scale were the fishmen.

Perhaps this explained why Charming Blue Goddess held the Fishman God authority.

Possessing such an authority made it remarkably convenient to recruit believers, spread the faith, and expand the divine domain's influence.

The state of the Charming Blue Church was dire. Beyond the White Hair Trench, it was practically a wasteland.

Only occasionally could scattered points of light be found.

Oh?

The fishman youngster had no sense of actual distance. Just as he was about to retreat, he suddenly spotted a unique believer's light point.

This light point shone brightly in the vast darkness, radiating a glow akin to an alchemy light bulb. To be precise, its luminosity fell short of Zhou Zhang but surpassed gold level believers like Zhang Yu Wan and Jiu Hua Bi.

The moment the fishman youngster saw it, he sensed this believer's light was different.

“Why does this believer feel so familiar and comforting?”

This sensation was unlike any Charming Blue believer he'd encountered before.

It felt like... one of his own.

Immediately, the fishman youngster focused his attention on it.

The next moment, shock rippled through his heart: “Xiao Guai? How could it be Xiao Guai?”

He had discovered the big guy!

Magnet Archipelago.

After failing to challenge Da Dou, the big guy had been knocked unconscious and fed a filling meal.

Da Dou hadn't immediately stowed the big guy into his storage bag. The reason was that the packing bag was special, excelling at preserving ingredients and maintaining freshness.

Once the big guy entered, his entire being was treated as an ingredient. Anything he consumed would be preserved, severely hindering his digestion.

The environment inside the storage bag also drastically reduced the big guy's efficiency in cultivating his battle qi.

Da Dou sat beside the giant, eating dry rations and drinking water to replenish his stamina and battle qi while also praying. He remained vigilant and protective.

After all, they were in the wilderness.

In his deep sleep, the big guy was plagued by nightmares.

On a colossal sea vessel, he was no longer a giant's form but had shrunk to the size of a normal infant.

The ship was immense, its deck as vast as a city.

The surroundings were gloomy, shrouded in mist that formed an endless fog.

The big guy could hear waves crashing against the hull. He ran across the vast deck, wailing, stumbling and falling as he fled.

As he ran, the solid deck beneath his feet suddenly softened, gradually transforming into a curved arc.

The arc became a curve, then dissolved into the sea.

The giant thumped into the sea and began swimming.

Calling out “Father” as he swam, he drifted aimlessly, searching for his home and the dragonman youngster.

The divine spell [Believer Perception] allowed the fishman youngster to discern the big guy's mental state, revealing this nightmare-like scene within his perception.

“So, when a dreaming believer is perceived by divine magic, the deity can also see the scenes within their dream.”

“Xiao Guai... he misses me.”

For a moment, the fishman youngster felt both touched and guilty.

Before leaving, he hadn't properly bid farewell to the big guy. The situation had been urgent back then, chasing after Lan Zao.

“He's been gone for so long. It's only natural Xiao Guai sees me as his sole support and seeks me out.”

“Thankfully, the others know Xiao Guai is my son. They must have been looking after him all this time.”

“Ah, I wonder how Lan Zao is faring now?”

There was nothing he could do. The dragonman youngster had truly given his all back then, killed once by Feng Lian's slash, then suddenly teleported straight into the Ocean Mother Nest.

Only now had he finally obtained the divine spell [Divine Domain Descent], securing a way out.

Before, leaving meant directly breaking through the Mother Nest, far too dangerous! Later, he learned this place was still part of Charming Blue Goddess' divine domain. He was relieved he hadn't forced his way through.

But to leave using the divine spell [Divine Domain Descent], the fishman youngster still needed time to experiment and explore this unfamiliar high level divine spell.

“The big guy never worshipped the Charming Blue Goddess. Why can I sense his presence with the Believer Perception divine spell?”

“Let's put that question aside for now.”

Divine spell - Divine Power Channel!

Gazing at the nightmare before him, the fishman youngster felt a surge of inspiration and activated another divine spell.

In the next instant, the divine power within him accelerated its consumption, weaving a wondrous energy conduit.

The conduit connected the big guy and the fishman youngster.

Thus, the fishman youngster descended into the big guy's dream.

In the dim nightmare world, a patch of mist suddenly shifted to a deep blue.

Then, a gale swept through, coalescing the mist into a colossal humanoid form, the likeness of the dragonman youngster.

To accommodate the big guy, the fishman youngster deliberately transformed his appearance into the familiar image from the big guy's memories.

“Father?”

Xiao Guai stared in shock at the enormous dragonman youngster standing directly before him.

“Father, Xiao Guai was looking for you!”

“Father, you're so big!”

As he came to his senses, the big guy cried out in joyful surprise, tears streaming down his face.

He danced excitedly yet did not drown.

As his state of mind shifted, the undulating lines of seawater enveloping him straightened, transforming back into the deck's form.

Xiao Guai stepped onto the deck, spread his arms wide, and ran toward the mist surrounding the dragonman youngster.

He grew larger with each stride, his form swiftly reverting to that of a giant.

The dragonman youngster smiled, extending his right index finger and slowly reaching toward the giant.

The mist-shrouded youngster's physique was mountainous, and his single finger was larger than the giant himself.

As the finger touched the big guy, the youngster spoke softly, “Xiao Guai, Father's come to see you.”

“Father... Father!” The giant clutched the youngster's finger, wailing with overwhelming sorrow.

“Father, Xiao Guai... missed you!”

“Father, let's go.”

“Take Xiao Guai with you!”

Seeing the giant's emotional state, the fishman youngster sighed deeply, his heart filled with tenderness.

Suddenly, he understood the truth.

“Xiao Guai isn't a Charming Blue believer. He never was, and he isn't now.”

“His true devotion... is to me.”

“He is my... fanatical believer!”

Chapter 811: The Furious Youngster – Da Dou Kidnapped My Son!

Believers could be categorized into roughly five levels based on their faith.

Casual believers, ordinary believers, pious believers, fanatical believers, and sainthood.

What defined a fanatical believer?

Fanatical believers pursued their object of worship with extreme zeal, reaching the height of fervor. They believed that only by worshipping their deity could they attain salvation or blessing.

They disregarded all rational thought, dedicating themselves wholly. Even if their deity commanded them to sacrifice themselves, they would obey without hesitation.

Moreover, they could not tolerate any criticism of their deity.

Typically, they would respond with persuasion, curses, or outright aggression.

For particularly egregious offenses, they would transform into violent mobs, launching brutal attacks on their perceived enemies.

When the dragonman youngster first discovered the big guy was his fanatic, he felt puzzled and bewildered.

But as he pondered deeper, he gradually understood.

By the standards of fanatics, if the big guy wasn't his fanatic, then what else could he be?

After the old shipwright's death, the big guy became utterly convinced: this dragonman youngster was the kindest person in the world to him, and his only family!

Whenever the youngster faced danger, the big guy would instantly plunge into a frenzied rage, throwing himself recklessly into protecting him, fighting to the death for him!

“Why did he become my fanatic?”

Reflecting on the big guy's life story, the fishman youngster gradually gained some insight.

The big guy had been abandoned as an infant, but luckily, he found care under the old shipwright.

As he grew, he proved to be mentally impaired, occasionally losing his mind.

Naturally, those around him treated him with contempt, disgust, and exclusion.

The old shipwright was the sole light in the big guy's life. He raised him through thick and thin, tirelessly caring for him, fighting for his right to exist, and even teaching him to swim.

The dragonman youngster understood the full extent of the giant's intellectual limitations. He admired the old shipwright's achievement in teaching him to swim, finding it almost unimaginable.

It was precisely because of the old shipwright that the giant, living in such a harsh environment, retained a core of kindness.

The old shipwright's relentless efforts to protect the confined, unique living space aboard the sea vessel, combined with the big guy's own mortality—unable to match those of the extraordinary... These factors collectively shaped the big guy into a timid and fearful character. Yet simultaneously, the violent elements in his bloodline lay dormant. When provoked, the big guy would erupt into fury, fighting with all his might to kill enemies or defend what mattered most to him.

This trait was vividly displayed on Mystifying Monster Island. To protect his father, the big guy pushed the ship forward himself, charging into the beast tide and willingly sacrificing himself.

He was closest to the old shipwright, while his relationships with others remained distant. This near-total isolation meant the big guy's personal world was incredibly narrow. The old shipwright was his entire world, the sole meaning of his existence.

But on Mystifying Monster Island, the old shipwright died.

Before his final breath, he entrusted the big guy to the young knight.

The old shipwright knew his significance to the big guy. He feared the big guy might lose hope and desperately wanted someone to care for him.

This act proved profoundly successful!

The young knight became his new father, taking the old shipwright's place. The old shipwright had judged correctly. The youngster possessed the spirit of a knight, honest, forgiving, kind, and held compassion for the big guy. He never abandoned him because of his dullness or moments of madness.

He never even felt disgust.

Not once!

Not even for a single second!

The young knight always kept the big guy by his side, protecting and caring for him. Perhaps his care wasn't always perfect or thorough.

But the youngster's intentions were genuine and his efforts sincere.

He had clashed with Bing Xiao to secure the giant-type intelligence potion for the big guy. He spared no effort to help the big guy master the Mindless Sleeping Posture Technique.

Though intellectually impaired, the big guy could feel the youngster's affection!

Precisely because of this, the big guy accepted the youngster from the bottom of his heart. He clung fiercely to the old shipwright's words: the youngster was his father, the sole meaning of his existence, the sky sheltering him, the sea embracing him, the earth bearing him.

The youngster was his sky, his earth, his sea, his entire world!

To him, the wider world was alien, even hostile, only his father was worthy of trust.

Given this mindset, it was only natural that the big guy became a fanatical believer.

With the youngster's arrival, the big guy's nightmares transformed into sweet dreams.

Massive mists coalesced into the form of the dragonman youngster, towering like mountains and peaks. The youngster's finger alone was thicker than the big guy's entire arm.

The big guy had already plopped down onto the deck, wrapping both arms around the dragonman youngster's finger and pressing his face tightly against it, weeping uncontrollably.

He sobbed uncontrollably, snot running freely.

It was as if he were a child lost outside, terrified for so long, desperately waiting for his parents to find him.

Now, the long-awaited father he'd called out for so many times had finally appeared. The poor child found immense comfort.

“Father, save me, save me.”

“Xiao Guai, danger!”

“Bad guy.”

“Bad guy!”

“Xiao Guai wants to go home.”

“But… can't go back!”

Amidst the big guy's wails, the fishman youngster's expression gradually shifted.

This was his first attempt at advanced divine magic. As a novice, he hadn't grasped how to gauge distance or direction between himself and his believer.

Thus, at first, he'd assumed the big guy was still in Snow Bird Harbor.

But now, he slowly began to detect something amiss.

The dragonman youngster had been away from the group for some time and was unaware of the events that had unfolded.

He had assumed that after his departure, the business plan had progressed smoothly, everything running orderly, even peaceful.

But now, from the big guy's broken, sobbing words, he sensed something was terribly wrong.

“Enemies threatening Xiao Guai?”

“What's going on?”

“Isn't Xiao Guai with everyone?”

“Perhaps he's referring to the nightmare encounter?”

The fishman youngster felt perplexed and grew tense.

He had to confirm.

The next moment, his focus shifted to the third layer.

Based on his previous experience with divine spells, he summarized the following:

With the first layer of attention, he could perceive light points. Each light point represented a believer.

The second layer allowed him to observe believers' mental activities or emotions.

The third layer enabled him to dig deeper for information.

The fishman youngster did this solely to gather more intelligence, aiding his ability to make the right judgment.

Soon, he sensed the various deep-seated information contained within the big guy.

“The big guy's strength has reached silver level? How did he progress so fast?”

“He's still cultivating the Mindless Sleeping Posture battle qi technique. Good lad.”

“His battle qi is exceptionally abundant now.”

“Not a single injury on his body.”

"This recovery ability... surpasses even my dragonman form. Truly worthy of the giant bloodline."

“How come he's got a huge amount of food in his stomach?”

The fishman youngster felt both delighted and relieved by the big guy's progress, yet more questions arose.

The information he received indicated that the big guy had eaten to his fill, and the food was exceptionally high level.

The silver level magic meals he consumed were downgraded from gold level, the most suitable sustenance for the big guy's cultivation!

From this perspective, the big guy lived even better than he did!

“I haven't obtained the big guy's bloodline, lifespan, or other details.”

“Wait, it seems I can delve deeper into the information?”

After deeply sensing the information, the fishman youngster discovered he still had reserves. Unlike when he'd sensed other Charming Blue believers before, where he'd hit a ceiling, now, with the big guy, he felt he could uncover more.

“Let's try!”

The fishman youngster attempted to focus his attention even more intensely.

Success!

The fourth layer of concentration allowed him to access the big guy's memories.

His memories unfolded like magic images, scene after scene.

Only they played in reverse, always from the big guy's first-person perspective.

Thus, the fishman youngster first witnessed the big guy opening his eyes. Through blurred vision, he saw Da Dou. Hearing Da Dou speak, the big guy unleashed a furious strike. Then, the big guy and Da Dou engaged in a fierce battle. Before long, he was knocked unconscious by Da Dou.

The memory continued to rewind.

The fishman youngster witnessed Da Dou leading the big guy to flee to an unnamed deserted island.

The big guy leaped into the sea to swim, Da Dou pursued him, and then the Honey Snow Tower suddenly appeared.

“Speed it up,” the fishman youngster thought, and sure enough, the rewind of memories accelerated.

He saw the big guy discover that “Zong Ge” (the alchemy puppet) had been ambushed and killed. Enraged, the big guy plunged into a berserk state, chasing the enemy all the way out.

Then came the big guy's bewildered arrival at Snow Bird Harbor's duel arena, where he participated in the eating contest between Da Dou and Ci Hai Shen, playing a crucial role in the proceedings.

The memory rewound once more.

The big guy conversed with Tripleblade. His training had yielded results, leaving him increasingly ravenous.

The big guy's memories came in fragmented segments.

Many truths remained hidden, even though the big guy was the direct point of view.

This was because during the battle between Da Dou and Shuang Lian, Da Dou had sealed the big guy inside a packing bag early on. The big guy was also frequently knocked unconscious. In a state of unconsciousness, he couldn't perceive the outside world, hence the fragmented memories.

Similarly, when Tripleblade struck the big guy three times, aiming to disrupt his physical memory of cultivating battle qi, the big guy held no awareness of this either. Thus, the fishman youngster witnessed none of it.

Even so, the youngster gleaned much from these fragments!

“The Zong Ge he saw was killed by an enemy ambush. He was actually impersonating me, so the one impersonating him was merely an alchemy puppet."

“The reason Xiao Guai was abducted by Da Dou was likely because Da Dou recognized his innate talent.”

The fishman youngster now knew Da Dou's name.

Memories of the eating contest were filled with spectators' discussions about Ci Hai Shen and Da Dou.

"Da Dou, you dared to kidnap my son!!" At that moment, the fishman youngster was extremely angry.

Chapter 812: Looking for Charming Blue Believers

“Xiao Guai is waiting for me, waiting for me to save him.”

“I absolutely must rescue Xiao Guai!”

At that moment, the fishman youngster made up his mind.

He wished he could fly to Xiao Guai's side immediately, but that was clearly unrealistic.

“How do I use the Divine Domain Descent?”

He'd have to experiment.

But the environment the fishman youngster now inhabited was rather peculiar.

“The Ocean Mother Nest is already part of the Charming Blue Divine Domain. What effect would using it here have?”

Employing the Divine Domain Descent within the divine domain itself seemed redundant.

What troubled the fishman youngster even more was the fear that triggering a massive reaction might awaken the slumbering Charming Blue Goddess, that would be disastrous!

“If the deity were roused, all the efforts I and the other survivors have made would be utterly wasted.”

The closer one drew to the deity, the more profoundly one felt the god's power—vast, boundless, and unimaginable.

The fishman youngster had only briefly tested the divine spell Believer Perception, yet it had revealed countless secrets. Through a god's eyes, one was as transparent as glass.

“Against other gods, we could rely on the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell. But against the Charming Blue Goddess? That would be extremely difficult.”

The fishman youngster suppressed his rage and continued analyzing the clues within the big guy's memories.

“That mage tower, I know it. It must be the Honey Snow Tower!”

Even before Bu Quan joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, no, even before she was invited—the dragonman youngster had gathered extensive intelligence on them.

Among them, Bu Quan's mentor, Shuang Lian, was a focal point.

Honey Snow Tower was also extensively covered.

Armed with this knowledge, the fishman youngster easily deduced the truth: “It must be that Shuang Lian received Bu Quan's plea for help, piloted the mage tower to assist us, and intended to help us reclaim the big guy.”

“Unfortunately, she failed.”

“Strange. With a mage tower deployed, how could Da Dou escape?”

“Seems he may be a magic chef, but his combat skills are formidable, at the very least, he possesses exceptional escape abilities!”

On this point, the fishman youngster's analysis was mistaken.

Da Dou's escape was pure luck.

At the time, Shuang Lian was under attack by the duelists and momentarily forgot about Da Dou, allowing him to regain his freedom.

The big guy remained unaware.

He had been sealed inside the packing bag earlier, missing both Da Dou's freezing by Shuang Lian and the disappearance of the Honey Snow Tower's teleportation.

“Most likely, Shuang Lian is still pursuing Da Dou.”

“The others know the big guy's potential. They'd never abandon a future powerhouse destined for at least the saint level.”

“Even if I can't escape immediately, I can relay this crucial intelligence to others, indirectly alerting Shuang Lian!”

The fishman youngster's eyes lit up at this thought.

As a gold level magician who had deployed the mage tower, Shuang Lian possessed abundant resources. There was a chance she could rescue the big guy.

Of course, the fishman youngster didn't pin all his hopes on Shuang Lian.

From what he knew: Shuang Lian had already failed once.

Therefore, his greater hope was that Shuang Lian would remain tied to Da Dou's trail, while he positioned himself as a surprise force. He aimed to arrive at the scene at a crucial moment in the future and intervene personally!

“I can now use the divine spells [Believer Perception] and [Divine Power Channel]. With these two, I can definitely send out a message.”

“If Bu Quan or Shuang Lian held faith in the Charming Blue Goddess, it would be much easier.”

“Unfortunately, though Bu Quan is deeply grateful to me, her gratitude falls far short of true devotion.”

The fishman youngster's reasoning was clear, his approach to problem-solving was decisive, he possessed the instincts of a natural leader. Faced with challenges, he never considered going it alone. Instead, he sought to leverage the strength of the team to increase their chances of success.

“First, we need to pinpoint Xiao Guai's current location. Then, by combining that with the position of the White Hair Trench, we can determine the direction.”

“This will give us the approximate direction of Ice Sculpture Island, allowing us to search in that area.”

“We'll comb through the entire island, find believers of the Charming Blue Goddess, deliver the divine decree, and use them to contact our other companions.”

The fishman youngster once again delved into the big guy's memories, locating the most recent segment.

The big guy battled Da Dou. Though his focus was entirely on Da Dou at the time, the surrounding scenery remained etched in his memory.

“Almost entirely yellowish-brown rocky hills and mounds, not a speck of vegetation. Not a single island, but a cluster of smaller islands, an archipelago landscape.”

The fishman youngster's heart swelled with joy.

Because this terrain was rather unique, the more distinctive it was, the more likely clues could be found.

He himself was unfamiliar with the Magnet Archipelago, but let's not forget he was aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish. And on this ship, there was also the tower spirit.

The tower spirit held War Merchant's database.

Though incomplete, the database still contained a vast formation of information. Nautical charts formed part of this collection.

“All thanks to War Merchant!” the fishman youngster exclaimed upon discovering the answer within the database. “So, this is Magnet Archipelago.”

The intelligence gathered far exceeded the fishman youngster's expectations.

“Who knew it was such a renowned archipelago?”

After reviewing the data, the fishman youngster distilled the three most critical pieces of intelligence concerning the islands.

First, the Magnet Archipelago was rich in earth elements, hosting vast numbers of earth elementals and a small quantity of magnetite elementals. The archipelago constantly radiated powerful magnetic force, severely affecting most metals.

Second, the cruise ship Golden sank within the Magnet Archipelago. This occurred roughly three thousand years ago and involved the Fifth Pirate King. The Pirate King feigned defeat before his archrival, luring the cruise ship Golden to the Magnet Archipelago. He exploited the magnetic force to weaken the enemy's combat strength, then launched a fierce assault, ultimately sinking the vessel.

However, the Pirate King did not have time to salvage the ship before the archrival's reinforcements arrived. Upon returning later, the sunken cruise ship Golden had vanished without a trace.

People speculate that this colossal vessel, nearly made entirely of gold, was drawn deep into the ocean floor of the Magnet Archipelago by magnetic forces. Thus, countless descendants have journeyed here, attempting to uncover the golden ship.

Yet, to this day, no one has reported discovering this immense treasure.

The third piece of intelligence: The Legendary Death Match. Two thousand years ago, two legend level individuals clashed upon the Magnet Archipelago.

One hailed from the Barbarian clan—the fallen druid Liao Ye. The other was a beastman, the high priest of that era, Hao Sang. Their powers were evenly matched, and both ultimately perished in battle here. Legend had it that each left behind a legacy of their power here. Before dying, they vowed to use these legacies to forge exceptional successors who would duel to determine the true victor.

Yet to this day, neither legacy has been found.

"Thus, the Magnet Archipelago became a haunt for treasure hunters, attracting visitors year-round. During certain seasons, the influx of people is overwhelming."

“Damn.”

“Da Dou likely came here to exploit the Magnet Archipelago's established transport routes to move himself and Xiao Guai.”

The fishman youngster felt a surge of urgency.

Within the Believer Perception's view, though only black and white existed, the relative positions of the believer corresponded to reality.

Using War Merchant's data, the fishman youngster pinpointed the relative positions of the White Hair Trench and the Magnet Archipelago, easily deducing the approximate direction of Ice Sculpture Island.

He then extended his perception along that path.

The results were dismal.

Desolate darkness stretched everywhere, revealing no trace of any Charming Blue believer.

Until he detected a faint glimmer of faith.

Focusing his attention there, he was disappointed to find that this devout level believer was a fishman shaman hired by an undersea merchant fleet.

After gathering and comparing the shaman's information, the fishman youngster quickly realized the shaman's location had already passed far beyond Ice Sculpture Island.

“This is bad.”

“There's not a single Charming Blue believer on Ice Sculpture Island?”

“Surrounded by sea on all sides, not a single one?”

“Seems the Charming Blue Church is truly in dire straits. The believers around me must be among its main forces.”

Though the fishman youngster had anticipated this grim conclusion, the actual confirmation still brought a wave of disappointment.

However, shortly after, he did discover a Charming Blue believer near the Magnet Archipelago.

On the Treasure Chest.

Tan Mo knocked on the hatch and reported to Tu Ji, “Lord, we're only ten nautical miles from the Magnet Archipelago.”

Tu Ji glanced at Tan Mo, his expression unreadable. "I can land anytime. Are you certain Hong Zhu is still there?"

Tan Mo hastily replied, “Absolutely. Our spies on the island confirm Hong Zhu hasn't departed.”

Tu Ji nodded, saying nothing more.

Tan Mo sensibly closed the hatch again.

He plotted silently: “Once Tu Ji lands on Magnet Archipelago, my moment will come.”

“With the Ice Sculpture Kingdom holding its National Ceremony, pirates will gather in droves. I can frame them to eliminate both Dao Hen and Hun Tong!”

Within the prison cell.

Dao Hen closed his eyes, praying silently.

“Fishman God, whichever one you may be, I implore you to hear my plea.”

“If you can save me and show me a way to escape, I, Dao Hen, will devote my entire life to you!”

Dao Hen was a silver level fishman, a renowned swordsman within his kind.

After being invited to participate in the Battle of Two Eyes Island, he was defeated and captured. He has been held captive by Hun Tong ever since, serving as evidence to counter the Empire's investigation.

After Hun Tong arrived here, orchestrated by Yan Yi, Dao Hen was turned and became the key witness framing Hun Tong.

The frame-up succeeded. Dao Hen witnessed the entire process and felt a deep sense of satisfaction.

Yet he also understood his own death was inevitable.

Unlike him, Hun Tong still failed to grasp the gravity of the situation, stubbornly trying to prove his innocence to the Empire.

Dao Hen certainly did not want to die.

Yet he was guarded so tightly that he had no strength to escape alone.

Trapped in this desperate situation, he could only turn to the gods in prayer.

Even though he knew there was a high chance of receiving no answer. But as a warrior, he possessed an unyielding will, so he prayed with all his might.

Don't overlook a crucial factor: one of the authorities of Charming Blue Goddess was a racial authority, the Fishman God.

Perhaps the pressure of impending death made him more devout.

Perhaps it was fate's subtle arrangement.

In any case, the fishman youngster discovered Dao Hen.

Chapter 813: Saving Dao Hen

The fishman youngster felt baffled, “Why is Dao Hen here?”

He clearly remembered that as his former enemy on Two Eyes Island, Dao Hen had been taken prisoner, why was he here?

The fishman youngster pondered over it while focusing his attention.

The first level of concentration allowed him to find Dao Hen.

The second level of concentration allowed him to learn that Dao Hen was scared of death and was chanting a litany deep within his heart.

The third level of concentration allowed him to learn of Dao Hen’s strength, current condition, and other facets of information.

The fourth level of concentration didn’t add anything else.

But the fishman youngster had surmised why, and he decisively used the divine spell – Divine Power Channel!

After the profound passage constructed from divine power was created, he and Dao Hen formed an instantaneous, space crossing channel between them.

Dao Hen sensed this anomaly and couldn’t help but shudder emotionally.

“God, I sense God’s gaze!”

“Heavens, a God really answered me!!”

Dao Hen normally had average faith, or to be more accurate, he was a believer who believed in a large swath of mainstream fishmen gods.

“Which grand existence answered me though?” At that moment, Dao Hen sensed the fishman youngster’s form, causing many doubts to arise from the bottom of his heart.

It was a whirlpool in an ocean, and in the depths of the ocean there was an immense shadow as high as a mountain that leaked out the boundless aura of the gods.

Normally speaking, due to the divine power communication channel, Dao Hen could see the fishman youngster’s appearance.

The fishman youngster naturally wouldn’t expose what he looked like, he instead did the same thing when he linked up with the big guy and decided to change his form.

As for why he picked this form, it was because it came from the nightmare that had haunted him.

“We have such an existence as a fishman god?” Dao Hen was bewildered with reverence when he obtained this answer in his heart.

It was the information the fishman youngster had transmitted to him.

“A Fish God, so it’s actually a Fish God!!” Dao Hen’s misgivings vanished in an instant, “Indeed, there is a God named Mist among the Fish Gods.”

Dao Hen guessed that the fishman Gods who were gazing at him was the three most mainstream ones.

They were the Ocean Guardian Cube Shell, the Star Crown Ink Concubine, and the Deep Sea Wisdom from Poetry.

Before becoming a god, Ocean Guardian Cube Shell was a fishman with a giant bloodline. Thus, their body was massive, and their scales gleamed. Cube Shell had three authorities as an Ocean God, a Defender God, and a Fishman God. They could raise tsunamis and gigantic waves, while also being responsible for protecting all kinds of lifeforms in the ocean from harm.

Fishmen shaman in Cube Shell’s church were experts at defensive type divine spells.

Star Crown Ink Concubine was a cuttlefish magic beast who became a god. They had three water attribute authorities as a fishman and magic beast god, a stealth god, and a water god. Their fishmen shamans were experts at controlling ocean magic beasts, spraying out ink shields, and changing water quality, ala capable of purifying and polluting water.

Wisdom from Poetry was a mermaid who before becoming a god was a wandering poet. She had gray scales and penetrating eyes that could see the secrets of the ocean and passed them on to other living beings. She was also a deep sea sage that often imparted the wisdom to survive in the depths to other lifeforms and also rescued some deep sea explorers.

Wisdom from Poetry learned all kinds of knowledge from the ocean, including how deep sea organisms behaved, how coral reefs grew, among other rules. She also used deep sea energy to heal wounded ocean creatures and guided lost people to her deep sea mystifying temple.

“I didn’t expect the Fish God Mist to answer me.”

Dao Hen used all of his strength to recall what he knew of Mist.

“The Fish God Mist’s origins are mysterious, some say they were a giant magic beast fish who became a god, others say they were a deep sea piranha who happened to devour some part of a god’s body and obtained divinity by a fluke.”

In Dao Hen’s memory, the Fish God Mist mainly instructed magic beast fish who had gained intelligence. Of course, fishmen also had people who believed in Mist, they, for the most part, were mysterious, ruthless, and hid in secret places.

“The God Mist really likes experts, and the fishmen who believe in them are generally powerful and unconventional.”

Dao Hen found a reason for why a god was watching him.

“From now on, I, Dao Hen, will serve you with all I have, my God, my grand Fish God Mist…”

Dao Hen began to pray again.

Before he prayed, he actually didn’t have any hope, he was just trying anything in his desperate state.

Now that a god was watching him, he realized that he had a chance of escaping! Under that ecstasy, he immediately changed his litany and turned towards Mist alone.

For this prayer, because it was not geared towards every fishman god, and only towards Mist, the fishman youngster immediately felt different.

In the divine spell’s dimensional perception, Dao Hen’s belief radiance began to grow brighter, and at the same time, the divine power channel also grew more stable.

As for why it wasn’t Charming Blue Goddess, but instead the Fish God Mist…

The fishman youngster understood why the moment he became a divine emissary.

Charming Blue Goddess had four authorities, deception, fishmen, water, and fishing.

These four authorities corresponded to her many god names.

The deception authority corresponded to the Goddess Charming Blue.

The racial fishman authority corresponded to the God Mist.

The water authority corresponded with the most divine names, like the Calm Lake God, the Wave God, and the Splash God.

The fishing authority corresponded to the Fishing Net Goddess.

Some gods only had one divine name, so why did Charming Blue Goddess have multiple? The more divine names one set up, the more was expended in transit. It was also detrimental towards believer unity, as believers under different divine names would fight and dispute in needless internal friction.

The fishman youngster knew what the reason was.

“Charming Blue Goddess had been attacked from all sides and sank into a deep sleep, her condition is quite bad and needed to maintain belief to heal and regain her strength. Belief is very important to her; therefore, Zhou Zhang took risks with blood sacrifice.”

“The same reason caused her to scatter the clergy and set up many gods. If it were a unified church, it would easily become a target of attack. If they were exterminated, the damage would be too disastrous. But believers under these many divine names don’t know each other, granting the greatest chance of preserving this secret. In the future, if one church is discovered, the others will continue to survive.”

In a nutshell, Charming Blue Goddess would rather expend some profits in transit and scatter her operations to reduce risks.

“Huh? I seem to remember that one of the gods Lan Zao believes in is the Fishing Net Goddess.”

“He was born into and grew up in a fisherman village, thus, he believes in the Fishing Net Goddess.”

“Perhaps I can use this to find him?”

The fishman youngster suddenly thought of something.

He was also unaware that Lan Zao had already returned to Ice Sculpture Island. The fishman youngster had just probed around and didn’t find Lan Zao’s faith.

It was because Lan Zao was in the divine domain Tranquil Hill, which concealed him.

After applying Divine Power Channel, the fishman youngster once again concentrated, and as expected there were four levels.

“My guess wasn’t wrong.”

“A simple divine spell’s [Believer Perception] concentration limit is the third level. But with the divine spell [Divine Power Channel], the limit of concentration is no longer limited to the third level.”

The fourth level of concentration allowed the fishman youngster to gain all of a believer’s memories.

The fishman youngster quickly read through Dao Hen’s memories.

Dao Hen’s memories were simple to understand, and apart from his imprisonment and suffering from ruthless torture and interrogation, the key thing was how he was instigated by Yan Yi’s people and was used by Tan Mo to frame Hun Tong.

When the fishman youngster comprehended the whole story, his complexion became somewhat heavy.

“Tan Mo, a navy rear admiral, simultaneously also an imperial secret spy investigator.”

“Tu Ji who comes from the Blood Light Punishment Institution!”

The two gold levels made the fishman youngster vigilant and at the same time, caused him to secretly let out a breath.

“Fortunately, they came because of Two Eyes Island, and not Mystifying Monster Island.”

“Mystifying Monster Island’s secret has not been exposed yet.”

In the battle of Two Eyes Island, Cang Xu had exposed himself as a necromancer, which led to a series of events.

Therefore, the Blood Light Punishment Institution only dispatched on gold level Tu Ji. While Tan Mo was responsible for investigating why Hun Tong had turned against his allies.

“If the empire found out what happened on Mystifying Monster Island…”

The fishman youngster didn’t dare to think about such dark things.

Regardless of whether it was War Merchant’s immensely valuable inheritance or the fact that he had secretly murdered the legend level Flame Dragon King, both would lead to sky eclipsing tsunami of suppression against them.

The fishman youngster put his feelings into order and reflected on what he should do about the current situation.

Dao Hen had to be protected.

This was not difficult for the fishman youngster.

But how specifically he should go about doing it needed meticulous consideration.

After thinking about it, the fishman youngster decided.

With an extremely verbose voice, he transmitted into Dao Hen’s heart, “Dao Hen, you went to war for the fishmen, you shouldn’t die by some plot. You still have a long journey in the ocean.”

“Under my protection, you cannot be seen. Seize this opportunity to leave this cage, like a clever small fish. Don’t splash unnecessary blood about…”

The fishman youngster then changed his tone of voice to tell Dao Hen a rough guideline of how to move next.

After he was done speaking, he used many divine spells.

The first was Deceptive Disguise.

Then he used the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell.

The fishman youngster was both a cleric and a magician, allowing him to easily use both spells. The magic’s brilliance instantly followed along the divine power passage and onto Dao Hen’s body.

The best thing was the magic fluctuation was on the fishman youngster’s side, while Dao Hen’s side was quiet.

Dao Hen disappeared, disguised as a ball of transparent air.

Divine spells were used in succession.

The shackled Dao Hen’s chains were unlocked and the magic formation stopped working, but another Deceptive Disguise spell made those occurrences undetectable.

Restoration type divine spells quickly healed Dao Hen to peak condition.

“My God!”

“My grand Fish God Mist, you are my master, you granted me a second life.”

“Your benevolence will be etched into my memory, I, Dao Hen, will only believe in you for the rest of my life!!”

After being aided, the freed Dan Hen shouted in his mind.

He had been rescued as he despaired, and the incomparable gratefulness caused his belief to rapidly rise from an ordinary believer to a pious one!

Chapter 814: The Big Guy is Stolen

Seeing Dao Hen’s conviction advance by leaps and bounds with his own eyes boarded the fishman youngster’s horizons greatly.

“Belief is related to the heart of a person.”

“Improving conviction is both simple and easy, and difficult and complex. It is a foundational undertaking of the gods.”

When the fishman youngster saw Dao Hen change like that, he suddenly thought of someone.

Another who had a similar experience as Dao Hen.

Chi Lai.

After the fishman youngster purchased Chi Lai on Two Eyes Island, Chi Lai had revealed his life’s story to him because they both were Justice God believers.

Because Chi Lai was chivalrous, he was imprisoned by a noble he offended, and while in prison, he received the Justice God’s gaze and even a divine revelation.

“Chi Lai at the time should be the same as Dao Hen when his conviction greatly increased.”

“My role right now is almost identical to the one the Justice God played at the time.”

“I understand a bit more on how to evangelize belief.”

Saving Dao Hen caused his faith to advance by leaps and bounds.

This is the power of salvation.”

“Anyways, he stopped at the pious level, and it seems the value of fanatics are very high.”

Dao Hen had become a pious believer of the Fish God Mist, which immediately triggered some follow up changes, causing somewhat of a reaction in the fishman youngster.

After all, the fishman youngster was Charming Blue Goddess’ divine emissary and was maintaining the divine power channel with Dao Hen.

“Exerting divine spells on Dao Hen is greatly depleting divine power and divine grace.”

“The divine power channel is also growing more stable.”

“Even interaction with the believer has become clearer, and my perception around Dao Hen has expanded a lot.”

There were improvements in all aspects.

“Hold on, it will be worthwhile to keep Dao Hen on this ship.”

“He is now a pious believer and also has Deceptive Disguise. It is feasible for him to hide at the enemy’s side long term.”

“If this is arranged, I can scout out a lot of information on Tu Ji and Tan Mo.”

The fishman youngster didn’t yet know the Treasure Chest also had the Secret Gate Church’s bishop and a saint level shield guardian on board too.

Dao Hen also was unaware of them too.

The Secret Gate Church’s two people had not disembarked from the ship since Two Eyes Island.

“Forget about it.”

“They aren’t headed towards Ice Sculpture Island, and the direction of their investigation is also wrong.”

Despite the fishman youngster dreading and being wary of Tan Mo, he still felt that the big guy’s safety was more important.

Thus, the fishman youngster decided to emulate a god’s connotation and gave an oracle to Dao Hen that he was to leave the Treasure Chest and go onto the nearby Magnet Archipelago.

“My God, your will shall be my path. Even if it leads to death, I shall die on this journey!” Dao Hen thought with prayer, then immediately began to act.

He had received many of the fishman youngster’s divine spells and had not only recovered his freedom but had been restored to peak condition and was now lively again.

Dao Hen tried to open the cabin door but wasn’t able to.

When the fishman youngster saw this, he used a divine spell through the divine power channel to open the cabin door.

The cabin door quietly swung open.

Dao Hen took this opportunity to flash out and escape his cell.

He excitedly glanced behind him, seeing that there was a locked up “Dao Hen” in there still.

Naturally, this was the fishman youngster using Charming Blue Goddess’ divine spell to create a façade.

If Dao Hen was saved in a more straightforward manner, it would bewilder Tan Mo and the others, cause them to wantonly search all over the place and would likely lead to more trouble on the horizon.

The fishman youngster would never act so rashly and thoughtlessly; he not only camouflaged the real Dao Hen, but he also made a fake Dao Hen too.

Dao Hen couldn’t find any flaws with his phony double.

“God really is omnipotent!” Dao Hen thought in admiration, and his pious level of belief became solid.

But at this time, God’s gaze suddenly vanished.

Dao Hen suddenly felt like something vanished from his mind, and it was difficult to conceal his sense of loss.

The Treasure Chest was a magic ship and used spatial alchemy technology to incase the ship’s internal space.

It would not be easy for Dao Hen to sneak out of this ship by himself.

As it goes without saying, the fishman youngster wanted to use more divine spells to guide him on the right path, however, something unexpected suddenly happened, forcing the fishman youngster to undo the divine power channel and return to the big guy.

Magnet Archipelago, present.

Dao Dou was wildly chasing while snarling, “Leave him alone, you damned thing!”

The big guy was being carried by a gold level earth elemental who was running like crazy.

This earth elemental was the leader of a nearby tribe who had been given directions by their god during prayer; thus, they came over to steal the big guy.

The earth elemental leader was very crafty and utilized its advantages to covertly approach the big guy from underground. It moved slowly through the mountain rock and successfully restrained their aura.

Then they suddenly dashed in, shocking Da Dou.

But it was too late.

The earth elemental had run away with the big guy in tow, and although it wasn’t fast, this was the Magnet Archipelago.

With the help of this place, the earth elemental used earth type match to slow Da Dou’s movement severely.

At the same time, he also had many underlings by the wayside obstructing Da Dou.

There were at least 20 silver level earth elementals and also some comparatively special magnetic earth elementals too.

This variant of earth elemental had a slimmer build than normal earth elementals and were a darker brown, with some even black in coloration.

Perhaps due to them being concentrated together, despite their small numbers, the magnetic earth elementals were the biggest obstacle to Da Dou.

They knew a very special racial magic. Each adult magnetic earth elemental knew four racial magics, they were Magnetic Imbuement, Polarity Change, Polarity Repel, and Polarity Attraction.

Racial magic – Magnetic Imbuement.

This magic’s effect could be stacked, and after Da Dou was affected by several Magnetic Imbuements, his entire body became intensely magnetic temporarily.

Racial magic – Polarity Change.

Under this magic, Da Dou’s magnetism could be easily switched between North and South as they wished.

Dao Dou wanted to pursue the earth elemental leader but was fully occupied with the magnetic earth elementals. When Da Daou wanted to eliminate the magnetic earth elementals, his magnetism would instantly shift, repelling him back forcefully.

“These wretched earth elementals, even with their strength in numbers, the key is that they are drawing power from the Magnet Archipelago!” Da Dou bit his teeth, unexpectedly feeling powerless as a gold level.

The entire Magnet Archipelago could be regarded as a giant magnetic rock.

“Why did that earth elemental steal him?”

“Humph, this won’t work out for you guys!”

A moment later, Da Dou used a flame totem and a divine spell.

His entire body ignited with flames, then with a bang, they vanished.

A split second afterwards, he appeared two hundred meters behind the earth elemental leader.

Both the earth elemental leader and Da Dou changed color.

The former was shocked that Da Dou had a spatial blinking ability, while the latter was stunned to see that there was a divine domain around the earth elemental leader!

Chapter 815: Wall Penetration Spell, Earth Walking Spell

“A divine spell.” Both Da Dou and the fishman youngster recognized it at the same time.

The divine domain spell was an environment altering divine spell, every god could use divine domain spells, but each had different effects.

This was because the actual effect was determined by each god’s divine power, authority, divine right, and other factors.

“Because of this divine domain spell, my connection to Xiao Guai is impaired.”

“If I use divine spells on Xiao Guai, the effects will also be weakened.”

But the fishman youngster did not panic.

Even if divine spells were weakened, it wasn’t by much and he still could save the big guy.

The divine domain spell sounded lofty, but it also depended on who was using it.

The enemy was a gold level earth elemental and not a saint.

Furthermore, fighters and magicians had methods similar to divine domain spells.

After reaching the gold level, a fighter’s battle qi could condense and leave their body and battle skills that molded a battlefield were constantly making new advances.

For example, take Teng Dong Lang when he used [Base] during his duel with the dragonman youngster.

This type of battle skill could temporarily create a battlefield environment that allowed the user’s battle qi to be transferred inside its range with almost no waste. After the battle skill dissipated, the remaining usable battle qi in Base could be recycled and reused.

After Teng Dong Lang used it, he immediately built an immense advantage that nearly pushed the dragonman youngster out of the fight.

Previously, the dragonman youngster had used the scroll – Lava Hell.

This magic was a battlefield type spell, it had immense might and proved useful, allowing the dragonman youngster to turn the tables.

“This…should be the Earth Elemental God’s divine domain.” The fishman youngster gradually identified it.

There were many elemental gods, mostly centralized in the four elemental planes.

The four elements were earth, water, fire, and wind. Each plane had many elemental gods and one main elemental god.

For example, the earth elemental plane’s main earth elemental god held supremacy over the entire plane. They and their many subordinate earth elemental gods formed a small earth elemental god pantheon.

“The Earth Elemental God’s domain places particular emphasis on stability, firmness, slowness, and heaviness to restrain fast movement, teleportation, and other such things.

“Da Dou should have teleported right behind his enemy, but he ended up 200 meters behind them instead.”

The fishman youngster maintained his divine power channel with the big guy, watching Da Dou in pursuit.

The young man gradually calmed down.

“The divine power channel is well maintained; I can help Xiao Guai escape when the time is right.”

“Now is not yet the time.”

First, although the earth elemental had kidnapped the big guy, they hadn’t attacked the latter yet and was only fleeing, so the fishman youngster wasn’t in a rush to act.

Second, Da Dou was still here, and there were eyes all casting their gaze here. The fishman youngster acting now would expose many secrets, after all one who could receive divine blessing and divine descent always attracted attention. The fishman youngster wanted to maintain secrecy as much as possible.

Third, the fishman youngster also felt curious. The big guy had no animosity with the earth elemental, why did they suddenly abduct him?

“What did Da Dou do? Did he cause the earth elemental to seek vengeance?” The fishman youngster guessed to himself.

Although the earth elemental’s sudden ambush was successful at snatching the big guy, Da Dou was pursuing close behind with staunch fighting determination.

Racial magic – Wall Penetration.

The earth elemental suddenly deformed and turned into earthy mud that wrapped around the big guy’s body.

A moment later, they crashed into a small tan colored hill. With the racial magic’s help, they seemed to be swimming through water and vanished from Da Dou’s sight.

But this action caused the fishman youngster’s mind to stir, “To penetrate through a wall, no ordinary gold level earth elemental can do that!”

Da Dou threw himself at the small hill.

“Don’t think of escaping!!” He furiously roared as he slapped it with his palms.

The two palms ferociously beat the small hill, instantly going two meters into it.

Gold battle qi condensed into a hand that opened his Packing Bag and took out a clump of seeds, then sprinkled it onto the small hill.

Next, Da Dou used a battle skill.

For a moment flames raged and quickly spread to cover the surface of the entire hill.

The fishman youngster had seen the big guy’s memories and immediately recognized it, “It seems Da Dou used this battle skill during the eating contest.”

The orange flames gradually fused the hill together, but strangely, they didn’t burn the seeds, on the contrary, they stimulated the seeds and caused them to grow wildly.

Consequently, something strange appeared.

In the raging flames, the seeds quickly took root and sprouted into wild growth. A few seconds later, a rug of green meadow, along with many trees sprouted from the earth.

Within a few more seconds, vines twisted like dragons and spread at shocking speeds. Tree roots dug deeply underground and swelled like inflating balloons.

The trees rapidly rose; the grass shook and quickly reached an even waist high height.

The more the flames burned, the smaller they got, like they were nourishment being absorbed by the plants.

The rock was covered with innumerable ticks and gigantic tree roots, shattered by twisting vines, and after a brief dozen seconds, the small hill was close to disintegrating!

The gold level earth elemental was exposed again.

Because they were wrapped around the big guy, it caused their volume to double and made them slower.

“Nowhere to run!” Da Dou howled, using his flickering flames again.

But the gold level earth elemental maintained the divine domain spell, making Da Dou unable to teleport within 200 meters of it.

Racial magic – Earth Walking.

The gold level earth elemental carried the big guy as it sank deep into the earth, drilling away to escape.

Da Dou’s eyes were red with anxiety, fuming with rage, he chased the earth elemental down and used battle skills to blow and smash his way through.

Bang, bang, bang…

He smashed the ground, causing rocks to fly and dust to waft.

However, the earth elemental successfully broke away from his line of sight.

Da Dou was helpless in the face of this crisis.

His destruction of that small hill had cost him most of his battle qi and spirit, it would be difficult to replicate it.

The Magnet Archipelago’s hill was not ordinary and was very hard.

In fact, even if Da Dou could do that again, it would be useless unless he was capable of turning the entire island into dust!

“Don’t tell me this is how I lose my clan’s saint and the future of Eruption Island?” Da Dou was just about to sink into despair when a figure rapidly approached.

The figure summoned their courage and yelled at Da Dou, “My Lord, I can track that earth elemental.”

Da Dou immediately stopped what he was doing and looked behind him, spying a human woman on the mountain top opposite to him.

That’s right, it was Hong Zhu.

“Can you help me find my enemy?” Da Dou’s flames glistened as he went to Hong Zhu immediately.

He looked down at Hong Zhu, looking nervous and his tone urgent. Because of the gold level giant’s furious and scared mood, his pressure was more pervasive and nearly suffocated Hong Zhu.

Chapter 816: Sacrificing the Big Guy

Hong Zhu was a treasure hunter. She went on treasure hunts, sold her findings for equipment, then went back to treasure hunting, this was how she acquired resources.

Hong Zhu had seen Da Dou feeding the big guy, and although she was frightened by Da Dou’s gaze, she knew he was not black-hearted.

Da Dou caused a big ruckus chasing down the gold level earth elemental, and Hong Zhu had been one of the first close by who were watching it unfold.

When she saw the earth elemental flee with the big guy, with Da Dou unable to keep up, Hong Zhu knew her chance had come.

“If I can use this opportunity to obtain this gold level magic chef’s friendship…”

“The Magnet Archipelago has many earth elemental tribes living here. The gold level earth elemental should be one of these tribes’ elders. They are natives here, and if we could unearth some clues from them, then that would be for the best.”

Hong Zhu had come to the Magnet Archipelago to search for hidden treasures.

The cruise ship Golden from three millennium past and the two legend level inheritances from two thousand years ago were the biggest lost treasures of Magnet Archipelago.

Despite being a silver level, Hong Zhu with a weaker cultivation level than Da Dou, she was a treasure hunter and was an expert at finding trails.

“I can try; I come bearing good intentions.” Hong Zhu swallowed her saliva, answering despite the massive pressure Da Dou exerted on her.

Da Dou nodded slightly.

At once, Da Dou looked downwards as Hong Zhu picked up earth elemental and magnetic earth elemental fragments.

She took out a few alchemy devices and used special tracking battle skills, quickly obtaining a result.

“I’ve investigated it, follow me. I should be able to find where the earth elemental tribe is.” Hong Zhu knew this was urgent and moved right after getting a result.

Da Dou was a spectator to her methods and trusted her a bit more.

Compounded with his incomparable worry about the big guy and knew there was no time to lose, he followed Hong Zhu away from the chaotic battlefield,

Halfway on the journey, Da Dou was dissatisfied with Hong Zhu’s slowness, so he picked her up and strode across the Magnet Archipelago, rushing towards a hillock.

The big guy was still sleeping through all of this, not reacting at all to the outside world.

The fishman youngster was paying close attention to him but didn’t wake him up.

The gold level earth elemental tribe chief had traveled in a straight line through the earth to return to their old nest.

“Is the sacrifice arrangement ready for use?” The earth elemental tribe chief asked.

He quickly got an answer that satisfied him.

“Block the cave mouth, start the ritual and have all fighters on guard. I need to carry out the sacrifice.” The earth elemental tribe chief commanded.

He came to a specially constructed cave.

The fishman youngster noticed that this cave was very spacious and had a divine statue standing in the middle of it. The statue was tall, sturdy, and sincere, it was of the Main Earth Elemental God.

Around the divine statue, there was a divine script formation laid out.

The magic formation covered almost the entire ground and was roughly 80 square meters.

The fishman youngster sized up the divine script formation curiously.

This was the first time he had seen such a pure divine script formation. Zhou Zhang had used a magic formation combined with divine script when conducting sacrifices.

The fishman youngster was unexpectedly able to understand and read it somewhat within a few glances.

“Is this the result of the passive divine spell, Divine Script Mastery?”

Although the Main Earth Elemental God’s divine script had many differences with Charming Blue Goddess’ divine script, the fishman youngster had a complete understanding of the fundamentals of Charming Blue Goddess’ language.

The gold level earth elemental tribe chief carefully put down the big guy beneath the divine statue.

The big guy snored lightly, and his originally swollen belly was gradually subsiding due to constant digestion.

The earth elemental tribe chief stood back a few steps, then put their hands together and hung their head in prayer.

Accompanying his litany was the transfer of divine power, causing the divine script formation to start glowing yellow.

The fishman youngster observed for a moment, making out more clues, “That damned earth elemental!”

“That is actually a blood sacrifice divine script formation.”

“He wants to treat Xiao Guai as an offering to the Main Earth Elemental God.”

“But…why?”

He braved Da Dou’s pursuit, and snatched away Xiao Guai, don’t tell me they know about Xiao Guai’s outstanding bloodline?”

The fishman youngster acted at once with Charming Blue divine spells.

When the earth elemental stepped into this cave, they rescinded the divine domain spell, allowing the fishman youngster’s connection with the big guy to become inseparably close once again.

Deceptive Disguise and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell!

The classic spell combination was first cast on the Earth Elemental God’s statue, to prevent them from actually paying attention to this.

Divine spell – Opportunistic Deception!

The divine spell’s power flowed through the divine power channel and bubbled from the big guy to cover the earth elemental tribe chief’s body.

Halfway through their prayer, the earth elemental tribe chief sensed the Main Earth Elemental God’s gaze.

“God, I have wrested control of this offering…” They were instantly excited, and their tone raised by an octave as they prayed more passionately and piously.

They were completely unaware they were being deceived.

In their field of view, the statue was issuing an earthen yellow halo. The halo enveloped the big guy, causing the latter’s body and soul to begin to collapse and break down like moist sandy soil.

The more the big guy disintegrated, the more the earth elemental tribe chief felt a wondrous power was pouring into them and beginning to attack the limits of their life level.

Bang, bang, bang!

At that moment, the cave shuddered intensely and sounds of fierce fighting could be heard.

An earth elemental charged into the cave to report about the enemy.
