As it turned out, Hong Zhu had successfully led the way, and now Da Dou was storming into the cave.

The earth elemental tribe chief was both frightened and furious, but they didn’t get up to fight, rather they intensified the defensives and continued with their prayer.

Their divine power expenditure accelerated, hastening the blood sacrifice. To them, the big guy was turning to sand at a faster rate. In actuality, the divine script formation around them had already been secretly destroyed by the fishman youngster and the divine power flowing into the broken divine script formation was rapidly dispersing uselessly.

“Charming Blue divine spells used rationally can produce such unexpectedly remarkable battle results!”

The fishman youngster’s initial usage had achieved a perfect result, inevitably causing his mind to shake.

“This is the power of deception?”

“Like the Imperial East’s martial philosophy [four ounces can move a thousand cattle], using this power at the opportune time can block other gods and toy with a gold level believer in the palm of my hand.”

The fishman youngster hadn’t traveled to this battlefield and only used a few divine spells to control the situation.

At this moment, the fishman youngster understand how strong he was.

A gold level divine emissary!

He knew several hundred divine spells, and if the high level ones were used sensibly, they could place him above normal gold levels.

“However, the iconic Charming Blue divine spells still need to be used quietly.”

The Charming Blue Church is in terrible circumstances, and Charming Blue Goddess is in a gravely wounded hibernation. If I use these divine spells publicly, the trouble that follows will be massive.

Chapter 817: Da Dou’s Bitter Struggle

Although earth elementals normally had racial magics that could penetrate walls and flee through the earth, their lairs had many tunnels and passages dug into them.

Because drilling through walls and fleeing through the earth cost physical strength and using racial magic depleted their magic crystal’s power.

As elemental beings, they should be thinking of every possible way to accumulate power into their magic crystal. Not only could it be used to cope with critical situations, more crucially, the magic crystal’s power could help them break through their life level.

Da Dou didn’t have a good experience digging his way into the cave.

He was a giant with a massive body and couldn’t freely move in these tunnels.

As a matter of fact, earth elemental tunnels were better than dwarven passages, which were narrower and caused giants to shrink back at the sight of them.

If it were possible Da Dou wouldn’t have dug his own tunnel. But he had no choice, and to him, it seemed the big guy was in imminent peril, and he had to race against time to save him.

If he were too slow, he might only find the big guy’s corpse.

Gold battle qi condensed into a giant hand, wildly tearing earth into the air as Da Dou expanded his underground passage.

At the same time, Da Dou also expended divine power to use the flame divine spell to bake the earth elementals attacking him.

Battle qi and divine power were being fiercely depleted, but Da Dou’s battle intent was staunch and unwavering.

Ding.

His fist smashed an earth elemental ambushing him from behind, then quickly took something out of his Packing Bag.

This was a rare gold level storage device; the Packing Bag also had an additional effect: it could keep things fresh.

Under normal circumstances, magic chef dishes had a short expiration date. Usually, it was to be eaten the day it was made, and occasionally it would last two or three days.

The longer time went on, the more the magic power of these magic dishes would dissipate, leading to their tumbling quality. This was the best scenario, more often, the magic power would be jumbled, and it would poison people who ate it.

But the Packing Bag could extend the expiration date of food to an extent.

Da Dou took out a dish from his Packing Bag.

It was a Candied Potato!

This was a gold level magic dish.

Da Dou tilted his neck, opened his mouth, and shoved down the entire Candied Potato.

After chewing it a bit, he swallowed it with a gulp.

His depleted battle qi was instantly replenished at a very swift recovery rate.

Battle qi was easy to replenish, while divine power was difficult to do so. Prayer was the main way but now was not the time for that.

After eating the Candied Potato, Da Dou took out some magic rainbow candy.

He didn’t peel back the wrapping due to time, deciding to throw them into his mouth wrapped instead.

He used his hard teeth to firmly crunch them into fragments, then swallowed again.

Blarg~

Da Dou’s complexion suddenly twisted.

So sweet!

The magic rainbow candy was quickly digested in his belly.

All kinds of magical radiances immediately rose from his body. Within a few seconds, Da Dou was empowered by kinds of amplification magic that affected his speed, endurance, defense, perception, and more by a small amount.

Da Dou took a deep breath, then continued to dig deeper.

The tribe chief had commanded that the earth elementals resist with their lives.

They formed a defensive line and used their racial magics together to reinforce the land.

Numerous silver levels disturbed Da Dou. He had to use more gold level battle qi to excavate the soil and enlarge the tunnel enough for Da Dou to walk through.

Racial magic – Earth Thorn.

Earth thorns suddenly appeared from nowhere. With plenty underneath his feet, along the narrow tunnel walls, and shooting down from the ceiling.

Da Dou had no choice but to use a gold level defensive battle skill to shield his body.

Most of the thorns were stopped, but a few penetrated Da Dou’s battle skill and cut into him.

This was the earth elemental’s homeland, it was their nest, and where they reproduced. It was only natural they operated and arranged it with utmost care.

The deeper the tunnel went, the stronger the defenses were and the more powerful the deployments.

There were many earth elementals living in the Magnet Archipelago, and they often had conflicts, arguments, and even fought against each other.

After all, the Magnet Archipelago was surrounded by the boundless ocean and the soil was finite, so earth elementals had to compete for resources to survive and grow.

Hong Zhu was behind Da Dou but maintained a long distance.

After finding Da Dou the right place, she also snuck into the tunnel.

“An angry giant really is scary.”

“He’s wounded!”

“It seems the stolen small giant is really important to him.”

Hong Zhu guessed at the relationship between Da Dou and the big guy while picking up the earth and magnetic earth elemental essences and magic crystals along the way.

“I’m rich!”

“Helping the giant find the enemy’s old nest was the right decision.”

“He surprisingly didn’t collect any of the silver level magic crystals, he really is heroic.”

Da Dou was focused on the big guy’s safety, and he didn’t dare to spend the brief time it would take to grab the magic crystals.

Hong Zhu smirked as Da Dou madly charged head, these gains were quite beneficial.

“Why hasn’t the gold level tribe chief appeared yet?”

“Now that the earth elemental tribe has their attention on the fire giant, I can go to their treasury and take away their valuables.”

Because earth elementals could penetrate through walls and the ground, they could often collect many treasures and resources from the hearts of mountains and the depths of the earth.

Hong Zhu took out an alchemy device and began to investigate which direction the treasury might be.

Da Dou’s attack speed grew slower, and Hong Zhu didn’t want to get mixed up in this. She was only concerned about taking this opportunity to fish for benefits and snuck away in another direction.

After a short time, he came to a wide and empty underground plaza.

“This must be an important location to the earth elementals. Even if it is noisy outside, it must still have guards here.”

Hong Zhu grew more excited.

The guards were two iron level earth elementals, while Hong Zhu was a silver level, capable of breaking through them.

But Hong Zhu didn’t think about breaking in with force.

Although these two earth elemental guards were a level lower than Hong Zhu, they would be very difficult to deal with if they actually fought.

The reason was that earth elementals had a comparatively special life structure, they had nearly no fatal weak points. Their only fatal weak point was their magic crystal. However, their magic crystal could move anywhere in their body, and as long as the magic crystal held energy, it could be expended and absorb the earth elements around it and quickly mend the elemental’s body.

Hong Zhu took out three alchemy items and used then in succession, then with a pained look, she resolutely drank half of a gold level potion.

Hong Zhu quickly went invisible, and her aura dwindled to an absolute minimum.

Right in front of the earth elemental guards, she carefully entered this closed area step by step.

It wasn’t the treasury she was looking to find, but…a graveyard.

But Hong Zhu became more excited.

Because the cemetery was where dead earth elementals were buried, where they left behind their magic crystals!

Hong Zhu began to stealthily dig up the graves.

Most of the magic crystals that were excavated were full of elemental energy, but a few were badly damaged and had little or no energy in them.

But even these broken magic crystals still made for good magic power crystal materials.

After digging up 16 magic crystals, Hong Zhu triggered the alarm in her inattentiveness.

The two earth elemental guards became furious and instantly spurred themselves into sweeping the entire cemetery.

Hong Zhu was forced to show herself and fight the earth elemental guards.

During the battle, she searched for a chance to break out of the cemetery and escaped.

After the two earth elemental guards pursued her for a distance, they returned to their post.

A group of earth elementals were informed of this, and they chased down the fleeing Hong Zhu while bellowing repeatedly.

Because a few silver level earth elementals entered the battle, Hong Zhu’s circumstances took an abrupt turn.

She wanted to escape to the surface, but she noticed that the tunnel was blocked with silver level earth elementals.

“Go find the fire giant!” Hong Zhu thought harshly, changing her direction to go deeper into the tunnels.

Da Dou had broken through three defensive lines, destroying a number of silver level earth elementals.

Racial magic – Rolling Stone!

More than a dozen earth elementals used this racial magic together.

Extraordinary power burst forth, converging and combining with each other to condense into a gigantic rolling sphere of stone.

The rolling stone rumbled the earth as it tumbled towards Da Dou.

The tunnel walls were reinforced, and Da Dou, unable to evade it in time, was forced to clench his teeth and endure it head on with his chest.

The gold battle qi hand quickly crushed the rolling stone.

Da Dou raised his arms and ferociously slapped the rolling stone with his palms, causing it to stop spinning.

But the sheer size and faint divine script glittering on the rolling stone’s surface meant the rolling stone’s might was not diminished as it hit Da Dou in the chest.

Three or four of Da Dou’s ribs immediately snapped, causing him to cough out blood.

He withstood the sharp pain, growling as he used an offensive battle skill, and with a string of three explosions, turned the rolling stone to bits.

The earth elementals rushed back in retreat, establishing a new emergency defensive line.

Hidden from view in the dust for a moment, Da Dou knelt to the ground and gasped for breath.

He took out another thing from his Packing Bag.

The challenging assault had depleted his stamina, battle qi, and more gravely. His body was greatly wounded and blood flowed incessantly. His fractured ribs caused him constant pain that made Da Dou’s eyes twitch.

He took out a magic dish from his Packing Bag.

This simple task caused his arm to tremble.

The earth elementals were always known for their defense. And to defeat the silver level earth elementals, he had to use a lot of battle qi to thoroughly destroy their bodies. If some parts of them remained, he couldn’t be sure if their magic crystals were hidden in it and allow the earth elemental to avoid death.

This tunnel was extremely rich with earth elements and within two or three seconds, an earth elemental could regenerate to as they were before.

Da Dou was really tired and greatly depleted.

He devoured a magic dish and made every second count as he recovered.

His wounds quickly stopped bleeding and began to heal. His fractured ribs were put back together, his battle qi reserves soared from rock bottom, his mind was like a clear spring flowing into a desert, and he became bursting with life once again.

Using battle skills consumed mental power too. The more complex a battle skill was, the more mental power it took.

Chapter 818: Da Dou vs the Earth Elemental Chief

Against Da Dou’s all-out attack, the earth elementals were paying with heavy casualties.

Taking advantage of when Da Dou was busy eating food and resting, the remaining earth elementals mustered together to build a final defensive line, still ready to defend it to the death!

The tribe chief was the only gold level in the tribe and had undoubtable prestige.

Although earth elementals were intelligent lifeforms, they didn’t have minds as flexible as humans and would rarely betray or rout.

Adding on the fact that Da Dou was attacking their home meant that these earth elementals were very angry.

Therefore, although the earth elementals had taken massive casualties, as long as the tribe chief remained undefeated, they still maintained morale.

“These stubborn rock heads, I will shatter each of you to pieces!” Da Dou clenched his teeth, straightened his chest, and began to attack again.

A few minutes later.

With a boom, Da Dou smashed a hole into the sacrificial cave.

He was covered in blood and cut and bruised all over, yet his battle intent had soared to its highest yet.

The earth elemental tribe elder was startled; they immediately stood up and turned around to look at Da Dou nervously.

The fishman youngster also coldly snorted when he noticed Da Dou.

“This Da Dou is really attached to Xiao Guai!”

“Instead of participating in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s ceremony, he would rather break its laws to steal away Xiao Gui, that indicates that he likely saw Xiao Guai’s potential.”

“A gold level potential would not cause Da Dou to be so excited. Therefore, he believes the big guy has at least a saint level aptitude!”

This matter made the fishman youngster aware of how attractive a saint level bloodline was.

It wasn’t just Da Dou; the gold level earth elemental tribe chief had stolen the big guy away because of this too.

But their goal was different from Da Dou’s.

They wanted to use the big guy as an offering to the Main Earth Elemental God in exchange for the Main Earth Elemental God’s divine favor, allowing them to break through the bottleneck in their life level.

“Bloodlines cannot be altered; this is common sense. But the gods can defy common sense and truly smash conventions.”

“Gods are omnipotent!”

As he thought that, the fishman youngster had a complex expression.

Although he was not a god, he was a divine emissary and after he learned many high level divine spells, the fishman youngster had a taste of how gods styled themselves as.

Just the divine spell Believer Perception had expanded his horizons greatly.

Although the fishman youngster had only been in contact with this church and divine spells for a short time, his clergy rank had risen step by step, and his understanding of the phrase “Gods are Omnipotent” had advanced by leaps and bounds. He now had a deeper comprehension of it than most clergy.

“But even if it is a god, improving bloodlines must come at a considerable cost.”

“If that weren’t so, they would have made batches of believers with high level bloodlines and cultivated them into experts with all they had.”

Considering the unfolding situation, the fishman youngster easily surmised this truth.

If gods didn’t pay a high price, then the world structure would be completely different. If this price was even a bit lower, then the saying “bloodlines cannot be altered” would not be a consensus of society.

The fishman youngster had seldom heard of times when gods acted personally to change a believer’s bloodline aptitude.

The fishman youngster came to realize that he was in a very rare situation!

“What is so different about this earth elemental that the Main Earth Elemental God grants them their favor?”

“I heard their litany; the Main Earth Elemental God seemed to have promised that they would break through the elemental’s life level bottleneck.”

“It seems the Main Earth Elemental God is capable of improving earth elemental bloodlines.”

“In terms of strength, the Main Earth Elemental God is the ruler of the Earth Element Plane and is the main god of a pantheon, they must be stronger than Charming Blue Goddess.”

“Charming Blue Goddess could change fishman bloodlines, so there is nothing strange about the Main Earth Elemental God being able to change earth elemental bloodlines.”

“Perhaps this is related to racial makeup of their clergy…”

“Charming Blue Goddess has fishman clergy, therefore she is capable of improving fishman bloodlines. The Main Earth Elemental God must have earth elemental clergy; therefore, they can change the bloodlines of earth elemental believers.”

The fishman youngster’s quick and smart mind quickly made a pile of conjectures.

“Truly a group of wastrels!” The gold level earth elemental tribe chief actually cursed.

They were now at the most crucial part of the sacrifice, and it could not be interrupted, or else all of their previous efforts would be wasted, unfortunately, this was when Da Dou had charged in.

During this impossible situation, the fishman youngster began to declare false oracles.

He told the earth elemental tribe chief that if they immediately repulsed the enemy and killed Da Dou, it would put the earth elemental tribe’s majestic presence and honor on display. As for the blood sacrifice, it could be suspended for now. After they won the battle, the earth elemental tribe chief could go back to it.

“My God, I will not betray the leniency and benevolence you have bestowed!” After the earth elemental tribe chief obtained this promise, they instantly became ecstatic and grateful.

Without the psychological burden, they immediately charged out and confronted Da Dou.

This battle was different from before.

Previously, the earth elemental tribe chief was persistently fleeing, doing their best to protect their offering, the big guy. They had no thoughts of fighting Da Dou at all. At the time, the blood sacrifice was the main event. Now, eradicating Da Dou has become their primary objective.

The fishman youngster’s false oracle was crucial, and while the earth elemental tribe chief was running away, they had been accumulating a lot of anger. Anger that their home had been invaded and hatred that their people had suffered disastrous casualties. Both spurred the earth elemental tribe chief into an energetically ruthless maneuver.

Bang, bang, bang…

Both sides fought fiercely, and for a moment, their blows caused rock to fly and earthquakes to tremor.

Da Dou’s eyes were already red from killing, warmed up with ample battle intent that burned like raging flames.

The earth elemental had been praying and sacrificing to maintain their peak state.

The giant and tall earth elemental fought like adult magic beasts battling with tooth and nail. They collided head on, and neither shrank back from the other’s heavy and powerful strikes.

Fists, bumping shoulders, headbutts, and other such attacks were thrown at each other, earnestly hitting flesh.

The cave was narrow, and this unusual battlefield caused them both to independently choose hand to hand combat.

Dao Dou was quickly battered badly.

While the earth elemental’s body was constantly shaking, with big chunks of it falling off.

But the earth elemental had the favorable location and quickly utilized the extremely abundant natural earth elements around to quickly regenerate their shattered body.

Da Dou knew that these exchanges were wholly against him, so he quickly used battle skills to constantly break through the earth elemental’s defenses and strike at their body.

But the earth elemental tribe chief had plenty of battle experience, and while they fought, they were also using their mental power to control the movement of their magic crystal in their body, perfectly evading Da Dou’s search.

Da Dou struck the earth elemental tribe chief’s body a dozen times yet didn’t even see a hint of their magic crystal.

After many fruitless efforts, Da Dou recognized reality and was forced to sigh to himself, give up on getting lucky, and start to use flame divine spells.

Chapter 819: Earth Breath

With a cry, Da Dou’s entire body suddenly ignited.

In a flash, he became a flame wreathed giant.

Because the earth elemental tribe chief was fighting him in hand to hand combat, they were immediately roasted by the flames, and the affected areas were baked like pottery.

At the same time, the Fire God spells also injured their spirit, causing them to growl again and again and fall back in a flurry.

Da Dou’s morale rose, the flame divine spells got instant results, allowing him to hold the advantage.

He seized this opportunity to chase down the kill.

Da Dou was impatient. Because unlike battle qi, he had limited methods to recover his divine power.

Although Da Dou wore a fire totem, this was from his teacher. He himself was not a high level believer.

If he had a high position, then that would be another thing.

The fishman youngster watched from the sidelines, calmly observing the two gold levels fighting each other to the death.

“With just a false oracle transmission, I was able to manufacture this good show, that was too easy.” The fishman youngster sighed incessantly.

At first, he wanted to fight the earth elemental chief by himself. But in the end, Da Dou had charged in, saving the fishman youngster a good deal of effort.

“Let’s add more fuel to the fire.” The fishman youngster thought.

He immediately used a divine spell.

A moment later, Da Dou saw the big guy lying under the Earth Elemental God’s statue, his body enveloped with a layer of yellow light.

The yellow light was turning the big guy into decomposing sand.

Da Dou anxiously glared at that, then with a roar, he abandoned his opponent and attacked the Earth Elemental God’s statue.

When the earth elemental tribe chief saw his opponent turn around and expose an opening, they ruthlessly lashed out at it.

Da Dou’s back was heavily struck by the earth elemental tribe chief, causing him to instantly cough up blood. However, he took advantage of this impact to accelerate towards the big guy.

It was the earth elemental tribe chief’s turn to be anxious.

He had been deceived by the fishman youngster’s divine spell earlier, and to them it seemed that the big guy was already dead. Most of his body had melted, with only the lower half of his body remaining.

But they couldn’t allow Da Dou to take the body away.

Despite the Main Earth Elemental God saying they had suspended the blood sacrifice to be continued later, if the sacrificial offering was dead, could the sacrifice still proceed? Wouldn’t God be angry and drop divine punishment upon them as a consequence? Perhaps God would be disappointed that the earth elemental tribe chief could not guard the offering, and simply deny this sacrifice?

The earth elemental chief didn’t dare to gamble on this.

After all, even if the Main Earth Elemental God didn’t keep their word, there was nothing the tribe chief could do. More crucially, based on the rules of sacrifice, if the big guy was snatched away, the Main Earth Elemental God wouldn’t be reneging on their promise if they backed out after that.

In a moment of desperation, the earth elemental tribe chief used their strongest offensive skill without the slightest hesitation.

Their chest deformed, revealing their inner heart, and exposing the magic crystal inside.

The earthy, yellow magic crystal suddenly shot out bright light, and faintly within that light was a large amount of natural magic runes. They drifted one after the other, appearing and disappearing, appearing to be a number of magic runes.

These runes mixed together in a flash and condensed into an energy ball that wrapped around the magic crystal.

A moment later, this energy ball suddenly erupted with resplendent light and formed a gigantic beam of light that shot through the air.

Racial magic: Earth Breath - Impact Pillar!

At the same time, the earth elemental tribe chief was also using a divine spell.

They made a stone barrier around the “big guy’s lower half” that covered the remaining blood sacrifice offering like a burial mound.

Divine spell – Yellow Stone Shield!

The area the earth elemental tribe chief was covering up was of course a fake, in truth, they were covering nothing.

In truth, the big guy had been moved by the fishman youngster earlier. The latter had utilized divine spells to quietly move his son into the corner and also used many divine spells to protect him immensely.

But the earth elemental tribe chief and Da Dou perceived things differently.

Da Dou saw that the big guy was still very much intact but had been covered up by Yellow Stone Shield.

He immediately used an offensive battle skill, smashing only a layer of the shield.

While the earth elemental tribe chief’s strongest attack was about to hit his back.

Da Dou’s back felt numb, and the fear in his heart caused his hair to stand.

He quickly evaded!

Earth Breath – Impact Pillar was almost about to hit him, as the light beam magic was also taking effect. Da Dou’s movements became heavy and slow, like he was burdened by an object weighing hundreds of kilograms.

If it were another, perhaps they would have been hit already, but Da Dou had a brilliant plan to escape this.

A moment later, he used the flame divine spell to turn into a flame that faded away.

A near instant later, he appeared in the corner of the cave, smoothly extricating himself from colliding with the light beam.

“Still failed!” The earth elemental tribe chief sighed to themselves.

Earth Breath was a racial magic, on the surface it appeared to resemble light, but that was not so.

Although it was very powerful, it was hard to hit an enemy with.

Earth Breath – Impact Pillar didn’t hit Da Dou, on the contrary, it struck the Yellow Stone Shield.

The earth elemental used the Impact Pillar battle skill with all of the strength, and it was their strongest offensive method, however, Yellow Stone Shield had firm defenses and Impact Pillar did nothing to it.

This also reflected the earth elemental’s fighting characteristics – their defense was far stronger than their offense!

However, the truth Da Dou saw in his eyes had been distorted by the fishman youngster.

Deceived by the Charming Blue divine spell, Da Dou saw the big guy’s shield break.

Da Dou glowered as he glared at that crack.

He hesitated for an instant, then let out a growl. He proceeded to shockingly jump in front of Impact Pillar to hold back the fatal attack that had not yet hit the sleeping big guy.

He used a defensive battle skill, but the sudden activation meant he was unable to maximize its defensive effects.

The earth elemental tribe chief was about to deactivate their battle skill regretfully, when suddenly, they saw their opponent do something stupid, causing them to immediately become ecstatic, causing them to both increase the flow of their magic power and also change their tactics.

Racial magic: Earth Breath - Pestle Grind.

The earth elemental tribe chief increased their energy output and altered their attack type from impact to grinding.

For a moment, earth type magic power bubbled from the magic crystal unrestrained, divulging itself as much as it liked!

The light beam’s diameter increased sharply by a third as Earth Breath constantly scoured Da Dou’s front.

Da Dou lowered his head and raised his arms to form a temporary shield to resist this attack with all he had.

He squeezed all of his gold level battle qi and condensed it around his body. But under the grinding Earth Breath, it was quickly pulverized and dissipated.

Under the battle qi was the divine spell Fire Protection.

But divine power was quickly being exhausted and the flames dissipated too.

Thus, Da Dou’s flesh was the final line of defense.

The fire giant inevitably hesitated, and he looked down at the opening in the Yellow Stone Shield, where he could see the shut eyed and sleeping big guy. He endured the pain and dispelled his plans of retreat; he would defend the big guy to the death and would never retreat!

The light beam lasted a full 30 seconds before it finally dissipated.

Da Dou remained standing, his feet already buried in the ground, leaving deep scars in the earth from the 2 meters he had been pushed.

Chapter 820: No Idea What to Do

Most of the flesh on Da Dou’s arms had been grinded away, exposing his badly damaged bones.

Poof.

He could no longer persevere and had to kneel.

His arms powerlessly drooped to the sides, blood and sweat mixing as they dripped down.

His face was a miserable pale white, he was gasping for air, and his originally bright and shining eyes were now downcast.

He had suffered serious damage!

When the fishman youngster saw this, his face gradually changed.

Da Dou looked exhausted and was kneeling on the ground, nevertheless, he immediately launched a counterattack.

Battle skill – Big Saucepan Lid!

Gold level battle qi whizzed and formed a saucepan lid that covered the earth elemental tribe chief.

The earth elemental tribe chief was still catching their breath, and couldn’t evade it, being subdued for the time being.

Da Dou couldn’t attend to his bleeding mouth and nose, shouting at the earth elemental tribe chief from behind, “Come over here, help me get him out.”

Naturally, he was not yelling at the earth elemental tribe chief, but Hong Zhu.

Hong Zhu had been following him, using his assault to get filthy rich. Da Dou was fighting against time to rescue the big guy and had observed her actions, not saying anything or stopping her.

When Hong Zhu heard Da Dou calling out to them, her heart suddenly jumped.

As a silver level, participating in a fight of this level was undoubtedly fraught with danger.

But a moment later, she clenched her teeth and ran over, avoiding the corner where the earth elemental tribe chief was and came to Da Dou.

As a treasure hunter, Hong Zhu had a strong spirit of adventure. Her main consideration at this moment was that since she had shown kindness to Da Dou previously, and gotten massive benefits as a result, she might as well take it a step further and work with him again.

Hong Zhu felt that if she did that, she would earn more profits!

What she saw was almost identical to what Da Dou saw, the big guy was still in good condition and lying on the floor. Even though the fighting was fierce, he was still sound asleep.

Hong Zhu clenched her teeth and said to Da Dou, “My Lord, I can’t carry him away.”

Da Dou replied, “I have a storage device on my waist called the Packing Bag.

“Put him in and leave immediately!”

Hong Zhu was overjoyed, even if Da Dou died in battle, she would obtain the Packing Bag. This was a gold level storage device!

At once, Hong Zhu took the Packing Bag after receiving access from Da Dou.

Da Dou forced himself to break open the Yellow Stone Shield that had been ravaged by Earth Breath.

Hong Zhu touched the big guy.

Then after putting “the big guy” into the Packing Bag, she ran away.

Of course, the truth was that she put a bunch of air into the Packing Bag, which was exceptionally hilarious to watch.

But the fishman youngster did not smile, instead he was in a somewhat complex mood.

Although Da Dou had kidnapped the big guy, the fishman youngster had seen him dash in bravely with no concern for his safety and fight bravely till he was soaked in blood; those acts had diminished his ire greatly.

Da Dou risked his life to vie with Earth Breath and protect the big guy.

“It seems he wholeheartedly wants to train Xiao Guai…”

Da Dou could have evaded the attack, but he decided to intercept Earth Breath at his own accord.

Because of Yellow Stone Earth Shield still being around the big guy, Da Dou couldn’t abscond with him.

Da Dou withstood Earth Breath for no less than 30 seconds, and even though his injuries grew heavier, he did not retreat and persevered until the end of the attack.

Not long after Hong Zhu left, the Big Saucepan Lid was overturned.

The earth elemental tribe chief saw that Hong Zhu had taken away the big guy’s lower half.

Without hesitation, he immediately wanted to pursue her.

Da Dou jumped over to intercept him.

The earth elemental tribe chief exploded in anger, and as they fought, the chief tried their best to break past Da Dou.

Da Dou was heavily injured, and his military strength had tumbled severely, thus, he was unable to last long.

Then the moment the earth elemental was about to break out, Da Dou’s heart surged as he grabbed the earth elemental’s body and used a gold level battle skill.

The battle qi formed chains that firmly bound Da Dou and the earth elemental tribe chief together.

The earth elemental tribe chief tried to break the battle qi chains but couldn’t for the time being.

He wildly attacked Da Dou, wanting to pursue their enemy, causing the battle qi chains to lose their effectiveness.

Da Dou didn’t have the strength to strike back and could only use his back to bear these heavy strikes.

Every attack caused Da Dou to cough out blood.

Very quickly, his backbones broke. The back injury was horrendous, yet he still wouldn’t let go no matter what.

Then before Da Dou was beaten to death, the fishman youngster softly sighed.

At that moment, he had a change of plans.

He acted.

Through Believer Perception and Divine Power Channel, he called out, “Xiao Guai.”

“Father!” The big guy immediately responded as he dreamed.

“Xiao Guai, I need to use your body, don’t be nervous. I need you to trust me completely.”

The fishman youngster prepared to use a divine spell for the first time ever, he was somewhat tense.

In contrast, the big guy nodded repeatedly, there was no hint of hesitation in him.

“Now, call my name.” The young man said.

The big guy shouted, “Father!”

The fishman youngster was startled, then smiled, “Excellent.”

Divine spell – Divine Descent spell!

Vast divine power suddenly poured in from within the fishman youngster, traveling through the divine power channel and quickly into the big guy’s heart.

In the corner, the big guy’s eyes suddenly opened. But he didn’t look normal, rather his eyes were rolled back white.

A moment later, Charming Blue Goddess’ divine light bubbled out, inking the whites of the eyes blue.

The big guy quickly stood up.

But he wasn’t the one in control, rather he had been replaced by the fishman youngster.

“This feels a bit new.” The fishman youngster seemed to have become the big guy, but not quite, there was still a layer of separation between them.

He could sense both his body and the big guy’s body simultaneously.

It was like he was divided into two people.

Without carefully savoring this experience, the fishman youngster sallied out.

He first controlled the big guy to run into the cave.

An earth elemental suddenly appeared in front of Hong Zhu.

“How many more are there?!” Hong Zhu was taken aback.

The earth elementals had formed a tight defensive line and forced Hong Zhu back.

She returned to the sacrifice rite cave, and upon seeing the earth elemental tribe chief ruthlessly beating Da Dou, knew the situation was far from good.

When Da Dou saw Hong Zhu return, he was extremely surprised, and his mind slackened.

The earth elemental tribe chief seized this opportunity to hit Da Dou to the ground.

Da Dou was dying from his wounds and was unable to stop them.

The earth elemental tribe chief didn’t know why Hong Zhu had returned, but they laughed upon ridding themselves of their hindrance, then attacked with ecstasy.

Hong Zhu turned pale, to her, she seemed to be in a pincer attack.

“I lost my gamble!”

“If I had known earlier, I wouldn’t have taken away that small giant.”

“I’m going to die!”

Hong Zhu did her best to use her battle qi, but strong earth type magic shackled her. She was like an insect who fell into amber, unable to budge at all.

In this moment of despair, Hong Zhu, Da Dou, as well as the earth elemental tribe chief saw the Packing Bag suddenly spray out a beam of light.

The light beam struck the cave ceiling, then broke away.

Then subsequently, a small giant fell to the ground.

It was the big guy!

The big guy had his head high and chest out, his entire both was immersed in earthen yellow divine light, his presence was immense, and his apathetic expression leaked an indescribable dignity.

The big guy attacked the earth elemental tribe chief.

With one strike, he threw the latter into the air and crushed half their body.

Da Dou’s and Hong Zhu’s eyes widened, rigidly staring at the big guy who descended like a heavenly soldier, feeling both incredulous and happy that they had escaped danger.

The earth elemental tribe chief was even more bewildered and frightened.

Unlike the other two, they didn’t see the big guy!

In their cognition, they felt an inexplicably heavy strike send them flying.

“What’s going on?!” The earth elemental tribe chief was still dazed when the fishman youngster’s follow up attack hit.

The battle progress was obvious at a glance.

A crushing victory!

The big guy was winning from start to finish, and his superiority could not be surmounted.

With the fishman youngster’s help, all kinds of gold level divine spells were enough to be a fatal menace.

The earth elemental tribe chief was not in a good condition to start; Da Dou had reduced his battle strength by a lot.

Most crucially was that Charming Blue divine spells were deceiving them. They were unable to see the culprit despite the big guy obviously being right in front of their eyes!

Finally, the earth elemental tribe chief’s courage was scared away. Faced with an unseeable enemy, they decided to abandon their plan of sacrificing to the gods and used a racial magic spell in an attempt to flee.

They were deceived however, they believed that they had activated the spell, but in actuality, they hadn’t mobilized their magic power at all.

The big guy hurried after them, reached into their body, and yanked out their magic crystal.

Without the magic crystal, the earth elemental tribe chief’s aura suddenly sank.

They subconsciously looked behind them, towards the big guy, despite not being able to see any trace of the enemy.

The fishman youngster used a divine spell to easily exterminate the earth elemental tribe chief.

The dust settled.

The big guy walked over to Da Dou and looked down at him.

Da Dou raised his head, barely able to look at the big guy, his stunned expression gradually waned, forced aside by a complex, forced smile.

“I…”

“I’m dying.”

“Although I stole you, it was out of good intentions.”

“I concede I had selfish motives, but it was beneficial to both you and Eruption Island. Only there will you have the space and sufficient resources to grow.”

“I didn’t expect that you were a pious believer…”

The big guy slowly raised his hand, and just as Da Dou thought he was about to die, cast a healing divine spell on him.

Da Dou suddenly stared blankly.

The divine spell’s healing was pretty good, and it immediately stopped his bleeding, but he still only had a breath of life.

Then a divine spell contract appeared in his mind.

“A slave contract?!” Da Dou was astonished.

The big guy slowly opened his mouth, “Live, or die?”

Da Dou was lying on his stomach, and no longer raised his head, instead he helplessly buried his face into the ground, laughing bitterly from there.

Soon after that laugh, the fishman youngster’s mind moved, sensing that Da Dou had accepted this divine spell slave contract.

Afterwards, he revoked the Divine Descent spell and before undoing the divine power channel, warned the big guy repeatedly.

To the side, Hong Zhu watched with amazement as the big guy’s grandeur suddenly vanished and his dignified expression also collapsed into foolishness.

The big guy’s eyes returned to normal, and he shook his head, however, the strong vertigo put him off balance and he fell onto his butt.

Thus, the big guy and Da Dou foolishly looked at each other not knowing what to do.

Chapter 821: Finding a Place for Xiao Guai

In the messy cave, the big guy and Da Dou looked at each other.

After a while, the big guy’s mind returned, and he remembered when Da Dou humiliated him in battle.

He suddenly flinched back and wanted to flee. Then at that moment, he recalled what the dragonman youngster had said to him repeatedly in his dream.

“Xiao Guai, don’t be scared.”

“Father has already taught that bad red skin giant a lesson.”

“He will listen to you now. You can eat his good food, and if there is a villain that wants to harm you, he will protect you.”

“Wait on that island for now, your father will come pick you up.”

Thinking of that, the big guy barely calmed down.

Meanwhile, Da Dou’s state of mind was endlessly churning, “That was the Divine Descent spell, if I’m not mistaken.”

Da Dou realized the big guy had changed.

Hong Zhu suddenly said, “Lords, we must leave without delay. Although this earth elemental tribe has been defeated, this fierce battle will cause other tribes to investigate.”

Those words were reasonable, and Da Dou propped his body from the ground with an arm and slowly got up.

After being healed by the fishman youngster, his injuries had stabilized, and his serious wounds became minor injuries.

He stood up and coughed twice, but unlike before, no blood came out.

His internal injuries such as his broken bones had been healed.

It could be said that without the fishman youngster’s divine healing spell, Da Dou would have died.

Of course, he had one final way of survival, that was praying to the gods as he neared death.

But Da Dou didn’t have faith in that, he was very clear on how ordinary his conviction was.

“Magnet Archipelago is too small, and earth elemental tribes constantly erupt into war to take territory from each other.”

“We must leave without delay.”

“Ma-, master.” Da Dou said to the big guy.

Hong Zhu’s eyes shrank, and her mind shook, “That really was a slave contract!”

Despite seeing the whole thing with her own eyes, she was sighing incessantly over it.

The grand gold level giant had been reduced to a slave; this end was utterly beyond Hong Zhu’s expectations.

However, upon careful recollection, she felt that this was both rational and natural.

The big guy under the Divine Descent spell was extremely formidable.

Inconceivably powerful!

The earth elemental tribe chief couldn’t even retaliate and had been thrashed the entire time. Hong Zhu also realized that the big guy under Divine Descent seemed to be able to interfere with the enemy’s senses and the earth elemental tribe chief’s attacks never hit anything.

This was the world of the strong.

Since the big guy under Divine Descent was so powerful, then Da Dou surrendering to him was reasonable.

Da Dou extended his right hand to the big guy.

The big guy stared at it foolishly, not daring to take it.

But at that moment, his ears rang with the fishman youngster’s encouragement.

“Father is looking at me.” When the big guy thought that he immediately swelled with courage.

A moment later, he extended his hand.

The two giants grasped each other’s hand.

Da Dou used his strength to pull the big guy up from the ground.

“Human woman, please lead the way.” Da Dou ordered Hong Zhu.

Hong Zhu hurriedly nodded.

Thus, Hong Zhu walked in front, followed by Da Dou and the big guy.

As Da Dou looked at the big guy behind him, he had mixed feelings.

He finally had a peaceful interaction with the big guy, but he didn’t expect it would be in this state, he had been reduced to the big guy’s slave!

White Hair Trench.

The fishman youngster’s mind returned to his body, and he examined himself.

“My internal divine power is nearly exhausted, and divine favor has been severely depleted by 2,000.”

But the battle results were unsurpassed!

The fishman youngster had used many divine spells, playing as the hidden hand that cleverly exploited the conflict between enemies. In the end, he annihilated the gold level earth elemental tribe chief and also subdued a gold level giant magic chef.

“As a divine emissary, when I use these divine spells, I deplete my reserves of divine power and divine favor.”

“Fortunately, the enemies had greatly exhausted the other, otherwise if I was just relying on my own power, saving the big guy would be an easy matter, but obtaining such a battle result would be nearly impossible.”

“Anyways, the large amount of expended divine power and divine favor might be related to Xiao Guai’s beliefs. He is not a Charming Blue believer; he is my fanatical believer.”

The big guy’s body was not affected by divine power and hadn’t been imperceptibly influenced by it over a long period of time.

Naturally, this would lead to a greater loss of divine power.

“If it were on my own body, I would have definitely saved a lot of divine power and divine favor.”

This battle had given the fishman youngster immense gains.

First, he tried out a high level divine spell, gaining valuable combat experience.

Second, he saved the big guy’s life.

Third, he tested out his own combat strength. A gold level divine emissary could fight and direct operations remotely from great distances. Relying on high level divine spells, (most importantly Deceptive Disguise), the fishman youngster could easily defeat gold level elites. But this also exposed his own deficiencies, such as his massive expenditure rate, making him incapable of a prolonged fight. To carry out such a battle, he needed believers to be in place beforehand.

Fourth, he obtained a gold level giant slave!

Saving Da Dou’s life was a thought out decision, not an impulsive one.

He had thought through three things.

The first was that Da Dou’s battle performance, where he truly protected the big guy with all his might had moved the fishman youngster.

Second, Da Dou’s background gave the fishman youngster misgivings. He came from Eruption Island, a legend level power. It would be for the best if they weren’t enemies.

Third, the fishman youngster had given sufficient consideration to the current situation.

Before this, he had believed the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was in a good situation. But from the big guy’s memory, the fishman youngster found out he was being too optimistic.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ branch had been attacked, a gold level assassin attacked “Zong Ge”. This was sufficient to make out that Zi Di and Zong Ge were in a nasty situation.

After the fishman youngster saved the big guy, he no longer wanted him to return to Snow Bird Harbor, it was not safe there and was instead overwhelmed with crisis.

The fishman youngster understood that despite the big guy’s life level advancing by leaps and bounds and his bodily essence being quite visible, the big guy was unable to protect himself, and if he encountered ambush, poison, or other such things, he would be easily disposed of.

The big guy had at least a saint level potential, and during the eating contest, he had exposed some of his aptitude. This caused Da Dou to steal him, surely others took note of that.

Under these circumstances, the fishman youngster sending the big guy back to Snow Bird Harbor might lead to another kidnapping him!

“This time, when I tried Believer Perception, I unexpectedly gained information from the big guy. Next time I might not be so lucky.”

After thinking it over, the fishman youngster felt that continuing to have Da Dou look after the big guy as they stayed temporarily on Magnet Archipelago was for the best.

For one, the big guy would free himself from the hidden parties that coveted his aptitude. Secondly, he could be safe for the time being from enemy attacks. Thirdly, Magnet Archipelago was a better place for the big guy to grow.

The big guy had been starving at the branch location.

Here, the gold level magic chef Da Dou could give the most suitable food to the big guy. This was not something the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was able to do.

To be more precise, after Da Dou compressed the food, he demoted it from gold level to silver level when feeding it to the big guy. Not even Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord had such lavish treatment!

“The slave contract can ensure Da Dou is obedient.”

“Even if Da Dou frees himself from the contract, he won’t harm Xiao Guai.”

“Moreover, Xiao Guai’s unconditional trust and obedience to me allows me to use Believer Perception to interact with him at any time. If something unexpected appears, I can take care of it.”

After comprehensive consideration, the fishman youngster felt that this was the optimal solution at the moment.

“If this happens, Dao Hen doesn’t need to rush over.”

He originally wanted them to come over here to prepare for a rescue after saving them.

The fishman youngster had extradited Dao Hen mainly for the big guy’s sake.

But he didn’t expect that the fierce battle between the earth elemental tribe chief and Da Dou had given the fishman youngster a golden opportunity. Ultimately, the fishman youngster became the beneficiary, taking advantage of Da Dou’s near death injuries to get him to sign a slave contract.

The big guy’s crisis had been removed.

Dao Hen was just a silver level, and it didn’t matter if he came here or not.

“Instead, he might as well stay on that imperial warship, that seems more appropriate.”

The fishman youngster attached great importance to Tan Mo and the others aboard.

The Blood Light Punishment Institution and imperial investigation team were both things he had to pay attention to.

He was also worried that Cang Xu would be exposed.

On Two Eyes Island, the exposed necromancer Cang Xu had a strong connection with the Wisteria Merchant Alliance, this was quite detrimental to the Wisteria Merchant Alliance.

Dao Hen could remain on Tan Mo’s ship, and with the aid of Deceptive Disguise and other spells, he could steal firsthand information and ascertain which direction the imperial investigation team and Blood Light Punishment Institution were moving to in real time.

This wouldn’t harm, either the dragonman youngster or the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, only benefit them.

Thus, the fishman youngster returned his concentration to Dao Hen.

He found to his surprise that Dao Hen hadn’t left the Treasure Chest yet.

The Treasure Chest used original and sophisticated spatial alchemy technology. Space layered on top of each other, and there were countless similar looking ship cabins and passages, it was just like a labyrinth.

Dao Hen not being able to get out was normal.

The fishman youngster resisted the urge to give Dao Hen directions. He waited until Dao Hen couldn’t endure it any longer as worry filled his heart. Then after several prayers were given, he used a divine spell to give him the right directions.

With these directions, Dao Hen was able to get through 19 levels of space and finally arrived at the Treasure Chest’s deck.

Upon seeing the sky above and the wide ocean, he was emotionally moved.

Just as he was about to jump off, he received the fishman youngster’s oracle. Unexpectedly, he was to return to the ship interior to carry out a spying mission.

Dao Hen’s expression shook, and he hurriedly complied, turning back towards the ship hold.

The fishman youngster had asked him to leave previously, and Dao Hen had failed.

The fishman youngster hadn’t revoked his prior order, rather he had helped Dao Hen smoothly leave the Treasure Chest. After giving him a glimpse of the sky and sea, he had Dao Hen go back.

This was not an unpredictable change in command, and it gave Dao Hen a feeling: the prior order was God testing him. At the same time, God’s true intent was incapable of being comprehended!

After Da Dou and the others left, as expected, other earth elemental tribes visited the battlefield.

Finally, they came to the sacrificial rite cave.

“This was the main battlefield!”

“Two, no three gold levels fought to the death here. In the end, our old enemy died in battle here.”

“Indeed, a loss of face, that grand earth elemental actually let outsiders raid their old nest.”

A few earth elemental tribe chieftains came together, and in the tense ambience, all of them wanted to partition the territory before them.

But a moment later, the Main Earth Elemental God’s divine statue suddenly erupted with light.

Soon after, incomprehensible divine power flooded over everyone present, causing all of the earth elementals present to immediately bow down in worship.

After half a minute, the divine statue’s light faded away.

The earth elementals stood up one after the other, all looking excited.

“The Main God issued an oracle!”

“Find that giant and eliminate him!!”

“No, capture him alive, he is the best sacrificial offering to our grand Main God.”

Chapter 822: Alchemy Bomb

Ice Sculpture Capital.

Dueling arena.

Zong Ge and a challenger were fighting; the battle was at its climax.

Although he was facing a silver level magician, Zong Ge was losing.

The female magician was flying in the air, maintaining three types of different flying spells to keep distance while constantly taking out alchemy bombs and explosive potions, and tossing them at him.

Boom, boom, boom…

The explosions were continuous.

Zong Ge was expressionless as he dodged the explosions in all directions.

Suddenly, he jumped on the wall, and like an artillery shell, charged at the magician in the air.

The magician sneered and took out a bundle of bombs, throwing it while simultaneously using an alchemy device to quickly withdraw.

Boom!

With a loud sound, a shockwave and dust filled the sky.

The magician gazed from afar, completely on guard.

“What is that sound?” The magician vaguely heard a sound in the wind.

A moment later, a dart came from behind her.

She hurriedly turned around and her eyes contracted violently, “No time to dodge!”

Battle skill – Auto-Target Seek.

Battle skill – Undetectable.

With those two battle skills, the dart flew towards the magician.

As it turns out, Zong Ge had used himself as bait to attract the magician’s attention, then used that opportunity to throw his dart in an arc.

His battle skills were simple and used adeptly, and with the quick empowerment of these battle skills and the explosion screening it, the dart had turned into a boomerang.

The magician’s mental power was finite, and most of it had been spent controlling her flight spells and throwing bombs, so she only had one layer of magical defense.

That wouldn’t do!

In that critical moment, the magician used a defensive item and without the slightest hesitation, suddenly took out a scroll from her cuff and tore it to defend herself.

“Long Fu’s weapon can automatically change shape; it is a very powerful gold level weapon.”

“But I just need to survive past the earliest stage, then I can use this gold level scroll to save myself!”

As that thought flashed through her mind, the boomerang cut her into two.

“How is that possible?!” The magician died on the spot.

The boomerang had gold level battle qi in it and it penetrated through her body, almost instantly extinguishing all of her vitality then and there.

Zong Ge left the dust cloud.

He was covered in dirt.

Using himself as bait for that explosion was rather dangerous.

Zong Ge slowly headed towards the magician, well aware of her lapse in judgement, “With Deceptive Disguise, others see me as a mere silver level, and my battle qi as silver level too. Therefore, she erroneously judged the boomerang’s firepower.”

Of course, this was also because Zong Ge had been suppressing his weapon’s strength since the battle began, only displaying silver level power.

Zong Ge’s gold level weapon was called the Striking Shifter; it had come from the Li Jian Clan’s arsenal.

It could morph into all kinds of weapons, and it really suited a weapon great master like Zong Ge.

Since he and Hua Tang had fought, this weapon had been completely exposed. Zong Ge never thought about using Deceptive Disguise and the Anti-Investigation and Prophecy spell on this weapon.

For one, the Li Jian Clan knew about it. Secondly, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had earned a lot of money from magic medicine, and as Commander Long Fu, even if he was just a silver level cultivator, he should have a gold level weapon!

This was reasonable.

After all, a gang member like Xiong Ju had the Gold Flower Hacksaw as a silver level.

Zong Ge approached the magician’s corpse, sighing to himself.

This battle was not easy for him; it was quite troublesome.

“The commander is still silver level and cannot reach the gold level yet, thus, I cannot use gold level battle qi attacks.”

“This time, my adversary might have come from that group of powerful enemies. She used explosives that cost around 500 gold coins, and her tactics greatly restrained ‘Long Fu’.”

“However, the gains from this battle weren’t small.”

Zong Ge looked at the gold level scroll in the magician’s hand.

He walked over to that side of the corpse and leaned down to pick it up.

But a moment later, his expression suddenly changed.

Boom!!

The magician’s body exploded in an instant.

Zong Ge used gold level battle qi to protect himself, but he was still hit.

He coughed twice and fell to the floor, his coughs containing purple blood.

“This is a poison bomb, the explosion wasn’t powerful, but it is very insidious!” Zong Ge’s brows locked tightly with a grave expression.

The magician’s body had been turned to slag, and the gold level scroll in her hand had gone with her too.

Zong Ge was more certain of his conjecture now.

Why would a normal challenger deliberately put an alchemy bomb in their body?

One knew that this kind of human alchemy and bodily refinement would greatly interfere with daily magician cultivation.

After drinking some healing medicine, Zong Ge was able to suppress the poison.

“I need to find Zi Di and have her examine me.” Zong Ge was not good at detoxification and prioritized asking Zi Di for help.

After cleaning up the battlefield, Zong Ge left.

As he pushed open the dueling arena gate, he saw a large number of onlookers.

“He’s coming, he’s coming!”

“It really is Long Fu.”

“I told you, even if the fighting gets loud, it won’t change the result.”

The crowd cheered and many people waved at Zong Ge.

Zong Ge wore a wooden face as he left, but behind him were constant cheers.

“Lord Long Fu, are you going to participate in the Grand Duel at the National Ceremony?”

“We look forward to it!”

“I have been buying your mount magic medicine.”

After Zong Ge decided to accept challenges, he had gone through several battles, and in every battle, he had savagely killed the challenger.

But this conduct did not hinder his increasing supporter base.

In the wake of dueling more often, his popularity in the royal capital had risen meteorically.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom had a strong taste for duels.

On a two story house across the street, Mi Fang watched Zong Ge turn a street corner through a chink in the window, then looked away.

He was a human but had outstanding looks that were very delicate and handsome.

This kind of appearance was close to the aesthetics of elves; therefore, Mi Fang had always been widely welcomed in by female elves.

Seeing Zong Ge’s reception, Mi Fang couldn’t help but think of when he became famous in the arena.

Unlike Zong Ge, Mi Fang’s admirers were mostly women. At the time, the female elf community had enthusiastically embraced him.

“Excellent, the tactic worked, Long Fu is poisoned!” Mi Fang was stirred when he looked at the detection device.

With Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s ceremony imminent, Mei Lin’s energy was tied up in naval defense and she was forced to reduce her attacks against the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

But that wasn’t to say she was leaving them alone completely.

One of the missions Mei Lin had arranged for was to dispatch different silver levels to openly challenge “Long Fu” so they could wear him down.

This time, it was a magician sent by Mi Fang.

The bomb tactics was also arranged by Mi Fang.

The result had satisfied him.

“I’ve weakened Long Fu yet again, if this continues, it will be possible to eradicate him.”

“It is a pity that he requests privacy and secrecy for every duel. After killing his enemy, he will take away their corpse and clean up the battlefield. This makes it hard for us to obtain detailed information.”

“Never mind that, we can take our time, there will eventually be a day when our goal is accomplished.”

Mi Fang knew he had to be patient.

Of course he wouldn’t challenge Long Fu right now.

To him it seemed that Long Fu was still very threatening. The best cautionary tale of that was the death of the Ax Gang Leader and the other three gold levels.

Only when Mi Fang knew what trump cards Long Fu hid and was certain he could withstand them would he enter the playing field.

Compared to the other duelists, Mi Fang was more motivated to deal with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Because he had joined the Jing Xiang Clan, who was currently competing with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps fiercely over their current mount magic medicine market share.

After the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Li Jian Clan had deepened their connection, they instantly held the advantage and firmly suppressed the Jing Xiang Clan.

“Long Fu is the signboard of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, and many people purchase the dragon rider potion because of him.”

“By eliminating him, the Jing Xiang Clan will have hope of turning the tables.”

“The national capital’s formation repair work is almost done. Soon, the Alchemy Guild’s hands will be free. They won’t stand and watch this development in the mount potion marketplace.”

Mi Fang knew more high level information; he had determined that the mount potion market had grown by leaps and bounds and had changed dramatically.

There was just one reason, the Alchemy Guild was acting.

This colossus was the overlord in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s alchemy market!

“When the Alchemist Guild exerts itself, it will be like an alchemy bomb being thrown into the water, it is sure to set off a gigantic wave in the market.”

“When that moment comes, the alliance of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Li Jian Clan will find it difficult to respond to the Alchemy Guild, perhaps this will give the Jing Xiang Clan more opportunities.”

Chapter 823: Zi Di Detoxifies

Zi Di raised her hand, her gaze focused on the test tube in it.

Zong Ge was standing by her.

Some of his blood had been drawn out and put into the test tube.

Zi Di used simple detection magic, then she pursed her brows.

Without battle qi suppression, Zong Ge’s blood began to darken and smell bad.

Zi Di tried to use mainstream detoxification methods. A portion of Zong Ge’s blood boiled, and the test tube’s temperature quickly rose, almost evaporating entirely from the test tube. Another part of the blood condensed into a solid, and another part of it corroded the glass test tube and fell out of it.

The mainstream detoxification methods did not work.

But it was not a complete failure.

The boiling blood evaporated into a starry black cloud. The frozen blood clot’s surface also had many angled star patterns across its surface. After corroding, the remaining glass fragments also seemed to slightly show a starry pattern under the sunlight.

“This is a very distinctive trait; it is a good clue.” Zi Di exclaimed.

She looked at Zong Ge, “Sorry to disappoint you.”

“This place lacks alchemy facilities, and I fear I am not strong enough.”

“To completely detoxify you, I need a lot of time and energy, and I cannot guarantee success in the end.”

“Perhaps this starry pattern clue can be investigated for some valuable information.”

“But at present, you have to suppress this toxin for the time being.”

Zong Ge faintly nodded, he had been prepared for this result and wasn’t that disappointed.

At least on the face of it, he was very tranquil.

“I can suppress this toxin temporarily.” Replied Zong Ge.

Zi Di sighed, looking worried.

Zong Ge’s condition was getting worse. Previously, he had suffered the Axe Gang Leader’s Blood Vengeance before they died, something he was unable to get rid of. After that, he was hit by Hua Tang’s battle skills. Now, he was affected by a peculiar poison.

It could be said that Zong Ge’s fighting strength was greatly impeded due to his declining condition.

Zi Di and Zong Ge had been trying to solve these problems.

But in practice, they didn’t make much progress.

Blood Vengeance was shot from the Blood Vengeance Axe, yet despite them capturing that weapon, they were unable to find a solution to it.

Until now, they did not even know what activated Blood Vengeance.

There was also Hua Tang’s unusual battle skill [Flower Willow]. After Zong Ge was hit by [Flower Willow], it seemed to have taken root and was constantly growing and strengthening from absorbing Zong Ge’s battle qi and vitality.

These undoubtedly ate at Zong Ge’s battle qi and life span.

Currently, Zong Ge and Zi Di were merely testing some aura suppression methods and preventing the use of battle skills to weaken the demands of [Flower Willow].

Now this strange poison had been mixed in. Zong Ge had to use his battle qi to suppress the poison while also needing to take medicine. This greatly increased his battle qi expenditure.

Zi Di said worriedly, “Perhaps we should decrease your dueling frequency.”

Zong Ge immediately shook his head, “Wrong. That will show I am weak and might lead to the enemy striking even harder.”

“I am still sure of the outcome of battles against silver levels.”

“This poisoning was my mistake, I was careless.”

“But next time, it won’t happen.”

Seeing Zi Di’s concern not being alleviated, Zong Ge added, “My current state isn’t all that bad.”

“The Blood Vengeance Axe’s power keeps me focused at all times and has greatly trained my body’s internal defenses.”

“After being hit by Hua Tang’s battle skill (Zong Ge’s assumption of who it was-), my suppression battle skills have been tempered a lot. My training results far surpasses previous records.”

“If my body can adapt to this strange poison, wouldn’t that be a great way to grow?”

Zong Ge’s positive mentality treated these challenges as an opportunity to improve himself.

He then said, “There will always be challenges, and so long as we continue to forge ahead, it will be the right decision.”

“It is necessary and worth it to brave the risks to do the right thing.”

“Who can avoid risks?”

Zi Din couldn’t help but inwardly admire Zong Ge’s declaration.

She admired Zong Ge’s courage and responsibility.

Of all of the survivors, perhaps only Zong Ge was capable of playing the role of the dragonman youngster well.

As long as these two didn’t collapse, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ morale would be ensured and supported.

Zong Ge really couldn’t avoid battle.

Because he was currently acting as Long Fu.

As the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ commander, Long Fu was a new influential figure on Ice Sculpture Island, and a big reason Zi Di’s business plan worked out so well was due to Long Fu’s renown.

Zi Di wavered, “Perhaps we can ask the Li Jian Clan for help, and let them help detoxify you?”

Zong Ge instantly shook his head, “We are only in a collation of mutual benefits. Because of benefits we can work together, and because of benefits that union can collapse.”

If they could get help from the Li Jian Clan, Zong Ge would have gone to them to deal with Blood Vengeance and Flower Willow already.

To detoxify, his blood needed to be drawn.

It was positive that Zong Ge’s physical condition would be analyzed. By doing that, a lot of information on him would be divulged, increasing the risk of exposure.

The survivors had heaven shaking gains from Mystifying Monster Island. Including two divine artifacts!

If the Li Jian Clan knew about them, what would they do?

The survivors were being searched for by the Bai Zhen Clan, the Bounty Hunter Guild had also issued a warrant for them, and even parts of the imperial navy were involved in the search.

Nowadays, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was losing to a strong mysterious power that existed in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s government. The Sheng Ming Secret Spy Agency also had their eyes on them because of Two Eyes Island.

If they were exposed, the survivors would be welcoming a calamity that would drown them!

The risks were too great.

It was the same if they asked the Li Jian Clan, other churches, and other magicians.

There was another sore spot.

It was the Pearl Bubbles.

Zi Di and Zong Ge didn’t have many Pearl Bubbles left, but they needed to use them in more and more places. If they asked other powers to heal Zong Ge, they had to use Deceptive Disguise and the Anti-prophecy and Investigation spell on him. The latter was fine, but crucially, the former used Pearl Bubbles.

The more and more powerful investigations there were, the more Pearl Bubbles would be depleted.

Zi Di’s and Zong Ge’s difficulties lay in a single place: they didn’t know when the dragonman youngster would return. This forced them to use the Pearl Bubbles cautiously and conservatively!

“Lord Commander, when will you finally return?” Zi Di had shouted from the bottom of her heart on more than one occasion.

Zong Ge was more realistic, “Unless it is as a last resort, there is no need to ask others for help.”

“This was my carelessness.”

“From now on, I will be more cautious of silver level challengers.”

“In fact, I am more worried about gold levels.”

“The enemy definitely has other gold levels; they don’t need to use such power right now. But I am afraid that other gold levels who have been enticed and exploited can make things difficult for us.”

The Ice Sculpture National Ceremony is not just a grand occasion for domestic experts; the National Ceremony’s Grand Duel often attracts foreign experts into joining it too. Regardless of whether it is glory or the prizes, both are always alluring.

“Long Fu’s” reputation in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, as well as the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ magic medicine business attracted gold level experts. It was likely that a gold level expert would be moved to first duel the silver level Long Fu to treat him as a footstool to bring about their fame in the early stages.

Zong Ge continued, “For instance, it is possible that Qi Ci Lang is coming to participate in the National Ceremony Grand Duel.”

Zi Di had found out about Qi Ci Lang from the imperial secret spy’s inquiries.

After that event, she emphasized buying information on the Thirteenth Prince and Qi Ci Lang.

On the market, there still wasn’t information that Qi Ci Lang would join the duel.

Thus, Zi Di and Zong Ge found out more about the Thirteenth Prince. They knew the latter’s prestige among the people was very high and that many citizens loved and respected this hostage prince. So much so that when the king’s throne was vacant, some voices within the kingdom hoped that the Thirteenth Prince would succeed to the throne.

This obviously didn’t happen.

The Thirteenth Prince was Heavenly Emperor Sheng Ming’s son, and both bloodline and legal principle disqualified him from succeeding the throne.

However, it could be made out that the Thirteenth Prince was very popular!

The Thirteenth Prince would never join the National Ceremony Duel.

First was his identity and second was his strength.

The Thirteenth Prince was an ordinary person and didn’t have any transcendent bloodline.

But Qi Ci Lang might.

The information Zi Di and Zong Ge gathered showed how powerful this gold level was.

To be more precise, he was incredibly powerful!

Qi Ci Lang’s strength was very visible, and more formidable was that he was blessed by many gods. The most widely publicized was that seven different gods blessed him with immortality.

Just one of these made a transcendent a sufficient threat.

Qi Ci Lang had seven.

Because of this, he was publicly nicknamed the Beloved Child of Seven Gods!

In short, after Zong Ge looked at the intelligence, he instantly judged that he was not Qi Ci Lang’s match. The gap between them was too great.

Despite both of them being gold level.

It could be said that the higher level a cultivator got, the bigger the gap could be between cultivators of the same level.

Zong Ge was serene, “If Qi Ci Lang appears and joins the National Ceremony Grand Duel, then that will further prove the Wisteria Merchant Alliance’s power.”

“At the same time, we also need to inspect and verify our flaws – our information gathering is weak, and at many crucial points, we have to rely on well known information. But since the latter can be purchased on the market, its value isn’t that high.”

Zi Di nodded, “According to the information, this Beloved Child of Seven Gods does not have the attitude of an expert, he has a brash temperament and is quite unscrupulous. He rarely shows mercy to the weak and might come challenge us.”

“I hope this doesn’t happen.”

“Because in the Ice Sculpture National Ceremony Grand Duel, if you refuse to fight, you can no longer participate.”

“That’s right.” Zong Ge nodded, “At least let me hold on for a while longer.”

Chapter 824: Five Level Zero Secret Orders

Zi Di’s tone was heavy, “But when that moment comes, we can only refuse.”

“After all, Commander Long Fu is only a silver level fighter.”

“Consequently, even if we decline, it won’t lose too much prestige.”

Prestige would undoubtedly take a hit, as the Ice Sculpture citizens greatly looked down on those who feared battle.

But if a silver level was facing a gold level, it was pardonable.

If it was a gold level avoiding battle, then their reputation would take a mortal blow. In the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, it would most certainly be a death sentence socially, and ordinary people would strongly disdain them.

Zi Di and Zong Ge spent some time in the kingdom and knew quite a lot about the customs here.

If “Long Fu” conceded or suffered a defeat, then their magic medicine business would be negatively impacted immensely.

Zong Ge was noncommittal, “The Thirteenth Prince is not a suitable candidate, but perhaps Qi Ci Lang is worth contacting.”

Zi Di and Zong Ge had discussed this before. Since the Wisteria Store’s communication channel could not be used, right now, they are now waiting in the capital for the National Ceremony, then would use it to look for appropriate imperial higher ups to contact.

From what could be gleaned from the information, Qi Ci Lang didn’t have a good temperament, but his network was formidable.

His seven blessings of immortality was the best proof.

Being on friendly terms with Qi Ci Lang meant they could get along with at least seven churches in the Sheng Ming Empire.

While Zi Di and Zong Ge were discussing, things were happening in the subterranean depths of the Ice Sculpture Capital, in the Ten Millennia Ice Lake.

The magic ship Ice Skate was disguised as a massive iceberg as it calmly drifted through the current.

Elder Yuan Ci was in the captain’s room, looking at magic images.

These images had the figures of many alchemists in it and also the magic ships they drove or the alchemy puppets they controlled.

Without exception, these magicians were all evacuating.

But Elder Yuan Ci’s expression was very unsightly.

Because while these alchemists were evacuating, Elder Yuan Ci had received some information.

The information had been conveyed by secret means, and it was both an invitation and also some kind of diplomatic note. Elder Yuan Ci was very familiar with and greatly loathed the sender.

He was inclined to interpret this information as true.

“There is trouble this time.”

“I thought I still had more time.”

“Now it seems I have to open the Wisteria Cache as soon as possible.”

To open the Wisteria Cache, one needed 5 level zero Wisteria Secret Orders.

Elder Yuan Ci counted them; he had one, the sender had one, and the newly emerged Gui Teng (Zi Di) had one. They had three in total.

The sender had already contacted another who possessed a secret order, and he needed to rescue the one who held the fifth, as they were currently in prison.

“Shi Liu…you old codger, I didn’t expect that I would come save you.”

“However, with my strength alone, rescuing him will be somewhat difficult.”

“It is a good time to contact Gui Teng and invite him to make a move. That will also be an opportunity to clarify his true allegiances.”

After Zi Di and Zong Ge parted ways, she received Elder Yuan Ci’s message.

After she determined who the imperial secret spy was, her trust of Elder Yuan Ci had increased greatly.

Elder Yuan Ci directly expounded on his purpose in contacting her, stunning Zi Di.

“Are you saying the Alchemist Guild’s Elder Jiu Jin is one of our merchant alliance’s elders?”

Zi Di didn’t think the Wisteria Merchant Alliance had such powerful contacts in Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s alchemy guild.

One knew that to become an Alchemist Guild elder, one had to be extraordinary. Even honorary elders could not be looked down upon.

Elder Yuan Ci said, “When our merchant alliance was at its peak, we had stores in all of Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s major cities and business dealings with the royal family.”

“At the time, we recruited Jiu Jin when operating in Ice Sculpture Island. We subsidized his promotion to being an Alchemy Guild elder, and in turn, he repaid us by helping the merchant alliance get their footing here and open things up.”

“After the merchant alliance was reorganized, because Jiu Jin was more inclined toward the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, he was not liquidated and still remains as a guild elder. He even took advantage of the situation to rise up and annex many of the merchant alliance’s assets.”

Zi Di’s expression slightly sank, “So you’re saying he’s a traitor.”

Elder Yuan Ci replied, “That depends on how you look at it. At least that’s what I believe. But there is also a subset of people who believed Jiu Jin did nothing wrong. He just had a working relationship of mutual benefits, and he had repaid the Wisteria Merchant Alliance. As for annexing the merchant alliance assets… if he didn’t do it, someone else would have. As the saying goes, keep the good things within the family, isn’t it better if one of our own took it?”

Zi Di heard the unspoken implication, “So he still has supporters in the current merchant alliance? Who are they?”

Elder Yuan Ci replied, “It is Cong Mang.”

“Who is Cong Mang?”

“Cong Mang is the Vegetable Pirates commander, he is a druid, but dual cultivates battle qi and is proficient in the staff.”

“When the Wisteria Merchant Alliance was offering maritime trade routes, they came into contact with these pirates.
