He was one the president personally selected, and we subsided and helped him expand his force to facilitate his entry into the Fire Beard Pirates. Several important maritime trade routes were protected by him.”

Zi Di was flabbergasted, “I didn’t expect that the merchant alliance had such arrangements on the sea.”

Elder Yuan Ci laughed, “Many business channels are built secretly and aren’t recorded. On the surface, our merchant alliance accounts are all clean.”

“With regards to this, the president and others, me included, were very careful.”

“Regrettably, when the imperial higher-ups wanted to act against us, they didn’t look at this proof of innocence.”

Zi Di understood it more or less.

Jiu Jin and Cong Mang were important partners the merchant alliance cultivated while expanding abroad. Therefore, they had a close relationship with the Wisteria Merchant Alliance, though they would never have the emotional base for it someone like Fei She had. When the merchant alliance was reorganized, or to be more precise, when it deteriorated, they had less loyalty.

Elder Yuan Ci then said, “To open the Wisteria Cache, we need 5 level zero orders. You and I have two, Jiu Jin and Cong Mang have two, and for the last one, we need to join forces and get Shi Liu out of prison, he also has a level zero order.”

“Who is Shi Liu?” Zi Di found that she had a near ignorance of her own merchant alliance’s personnel.

Elder Yuan Ci did not conceal anything, because he needed Zi Di’s full cooperation. Right now, he needed to display his sincerity, “Shi Liu is an earth elemental; to be more precise, he is a magnetic earth elemental. As for why he has a level zero order, I am unsure, the old president had personally given him one.”

“Why was he imprisoned and where is he held?” Zi Di questioned.

Elder Yuan Ci sighed, “He came from the Magnet Archipelago, that group of islands as you might have heard is involved with two hidden treasures. People have searched for them for countless millennia but never found them. Of course that isn’t what we’re focused on. But it is said that Shi Liu once came across a part of one of these treasures and that he knows crucial clues to them.”

Zi Di pondered, “If this happens, we will have five level zero orders. But how will we work together?”

“Truth be told, what I really want to do most right now is search for Miss Zi Di.”

“If this matter wastes too much time and effort, involves my energy, is too risky, and not enough is given to me, I won’t do it.”

Zi Di retreated to advance to pry for more information.

Elder Yuan Ci replied, “Time is quite scarce.”

“This is also the information Jiu Jin told me about.”

“The Kingdom has been preparing for many years here, starting since the current king ascended the throne, and only when it finished this year did information begin to leak.”

“The royal family has constructed a super magic formation on the Ten Millennia Ice Lake. If this magic formation is activated, it will increase the royal family’s supervision and defense of the Ten Millennia Ice Lake immensely.”

“Therefore, Jiu Jin is especially worried that if this chance is missed, then stealthily excavating the Wisteria Cache will be extremely difficult or even impossible in the future.”

Zi Di knew the whole story.

Her brows tightly creased, “How does Jiu Jin know about my existence?”

Elder Yuan Ci replied, “He doesn’t. Only that I stipulated that the Wisteria Cache’s distribution is related to the number of secret orders we invited in.”

“We two have the biggest advantage and are also the strongest. Naturally, we hold a big share. Next is Cong Mang, you Gui Ten, and Shi Liu. It will depend on how much you contribute to this excavation.”

“Don’t worry, we will discuss the next steps beforehand and will sign an inviolable contract.”

“Of course, the prerequisite is that we are able to get 5 level zero order holders.”

“So that’s how it is.” After Zi Di responded with that, she sank into contemplation.

Elder Yuan Ci didn’t urge for an answer and calmly waited.

Zi Di thought about it, she naturally didn’t need to rescue Shi Liu. From Elder Yun Ci she had learned that as Zi Di, the old president’s sole daughter, she could make three level zero orders.

In her plan, she, Fei She, and Yuan Ci already had 5 of them already. They didn’t need Jiu Jin, Cong Mang, and that Shi Liu.

Therefore, she had ordered Fei She to rush over from Snow Bird Harbor.

But the plan couldn’t keep up with change.

“Elder Yuan Ci cannot be completely trusted.”

“It would be for the best to contact Jiu Jin and make a judgement in person. At the same time, I can also try to inspect the other people too.”

“I cannot tell them who I really am. Perhaps that can be utilized to hold an advantage.”

“Jiu Jin is an Alchemist Guild elder, and to open the cache, we might need his official protection.”

“Cong Mang is powerful and is a member of the Fire Beard Pirates. If he is cast away, it might infuriate him, provoking more trouble.

“Shi Liu…rescuing him will be risky.”

After a time of pondering, Zi Di informed Elder Yuan Ci through the communication device, “I can do it, I am inclined to work with you.”

“But before that, I need to personally discuss things with Elder Jiu Jin and Captain Cong Mang.”

“Elder Yuan Ci replied, “I understand, I will help you contact that old bastard Jiu Jin.”

“But Cong Mang is still on his way here. You may have to wait until after we work together to rescue Shi Liu to see him.”

Zik Di frowned, “He is a member of the Fire Beard Pirates, won’t a hasty infiltration into Ice Sculpture Island be challenging?”

Elder Yuan Ci nodded, “The Ice Sculpture National Ceremony is upon us, the capital’s defenses are tight and won’t be easy to get past.”

“But this time, the Fire Beard Pirates’ fourth in command Da Han has also rushed over too, he is a saint level existence who also has a saint level magic pet. Therefore, Cong Mang following in his stead will be very safe and the kingdom’s navy won’t be able to deal with him.”

“After he gets onto the island, Jiu Jin, that local bully, will be responsible for providing him support. Therefore, there will be a high chance we’ll meet without a hitch.”

Chapter 825: Elder Jiu Jin

When it came to the Wisteria Cache, Yuan Ci and Jiu Jin were very meticulous.

After receiving Zi Di’s response, Yuan Ci immediately used a secret channel to convey Jiu Jin the latest news.

When Yuan Ci told Jiu Jin he had found another bearer of a wisteria level zero order, the latter felt pleasantly surprised.

Jiu Jin wanted to inquire about Zi Di.

Yuan Ci didn’t hide anything and told him how he had saved Zi Di.

When he heard Zi Di was a necromancer, Elder Jiu Jin couldn’t help but be apprehensive, “Are you certain you can rule out this being a ploy of then imperial secret spies?”

Elder Yuan Ci coldly snorted, “He personally refined a level zero order in front of me.”

Elder Jiu Jin shook his head, “The level zero secret orders are maintained by a formidable contract related to a Demon God. But since it is a contract, it is possible they can be broken. You also know the imperial secret spies have never given up their search for the Wisteria Cache.”

“They are also a necromancer…”

“Alas, at the time, I was nearly taken away by the Sheng Ming Empire. I relied on the Alchemist Guild’s power and proved that I didn’t have a deep relationship with the merchant alliance, so I was able to avoid disaster.”

“If this is a trap from the Sheng Ming Empire secret spies, a meeting alone with that necromancer is the best incriminating evidence. Without reasonable grounds for the Alchemist Guild to protect me, this concrete evidence put me in big trouble!”

Elder Yuan Ci coldly snorted again, “Jiu Jin, you are still the same, excessively worrying over everything.”

“You don’t think I considered this possibility?”

“But so what?”

“You should understand the current circumstances more that me. You were the one who gave me the information.”

“The royal family will completely take charge of the Ten Millennia Ice Lake. Perhaps the lake bottom won’t be supervised, but I explicitly told you that the Wisteria Cache is on the same layer as the Ice Skate!”

“By then, even if we collect a full set of 5 Wisteria secret orders, we won’t be able to open the Wisteria Cache, won’t that be regrettable?”

Elder Jiu Jin sank into silence.

Elder Yuan Ci looked at the communication device before him, but it seemed like he could see into Elder Jiu Jin’s heart, and the corner of his mouth twitched, “Magician Gui Teng could or could not be a spy.”

“For now, we can thank him for appearing. After all, with him, we finally collected 5 Wisteria secret orders, right?”

“His appearance really is a fortuitous opportunity, so what?!”

“The area where the Wisteria Cache is, is where our Wister Merchant Alliance’s secret cam pit, it is our home ground. And around it is the Ten Millennia Ice Lake.”

“How many people can the imperial secret spies dispatch into it?”

“Based on the risks and profits, I have decided to gamble on it!”

Jiu Jin clenched his teeth and couldn’t refrain from silently cursing, “You have become a fugitive, Yuan Ci, detested by all. Hiding all these years in the Ten Millennia Ice Lake and the capital. I am different, I have a normal life, I am still a highly important Alchemist Guild elder…”

Jiu Jin hesitated for a good while, but in the end, the allure of the Wisteria Cache was too much, so he grinded his teeth and said, “Fine, if I win this gamble, I won’t need to struggle so hard anymore.”

“I will personally meet with Gui Teng, I will personally examine him!”

Yuan Ci cackled, “What a coincidence, Gui Teng also wants to personally speak with you.”

An in person meeting had risks, but it was absolutely necessary.

With regards to it, both Zi Di and Jiu Jin understood why. Yuan Ci had an even greater understanding of it: this was a crucial step to gain tentative trust in each other; it could not be omitted.

Thus, very quickly, from the abandoned alchemy laboratory, Yuan Ci personally arranged a meeting between Zi Di and Jiu Jin.

Jiu Jin was an old snow elf with a goatee and circle framed glasses.

His face was wrinkled and had a cold complexion, revealing his diligence and that the main theme of his life was enduring and struggling.

Before Zi Di came, she had purchased some information on Jiu Jin and learned that although Jiu Jin’s bloodline was gold level, it was not outstanding. If it wasn’t for the Wisteria Merchant Alliance’s backing, he couldn’t have become an Alchemist Guild elder.

His alchemy attainment wasn’t that deep, and he only researched alchemy runes.

That field required the least amount of alchemy attainment.

While Zi Di sized him up, Jiu Jin looked surprised, “I have taken a risk in meeting you, Magician Gui Teng. Will you not take off your hood? Will you have at least that bit of sincerity?”

Zi Di chuckled, “Sorry, I just didn’t want to do more than was required. You also know that as a necromancer, I won’t expose my true features lightly for my own safety.”

Despite saying that, Zi Di still took off her hood, exposing her “real appearance”.

Her exterior features was that of an ordinary, middle aged human male.

Deceptive Disguise and the Anti-prophecy and Investigation spell gave her no flaws in her appearance.

Jiu Jin narrowed his eyes, quietly using an alchemy device. Then after a moment, he frustratedly gave up his efforts.

His investigation results all showed Zi Di’s displayed human appearance.

This was obviously impossible.

Elder Jiu Jin was well aware of that.

He shrugged his shoulders, “At least your disguise abilities have been shown off.”

“Perhaps you can directly infiltrate the prison and effortlessly carry away Shi Liu.”

Zi Di shook her head, “Although I am a necromancer, I am still a magician. I cannot recklessly run in and put myself into danger.”

“This prison break needs to be schemed well.”

Next, the two people talked with each other, leaving behind their initial impression.

Elder Jiu Jin naturally maintained his strong doubts about Zi Di, but he didn’t reveal it to her face.

Zi Di indirectly verified Yuan Ci’s words from Jiu Jin, but she didn’t believe them fully.

She still tried an indirect approach with the alchemy elder to learn some information.

But Jiu Jin kept his mouth guarded in many areas, and he explicitly stated, “I am an Alchemist Guild elder, I signed a contract and cannot divulge high level secrets at will.”

“If in the future, an enemy exploits the kingdom’s secrets I accidentally leaked, the latter half of my life is finished! The gains I worked half my life for will vanish into nothing.”

Zi Di could only focus on inquiring about the Wisteria Cache.

Concerning this, Jiu Jin actually gave as much as he could, after all, it concerned their success or failure in it.

“Don’t have such wishful thinking.”

“The super magic formation is extremely powerful. It uses all sorts of alchemy rooms, magician towers, temples, and other locations around the ice lake as its formation bases, it is nearly perfectly integrated with the frozen lake and is not disturbed by the laws of time.”

“This gigantic engineering project was started the year His Majesty the King took the throne. Our Alchemist Guild was the primary builder, and every year, we dispatched large quantities of magicians to work on it. I have done so many times.”

“But now that those formation points are connected with each other, people can make out clues.”

“Since someone has seen through it, the royal family has officially disclosed this grand project. However, at this time, it is only limited to higherups like me.”

“His Majesty the King truly is a man of grand planning; I feel ashamed of my scorn of you.”

“This truly is an unprecedented undertaking. The most crucial thing is that the royal family actually endured silently until now, only revealing it when it could no longer be hidden.”

When that was said, Elder Jiu Jin shook his head and sighed, full of admiration for the current Ice Sculpture King.

Then a moment later, Elder Yuan Ci relentlessly pulled the rug out, “Ok, stop acting. Even if you show off your patriotism, if you are arrested now, it will be impossible for the kingdom to make allowances for meeting a necromancer in secret.”

Jiu Jin’s expression suddenly stiffened.

Zi Di was expressionless. She shifted the topic to dilute the awkward mood, asking, “About rescuing Shi Liu, Elder Jiu Jin, what can you contribute?”

Elder Jiu Jin promptly waved his hand, “How can I get involved with this? When an investigation comes, I won’t be able to keep my position as an Alchemist Guild elder!”

Zi Di coldly snorted, “You are an Alchemist Guild elder, a high position, it will be far easier for you to act than us. Rescuing Shi Liu will benefit us all. Without that level zero secret order, all of our efforts will be wasted.”

Elder Jiu Jin shook his head, his attitude firm, but his tone more moderate, “I am really powerless to do anything. Shi Liu is locked up in Ice Prison, it is the capital’s most important prison, and it has tight defenses. Ice Prison’s large formation can draw power from the Ten Millennia Ice Lake and possesses both the power of ice and time.

“I am only an Alchemy Guild elder; how can I influence Ice Prison’s guards?”

“But don’t worry, since I have chosen not to get involved, you two will get more contribution, and when the time comes to divide up the cache, you will take a larger share, isn’t that enough?”

“I won’t say anything else on the matter!”

“Of course, if you think that acting now is too uncertain, I can suggest that there is no harm in waiting for Cong Mang to arrive.”

Zi Di looked at Yuan Ci, “Do you have a plan for this jail break?”

Yuan Ci smiled, “I have one, perhaps we won’t need to wait for Cong Mang to converge with us.”

“Are you sure?” Elder Jiu Jin’s brows pursed, of course he was hoping the success rate of this operation would be higher.

Yuan Ci nodded to him, “I am certain.”

Not long after, the three left in different directions from this secret room.

As soon as Jiu Jin returned to the Alchemy Guild, he was summoned by the guild president.

When he saw the president, he noticed there was another in the room.

Jiu Jin smiled, and he bowed and saluted first, “Lord President, Great Master Hua Ni.”

Hua Ni was a female snow elf, and a great master in potions. She faintly glanced at Jiu Jin, but didn’t speak, making visible her haughtiness.

The president smiled, “Elder Jiu Jin, the construction and testing of the magic formation during this period truly was tough for you.”

Jiu Jin promptly stated that although he was tired, it was his duty, and he wanted to contribute to the nation’s strength.

The president continued, “The guild is currently focused on holding the Warm Snow Cup Alchemy Grand Contest. But the repairs and construction of the ice lake formation is finally complete, thus at long last, there is strength available to attend to some other areas.”

“Elder Jiu Jin, do you know what the hottest potion in the kingdom is right now?”

Jiu Jin heard the hearsay and his eyes flashed as he immediately replied, “The dragon rider potion.”

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s alchemy guild naturally had been watching the fiery sales of the dragon rider potion. But they were constrained by insufficient manpower and other notions, thus, they didn’t act.

The Alchemy Guild president went on, “I won’t beat about the bush with you, Elder Jiu Jin. The mount potion market is massive, and we cannot allow a foreign mercenary corps into it.”

“Therefore, I appoint you as the leader of a group and the representative of our guild that will occupy the mount potion market share.”

“We have always been a hegemon in the kingdom’s magic potion marketplace. This Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has gotten a bit out of hand, go teach them a deep lesson.”

Chapter 826: Recruiting the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps

In the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, the Alchemy Guild has always been a hegemon in the alchemy marketplace, holding a large market share in sectors like potions, alchemy equipment, alchemy goods, alchemy munitions, and other products.

Zi Di used her outstanding business acumen to dig out an as yet uncontested sector in the potion market and with the help of Long Fu’s nationwide momentum to sell a lot of dragon rider potions. The potion business was very prosperous, and its influence extended through the entire kingdom, and it caused a commotion within the industry and its profits even provoked envy.

The Alchemy Guild hadn’t intervened, but not because they didn’t notice the survivors’ activity.

They were in charge of the final constructions of the ice lake formation, and it involved most of the guild’s manpower and resources, but not to the point where they didn’t have any labor to spare.

The real reason was that there was a lot of them.

The more people there were, the more opinions they had. The larger group, the more chaotic they were, and it would be hard to coordinate the interests of all parties involved.

If an organization was likened to a ship, it was always easier to turn a small ship.

The dragon rider potion’s lively sales made it known to all alchemists that it contained enormous benefits.

But who would act?

The Alchemy Guild had many capable people, and so long as one of them acted, they could challenge and trouble the survivors.

But these able people could not freely act as they wished.

Because the Alchemy Guild had both public and many more unspoken rules.

These rules bloated the organization, but at the same time, their existence guaranteed the organization’s internal friction would be lower.

According to these rules, the first choice was Hua Ni.

Hua Ni had many contributions in the potion sphere and was a great master and also head of the potion division in the Alchemy Guild. She was also responsible for dealing with the kingdom’s potion market.

If she didn’t act, then others would find it hard to justify their actions, even the president was like that.

In fact, this generation’s Alchemy Guild president was quite weak.

Jiu Jin was pleasantly surprised to be suddenly entrusted with this task.

He was happy because the mount potion market had immense benefits. If he could execute this mission, his gains would be many, and it was astonishing that it wasn’t the Great Master Hua Ni who decided to take on this task.

But in an instant, Jiu Jin understood, and on the spot, he stated to Hua Ni that he wouldn’t dare exceed things, and everything would be under Great Master Hua Ni’s purview.

This attitude pleased Hua Ni, and she replied, “Elder Jiu Jin, you don’t need to be mentally burdened by this. This is my request. I want to join the Warm Snow Cup Grand Contest.”

Jiu Jin was surprised, “Great master, you also want to join the Warm Snow Cup?”

Hua Ni already had success and recognition, and to Jiu Jin it seemed she didn’t need to take this risk.

The guild president explained, “This Warm Snow Cup is unusual, during the National Ceremony, other powers might send people in to strut about. Therefore, the guild has dispatched a powerful person to keep watch.”

Jiu Jin revealed a sudden clarity, “So that’s how it is.”

At the same time, he also realized that the other Alchemy Guild bigwigs who convinced Hua Ni to give up the mount potion contest must have had a hard time doing so and had to give up many benefits during it.

“Because of my low alchemy strength, I was chosen to tidy up the domestic market.”

Jiu Jin promptly expressed that since he was being entrusted with this task, he would live up to that trust!

The president coughed, “You must be attentive, your opponent this time is the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. These people have a special background; they might be foreign arms merchants. Their branch in Snow Bird Harbor has been attacked, and because of Bu Quan, Shuang Lian was involved and has been unaccounted for since that day.”

“Therefore, you have another task too, that is to look for the whereabouts of Elder Shuang Lian from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

Jiu Jin was just about to accept the order when Hua Ni interjected, “This mission is secondary, your main energy should be focused on rectifying the market.”

Jiu Jin suddenly hesitated.

He was sandwiched between the Alchemy Guild president and Great Master Hua Ni.

Hua Ni looked at the president, “Shuang Lian’s life ice sculpture hasn’t shattered yet, that proves she is still alive.”

“Previously, when we dispatched outstanding investigators, they found traces of spatial fluctuations. It is very clear she and her magician tower suffered a spatial banishment spell.”

“With the Honey Snow Tower protecting her, it is unnecessary to worry about her.”

“It is better to just wait patiently, and when she sends a distress call, we can deploy a space teleportation formation and usher her return directly. Doing this saves both time and effort!”

The Alchemy Guild president’s face sank.

Great Master Hua Ni and Magician Shuang Lian had conflicting views and were opposing factions within the guild.

Hua Ni sounded pompous, it was clear she wanted to push aside Shuang Lian so she would continue to wander about and wouldn’t have enough time to participate in the Warm Snow Cup.

If Hua Ni had success in the Warm Snow Cup, she could undoubtedly get more honor and benefits. By the time the National Ceremony finished, even if Magician Shuang Lian returned, because of her contributions, Great Master Hua Ni would be above them.

This was Hua Ni’s plan, and it was not what the Alchemy Guild president wanted to see.

On the one hand, as a weak head, he needed to keep the internal factions in balance.

On the other hand, he was worried that during this National Ceremony, all sorts of powers would dispatch experts to suppress the Ice Sculpture Kingdom during the Warm Snow Cup. If that happened, the royal family and every great noble would look into it, and as president, he would definitely be in trouble!

Therefore, from his point of view, he naturally hoped Magician Shuang Lian would return on time.

But at this time, Hua Ni gazed into his eyes, “Lord President, did you know? Magician Shuang Lian already knows the Ice Breaker spell.”

“With such magic, how is she afraid of getting caught in a spatial trap?”

The Alchemy Guild president and Jiu Jin both flashed with alarm.

The president was somewhat incredulous, “Wasn’t the Ice Breaker spell’s research stuck in a bottleneck? The designed magic model was massive, it didn’t seem possible for one magician to cast by themselves, it required too much spirit power, and its magic power expenditure hadn’t been optimized either.”

Jiu Jin also threw Hua Ni a look of deep concern.

Although the Alchemy Guild mainly focused on alchemy, and was not a magic guild, magic and alchemy were intrinsically connected with each other.

Jiu Jin knew about the Ice Breaker spell.

This spell’s research began several hundred years ago. It began with some Alchemy Guild elder using a Great Pyrolysis spell during some alchemy experiment. Although the experiment failed, he still gained inspiration and direction from it.

Based that direction, he took the Great Pyrolysis spell as the foundation of his research, with his goal being to research an ice type Great Pyrolysis spell.

The Great Pyrolysis spell was always a high end spell and was powerful to such a terrifying degree that it could even disintegrate divine artifacts!

The Ice Breaker spell was roughly an ice type Great Pyrolysis spell.

A magician who could wield this magic would see their fighting strength soar enormously. Not just that, the results of this magic design was certain to cause a sensation throughout the nation’s realms of magic, and even the entire Main Plain. It would also cause all powers and countless experts to raise their eyebrows at this astonishing achievement.

Hua Ni looked at the two and smiled, “What are you thinking about? Shuang Ni’s advantage lies with her resources; it would be impossible to cast the Ice Breaker spell by herself.”

“She indeed improved upon the initial research, but she geared it towards her magician tower.”

“Now, her magician tower has loaded in the Ice Breaker spell’s magic model and has no problem using it.”

Hua Ni was worthy of being Shuang Lian’s long time rival, she had a greater understanding of the situation than Jiu Jin, and even the president.

“So that’s how it is.” Jiu Jin coughed.

This kind of improvement was quite ordinary.

A single magician still couldn’t use Ice Breaker, but a magician tower had a greater magic power reservoir and also a tower spirit to assist, making it easier.

How many magicians could have their own tower?

Therefore, even if Shuang Lian had improved it somewhat, it was only minutely, and it was not enough to shock others with its development.

The president’s finger tapped the table, his expression somewhat indeterminate.

Shuang Lian’s magician tower was equipped with the Ice Breaker spell, this was something that both made him apprehensive and annoyed.

“I was actually completely ignorant to such significant information!”

“Magician Shuang Lian has hidden herself so deeply. I vainly wanted to help her wholeheartedly to balance out the factions.”

“The truth is that you were already the most forefront of the factions. It’s no wonder that when you went missing, the other factions were all similarly indifferent.”

When he thought that, the president’s mind had changed.

He looked at Jiu Jin, “Magician Shuang Lian is worthy of being my guild’s elder, her astonishing strength worries me too much.”

“Elder Jiu Jin, your objective this time is to wrest away market share in the mount potion sector.”

Jiu Jin promptly expressed his understanding and that he would make an all-out effort.

Hua Ni had another command.

She asked, “What do you plan to do?”

Jiu Jin replied at once, “Be at ease, Great Master Hua Ni, our force is substantial and is the kingdom’s sole hegemon.”

“Now that I have the Lord President’s and your appointment, I can immediately mobilize personnel and change some alchemy factory merchandise lines, then dump the goods nationwide.”

“How many alchemy facilities does the Li Jian Clan have? How many master alchemists? How can they compare to us?”

Jiu Jin was of the opinion that their side had a massive advantage in this war and had no chance of losing.

But Hua Ni’s face sank, “Idiot, why do you only consider using power to suppress people?”

“First try to recruit them.”

“I’ve researched the dragon rider potion. Its design is very ingenious and also very original.”

“Recruit the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, they might not just have a single magic potion recipe.”

Jiu Jin exposed confusion and admiration, “I understand, Great Master Hua Ni.”

“My horizons are too small.”

“Your plan is better! By recruiting them, not only will the mount potion market be ours, but their talents will also be ours too!”

“But at what price should we open this recruitment?”

Hua Ni coldly snorted, “Regarding such minor details, do you need me to tell you? You are responsible for this, not me.”

“Understood, understood.” Jiu Jin nodded his head and bowed, while in actuality, he was grumbling to himself.

Hadn’t he considered recruiting them?

Of course he did.

But recruitment came at a price, a difficult task.

It was easy to make mistakes.

But using overwhelming power to suppress people was never the wrong choice to win.

There is nothing that could be done.

Hua Ni’s opinion had been explicitly stated, and the president had no objections, so Elder Jiu Jin could only obey it.

Chapter 827: Threatening and Forcing

The defenses were tight inside the inn.

“Lord, Lord President.” A travel-worn Fei She saluted Zi Di, he looked excited, “It is indeed good to see you safe and sound.”

Since Zi Di summoned him, Tripleblade had mustered a group of people for Fei She to hide in. On the face of it, they were here to examine accounts, but in reality, they were there to transport the key figure Fei She.

Of course, they would also be doing the account auditing.

Zi Di smiled upon seeing Fei She again, a cordial feeling flowing through her, “Sorry, Elder Fei She, for forcing you to brave the journey to the Ice Sculpture Capital.”

Fei She immediately shook his head, “If the enemy wanted to attack, staying in Snow Bird Harbor would not be safe at all.”

“Furthermore, when me and the old president built things from scratch, I have experienced far more dangerous situations than these many times over.,”

“Lord President, you gave the right command.”

“It’s a pity that I am the only survivor to come. I have failed to live up to your trust.”

Before departing, Fei She had written a secret letter to Tripleblade to tell him to stop him from further rescue operations.

Zi Di had known about this earlier and nodded, “Tripleblade will remain over there, what else can be said? If the big guy was at Snow Bird Harbor, then it would be another matter.”

Zi Di was just about to tell Fei She about Yuan Ci, Jiu Jin, and Wisteria Cache.

After all, Fei She also possessed a level zero Wisteria Secret Order, this business might need his participation in the future.

Then at that moment, a visitor came calling.

“An Alchemy Guild envoy?” Zi Di raised an eyebrow, soon after her surprise vanished completely.

“It’s about that time.” Zi Di whispered.

Fei She immediately felt nervous, “Lord President, they come with ill intent.”

As an elder of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance, Fei She knew quietly clearly that if Zi Di wanted to establish herself in the ice Sculpture Kingdom’s alchemy market, her biggest enemy was Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s Alchemy Guild!

The latter was massive and was entirely worthy of the title hegemon. They held market share in almost every alchemy market sector in the kingdom.

Although Zi Di had opened a new market with mount potions, the Alchemy Guild would not stand by idly and look on helplessly as the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps raked in profits.

Therefore, regardless of whether it was Zi Di, Fei She, the other survivors, or even the Li Jian Clan, they had all come to the same consensus. Sooner or later, they had to bump against the Alchemy Guild.

There would be a contest, or even several contests between them.

It was only a matter of time.

Zi Di let out a breath, then ordered an attendant to prepare. She wanted to be on good terms with the Alchemy Guild envoy.

Fei She promptly said, “Lord President, let me join you.”

Zi Di nodded, consenting to this request.

A short while later at the parlor, Zi Di gave Magician Jiu Jin a weird look.

She didn’t think that she would be able to see the other in such a short time span.

Jiu Jin didn’t see Zi Di’s and Fei She’s true features; the survivors had Deceptive Disguise and the Anti-prophecy and Investigation spell on them.

Right now, Fei She looked quite different from when he was meeting secretly with Zi Di.

“Magician Yao Ma, I am Elder Jiu Jin of the kingdom’s Alchemy Guild. I am very happy to see you and believe that you are happy to see me. Because this time, I brought you an opportunity that can change your life!”

Jiu Jin wore a smile and spoke pretentiously, rather than speaking cordially, he instead released his aura and tried to suppress Zi Di.

Zi Di dealt with it, “What is this wonderful opportunity?”

Jiu Jin directly stated his purpose in coming, the Alchemy Guild’s higher-ups admired the dragon rider potion recipe and wanted to recruit the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Zi Di showed astonishment and feigned puzzlement, “Aren’t the Alchemy Guild and mercenary corps in different lanes of business?”

Jiu Jin replied, “Our Alchemy Guild has plenty of money, and hires many mercenary corps all year round. Among them, there are three five star mercenary corps.”

Five star mercenary corps had at least a gold level cultivator.

Zi Di continued to ask, “May I know what we will get if we accept the guild’s offer?”

Jiu Jin’s smile grew, and he offered an opening price.

Zi Di suddenly laughed, “This price…Elder Jiu Jin, don’t make jokes.”

Jiu Jin pretended not to understand, “How can that be? This reward is clearly marked. Do you think a grand Alchemy Guild elder would swindle you?”

Zi Di’s complexion turned cold, “Don’t speak in circles, state it directly Elder Jiu Jin. Your Alchemy Guild wants to recruit my corps not for some mercenary missions, rather you are seeking the dragon rider potion, is that so?”

Jiu Jin coldly snorted, “Since you’ve uncovered that, then let’s have a straightforward discussion.”

“As outsiders, don’t you think you’re overextending?”

“The mount potions have too many benefits, a minor mercenary corps like yourselves cannot swallow it. If you have a backer behind you, bring them to come speak with us personally.”

Jiu Jin believed that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ backer was an international arms smuggler.

Deliberately having Zi Di bring forth her big boss was actually a type of coercion.

As everyone knew the chances of an arms smuggler leader appearing in the flesh, it was too risky.

Zi Di then asked, “Isn’t the Alchemy Guild being too overbearing?”

“The Ice Sculpture Kingdom is an open marketplace; every year your distinguished country has vast exports and imports.”

“If the alchemy marketplace here is your Alchemy Guild’s exclusive property, then please show your country’s legislation and we’ll comply with it earnestly.”

“Humph!” Jiu Jin banged his teacup into the table and continued to pressure, “Magician Yao Ma, do you think I have come here with a request?”

“Us recruiting you is showing you benevolence.”

“When our guild acts, do you think you can sustain yourself? Your current business is quite prosperous, but it was only established under the prerequisite that our guild remains inactive.”

“Come look at this.”

Jiu Jin took out some information and placed it on the table.

Zi Di took it and looked at it, her pupils suddenly contracting.

This potion recipe was the dragon rider recipe, personally reverse engineered by Hua Ni.

“Now do you understand?” Jiu Jin arrogantly replied, “We are completely able to strike hard at the dragon rider Potion, but we haven’t done that yet.”

“Savor it carefully, and you can taste our sincerity and goodwill.”

“As a senior master alchemist, I still want to give you youngsters some words of advice. I want you to treasure this opportunity, as such an opportunity is very rare to see in your life.”

Jiu Jin’s attitude was quite high, putting himself as a senior.

Fei She stood behind Zi Di, playing the role of an attendant.

He didn’t recognize Jiu Jin and wasn’t aware that this Alchemy Guild elder was once a member of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance and also possessed a level zero Wisteria Secret Order.

Right now, Fei She wished he could slap Jiu Jin across the face a couple of times.

“This old fool says the words sincerity and benevolence, but the price he offered is low, and he also used a deciphered recipe to threaten us.”

“The Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s Alchemy Guild has been a monopoly for too long, this overbearing style is something that people despise.”

“The problem is that Jiu Jin is right. The Alchemy Guild is too powerful, and although the Li Jian Clan is a top clan within the kingdom, they cannot compete with the Alchemy Guild in terms of alchemy.”

“When the Alchemy Guild acts and makes large amounts of potions before dumping onto the market, our potion business will be heavily damaged and take a devastating dive.”

Fei She was a Wisteria Merchant Alliance elder and had been involved in commerce for many years, he knew there were two reasons Zi Di had succeeded.

The first was using Commander Long Fu’s reputation to mold a public reputation during the initial stages.

The second was Zi Di capturing an opportunity. After she threw herself into the mount potion market, she made an all out effort, and with the help of the Li Jian Clan’s channels, quickly seized it. Before others could react, she had gained a foothold and seized a crucial position.

But on the market, price was the main, and even the deciding factor.

When the Alchemy Guild disregarded costs to dump product, the cheap price would win over the customers compared to their feelings for the dragonman youngster. The survivors’ early control and “territory” would also be quickly lost.

What about being recruited into the Alchemy Guild?

That was also out of the question.

According to Jiu Jin’s conditions, he undoubtedly wanted the survivors to hand over the business foundation they laid without resistance.

Not only that, but after the survivors joined the Alchemy Guild, they also had to work for high level members like Elder Jiu Jin like cattle.

That simply was intolerable bullying!

Zi Di appeared embarrassed, “This matter is too important, and I am not the master. I need time to ask for instructions.”

Jiu Jin coldly snorted and shook his head, “Youngster, my time is very valuable, you can’t squander it like that.”

“Today I will get an answer.”

“You can communicate immediately; I will wait here for it.”

“Remember, you only have ten minutes.”

Zi Di grew unsightly, and after throwing out some polite words, left the parlor with Fei She, leaving Jiu Jin inside.

Fei She felt doubtful, “This Elder Jiu Jin has a terrible attitude. He doesn’t want us to join the guild. Lord President, what should we do?”

Nevertheless, Zi Di smiled, her expression and tone just now were all just a pretense.

“If it were another, I might not be able to do anything about it. But of all people, why is it him?”

“Huh?” Fei She was curious, “Lord, you already have something?”

“I’ll tell you the specifics after this matter. Meeting me is also his misfortunate. Wait a moment, then it will be my turn to threaten him.” Zi Di chuckled.

Fei She felt even more curious, thinking, “What does the Lord President have against him? It seems this information is not simple.”

Fei She didn’t know the leverage he was so curious about was actually Zi Di herself.

Chapter 828: Jiu Jin, You are a Close Contact!

In the parlor, Jiu Jin was contently drinking tea.

He didn’t sincerely want to recruit the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

This was in accordance with his interests.

Although he promised Great Master Hua Ni that he would go recruit the survivors, from start to finish, he planned to botch this assignment.

Because this matter was very troublesome.

Firstly, he needed to rack his brains during the negotiation with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Secondly, his negotiation might be criticized by the Alchemy Guild’s higher-ups.

Therefore, rather than spending his stamina and bearing the risks doing this, he was better off using strong pressure to move them along the path of refusing to be recruited.

When Zi Di refused, then he could justify organizing manpower to conduct large scale potion production, then dump the goods onto the marketplace.

At that time, he would have adequate time and opportunity to embezzle and fish out benefits.

“It seems this is my fortune.”

“One is the royal family’s move, forcing that guy Yuan Ci to no longer sit still and cooperate; there is a chance of obtaining the Wisteria Cache.”

“The second is that a lucrative job fell onto me. The alchemists who are stronger than me are obligated to comply with the royal family’s orders to oversee the Warm Snow Cup.”

“Hehe, I didn’t expect that sometimes, being a bit weaker has its benefits.”

Jiu Jin happily drank his tea as he thought that.

But a moment later, there were two thuds.

Two silver level alchemy puppets suddenly charged in and pincered Jiu Jin.

Yao Ma (Zi Di) soon appeared holding an alchemy crossbow pointed at Jiu Jin.

Jiu Jin was stunned for a second, then he swapped to laughing, “Magician Yao Ma, what do you think you’re doing to me?”

“Don’t be impulsive, you wouldn’t want to kill me over some argument?”

“Even if you kill me, you can’t escape. This is the Ice Sculpture Capital!”

Jiu Jin tried to steady the impulsive Yao Ma as he thought with a growl, “What is going on?”

He couldn’t understand why this was happening. After Magician Yao Ma asked for instructions, he received an order to kill him!

“Even if your backer wants to kill me out of anger, you are also a sacrificial pawn, calm down youngster!” Jiu Jin continued to persuade.

Zi Di quietly chuckled, Elder Jiu Jin, you are in close contact with a necromancer. Me killing you will rid the people of a scourge, and not only won’t I be arrested, but I will also be rewarded by the royal family.”

“Slander!”

“I am unable to make sense of your drivel!!”

“Magician Yao Ma, if you want to haggle, there is no need to use such methods.”

“Contacting a necromancer is no joke. Even if you want to slander and frame me, don’t use such grounds.”

“You might not understand, but every mention of necromancers is investigated by the royal family.”

“When you’re investigated, your secret of framing me will be exposed, you are better off thinking of another excuse.”

Although Jiu Jin’s heart was beating wildly, he calmed himself and continued to talk persuasively.

Zi Di chuckled and took out an alchemy device.

She put on an act as she displayed the device to Jiu Jin, “Take a look, this is the best evidence.”

“Elder Jiu Jin, don’t think about disavowing it.”

Jiu Jin rigidly glared at the mysterious device in Zi Di’s hand, still reluctant to admit his guilt, “Magician Yao Ma, your method is too clumsy.”

“You don’t think that taking out some presentable alchemy device at random is considered definitive proof?”

“As an Alchemy Guild elder, I have never heard of such an alchemy device.”

Zi Di sneered, “Now you can hear and also see it with your own eyes.”

“I shall present it to you.”

“This is one of our flagship alchemy products, it is called the Necromancy Aura Detector.”

“This instrument detects a lot of necromancer aura on you!”

“This level of aura indicates that you have had a long and close conversation with a necromancer. Furthermore, it hasn’t been a day since that happened.”

Jiu Jin seemed to have been struck by lightning.

He shouted, “Slander, this is pure slander.”

“I want to leave, and you want to block me, you are attacking me.”

“I am an Alchemy Guild Elder, if I die here, to say nothing of everyone in your mercenary corps, even your master backing this plan will be eradicated.”

Zi Di was not worried, and she gestured, “Elder Jiu Jin, please then, do not hesitate to leave.”

“But following this, I will take this evidence and submit a report to the Ice Sculpture Government.”

Jiu Jin berated and cursed Zi Di loudly, saying she was slandering him. But regardless of how he cursed her, he didn’t leave the parlor.

“What is that thing? How can there be a device like the Necromancy Aura Detector?”

Jiu Jin’s heart went ice cold, and his mind rapidly pondered how he should deal with this gigantic crisis.

He didn’t doubt Zi Di was deceiving him.

Not just because Zi Di’s conjecture was wholly in accordance with the facts. It was also because it was quite strange for Zi Di to denounce him for being a necromancer or being closely connected to necromancer activity.

No normal person would ever use such grounds to bargain with.

This was not a bargaining strategy.

Zi Di stared at Jiu Jin and smiled, “To be frank, I never thought I would have such good luck.”

“This alchemy device sample on me was originally planned to be used during the Warm Snow Cup Grand Contest.”

“As a result, it vibrated in my pocket.”

“Originally, I was uncertain, but when I left, I measured it twice. Each time the outcome was the same.”

“Elder Jiu Jin, I didn’t expect that you, a grand Alchemy Guild higher-up, would actually be a secret close friend of necromancers.”

Jiu Jin resolutely denied it and shook his head, “I am not, I haven’t, don’t talk drivel!”

“Do you think taking out a broken thing at random can intimidate me?”

“Hehehe.” Zi Di laughed, “It seems you still aren’t willing to face reality?”

“Let me tell you something, it is about this alchemy device’s design principle.”

Some types of this alchemy device really did exist.

The inventor was War Merchant.

At the time, the survivors on Two Eyes Island had run across a necromancer. Zi Di had once spent some time carefully researching the Necromancer Aura Detector in War Merchant’s database.

Therefore, she understood much of its design principles.

After saying some of it, Elder Jiu Jin’s look changed again, becoming pale as paper.

Although Elder Jiu Jin’s alchemy mastery was not that high, that depended on who he was being compared to. Compared to other elders, he couldn’t compete and was at the bottom. But compared to Zi Di, he far surpassed her.

He had researched alchemy runes for many years and seen enough with his own eyes.

Therefore, by just listening to Zi Di’s opening statement, he once again confirmed that the other party had such an alchemy device!

“What sin have I committed to have the misfortune of running into such a thing?!” Jiu Jin angrily trembled all over, his head filling with cold sweat.

Not long ago, he was still sighing over his luck, and now he was only thinking he was the unluckiest guy under the sun.

“Magician Gui Teng and also Yuan Ci, if anything happens to me, I’ll sell you both out!”

Jiu Jin was totally oblivious that the person who was testifying and his necromancer contact were the exact same person before him.

Zi Di completely held the advantage and calmly said, “It doesn’t matter, Senior.”

“Come, let’s sit down and have a nice chat.”

Zi Di sat down first, wearing a smile.

Seeing that smile, Jiu Jin felt a chill run down his spine, and he really wanted to kill that silver level magician.

“Don’t be impulsive, being impulsive makes it easy to make serious mistakes.” Zi Di mischievously winked at Jiu Jin, “If something happens to me, elder, your secret will be spread across the entire capital.”

Jiu Jin felt extremely depressed, but after sitting down, he calmed down a bit.

He glared at Zi Di, biting his teeth, “Magician Yao Ma, if you believe you can rely on this to blackmail me, then you are gravely mistaken.”

“Am I the type only interested in saving my own neck!?”

“If you are too excessive, at worse, we will break up. Even if I die, you will be dragged down too.”

Jiu Jin’s face was hardened and his eyes red and bloodshot, he looked like a pressed gambler.

Zi Di wasn’t the slightest nervous.

She even disdainfully sneered at him on the sly.

If this was her first meeting with Magician Jiu Jin, perhaps his attitude could fool her.

But Zi Di and Jiu Jin had met and conversed before, and during that, she grasped Jiu Jin’s character.

He excessively treasured his own plumage and was exceptionally reluctant to part with his property and power.

Elder Jiu Jin’s base nature was known by Zi Di already.

Next, Zi Di didn’t force it, rather she used her words to placate Elder Jiu Jin as much as possible and let him drink tea.

Jiu Jin drank the tea with an ashen face and a vengeful expression, once again leaking his numbness while trapped in desperate straits.

Zi Di’s smile grew stronger.

Comparing the expression of both, one could determine who was definitely winning this discussion.

The following negotiation was rather effortless for Zi Di.

After spending less than half an hour, talks came to an end.

The parlor door opened and with a deathly pale complexion, Elder Jiu Jin staggered out.

Each of his steps was as stiff as a zombie’s, and he miserably left the hotel.

At the window, Zi Di and Fei She calmly watched Magician Jiu Jin leave.

Fei She was very delighted, “Amazing, we hold this leverage, and we also forced him to sign a confession, there is also the magic images as proof. From now on, I’m not worried that Magician Ju Jin won’t submit to us!”

Fei She looked at Zi Di with admiration and gratification.

He greatly approved of Zi Di’s method for handling this. She didn’t rush to squeeze the benefits out of Jiu Jin, rather she kindly consoled Jiu Jin. It was suitable to slowly cut the meat to obtain long term profits.

“Anyway, what exactly is going on?” Fei She was exceedingly curious.

“Actually, Elder Jiu Jin is a member of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance, he also has a level zero Wisteria Secret Order.”

“What?”

Chapter 829: All Spat Out

Soon after, Zi Di told Fei She of the situation in detail.

Fei She replied, “So, so that’s how it is.”

He felt delighted to witness a comeuppance, “A traitor like Jiu Jin is more infuriating, our merchant alliance subsidized him and helped him become an Alchemy Guild Elder. But in the end, he was the first to stab us and swipe most of our property.”

Zi Di smiled, but her eyes were leaking with cold intentions, “It is understandable. After all, he and the Wisteria Merchant Alliance don’t have an emotional base, only collaboration and exploitation.”

Fei She sighed, “The merchant alliance’s arrangement on Ice Sculpture Island was too impetuous, the recruited associates were actually allocated level zero authority.”

“This was not the old president’s style. He was forced to do that, for the situation at the time was getting more urgent and he had to hasten arrangements.”

“I didn’t expect he would place the Wisteria Cache in the Ten Millennia Ice Lake.”

“It was a wonderful plan, unfortunately, this generation’s Ice Sculpture King employed a long term stratagem that began since he took the throne. Due to the magic formation on the lake bottom, we are forced to open the Wisteria Cache as soon as possible.”

“There is also Yuan Ci, I didn’t expect that old fellow to not be dead and always managing to stay hidden.”

“Humph, he surprisingly wanted to secretly open the cache…”

Fei She loathed Yuan Ci’s display of disloyalty.

Lord President, what should I do next?”

“With me and you, we have four level zero secret orders. There is also Jiu Jin who was forced to sign a contract with us and can be considered partially on our side.”

“If that happens, we already have 5 level zero secret orders, that is enough to open the Wisteria Cache.”

Zi Di nodded, “It will depend on how the situation develops.”

“After all, Yuan Ci is the only one who knows where the Wisteria Cache is.”

“This time our luck is pretty good that Jiu Jin decided to drop in to visit. After today’s negotiation, we have established many advantages, and the Wisteria Cache has great prospects for the future.”

Fei She looked worried again, “Although Elder Jiu Jin has been secretly suppressed by us, he is not the only elder in the Alchemy Guild.”

“It can even be said that he doesn’t have much clout in the guild.”

“Lord President, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s alchemy technology is a standout on the Main Plane, this country’s alchemy guild is very strong and cannot be treated lightly.”

Zi Di glanced at Fei She, knowing the unspoken implication from experience, “You are worried about the betting agreement?”

“That’s right.” Fei She nodded, sincerely admonishing, “Lord President, why did you want to take the risk, Jiu Jin has already been captured by us, we can force him to sign a more dependable and secure contract.”

Zi Di laughed softly, explaining, “I actually considered doing that, but after careful thought, I still think having the outer appearance of a betting agreement is more appropriate.”

“For one, this is the result of our negotiation with Jiu Jin, and not the final outcome. Jiu Jin still needs to petition for it at the Alchemy Guild.”

“Second, we need to treasure the gains of this victory. If the agreement was more inclined towards us and it brings about Jiu Jin being fired from the Alchemy Guild, it will be more devastating for us.”

“Finally, only through the form of a betting agreement can we get more and better benefits from the Alchemy Guild. The profits are usually relative to risk, right?”

That sentence greatly reduced Fei She’s worry, but it wasn’t completely gone, “Even if it is a betting agreement, the Alchemy Guild might not agree to it.”

“No, I am rather certain they will accept it.” Zi Di nevertheless smiled confidently, “Don’t forget, this isn’t the Sheng Ming Empire, it is the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

Fei She was startled, then soon saw the light, “I understand, it is the dueling ambience here!”

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s territory was surrounded by islands, and their ancestors came from the Frost Continent. That history and the geography here formed a unique country culture.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s citizens felt strongly about, watched, and participated in duels.

The alchemists here fundamentally also harbored those trends. These deep cultural genes existed in their hearts and couldn’t be altered.

A bet was actually another form of dueling.

The Alchemy Guild’s magicians were arrogant and self-important, if they refused, they would subconsciously feel shame.

They were almost certain to agree to it!

“The Alchemy Guild wanting to recruit us divulges that they have scorn for us.”

“They would never think we have War Merchant’s legacy on us. Our chances of winning this bet is over 90%.”

“This is a very good opportunity for us to deal with an incomparably strong competitor like the Alchemy Guild.”

When Zi Di said that her eyes became sharp, “There will inevitably be a day when I will make these people return all of the things they’ve taken from us!”

“Lord President…” For a moment, Fei She saw the old president’s shadow in Zi Di.

Dueling Divine Domain.

Tranquil Hill.

Bing Xiao, the human ringleader, Lan Zao, and the other Bayonet Gang members were watching a battle.

The battle was between a bearman and a humanoid modified by alchemy.

The bearman was strong and sturdy, and his face had a cross shaped scar, it was Xiong Ju.

The humanoid modified by alchemy was Spring Boxer.

The two were silver level fighters, and their battle had reached their fiercest moment.

Xiong Ju was using a silver level broadsword and attacked violently, his battle skill usage was very skilled, and his wrestling technique was even more ruthless and efficient.

Spring Boxer wasn’t using his customary hands, rather he was wielding a silver level whip.

Battle skill – Quick Lash.

Battle skill – Augment Whip.

Battle skill – Curved Whip Sweep.

…

Spring Boxer repeatedly used whip type battle skills to form shadows of whips that filled the entire sky in a prestigious offensive.

“He’s just like Gang Leader Teng Dong Lang.” The human ringleader sighed.

Spring Boxer absorbed many of Teng Dong Lang’s memories, and he gained a deep mastery of these battle skills from nothing.

Although he was mainly recuperating in Tranquil Hill, his battle strength had improved a lot.

This was the charm of Tranquil Hill’s inheritance.

“It’s no wonder Bing Xiao and Spring Boxer wanted to bury duelists on Tranquil Hill by any means necessary.” The human ringleader’s eyes flashed.

He was a spy and had the important duty of scouting out information on Tranquil Hill.

Nowadays, after living on Tranquil Hill for many days, he had gathered much first-hand information on the place.

But he couldn’t leave.

Therefore, this information could not be delivered.

Lan Zao and he were the same, likewise spies, but Lan Zao was working hard moreso for the dragoman youngster.

He was spying on Tranquil Hill to help the dragonman youngster find a way to resurrect Zi Di and Cang Xu.

Reality changed far faster than plans did.

Lan Zao was unaware the dragonman youngster already knew the Resurrection spell.

He was closely watching the battle unfold, “Spring Boxer is very strong, if it was before, Xiong Ju wouldn’t be his match.

“But now, Xiong Ju has also lived on Tranquil Hill for many days, his strength has also improved quickly!”

Xiong Ju was a fanatic of the Dueling God!

He rigorously kept to dueling rules and abided by dueling philosophies. At the time, during his duel with the dragonman youngster, he was unwilling to abide with the demon hybrid ringleader’s interference.

As Spring Boxer recuperated, Xiong Ju’s strength was also changing every day.

He trained here and constantly received divine enlightenment that gave him all kinds of battle experience from his forebearers.

Previously, Lan Zao could fight Xiong Ju for a bit as their sparring partner. But as time passed, the gap between Lan Zao and Xiong Ju gradually increased and Lan Zao could no longer be his sparring partner.

“Becoming a Dueling God fanatic comes with substantial gains.”

“But it’s a pity, no matter what I do, I cannot completely believe in the Dueling God.”

Lan Zao felt very regretful.

Faith was not something a person could deepen through wishing it to be so.

On the one hand, using gains as a lure had their limits. Using gains as a lure to increase conviction had many big systemic problems.

On the other hand, Lan Zao was a foreigner and was unlike Xiong Ju who was wholly indigenous and had been immersed in a dueling ambience since childhood. Lan Zao had come here to conspire and scheme. Naturally, he was wary of the Bayonet Gang remnants and this divine domain.

Thus, the estrangements in his heart caused him to be unable to effectively improve his belief in the Dueling God.

In short, it is very hard for people to truly deceive themselves!

Suddenly, Xiong Ju growled and used a battle skill to close the distance between him and Spring Boxer.

Xiong Ju waved the broadsword and constantly attacked.

Spring Boxer retreated again and again, until finally, he was forced to abandon the whip and use the fists he was most familiar with to fight Xiong Ju in close quarters.

Although he had received Teng Dong Lang’s memories and battle experience, he hadn’t adapted to them much.

Spring Boxer had gotten a lot, but his fighting strength hadn’t improved significantly.

Looking at Xiong Ju, it was utterly different.

Because his belief was deep, was constantly training, and had received divine enlightenment, inheriting battle experience from his precursors.

These inheritances all had a clear objective, they were based on Xiong Ju’s own battle style, methods, and skills, among other things.

Like Lan Zao practicing Air Cannon, who had obtained some experience from others about this battle skill from others.

Spring Boxer had started from scratch while Xiong Ju was reinforcing himself.

Therefore, in close quarters combat, Spring Boxer immediately became visibly fatigued and fell into a disadvantage.

“Ok.” Bing Xiao promptly shouted at them to stop, “Xiao Tan’s condition isn’t good, and still needs to recuperate. This is just practice and sparring, continuing to fight is going too far.”

In the past, Spring Boxer would have been delighted to keep battling, even losing didn’t matter to him.

But now, after absorbing Teng Dong Lang’s memories, Spring Boxer could deliberate his position. In the future, he would become the Bayonet Gang Leader, and if he was defeated by Xiong Ju, his prestige would be greatly damaged, thereby destabilizing the cohesion of the entire Bayonet Gang.

Therefore, Spring Boxer stopped without delay.

“Not going to fight?” Xiong Ju wished to continue battling.

Then at that moment, several loud sounds could be heard on the hill.

The Bayonet Gang remnants followed the noise and saw shadows flying and fiercely fighting above Tranquil Hill.

“What’s going on?”

“It seems there is an enemy attack?!”

Chapter 830: Gu Dong vs Shuang Lian

Dueling Divine Domain.

Tranquil Hill.

Halfway up the hill.

A fierce battle was unfolding.

“Damn it!” Gu Dong dodged left and right, looking exceptionally sorry.

“How did Shuang Lian smuggle in two gold level snow elementals into Tranquil Hill?” Gu Dong was unable to answer this question.

If it weren’t for the snow elementals moving more as they climbed up, Gu Dong would not have noticed them.

“If it weren’t for the imminent National Ceremony and the strength here being reduced, it wouldn’t be so dangerous right now!”

Gu Dong bitterly struggled and was on the downwind.

Fortunately, he had already asked for help and was now looking for when Mei Lin came in to reinforce him.

“Before that, I must persevere and hold the final defensive line.”

Gu Dong understood that on the peak of Tranquil Hill, there were a number of duelist tombstones in the cemetery. The names on them were extremely important information.

Regardless of what Shuang Lian was fighting for, as long as she saw these tombstones, the secrets of these duelists would be exposed.

Honey Snow Tower.

Shuang Lian and the tower spirit were merged together as they controlled the gold level snow elementals with all their strength and a large quantity of alchemy puppets in battle.

“I hold the advantage in this battle!” Shuang Lian stirred herself with exuberant morale.

The two snow elementals were gold level; they had been arduously subdued by her to suppress the demi-plane’s main force. They were now mobilized here.

Apart from them, there were also numerous alchemy puppets. A considerable portion of them had been produced by her and her students day and night for this. Some were for combat, but many more were for investigation and surveillance.

After touching down on Tranquil Hill, a huge volume of data was acquired by these alchemy puppets and then transmitted back to Honey Snow Tower.

Shung Lian held the initiative, and she constantly directed these alchemy puppets to break through the defenses from all directions.

While Gu Dong was just one person.

Luckily, after fighters reached the gold level, their attack range and methods underwent a qualitative change. Adding on the fact that the higher up on the hill he was, the smaller the area he needed to protect, this allowed Gu Dong to barely hold back the tide.

But battle qi was rapidly being depleted, and Gu Doing could only last for a short time.

“As expected, Teacher’s expectations weren’t wrong, that hill is very important to this space.” Bu Quan was in a control room, looking over the data with bright eyes.

“With this data, the Old King’s Crowbar is bound to pry open much more space!”

Racial magic - Hail Explosion.

A snow elemental under Shuang Lian’s control condensed hail within its body and constantly sprayed it out.

Several hundred hailstones shot out, each one able to explode. With booms and cold air, they shattered into ice shards that had terrifying cutting capabilities.

Gu Dong originally wanted to evade them, but a moment later, he saw the other snow elemental charging up the hill in spite of its injuries.

Gu Dong was forced to dispel his intention of evasion. He stretched out his palms and out flowed large amounts of gold battle qi.

The battle qi condensed into a battle skill, forming a wall.

The wall blocked the hail explosions and also barely stopped the charging snow elemental.

Although the snow elemental was hit by Hail Explosion, because it was a snow elemental, it had a high resistance to ice magic, therefore, it wasn’t damaged that much.

The two snow elementals joined forces and madly attacked Gu Dong’s battle qi wall.

Every thump created more cracks in the battle qi wall. Gu Dong repeatedly fell back, wildly pressing his battle qi to mend the wall while rousing his spirit, wanting to use a battle skill to alter this detrimental situation.

But at that moment, an alchemy puppet suddenly burst with a pink halo.

The halo of light passed through the battle qi wall, and when it shined on Gu Dong’s head, it caused his willpower to slacken, and he was unable to focus his mind.

“Not good!” Gu Dong felt things were going south, and he resisted it with all he had. But he was trapped in a cycle of focusing his mind and then losing it. For a moment, he couldn’t use other battle skills.

“This is the Divine Halo Scattering Device; it is said that the magic model it uses comes from a modified version of the God of Love’s divine spell.” Bu Quan looked at the magic image in the control room.

“Who?” Shuang Lian exclaimed, revealing her surprise.

A moment later, the flying puppet suppressing Gu Dong was struck by an artillery shell.

The flying puppet was blown away by the explosion, and the halo strayed off course, allowing Gu Doing to catch his breath.

Gu Dong successfully used a battle skill to smoothly retreat while simultaneously trapping the snow elementals in place temporarily.

“Huff hoo! Reinforcements came so soon?” He panted heavily, looking at where the reinforcements were with anticipation.

“So, it turns out to be them.”

“The one coming to help was not Mei Lin and the others, but rather Bing Xiao and the others.

As Gu Dong and the snow elementals fought, the Bayonet Gang remnants took note of them. Although Bing Xiao was just silver level, as a duelist, he knew how important Tranquil Hill was, therefore, he didn’t hesitate and immediately gathered everyone to rush over to help.

“Hen Fa, well done!” Spring Boxer loudly praised.

Lan Zao was still currently just an iron level, but his limits had reached the silver level.

Bing Xiao, Spring Boxer, Xiong Ju, and the human ringleader were all silver level, but didn’t have long ranged battle skills. This immediately gave Lan Zao a golden opportunity to distinguish himself.

Lan Zao grabbed hold of it, and although he hadn’t damaged the silver level flying puppet, after running crucial interference, it affected the war situation.

“Roar!” Xiong Ju held his broadsword and took the lead, charging into the alchemy puppets.

These alchemy puppets only went up to the silver level, and they were detection and surveillance types. They were not Xiong Ju’s match.

Xiong Ju displayed the bearing of a fierce general as he charged into the puppets from the left, killing and scattering them.

Soon after, the Bayonet Gang remnants also charged into the enemy.

Shuang Lian was not good at puppet combat, and at first, she didn’t command them to retreat, rather she let them pile up as she met the Bayonet Gang remnants with force.

But very quickly, she noticed that her puppets had been severely reduced, while the Bayonet Gang remnants were fierce dragons and tigers.

These gang members had experienced the gang’s collapse, fled from Snow Bird Harbor, survived Flower Skirt Island, and other cruel culls; those that remained were the elite.

These elite in the divine domain had also gone through active training and received continuous divine blessings, causing their strength to swell by both small and large amounts.

Shuang Lian realized her mistake and promptly pulled the puppets out.

The puppets had been reduced by more than half their numbers.

Shuang Lian clenched her teeth and changed her strategy, scattering the puppets.

The Bayonet Gang remnants weren’t able to meddle in a gold level battle, and under Bing Xiao’s command, they targeted the puppets and spread out to chase them down.

“Stabilize the front!” Gu Dong obtaining timely help immensely improved his battle qi depletion.

This was because he no longer needed to divert his attention to deal with the alchemy puppets.

With the Bayonet Gang remnants sharing the burden, he only needed to deal with the snow elementals.

Although the snow elementals were gold level, and that there were two of them, they didn’t hold the advantage against Gu Dong in combat.

In this aspect, Shuang Lian was in a somewhat awkward situation.

She used her spirit to control the snow elementals in battle and could fully display the snow elemental’s true power. This was because she was a magician and was no expert in close combat, she more so used the snow elementals to bomb indiscriminately with their racial spells. The benefits of this was she was able to coordinate both snow elementals in concert.

And if she abandoned direct control and gave the snow elementals freedom to act on their own, these snow elementals would fight alone. They would obstruct each other, and if that happened, although their individual combat strength would be better, their combined power would be worse.

Gu Dong had plenty of combat experience, and as a duelist, he naturally was no ordinary gold level fighter.

If Shuang Lian wasn’t directly controlling them, he would have cleverly exploited the two snow elementals hindering each other and conversely be winning.

Shuang Lian didn’t divert her attention, and maintained control over the snow elementals, yet was unable to break through.

Meanwhile, her alchemy puppets were gradually decimated by the Bayonet Gang remnants. The puppet with the Divine Halo Scattering Device didn’t escape and was dismembered.

“Defeated…damn it.” Shuang Lian was gloomy.

“Those people had a crucial impact on the war situation.” Shuang Lian stared at the Bayonet Gang remnants in the magic images, her eyes containing her hatred.

She didn’t think so many would descend from Tranquil Hill.

Bu Quan saw Lan Zao in the magic images but didn’t recognize him.

For one, Bu Quan was not familiar with the other survivors. Secondly, after Lan Zao left, because his bloodline had been purified, his own form had gone through great changes.

Even now, although Deceptive Disguise had been exhausted, the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell was still maintained, though it wasn’t far from dissipating.

But because his appearance had radically changed, Deceptive Disguise didn’t have to do anything.

“Ordinary attack methods will be suppressed when approaching the hill. I fear the only effective attack will be Ice Breaker.” Shuang Lian wavered.

Ice Breaker’s magic model had been improved by her so her magician tower could use it.

But it was barely able to.

Even if the magician tower was using it, just a single usage depleted 78% of its magic power reservoir, and even the tower spirit would momentarily crash, then sink into hibernation and forced recovery.

Ice Breaker was the Honey Snow Tower’s strongest attack, and also what Shuang Lian was relying on to escape this trap.

“Just using Ice Breaker might not work. I need to pair it up with the Old King’s Crowbar to be able to attack that hill.”

“But if I do that, the consequences will be hard to predict.”

Shuang Lian was unable to decide for the moment.

Firstly, she was unable to assess the actual combat results. She should only calculate that it could harm the hill but was unable to measure to what degree.

Secondly, when she used this method, it was bound to trigger the enemy’s guard and even restraint. In the future, if the enemy targeted her, and she was unable to, then how could she free herself from this divine domain space?

It could be said that Ice Breaker was the source of both Shuang Lian’s confidence and sense of security.

Swish.

Space fluctuated, and Mei Lin’s figure appeared on Tranquil Hill.

“Finally, someone came!” Gu Dong sensed Mei Lin’s familiar aura and immediately cheered.

This scene was also detected by Shuang Lian, spurring her into a decision.

“If this battle cannot be won, then this might be my only chance. After this, the enemy will stringently guard that hill!”

It was time to use Ice Breaker!

A moment later, Honey Snow Tower shook as massive amounts of magic power surged and collected into a magic model.

Boom!!!

A snow white beam of light suddenly shot out towards Tranquil Hill.

Halfway there, the beam of light grew smaller and thinner, suppressed by the divine domain.

King’s Crowbar!

Shuang Lian immediately used this legend level artifact. For a split second, Honey Snow Tower’s connection to the demi-plane vanished, and Ice Breaker’s light beam quickly restored to its original state.

Tranquil Hill’s power was shifted by the crowbar and was unable to achieve perfect suppression.

“Not good!” Mei Lin promptly tore a scroll, but the defensive magic only lasted a second before it broke.

Gu Dong was tangled up with the snow elementals.

The light beam ruthlessly bombarded Tranquil Hill, and light white as snow rapidly permeated everything.

The Bayonet Gang remnants fled from the disaster unfolding on the hill in disarray.

The snowy light spread too quickly, and unable to outrun it, Lan Zao was submerged by it.

“I’m…going to die.” In a split second, Lan Zao froze into an ice cube, and his thoughts slowed.

“Don’t worry, you’re not dying.”

The fishman youngster’s voice suddenly transmitted in his mind.

Chapter 831: Young Man – Lan Zao is Also in the Divine Domain?

White Hair Trench.

Inside the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

The fishman youngster was in the process of using the divine spells [Believer Perception] and [Divine Power Channel].

He was currently connecting with Lan Zao!

The fishman youngster finding Lan Zao was no accident, it was his intention.

Ever since he obtained high level divine spells and began to try them out, the experience of saving the big guy had given him a far greater understanding and mastery of these divine spells

After the fishman youngster meticulously found a place for the big guy, he had the energy and time to search for Charming Blue believers that might exist near Ice Sculpture Island.

The fishman youngster understood that where believers were placed in [Believer Perception] corresponded to where they were in reality.

Through War Merchant’s navigational data, with the position of the Magnet Archipelago and White Hair Trench, he could easily determine where Ice Sculpture Island was.

Although last time he surveyed it, he didn’t find any Charming Blue believers on or near Ice Sculpture Island.

But this did not hinder his search.

The reason was simple, believers were intelligent lifeforms and could move around.

Therefore, the fishman youngster planned to use the divine spell to search around every so often during his cultivation in hopes of finding a Charming Blue believer who happened to move near Ice Sculpture Island.

“Found one!” The moment the fishman youngster discovered Lan Zao, he was delighted.

Then he got a inordinate feeling from Lan Zao.

It was a bit like the big guy, but it was also different.

This unordinary feeling was something the fishman youngster greatly valued and he immediately focused his attention on it.

“It’s actually Lan Zao!!” The fishman youngster instantly recognized him, despite the latter’s appearance, physique, and other physical features being different due to his bloodline purification.

The divine spell [Believer Perception] was a faith connection and a soul link, while appearance and so forth were superficial things.

The moment he found Lan Zao, the fishman youngster was very excited.

To save Lan Zao, he had sailed out alone with the Deep Sea Monster Fish. He didn’t expect the series of events that followed that caused him to be trapped in the Ocean Mother Nest.

Rescuing Lan Zao had been shelved since.

Then, just now, the fishman youngster “accidentally” found Lan Zao.

Then he also found out why the feeling Lan Zao gave him was relatively special and similar to the one the big guy gave off.

“Lan Zao believes in the Fishnet Goddess, in other words, he believes in Charming Blue Goddess.”

“At the same time, he is actually also my believer too?”

Lan Zao was a pan-believer who believed in many gods.

“He believes in the Fishnet Goddess because he was born into and grew up in a coastal fishing village. In this kind of environment, having plenty of religions is not strange.”

“Why does he bear faith in me? Perhaps because I forgave him on Mystifying Monster Island and also saved him many times from calamities?”

“Therefore, he followed me not just to repay his debt of gratitude, but also due to an element of faith?”

The fishman youngster noticed that Lan Zao’s faith in the Fishnet Goddess was merely as a pan-believer, yet Lan Zao’s faith in him reached the pious level.

“Hold on, Lan Zao’s location…it indicates he’s returned to Ice Sculpture Island? Where is he right now?” The fishman youngster focused his attention and discovered that Lan Zao was engaged in battle, and also found the surroundings were quite extraordinary and intriguing.

The fishman didn’t know Lan Zao was on “Tranquil Hill”.

At that moment, Honey Snow Tower fired Ice Breaker.

The gigantic beam of light bombed Tranquil Hill with peerless power.

Heaven and earth shook as the snow light spread.

Lan Zao couldn’t evade it and was being drowned by it.

“I’m…going to die.” In a split second, Lan Zao froze into an ice cube, and his thoughts slowed.

“Don’t worry, you’re not dying.” The fishman youngster’s voice suddenly rang in his mind.

Lan Zao was startled, thinking he was hallucinating and hearing voices before death’s door.

But a moment later, tempestuous power sprung from his body and helped withstand the external threat while simultaneously applying Deceptive Disguise, causing Lan Zao’s external appearance to not change whatsoever.

Divine power protected Lan Zao and saved his life.

Lan Zao was wonderfully surprised, and he could only shout in his mind, “Lord Commander!!!”

But in reality, he was frozen and couldn’t produce a sound.

However, the excitement and joy of his soul could still be sensed clearly by the fishman youngster.

In White Hair Trench, the fishman youngster smiled, “Lan Zao, you made it hard for me to find you. I haven’t returned yet, while you actually made your way back to Ice Sculpture Island first.”

Lan Zao was puzzled, “Lord Commander, I am currently in the Dueling Divine Domain.”

The fishman youngster: “?”

Lan Zao wanted to keep explaining, but the fishman youngster laughed before that, “No need to explain, I’ll take a look personally.”

At once, he focused his attention and began to thumb through Lan Zao’s memories.

Thus, the fishman youngster saw the conversation between Spring Boxer and Lan Zao, Spring Boxer called him “Hen Fa”, and he told “Hen Fa” that he had to believe in the Dueling God, otherwise his life would be in danger.
