Then, the fishman youngster saw Lan Zao on Tranquil Hill, training his battle skills diligently in the Dueling Divine Domain, and absorbing the battle experience of dead experts. In addition, he also saw Xiong Ju and the others sparring with each other.

Then, the fishman youngster saw something important. Lan Zao and the others were bundled up and carried into the Dueling Divine Domain, and on the top of Tranquil Hill, they saw tombstones!

The fishman youngster saw Lan Zao play the role of “Hen Fa”, receive spy training on Flower Skirt Island, and participated in a wild duel.

Finally, the fishman youngster saw Lan Zao participate in the Snow Bird Harbor battle, when he “saved” Bing Xiao and withdrew on Feng Lian’s pirate ship.

The fishman youngster: …

He didn’t expect Lan Zao to have had such a marvelous and suspenseful experience during this time.

“So, this place is the Dueling Divine Domain.”

“This is a living divine domain and is on Ice Sculpture Island. Therefore, [Believer Perception] detected Lan Zao’s location on Ice Sculpture Island.”

“Lan Zao had entered the divine domain at an earlier time, but my previous searches didn’t find him. This might be due to the divine domain being destabilized by a mysterious enemy attack, causing a spatial crack and allowing me to finally find Lan Zao!”

“Dueling God…I have never heard of such a god.”

“Who is the one fighting in the Dueling Divine Domain and has that magician tower?”

The fishman youngster didn’t notice that the person who was attacking was the outsider he expected to save the big guy – Shuang Lian.

Even though he had found a minute magician tower in the distance pictured in Lan Zao’s memory.

Shuang Lian could see Tranquil Hill from the top of her magician tower.

On Tranquil Hill, Lan Zao could also see Honey Snow Tower, but he didn’t know what it was because it was too far away.

In spite of Lan Zao knowing information about Bu Quan.

This was also why before Lan Zao went to battle, he did it without the slightest hesitation and used Air Cannonball to knock the alchemy puppet askew to get Gu Dong out of his predicament.

Since Lan Zao didn’t know intelligence on the enemy, neither did the fishman youngster know it was Shuang Lian.

None of that would hinder his following actions.

“Lan Zao is now in a divine domain, rashly helping him will have immense hidden dangers.”

“I must make up for this without delay!”

Chapter 832: Dueling God’s Favor

As a divine emissary, he could look through a believer’s memories. It really made things easy!

Not needing Lan Zao to waste time verbally explaining things, the fishman youngster learned about everything in a matter of seconds.

He instantly made a judgement: Lan Zao was in danger!

The danger was not just the powerful enemy attack, but also his “teammates”.

“How to save Lan Zao?”

The fishman youngster first thought of getting Lan Zao to leave directly.

It meant using the Divine Domain Descent spell.

This was a feasible theory. Because Lan Zao not only a pan-believer in Charming Blue Goddess (Fishnet Goddess) but was also a pious believer in the fishman youngster.

The fishman youngster could use divine spells like Divine Power Channel, have him pray, then direct divine favor onto him, thereby allowing him to use Divine Domain Descent on them.

Through this portal divine spell, Lan Zao could return to the Charming Blue Divine Domain.

Lan Zao had experienced this method once before – when he left Flower Skirt Island, it was through Divine Domain Descent, and it instantly brought him to the Dueling Divine Domain.

“But now, it is not the opportune time to use Divine Domain Descent.” The fishman youngster understood.

On the one hand, using this divine spell on Lan Zao would expend a large amount of the fishman youngster’s divine power and divine favor.

The former was not a problem.

After the big guy incident, the fishman youngster’s divine power had already been replenished.

But too much divine favor would be consumed, currently it had only recovered to 6,000 as of now. The fishman youngster was uncertain if it was enough for a Divine Domain Descent.

On the other hand, there was many hidden dangers in choosing this.

One was that Lan Zao entering Charming Blue Divine Domain did not mean he could return to the fishman youngster’s side. It was possible he would return to the main part of Charming Blue Divine Domain.

It was where Charming Blue Goddess’ main body should be hibernating.

If Lan Zao was hastily teleported there, would it awaken Charming Blue Goddess?

“If Charming Blue Goddess wakes up, they will surely notice us. I fear that will be worse than becoming imperial fugitives!”

The other hidden danger was the Dueling God.

If Divine Domain Descent activated on Lan Zao while he was in the Dueling Divine Domain, placing te Charming Blue Divine Domain in the Dueling Divine Domain, how would the Dueling God respond?

Fly into a rage? Then drop divine punishment?

That was possible, what was more likely was that Charming Blue Goddess would wake up!

“It would be great if I had experience in using Divine Domain Descent.”

The fishman youngster wasn’t confident.

Because he had never tried using it.

Based on his plan, next prayer he would ask for these experiences. That would be the safest way.

“It would be better if I didn’t rush to use Divine Domain Descent.”

“Furthermore, if I save Lan Zao now, it will rather undercut the perilous journey he has taken.”

The fishman youngster had a good understanding of what happened to him after looking through his memories.

“I have never heard of a Dueling God. I have looked up information on a dozen churches in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, and I have never seen such a god or clergy.”

“This has been developed secretly.”

“By means of Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s blessed dueling atmosphere, the Dueling God Church has grown well.”

“At the peak of Tranquil Hill there are tombstones with the names of many people. Mei Lin, Teng Dong Lang…these are all gold levels!”

“Wait a moment, if Teng Dong Lang is a duelist, and Spring Boxer is his coffin bearer, then the people who attacked the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps at Snow Bird Harbor was the Dueling Church and the other duelists?”

The fishman youngster didn’t know that Zong Ge had assassinated the gold levels Ax Gang Leader, Jia Bing, and Lin enroute to the capital.

If he knew this information, he would be more inclined towards this hypothesis.

The fishman youngster’s intuition told him that Lan Zao would gain more if he remained here.

“Deceptive Disguise is very reliable and can effectively help Lan Zao.”

The fishman youngster wouldn’t have Lan Zao blindly take risks; he wanted certainty.

“Besides, with me, it won’t just be a simple Deceptive Disguise spell.”

“I can even help Lan Zao gain the Dueling God’s favor!”

The fishman youngster had practiced blasphemous sacrifice a lot.

After thinking about it, the fishman youngster began to act.

First was the classic spell combination – Deceptive Disguise and the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, followed by Divine Power Channel, empowering Lan Zao.

Then it was the divine spell [Believer Conversion].

With this gateway divine spell, Lan Zao’s belief in other gods would be converted into belief in the Dueling God.

Of course, this was merely a disguise.

The power of his conviction would still go to those other gods.

In short, this series of divine spells were conveyed to him one after the other.

Lan Zao quickly became a “pious level” Dueling God believer.

Spring Boxer did say that and even used the threat of death on Lan Zao. Therefore, the fishman youngster understood that being at least a pious level believer met standards.

“Lord Commander, what is this?” Lan Zao was quite amazed.

He knew about Deceptive Disguise, after all he had been boosted by it before.

But he didn’t know the other divine spells that came afterwards, and it dazzled his eyes.

“Now is not the time to explain, what is coming next is important.” The fishman youngster replied, “Do what I say!”

“Understood, Lord Commander.” Lan Zao’s loyalty to the young man was clearly visible, he had not a shred of hesitation.

Thus, a moment later, Lan Zao prayed in his mind.

“Grand God, supreme Dueling God.”

“You are an endless target of reverence; You are the heavenly law I pursue throughout my life.”

“Now, I am trapped in ice, unable to free myself.”

“This endless cold shackles me, bringing me despair and misery.”

“Only Your divine finger can save me from this crisis.”

“Ah, Dueling God, please listen to my prayer.”

“Your glory and mercy brings me incomparable marvel.”

“I wish for Your almighty favor to descend upon me, to save us pious believers.”

“We forever venerate Your supreme authority and offer our humble spirits and devotion in prayer.”

“In Your care and benevolence, we seek salvation. Only through You, our eternal and only Lord God.”

After a few seconds, a growing beam of light fell from the sky and covered the frozen Lan Zao.

Ice Breaker was still causing havoc with its snow light, but the beam of divine power simply penetrated the snow light and instantly dispelled the ice around Lan Zao.

Soon after, that light spread and turned into a vast curtain of light that spread to other people.

This included Bing Xiao, Xiong Ju, and the others, they had met the same end as Lan Zao, but at this very moment, they had been saved!

“This is my God’s divine blessing!” Gu Dong took in this scene as he fled on the precipice of the snow light.

Not just him, but also Mei Lin flying in the air. The navy rear admiral stared in Lan Zao’s direction, “The light spread from that fishman’s body, and formed a divine power light curtain. Who is he?”

In the Deep Sea Monster Fish, the fishman youngster coughed and relinquished his worries.

“Excellent, my luck’s pretty good.”

“After that prayer, Lan Zao has obtained the Dueling God’s divine blessing.”

The fishman youngster’s conjecture wasn’t wrong. If Lan Zao was in the outside world, the possibility would be unlikely. But he was in a divine domain, and like the fishman youngster praying, he could receive an immediate response from a god.

Not just that, the fishman youngster had also covertly given Lan Zao many divine spells. These divine spells played an extremely important function during blasphemous prayers!

If that prayer didn’t call forth the Dueling God’s divine blessing, the fishman youngster planned to take a risk and use Charming Blue divine power for deception.

It also had to be disguised as Lan Zao’s own strength.

Otherwise, disguising it as the Dueling God’s own divine power would be an enormous provocation that would bring the Dueling God’s ire to its peak!

“If that happens, then there would be two gold level witnesses.”

“The Bayonet Gang remnants will find out after this that they were saved because of Lan Zao. Lan Zao’s pious level belief will help him establish himself and his dangerous situation will be greatly alleviated.”

Honey Snow Tower.

“That is…divine blessing!” Shuang Lian looked as gloomy as water.

Because the divine power light curtain not only aided the Bayonet Gang but also mended Tranquil Hill.

Tranquil Hill had been split apart massively by Ice Breaker, but as of this moment, the light curtain was quickly mending that mountainous crack.

At the same time, the divine power light curtain was also suppressing Ice Breaker’s beam, reducing it once again.

“Stop Ice Breaker!” Shuang Lain decisively yelled when she saw this.

The cast only lasted 8 seconds, yet over 75% of Honey Snow Tower’s magic power reservoir had been expended.

The tower spirit in charge of maintaining the magic model had already crashed and did not respond.

Seeing Honey Snow Tower’s declining aura, Mei Lin and Gu Dong immediately grabbed ahold of this opportunity, quickly leaving Tranquil Hill and charging in.

Shuang Lian was crystal clear about the consequences of discharging Ice Breaker.

She remained calm in the face of death, and she replaced the tower spirit to control the magician tower in combat.

Bu Quan was energetically collating the data and studying it.

The King’s Crowbar once again changed directions, no longer affecting the Dueling Divine Domain, but rather the magician tower.

Mei Lin’s and Gu Dong’s many attacks were shifted away, then were counterattacked.

This made the two gold levels quite frustrated.

The legend level artifact gave Shuang Lian a nearly invincible position.

Magic scroll – Spatial Anchor.

Mei Lin couldn’t use Spatial Anchor, but she had a spell scroll.

Spatial Anchor was not used on Honey Snow Tower, but rather the spatial crack.

Ice Breaker’s power was very formidable, and although Tranquil Hill was mended by the Dueling God’s light, halfway between it and Honey Snow Tower, there was still a massive spatial crack.

Mei Lin couldn’t repair this point in space and found that she was unable to attack Honey Snow Tower at the moment, so she changed her target. First she would use Spatial Anchor to solidify space to prevent other outsiders from getting into the divine domain.

At the same time, it was also to prevent Shuang Lian from transmitting a distress call through the spatial crack.

“Humph!” Shuang Lian didn’t stop it.

On the one hand, she didn’t have surplus military strength. On the other hand, she had more hope in the future.

“Although Ice Breaker was exposed and didn’t achieve anything, I still gathered a tremendous amount of data.”

“With this data and with the help of the Old King’s Crowbar, I am sure to shift even more of the divine domain’s space!”

“The future is mine!”

Chapter 833: Lan Zao Obtains Armor

Dueling Divine Domain.

The battle that occurred from Shuang Lian’s sneak attack had come to an end.

Shung Lian’s troops had lost on the battlefield, and the duelists had defended Tranquil Hill successfully.

The most important person in this battle was none other than Gu Dong.

When he detected the gold level snow elementals raiding Tranquil Hill while on duty, he immediately reacted to stop them. Furthermore, during the battle, despite the many difficulties, he did not cower and really planned to defend the place to the death!

The Bayonet Gang remnants played a crucial role, and among them, the iron level Lan Zao was especially impressive.

After cleaning up the battlefield, Mei Lin received the information, and even she had to admit that Lan Zao’s Air Cannon had a very subtle effect, and he indirectly saved Gu Dong.

Gu Dong then was being impaired by an alchemy puppet, and was in quite the passive situation, but thanks to Lan Zao’s assistance, he was able to recover for a breath.

Long experiences on the battlefield gave Mei Lin a clear understanding that on the battlefield, being able to “recover for a breath” was quite pivotal.

“I didn’t expect the Bayonet Gang remnants to actually have such an effect.” Mei Lin would never have thought of this happening.

She allowed Bing Xiao and the others to stay on Tranquil Hill to rest and recuperate, mainly to prevent undiscovered traitors from revealing information, and didn’t expect such a gain during this battle.

“From another point of view, Shuang Lian is not a battle-oriented magician. She didn’t grab hold of crucial battle opportunities and her troop’s formation, and composition had serious problems.”

“Let’s not speak more on it, if there was just one more Divine Halo Scattering Device, the war situation might be far different. Shuang Lian might have been able to assault the summit of Tranquil Hill.”

“Even so, Shuang Lian cannot be looked down upon, to think she had such a spell!!”

Mei Lin ran her eyes across the battlefield, and saw the gigantic rift valley of ice, her expression heavy and gloomy.

This new ice rift valley was created by Ice Breaker.

Mei Lin did not know it was the Ice Breaker spell, nor did she know it came from the Great Pyrolysis spell, but she saw how powerful this class of spell was.

“Under normal circumstances, this magic cannot damage the divine domain.”

“However, the Dueling Divine Domain has no master, and Shuang Lian unfortunately has the legend level artifact, the Old King’s Crowbar too.

After shifting the divine domain, Ice Breaker still retained its considerable might.

“There is a spatial crack in the Dueling Divine Domain, and although the divine domain is automatically repairing itself, it’s at a slow rate. Honey Snow Tower might not be able to get out, but it’ll be easier to send a distress call.”

“The top priority is mending this spatial crack, thus ensuring the Dueling Divine Domain is kept hidden!”

Mei Lin was unable to fix this problem herself.

“It seems I have to ask the royal family to use the Iceberg Crystal Ball.”

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom was a legend level maritime power, and despite the current king not being a legend level, the royal family had a legend level foundation.

The Iceberg Crystal Ball was one of those things.

Like the Old King’s Crowbar, it was also a legend level artifact!

“Apart from that, in order to prevent Shuang Lian from attacking again, we need to garrison a second gold level here.”

According to the information, Mei Lin knew that Shuang Lian’s demi-plane only had two gold level snow elementals. In this battle, Shuang Lian suffered disastrous damage, and on paper, she wouldn’t be able to launch an offensive of similar strength again.

But it is better to be safe than sorry, and magicians really had a lot of skills.

Especially since Shuang Lian also had a magician tower!

The magician tower could attack and defend, it was also a formidable base of manufacturing, and perhaps Shuang Lian could make gold level puppets in her magician tower?

Men Lin fully anticipated this happening.

Therefore, she chose to increase her gold level transcendent power.

“The National Ceremony is near at hand, and getting gold level military strength isn’t easy.” Mei Lin sighed to herself.

Although there were many gold level duelists, they were rather strapped in actual usage.

Mei Lin was busy with the navy, and she needed to protect and patrol. Da Han and other pirates were flocking over, putting Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s strong frontier defenses under pressure.

Gu Dong was worthy of his army background, he was reliable, and Mei Lin felt relieved that she appointed him. This battle also saw how reliable Gu Dong was. Most crucially, he was already retired. Without a doubt, following this, he would be the main person on duty, and even be a permanent resident here.

Bao Li Gen was an Ice Sculpture Army soldier, and not only would he be participating in the National Ceremony Grand Duel, but he also held an important seat in the army exercise.

The first seat, Long Meng, was not from a military background and Mei Lin did not have jurisdiction over him.

Zhu Gan was a foreign elf.

Mo Gui Ji was mainly focused on the National Ceremony grand duel; he really wanted to win it!

Xue Tuan Zi wanted to participate in the Warm Snow Cup; she had been preparing for it for a long time.

Qing Hong Yan was a rabbitman and had a worrying condition. If he went mad, the duelists had to deal with him.

Yun Zhong was the newest member of the duelists. He had an undisciplined temperament and valued freedom. Despite the Li Jian Clan offering him a high price, he didn’t join them. Mei Lin also tried to invite him at a high price, but he rarely showed interest in acting.

Next was He Gai Tou, and putting it nicely, he was shrewd, while putting it bluntly, he was treacherous.

Finally, there was Yi Jiu.

He was an assassin from the Thieves Guild, and since joining, and because of last time, he had a grudge against Mei Lin. If Mei Lin wanted to use him again, she would have to pay at least triple this time!

Due to this, Mei Lin barely had any duelists she could use.

“Damn you, Long Fu!”

“Teng Dong Lang, Jia Bing, Lin, and also the Ax Gang Leader were all slain by your hand.”

“Those four had a military background, and were as reliable and stable as Gu Dong, but they were lost one after the other.”

“If they hadn’t died in battle, today’s situation would not be so passive!”

The duelists had complicated origins, and the largest faction among them was the military faction, headed by Mei Lin. But Long Fu (Zong Ge) inflicted unprecedented losses on the military faction, and it caused Mei Lin a heartache ever since.

In the end, gold levels were the core of transcendent forces, and only that core could stabilize things.

Mei Lin didn’t know Zong Ge was impersonating the dragonman youngster, and to her, it seemed Long Fu had slain no less than four duelists, all from the military faction!

Mei Lin’s hatred towards the dragonman youngster was the kind that would not be resolved until one party was dead.

Not long after, the battlefield cleanup finished, and it was confirmed there were no alchemy puppets on Tranquil Hill.

Mei Lin summoned the Bayonet Gang remnants, evaluated them, and bestowed rewards upon their merits.

“In this battle you have contributed greatly. Whatever you need, say it and I will meet it as much as possible.” Mei Lin smiled.

She looked at Bing Xiao, “You are their leader, speak first.”

Bing Xiao exposed a rarely seen expression, apprehension and expectation, “Based on this merit, can I leave behind my inheritance on Tranquil Hill’s summit?”

Mei Lin shook her head, “I don’t have the qualifications to give you such an arrangement. You are all believers of the Dueling God. I believe you all received God’s divine blessing during this battle already, isn’t that so?”

The Bayonet Gang remnants, including the human ringleader, had received divine favor.

Of course, everyone received a different amount of divine favor.

Lan Zao said, “The greatest divine blessing bestowed is the one that protected our lives!”

Those words caused everyone to look at him.

“Hen Fa speak true.” Xiong Ju loudly approved, “That we are still alive is the greatest gain! Ah ha ha.”

Spring Boxer clapped Hen Fa’s shoulder, “Thanks to you, Hen Fa.”

Lan Zao promptly responded, “I only fought with all of my strength, and when death approached, I prayed in desperation. This is all the Dueling God’s benevolence!”

Those words caused everyone to nod repeatedly.

Lan Zao seized the opportunity to inquire, “Lord Mei Lin, I also want to become our Lord’s holy warrior. After enduring this battle, I have become a pious believer!”

Mei Lin shook her head, “I will say whether or not you become a holy warrior is not for me to decide.”

“But I can give you all a good suggestion, that is to participate in many duels.”

“Our Lord is the Dueling God, what acts do you think He will favor?”

The human ringleader’s eyes shined, feigning excitement while in actuality he was declaring what he had ruminated on for a long time, “The National Ceremony Grand Duel! The kingdom wants to hold the most majestic duel during this celebration.”

Mei Lin shook her head, “In the short term, you won’t be able to leave.”

“The following arrangement needs you all to be garrisoned here at the foot of Tranquil Hill for a long time.”

“I will supply your daily needs and cultivation resources to your satisfaction.”

The human ringleader’s heart sank, unavoidably feeling disappointed.

He had gathered enough information but was unable to get out of here.

Bing Xiao understood Mei Lin’s purpose in this, he nodded, then raised an additional request, “Can you provide me with a gold level giant attendant?”

Bing Xiao wanted to cultivate another gold level giant slave, and he had the means to do so.”

But the Ice Sculpture Kingdom had the slavery ban, therefore, Bing Xiao said “attendant”.

“Based on your contribution this time…I can.” Mei Lin consented.

Of course, she knew that attendant meant slave.

Gold level giant slaves were actually very pricey, but Mei Lin considered future battles and found that as of now, Tranquil Hill really needed more defenses.

Next Mei Lin asked Spring Boxer.

She knew Spring Boxer was Teng Dong Lang’s coffin bearer and successor, therefore, his status in her eyes was rather high.

Thirdly, Mei Lin asked Lan Zao.

At the fishman youngster’s behest, Lan Zao stated, “I hope I can get gold level full body armor.”

Mei Lin hesitated, but despite the kingdom’s armor prohibition, she still agreed to this request.

After dealing with things appropriately, Mei Lin immediately departed Tranquil Hill. She had already been missing for some time, and the navy still needed her to show her face.

The fishman youngster who had been watching the entire time also withdrew his focus.

“If this happens, Lan Zao will be temporarily safe there.”

“Not only that, but he will also have gold level full body armor. With that armor, his defense will surpass both mine and Zong Ge’s.”

Lan Zao was only an iron level right now, and his bloodline limit was the silver level. Even if he was a silver level, having gold level armor would be rather luxurious.

Due to the armor prohibition, Xiong Ju only had the Gold Flower Hacksaw, and no armor, let alone full body armor.

Lan Zao being able to get it was extremely lucky. For example, if it wasn’t Mei Lin who came, and another, it might not have happened.

“Mei Lin.”

“If I remember correctly, isn’t she the rear admiral of the first fleet? Although she is a mermaid and not a snow elf, she is one of the representatives of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s military.”

The fishman youngster sank into pondering and recollection.

After the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps came to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, they had purchased a lot of information.

This information included all sorts of aspects of the kingdom. One of the main focuses of it was on military affairs. Mei Lin was a famous figure, so of course, the survivors learned about her.

“I didn’t expect this military figure to secretly be one of the Dueling God’s holy warriors.”

“In what areas can I exploit this secret?”

“Were they the ones who raided the mercenary corps branch in Snow Bird Harbor?”

Chapter 834: The Young Man’s Advancement

White Hair Trench.

Before the gold altar.

The Charming Blue Archbishop, octopus-man Zhou Zhang was leading numerous believers in a new round of teleportation blood sacrifice.

She prayed loudly:

“Grand Charming Blue Goddess, our God, our mother.”

“In this deep sea valley’s sacred ceremony, we have gathered before Your altar and offer You blood and souls in praise.”

“Charming Blue Goddess, You are the God of a Thousand Faces, the Monarch of Deception.”

“Your power rushes forth like the torrential ocean, Your wisdom cares for disturbed hearts.”

“Please accept our blood and awaken the sacred instincts fast asleep in our hearts. Let our eyes be filled with blurred radiance and win the blessing of destiny with deceit and intelligence.”

“We bend our knees in propriety, forever venerating and praising You, forever serving Your will.”

…

Zhou Zhang’s prayer finished, and the teleportation blood sacrifice officially began.

The believers were fanatical and hopeful.

There were a large number of observers nearby, Jian Gui was among them.

He had a grave expression, and his attention was focused on Qi Ling, and sometimes would deviate to Cang Xu.

This time, the teleportation blood sacrifice had an obvious difference.

It was the offering, apart from the large quantity of ocean beasts, there was also many undead lifeforms mixed into it.

These undead lifeforms had been tested by Cang Xu’s undead bloodline detection magic and selected very carefully; they all had his Hanging Corpse Tree bloodline.

The great formation activated, and the light of divine script and magic runes interweaved together.

Space fluctuated, and the believers and offerings were teleported away.

“Coming in!” Cang Xu was fully focused and was the first to react after teleportation.

The fishman youngster, who had been waiting for a long time, had already begun to move.

Under the effect of Deceptive Disguise and other divine spells, they were more efficient and stable than last time.

The saint beast Snow Pear Octopus was once again duped by them.

The undead lifeforms were offerings and were unable to move, so naturally, the fishman youngster effortlessly absorbed them with the Bloodcore.

Qi Ling was taken captive and imprisoned in the long-horn cage.

Apart from that, there was also the silver level pirate called Ruan Tang Ren.

“I found Lan Zao. You won’t guess where he is now!” The fishman youngster informed Cang Xu of how he helped Lan Zao not so long ago.

Several days ago, Cang Xu knew that the fishman youngster had been promoted to be a divine emissary and came to the big guy’s aid.

Hearing about Lan Zao now, he was still amazed.

“Dueling God?”

“The Ice Sculpture Kingdom doesn’t have that kind of god.”

“That indicates that the Dueling God is not an Ice Sculpture Kingdom god, and their church is not allowed to preach yet.”

“However, according to the gods and church development rules, they are certain to move from behind the scenes to center stage. But currently, they are comparatively puny and are in the clandestine development stage.”

The fishman youngster replied, “They are not puny, Tranquil Hill’s gravestones had many names I have seen in information reports.”

“They were all gold level.”

“Thus, I surmised that the other names I didn’t recognize were also gold level too.”

“Those that had their names on Tranquil Hill’s tombstones were probably the Dueling God’s holy warriors. As of now, I can confirm Teng Dong Lang, Gu Dong, and Mei Lin. There are all gold level, with no exceptions.”

Cang Xu nodded, and his brows slightly creased, “This really isn’t in accordance with common sense.”

“Normal church development goes from small to large, from weak to strong.”

“But the Dueling Church doesn’t have basic personnel, it just gold levels, and a lot of them.”

“However, this should lead to a special environment being created.”

“Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s general mood is something you should have the deepest perception of Lord Commander. Duels are prevalent there, and the entire nation participates in duels, watches duels with great enthusiasm, many people want to comply with dueling rules, rely on duels to solve daily issues, important conflicts and other contradictions.”

From this, one can see there is a robust foundation for Dueling believers among them.”

“The Dueling Church just hasn’t moved into the open yet and obtained permission to preach. When they get and begin to evangelize, I believe they will swell massively within a short time period at all levels of society. It is completely possible they will grow instantly into a large church.”

The fishman youngster approved of Cang Xu’ analysis.

He continued, “I thought about it later.”

“The reason why the Dueling God has been secretly developed is because the Ice Sculpture Kingdom does not have a war god.”

“If a war god existed, they would have gained dueling clergy already, instead of using dueling belief to create a new god.”

The fishman youngster had a better understanding of churches than Cang Xu did.

He knew how many gods were born from abundant and substantial foundations of faith.

Cang Xu replied, “Lan Zao got your help, and can now gain a foothold among the Bayonet Gang remnants, along with their trust.”

“I didn’t expect his circumstances to end up like this.”

“Lord Commander, your promotion to divine emissary was really wonderful. Your decisions and actions were very rational.”

The fishman Dao Hen was still on Tan Mo’s Treasure Chest, giving the survivors first hand intelligence on the enemy.

Lan Zao found a place on Tranquil Hill and would continue to spy on the divine domain, along with Mei Lin and the other holy warriors.

Finally, there was the big guy.

Because the Snow Bird Harbor branch was attacked, it wasn’t suitable for him to return to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, so he might as well stay on Magnet Archipelago and cultivate.

The fishman youngster manipulated the gold level giant magic chef into becoming the big guy’s slave. He believed the former was capable of helping the big guy grow.

The two walked into the hold while talking.

Under the fishman youngster’s gaze, Cang Xu entered the Bloodline Purification Formation again.

The fishman youngster urged the Bloodcore’s magical energy and activated the formation.

A short time later, an emotional Cang Xu left the formation, “My Hanged Corpse Tree bloodline concentration has reached 7%!”

The fishman youngster with a 90% Surging Wave fishman bloodline concentration slightly frowned.

From Cang Xu’s body, he could see how hard it was to purify his bloodline.

Cang Xu worked himself half to death and used the Undead Bloodline Detection spell countless times to pick out these undead lifeforms.

With the help of Zhou Zhang’s platform, he had sought out personal gain.

After all this trouble, his saint level bloodline concentration only reached 7%.

The fishman youngster looked at Cang Xu and contemplated, “Purifying the Hanged Corpse Tree bloodline is far harder than normal bloodlines.”

“This time, you leveraged Zhou Zhang’s power, along with consuming Jian Gui’s, Qi Ling’s, and Xiao Wang Zi’s accumulations.”

“In the Main Plane, it is very difficult for us to find a lot of undead lifeforms.”

Cang Xu sighed, “I have considered this already.”

“The place with the most undead lifeforms is naturally the Ghost World. But we aren’t strong enough to explore it yet.”

Cang Xu had gone to the Ghost World before, and there were precarious gold levels everywhere, with each step dangerous. Only a saint level had the qualifications to explore it.

“So, saving and contacting the orthodox undead is an important trading channel.”

As the head of the undead orthodoxy, You Shen had the most necromancy resources on the Main Plane. The twelve saints were almost all saint level, who were exploring the Ghost World all year round and raking in resources from it.

“If we can trade, we can pick more suitable undead lifeforms. The closer they are to the Hanging Corpse Tree on the bloodline archive, the better.”

“From this point of consideration, I believe it is still better to let Jian Gui go free.”

Magician Qi Ling was already their prisoner.

Jian Gui spent energy maintaining their small group, and now he was alone, a leader without a team.

Based on the fishman youngster’s and Cang Xu’s current power and arrangements, they had a large chance of taking Jian Gui captive!

Capturing a gold level magician alive, a necromancer at that, was very difficult. But currently, the fishman youngster had become a gold level cleric and was a divine emissary who knew several hundred divine spells, with only a small portion of high end divine spells he was inexperienced in and hadn’t mastered yet.

They could take him captive, but Cang Xu suggested that they didn’t.

Instead of taking Jian Gui captive, they were better off letting him go. If that happened, Cang Xu could continue to draw support from Jian Gui and contact other undead orthodoxy. In accordance with his current identity as a Wisteria Merchant Alliance elder, it was entirely possible for him to reestablish this trading channel.

Of course, the two also thought about taking Jian Gui captive first, tampering with him, then letting him go.

But after careful consideration, it was more reliable for them to just let him free.

Although the two had confidence in Deceptive Disguise, they had to consider You Shen, the legendary magician whose name shook the entire world and the primogenitor of the undead faction. So, it was still better and more reliable to let him go with no funny business involved.

Deceptive Disguise had been developed by War Merchant, it was an improved version of Charming Blue’ divine spell and drew from Charming Blue’s divine artifact, the Mermaid Fairy Tale.

War Merchant was a legend level magician, but his influence and accomplishments, among other aspects, could not compare to You Shen’s.

The fishman youngster replied that if nothing unexpected happened, they would go with Cang Xu’s suggestion. When the time came, if Jian Gui couldn’t do it alone, then the fishman youngster would surreptitiously help him without the former finding out.

Taking Jian Gui captive meant that at most, the survivors had another captive. It would also deeply offend the undead orthodoxy.

But letting him go would on the contrary borrow his authority to communicate with the undead orthodoxy and the benefits that followed were obviously greater.

“Next, we have a new round of blasphemous prayer testing.”

The fishman youngster entered a specialized prayer cabin.

He loudly prayed, speaking forthright about his request this time. He asked for usage experience on high-end divine spells, especially Divine Domain Descent.

The fishman youngster wanted to quietly escape Ocean Mother Nest and perhaps he could rely on this.

Boom!

Like last time, before the fishman youngster finished his meticulously prepared prayer, a beam of divine prayer fell onto him.

After the beam of light dissipated, the fishman youngster slowly got up from his kneeling position.

“Gold level fighter…”

“Furthermore, my Surging Wave fishman bloodline concentration has reached the standard of the god level.”

“Divine favor is once again abundant and has returned to 20,000.”

But the fishman youngster didn’t stand up happily.

Because what he wanted most was not what he got.

Upon leaving the ship hold and going through the identification procedures with Cang Xu, the fishman youngster told Cang Xu about what just happened, and when the latter learned of it, he too became somewhat gloomy.

“That was the exact situation during your last prayer, Lord Commander.”

“Although your prayer had explicit requests and also used divine favor to direct it, the divine blessing given was not what we wanted.”

“Why is it like this? The situation is different from before!”

“Lord Commander, can you say whether Charming Blue Goddess has woken up already?”

Chapter 835: There Are No Differences in Death

“Lord Commander, do you think Charming Blue Goddess has woken up already?”

Cang Xu’s sudden question caused the fishman youngster’s heart to inevitably shake.

He thought about it, then lightly smiled, “How is that possible, our conduct and deeds profane the gods! If Charming Blue Goddess was already awake, they would absolutely fly into a rage and inflict the harshest punishment upon us.

“It isn’t like how it is now, constantly receiving divine blessings.”

Cang Xu sighed, still looking worried, “Lord Commander, my conjecture is not baseless, it is founded on two facts.:

“The first one is me.”

“I am a necromancer, but I became a Charming Blue believer and obtained special divine spells via divine blessing that can let me guide others towards the same beliefs. This divine spell involves the secrets of the soul and not even Zhou Zhang was bestowed with them.”

“This special divine spell was clearly created. How can Charming Blue Goddess create divine spells while they are sleeping?”

“Second, are your last two prayers Lord Commander.”

“We expended divine favor and during prayer, according to common sense, should have pinpointed the divine blessings.”

“But your Lordship’s two divine blessings were not the ones your litany requested.”

“It seems like Charming Blue Goddess doesn’t want to learn how to leave this place, they want you to stay here.”

Those words chilled the fishman youngster’s heart.

He went silent for a moment, then slowly shook his head with a heavy look, “You’re right, both of those are facts.”

“But I am more inclined to believe that due to the Charming Blue Church’s desperate situation, there cannot be construction without destruction, and they created these special divine spells.”

“If Charming Blue Goddess was awake, they would definitely notice us. Our methods can handle other gods, but it cannot deal with them. Under these circumstances, they can do far more than we can and won’t ignore us taking advantage of them.”

“I think that this should be the Godhead handing these affairs, and that Charming Blue Goddess’ main body is sleeping.”

The fishman youngster was a divine emissary and had obtained a lot of pertinent experiences. Adding on War Merchant’s database, and he had a good deal of understanding about the secrets of the gods.

He knew about Godheads.

What were Godheads?

Simple, they were equivalent to an enhanced type of tower spirit.

Shuang Lian’s tower spirit in Honey Snow Tower was created from a severed portion of Shuang Lian’s soul. Tower spirits possessed intelligence and could handle many affairs, they were loyal and devoted to executing all of its master’s commands.

Gods could be fast asleep, but Godheads could still carry out tasks without sleeping or resting. These tasks were personally set up by the gods.

“I am only meeting some criterion Charming Blue Goddess set before they sank into slumber.”

“I used the divine spell [Believer Perception] many times and understand thoroughly how hard pressed the Charming Blue Church is at the moment. They really need a powerful figure.”

“But not everyone can become such a key figure.”

“People like the saint level Zhou Zhang, who has reached her limits. Although she is an Archbishop, she cannot improve the Charming Blue Church’s truly difficult situation.”

“Aptitude and bloodlines are the most crucial thing. Since I have the Surging Wave fishman bloodline as my foundation, that represents that I have a chance of being that person.”

“Therefore, Charming Blue Goddess has energetically invested in me.”

“On the one hand, cases like mine are extremely rare. On the other hand, their situation doesn’t allow them to be patient and do careful selection.”

After pausing, the fishman youngster continued, “Indeed, my last two blessings are inconsistent to what I was praying for and can no longer be directed with divine favor.”

“But I don’t believe this is Charming Blue Goddess waking up.”

“If they have awakened, they would instantly see through our disguises, and would they continue to invest in me then?”

“Compared with this kind of possibility, I am more prone to believe that Charming Blue Goddess’ main body is still sleeping and formulated a specific arrangement before that happened. Through the Godhead, she made sure to cultivate and assist top notch talents.”

“Their original plan should be to cultivate a qualified divine emissary. Because their main body is sleeping, they really need help in this aspect.”

The fishman youngster’s words were reasonable, and Cang Xu nodded to himself, following it up with detailed questions, “What does Charming Blue Goddess want to do with the divine emissary they so vigorously cultivated? What is their plan or objective?”

The fishman youngster muttered to himself, “Developing the church and expanding belief should be the most important goal.”

“Belief is very important for the gods. Although the gods’ power does not come from faith, faith can be regarded as the most adaptable cultivation resource.”

“As far as a god like Charming Blue Goddess is concerned, the more that believe in them, the more it will help them heal when slumbering. Faith is equivalent to their last hope.”

Cang Xu raised another possibility, “I see that Charming Blue Goddess seems to be cultivating your Lordship in a very urgent manner. Are they cultivating top tier combat strength to deal with a powerful enemy soon to arrive?”

“My Lord, although your strength is currently limited to the gold level, the effect of Divine Descent can instantly raise the recipient’s transcendent level. This has been recorded in every large church.”

“As far as the gods are concerned, they are only lacking a qualified temporally vessel, nothing more.”

“I am referring to the historically famous Youngster Mercenary Corps, all of their members met miserable ends.”

The fishman youngster nodded, and his tone became somewhat heavy, “As far as [Divine Descent] is concerned, I have used it and can confirm it is just as you described.”

“If I use [Divine Descent] on myself, then it will be more powerful than when it was used on the big guy, it will be smoother, and there will be minimal hindrances and waste. After all, my current life shape is a Surging Wave fishman form, and my bloodline concentration has reached god level.”

When he said that, the fishman youngster sighed and began to pace about with his hands behind his back.

He reflected while strolling.

After a few circles around, he stopped and looked up at the metal ceiling, “Perhaps I should leave this place.”

“Through divine blessings, I was able to obtain enormous improvements, but this is looking more and more like a sickeningly sweet trap.”

“Furthermore, the mercenary corps needs me. I am their commander.”

Cang Xu was silent.

Several days ago, the fishman youngster had learned about the mercenary corps’ branch’s experiences and predicaments from the big guy’s memories, and since then, he had relayed them to Cang Xu.

Therefore, Cang Xu too knew about the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ situation to an extent.

Hearing the fishman youngster’s words, he couldn’t help but admire him with gratitude inwardly.

The fishman youngster having such decisiveness really wasn’t easy!

One knew that having such means to improve oneself really was an exceptional stroke of luck.

If the fishman youngster relied on himself to become a gold level lifeform, it would have required a long time of arduous work.

Nowadays, his life level was not just limited to the gold level, both his fighter and cleric level had been promoted to the gold level too.

The young man also knew hundreds of divine spells and had experience in using most of them.

These gains would have taken at least ten years if he was working on his own!

More importantly was the fishman youngster’s bloodline. He now had a god level bloodline – that was not something that could be changed with time and effort!!

Cang Xu was also involved in blasphemous prayers.

He consequently benefited and had become a true silver level magician and was no longer pretending to be one anymore.

Cang Xu’s earnings could never compare to the fishman youngster’s, but he felt the urge to continue.

And now, the fishman youngster wanted to leave without hesitation.

While the benefits he was expecting were still tremendous!

To resolutely part with these enormous benefits and believe leaving was the correct decision, the fishman youngster’s humanity flashed at this moment and his leadership charisma emanated.

Cang Xu admired the fishman youngster while simultaneously not wanting him to leave.

Human nature was complex.

Upon seeing how the fishman youngster behaved, the old necromancer felt gratified to once again confirm how reliable he was and deserving of being followed.

But Cang Xu didn’t want the fishman youngster to sacrifice such massive benefits as a result!

He was loyal to the dragonman youngster, and more times than not, thought over the fishman youngster. Cang Xu was looking forward to the fishman youngster getting stronger, putting the others below that. Cang Xu found nothing wrong with sacrificing the other members for the sake of the young commander’s gains.

Of course, it would be better to ensure the other members survived as much as possible while meeting the young commander’s needs.

After all, the survivors were in the same situation, people in the same boat.

With the Bloodcore guaranteeing bloodline talent, the survivors treasured trust and loyalty to each other.

Thus, Cang Xu advised, “Lord Commander, you don’t need to be in a hurry to leave.”

“According to the information I have gained, the attacking enemy is very small, and even if they beheaded ‘Zong Ge’, there was no visible gold level auras. At least the big guy didn’t sense any, and that indicates the enemy has been curbed.”

“After all, when a gold level power is dispatched and carries out an assassination, then it will be a noisy affair. Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord will have stated it so; to say nothing of our prior collaboration together, he is our friend.”

“On the one hand, Shuang Lian is an Alchemy Guild elder. Her power cannot be underestimated. When the Alchemy Guild saved Bu Quan, the Bayonet Gang was forced to pull back, making clear the Alchemy Guild’s power. Bu Quan has joined us, and Shuang Lian is still her teacher. The latter used her magician tower in an attempt to get back the big guy, this proves that Shuang Lian has at least a cooperative relationship with us, and values us considerably. I surmise that president Zi Di might have used War Merchant’s data to exchange for benefits.”

The aforementioned factors also means the mercenary corps hasn’t sunken to a crisis that is a matter of life and death. Therefore, Lord Commander, you don’t need to worry too much.”

“To you, staying here will bring more benefits. To be more precise, massive benefits.”

“How could a chance to advance your strength by leaps and bounds exist in the outside world?”

It would be very difficult to recreate if missed.

It would be too great of a pity.

“This is the world of the strong, and the stronger one is, the better.”

“Lord Commander, suppose you become a saint level here, then return to the mercenary corps, wouldn’t that help the others tremendously?”

“Of course, even if you become a saint level, you cannot deal with a Sheng Ming Empire arrest warrant. But at least it can become an important negotiation bargaining chip.”

“Saint level, that at least will make the empire no longer look down on us. It will be crucial in future redemption talks!”

“If you don’t become a saint level, I fear these divine artifacts will be sold cheaply.”

“Alas…” Cang Xu deeply sighed, “I was worried about Charming Blue Goddess waking up, but that was just sensing some oddities.”

“We are too weak and are not Charming Blue Goddess’ match. If they truly awaken, not only will me and you Lord Commander meet with a catastrophic disaster, but our other companions will also be tracked down and won’t be able to hide.”

“However, I am now thinking that it is precisely because the strength between our enemies and us is so vast that we don’t need to think about Charming Blue Goddess awakening too much.”

This kind of strategy seemed like lying flat and giving up, but upon careful thought, it was nevertheless the most suitable policy.

After all, doing this had enormous gains.

Of course, the risks were massive.

But after thinking it through, if their redemption did not work and they were hunted down by the empire, they would die. If Charming Blue Goddess noticed them, they would be executed as punishment and also die.

What was the difference between them?

Chapter 836: Transforming Captives

The fishman youngster was visibly moved.

Cang Xu was worthy of being a think tank; his words were very reasonable.

Seeing the fishman youngster’s expression, Cang Xu continued, “How much help will you provide to our comrades if you return as a gold level?”

“If it was Snake Rat or Two Eyes Island, it would be a lot. However, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is on Ice Sculpture Island now, and gold levels can only do so much.”

“Ice Sculpture Island only has three saint levels, and if your Lordship reaches it, then it would be different.”

“Lord Commander, Zong Ge has been a peak silver level for a long time. It is very possible he has become a gold level.”

“If you become a saint level, upon your return, Zong Ge will completely acknowledge the gap between you and your Lordship. By doing this, we might be able to put an end to a hidden danger within us and your leadership will be absolute!”

The fishman youngster nodded repeatedly but still had misgivings.

Cang Xu chuckled, he understood what the young man had misgivings about.

Thus, Cang Xu considerately replied, “Naturally, we also cannot stubbornly stay here and ignore our comrades’ perils.”

“So, we need to gather information, and only with more detailed information can we issue a more accurate judgement!”

“Currently, although Lan Zao is on Ice Sculpture Island, he is in an unusual situation, and cannot act as our source of information, he is unable to contact Zi Di, Zong Ge, and the others.”

“Therefore, I propose we think of a method so the big guy can make discreet inquiries on the latest news on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

The fishman youngster spoke at that moment, “I have already done as you proposed. I planned on letting Hong Zhu leave Magnet Archipelago, she is our contact with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Currently, she recognizes that the big guy is a member of our mercenary corps.”

“Thus, let her go to Ice Sculpture Island to collect information and form an understanding with us.”

But Cang Xu shook his head, “Lord Commander, that arrangement is not appropriate.”

“Don’t forget, during the battle of Two Eyes Island, I was affected by the Devil Gold Coins and exposed myself as a necromancer. During that battle, Hong Zhu fought alongside me.”

“Therefore, she is suspicious. Although she immediately fled from me after I was exposed, it cannot wash away the suspicions on her.”

“The Ice Sculpture Kingdom has the same disposition towards necromancers as other powers, and if Hong Zhu is noticed, it might cause needless twists and turns.”

Cang Xu’s warning caused the fishman youngster to slap his forehead, “Oh you’re right.”

“Hong Zhu is still in relative danger.”

“Her relationship with us means Deceptive Disguise and the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell cannot be used on her.”

Cang Xu continued to propose, “Hong Zhu and us are destined to be together. Previously, she chose to attach herself to Da Dou, reflecting her strong qualities. At the very least, she is an expert in tracking and detection, these capabilities can effectively complement Da Dou and will help the big guy grow better.”

“Therefore, it would be better if she continued to stay by the big guy’s side.”

“On the Magnet Archipelago, Hong Zhu is still quite safe.”

“If we want to obtain information, Da Dou can step up and hire some personnel to go into the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

“The big guy is a member of our Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, and our operations are very normal! So, wanting to make inquiries for intelligence about us doesn’t need to be secret and can be done by outsiders.”

“The more people there are, the greater the quantity and quality of the information gathered.”

The fishman youngster sighed, “Doing that really is more dependable.”

Cang Xu then said, “Actually, there is another way.”

Cang Xu’s life experience was far richer than the young man’s, and he had more ways of thinking than the latter.

To him, it seemed that these many problems had many solutions.

Cang Xu said, “Lord Commander, don’t you think keeping those captives locked up is somewhat wasteful?”

Thanks to the Ocean Mother Nest being a hunting trap, the fishman youngster had gained quite the harvest.

As of now, he held Lady Sea Snake, Ruan Tang Ren, Xiao Wang Zi, Ku Feng, and Qi Ling captive. They were all silver level and locked up in longhorn cages in a death like state.

The fishman youngster wasn’t stupid, so when he heard Cang Xu’s idea, he immediately replied, “Are you saying we should transform these captives?”

Cang Xu nodded heavily, “Lord Commander, you now have amazing skills. Each time you do a blasphemous prayer, you fool Charming Blue Goddess. Earlier, you used all kinds of divine spells to help the big guy and Lan Zao avert danger and also profaned the Dueling God!”

“Since you can deceive the gods, don’t tell me you can’t deceive these captives?”

“We have the memory crystal creation device, and all kinds of memory modification methods. Since we can trick them into thinking they are dead, why can’t we deceive them into our service?”

The fishman youngster’s heart beat incessantly at that.

He hadn’t thought of this before, as he was mainly focused on the big guy, Lan Zao, and himself.

There was another reason, even if he did transform these people, he didn’t have anywhere to use them.

Cang Xu saw things quicker than the fishman youngster.

Nowadays, with Divine Power Channel, Divine Descent, and Divine Domain Descent, along with other comprehensive methods, these subordinates had ample places to show off their skills!

The fishman youngster agreed, “Then next we should plan carefully and give every captive forged memories.”

“Using the Memory Concealment spell, we can hide their original memories beyond their notice.”

“It would be for the best if we have them believe in Charming Blue Goddess. If that is done, I’ll be able to thumb through their memories and deepen my control over them.”

“Even if the transformation is successful, these people have to be used carefully. After all, hidden dangers exist, and if they recover their memories, that in of itself can reveal many of our secrets.”

Cang Xu added on, “As far as modifying the necromancers’ memories is concerned, you need to pay close attention as their humanity is their anchor.”

“Necromancers need to vie with negative energy corrosion, and every powerful necromancer has a powerful human anchor.”

“For example, Xiao Wang Zi’s human anchor is his parents. Qi Ling’s human anchor is her fishman clan and Ku Feng’s is his pursuit of the blade.”

“These human anchors need to be retained and should be emphasized further. Otherwise, these necromancers will lack that human anchor, and as the days go on, it is very possible that they will be thoroughly eroded by negative energy and be reduced to base undead lifeforms, relying only on instinct and won’t be an intelligent necromancer.”

The fishman youngster took Cang Xu’s reminder to heart.

“You at my side really puts me at ease.” The fishman youngster praised.

Cang Xu smiled, “My Lord, it is my honor that I can follow you.”

Cang Xu felt he was extremely lucky to meet the fishman youngster. On Mystifying Monster Island, it was because of the young man that he had been saved many times, and it was again because of the young man that he had a greater future.

If Cang Xu was anywhere else, he would never have gotten such things.

What made Cang Xu the best was that the young commander never denigrated or feared him because of who he was. The young man really treated him as one of his people.

This respect was a luxury Cang Xu couldn’t get anywhere else!

The fishman youngster was also glad that he could receive Cang Xu’s help.

Since Mystifying Monster Island, Cang Xu has done countless, irreplaceable functions for him.

Now, although the fishman youngster was far stronger than Cang Xu, Cang Xu’s wisdom still gave the young man constant support and assistance.

Alone, a person had limited capabilities, awareness, attention, and feelings, among other things, and their considerations were not comprehensive and deep.

The fishman youngster had gotten a deep impression of this from this conversation.

Cang Xu had given him poignant advice.

Next, the two deliberated on how to forge memories for the captives.

The discussion only had preliminary results when Cang Xu was forced to leave.

The teleportation blood sacrifice only lasted a short time.

Cang Xu departed Ocean Mother Nest and returned to the numerous believers, again gaining the Charming Blue believers’ admiration and praise.

To them, it seemed that every blood sacrifice teleportation was life threatening.

Of the believers that were teleported in, only some of them returned. Many people didn’t have firm convictions and would be eaten by the saint beast Snow Pear Octopus.

Therefore, the vast majority of the believers who managed to return didn’t do it again.

Cang Xu was the exception.

Since participating in his first one, he had joined every subsequent one after!

This kind of undaunted behavior could not be done by ordinary people, and it caused Cang Xu’s prestige to rise like the tide with the Charming Blue Believers, and it accumulated each time to a considerable amount.

The fishman youngster in the Deep Sea Monster Fish was actively consuming the contents of the divine blessing while perceiving other believers.

He was mainly searching for a second Charming Blue believer on Ice Sculpture Island.

He maintained close attention to a few believers, which were the big guy, Dao Hen, and Lan Zao.

The big guy and Lan Zao were safe and were getting increasingly better treatment.

Dao Hen had been set up to stay on the Treasure Chest, and not long after, he gave the fishman youngster important information – Tu Ji left the ship!

As a member of the Blood Light Punishment Institution, Tu Ji was responsible for chasing down and hanging necromancers. He was the fishman youngster’s most important spying target.

But the Treasure Chest suddenly stopped in the boundless ocean, and Tu Ji jumped in, and vanished.

Dao Hen had received orders to stay on the Treasure Chest, focused on monitoring and gathering information on Tan Mo and Tu Ji.

Dao Hen didn’t anticipate Tu Ji just jumping overboard.

Tu Ji also did it late at night, and it wasn’t easy for anyone to notice him.

Dao Hen hesitated at the time and didn’t choose to follow. To him, it seemed that staying on the Treasure Chest was God’s decree.

“The Fish God Mist did not ask me to leave.” Dao Hen thought and didn’t pursue.

By the time the fishman youngster looked through his memories, it was too late.

“Compared to Tan Mo, following Tu Ji is more profitable.” The fishman youngster regretted it.

But it was clearly too late for Dao Hen to chase after him.

Might as well keep Dao Hen on the Treasure Chest.

“Tu Ji jumped into the ocean; this is absolutely not a suicide attempt.”

“Could there be a necromancer hiding on the seafloor?”

“More probable is that he needs to come ashore on some nearby island. But which island?”

The fishman youngster didn’t know Tu Ji was coming for Hong Zhu.

Since Tu Ji had learned about Hong Zhu from Yan Yi, he didn’t tell Tan Mo about it.

Dao Hen was also unaware of such a secret.

The fishman youngster felt things were anything but reassuring and immediately consulted War Merchant’s ocean map.

Very quickly, he got an answer.

“It is Feng Awakening Island.”

This was an isle, close to a barren island. It had spare trees and scant vegetation.

Chapter 837: Tu Ji Comes to the Island

According to common sense, few isles like Feng Awakening Island were even recorded.

The reason why it was is that it was special, because historically, a famous anti-demon hero [Qing Feng Xing], had regained consciousness here.

Despite her heavy injuries, she gathered the remnants of her army here and turned around the naval battle, successfully routing her pursuers.

From this, the Main Plane’s allied armies officially began their counteroffensive against the demon army.

Feng Awakening Island coast.

It was desolate and uninhabited and could be seen over in a glance.

A figure slowly walked out of the seawater and stepped foot onto Feng Awakening Island.

He looked ordinary and his clothes were too, but he displayed a lofty, yet threatening dauntlessness, it was Tu Ji.

“Feng Awakening Island, when young Qing Feng Xing lost consciousness due to her injuries, she was awakened and cared for by a passing druid here. This island was consequently given its name from that.”

Tu Ji looked around, his gaze quickly fixing on something.

A moment later, light and shadow fluctuated, and a silhouette awkwardly became visible.

“Lord Tu Ji, my code name is ‘Awaken’, I was ordered to welcome you.” It was an imperial secret spy.

Tu Ji replied, “I didn’t expect an imperial secret spy to be here. You spent quite the effort to build a teleportation formation too. I want to use that teleportation formation to go to Magnet Archipelago.”

“Yes, I have been briefed by my superiors on this. Please follow me!” The imperial secret spy led the way.

Tu Ji followed after the imperial secret spy “Awaken” to the magic formation.

Very quickly, he saw the real Feng Awakening Island – a castle that stood in the middle of the isle.

The castle covered a massive area and stood tall, nearly dominating 80% of the isle. The castle was lined with ballista, cannons, and had numerous magic runes engraved into its walls, displaying its formidable fighting strength.

Upon entering the castle, Tu Ji was astonished to find that the castle had a considerable garrison.

“This is your secret spy headquarters?” Tu Ji curiously inquired.

Secret spy “Awaken” chuckled, “Lord Tu Ji, you aren’t part of our organization, please forgive me that I cannot tell you about such secrets.”

Tu Ji coldly snorted and didn’t demand an answer.

He followed Awaken down a path, until finally arriving at a two floor high platform with a teleportation formation on it.

This teleportation formation was built as a towering spatial gate. Inside the gate frame, there was a constantly spinning starry river vortex that disseminated a strong spatial fluctuation.

“This is a large-scale teleportation gate; it can teleport both a large amount of lifeforms and goods.”

“So that’s how it is, they use this teleportation gate to resupply.”

Generally speaking, the Secret Spy Agency cannot have too many people garrisoned. After all, doing intelligence wet work and doing military affairs were two very different things. The secret spies were not an army.

Having so many people garrisoned somewhere was very rare for the Secret Spy Agency.

Because every day, people needed to eat, drink, defecate, and urinate, all of which needed a large amount of supplies. Transporting supplies was sufficiently troublesome and was very difficult to mask, and if an enemy was motivated to check into it, it wouldn’t be hard for them.

But seeing this teleportation gate, Tu Ji had an understanding.

“Feng Awakening Island is desolate and uninhabited, it is of no interest to anyone, it is a good place to build a secret base.”

“Feng Awakening Island is also near Magnet Archipelago, which sees vigorous streams of people every year and its ports are a picture of an endless river. Perhaps the Secret Spy Agency first transports supplies to Magnet Archipelago, then through a hidden teleportation gate to deliver them to Feng Awakening Island.”

“I will be traveling by this supply route.”

Tu Ji obviously thought this and didn’t vocalize it.

After waiting for a moment, Awaken had him step into the teleportation gate.

As he walked towards the teleportation gate, Tu Ji could feel it drawing him in.

He simply used a defensive battle skill to protect himself, then let himself be pulled into the gate.

In a split second, he landed on the ground, and he entered an underground plaza on Magnet Archipelago.

Naturally there was an imperial secret spy waiting for him to show the way.

Because Tu Ji was not an imperial secret spy, he used his authority as a member of the Blood Light Punishment Institution to requisition manpower and resources. Therefore, the imperial secret spy wanted to immediately lead Tu Ji out of this covert base.

But Tu Ji wasn’t in a hurry, instead he asked for information on Hong Zhu.

The imperial secret spy took out some materials and gave it to Tu Ji, “My Lord, this is all of the information we have on Hong Zhu. You can take your time reading it on your own.”

But Tu Ji shook his head and opened it on the spot.

After looking through it, his complexion sank, “This is the information you’re giving me? The newest information the grand Imperial Secret Spy Agency has on Hong Zhu is ten days old?!”

Tu Ji was dissatisfied.

The imperial secret spy bitterly spread his hands, “My Lord, our mission isn’t to investigate a silver level treasure hunter.”

“You’re skimping on me! Several days ago, I issued a command that you all should investigate Hong Zhu with all you had!” Tu Ji coldly snorted with inquiry.

The imperial secret spy shirked responsibility, “Lord Tu Ji, it wasn’t that we didn’t try, but Magnet Archipelago’s terrain is complicated. Because of the magnetic field variations, every isle can change position due to that. Not only that but don’t be fooled by the archipelago’s bare surface bereft of life. As a matter of fact, there are many earth elementals living underground and even magnetic earth elementals.”

“There are no lack of gold levels among the earth elementals, and they are the masters of this place. If it were not for all these earth elemental tribes fighting each other, outsiders like us would never be able to regularly move throughout the area.”

“Hong Zhu is a solitary treasure hunter, and it is difficult for us to follow her tracks. She is an expert at tracking, surveying, and conversely, her anti-detection ability is quite good.”

Tu Ji’s eyes transmitted cold daggers as he tightly stared at the imperial secret spy, “These problems were to be handled by you people, not me.”

“I simply issued an order, and you were responsible for gathering information on Hong Zhu.”

“I came here to chase down a necromancer, and right now, Hong Zhu is the only trail.”

“To cover my tracks as much as possible, I didn’t land on Magnet Archipelago directly, instead I went to Feng Awakening Island to secretly teleport here.”

“4 hours.” Tu Ji raised four fingers, issuing an ultimatum, “I will give you that much time, and at the end of it, if I don’t get any valuable clues, I will see your head roll and condemn you as a close friend of the necromancer who was protecting them!”

The imperial secret spy instantly changed color and wailed, “My Lord, that is slander.”

“You cannot do this!”

“We are both imperial citizens; I have worked hard for the empire.”

Tu Ji sneered, “Don’t shout, I learned this method from your colleagues.”

“Don’t think I’m joking with you.”

“Now, you only have 3 hours and 59 minutes.”

The imperial secret spy firmly grinded his teeth and turned heel. Within a step, it turned into a jog.

He was cursing wildly in his heart: Tu Ji, you madman! Then he began to deploy as much manpower as he could to frantically search for Hong Zhu’s whereabouts.

The one cursed as a madman was actually rather calm.

As a member of the Blood Light Punishment Institution, he really hunted and killed necromancers regardless of the perils. But on the other hand, their reputation for being insane had also been intentionally spread by them.

The scene before him was Tu Ji using his reputation for being crazy to mobilize the full force the imperial secret spies here.

He wasn’t disappointed, and 1.5 hours later, the imperial secret spy came back to find Tu Ji with a sweat soaked head and reported, “Several days ago, someone saw Hong Zhu go to this isle. This information was tentatively confirmed and verified.”

Tu Ji was expressionless, “Tentatively confirmed? Then you will follow me there.”

“If I don’t find Hong Zhu, I’ll have to come back and behead you according to the law.”

The imperial secret spy’s body and mind quaked.

But Tu Ji was forcing him, so he had no choice but to brace himself and leave with Tu Ji.

Meanwhile.

After sending off Tu Ji, Tan Mo relaxed.

Upon obtaining the latest report, he couldn’t help but smile, “It is confirmed, Tu Ji has made landfall on Magnet Archipelago.”

“Obviously he could have directly landed, but going through the trouble is actually the professional way of doing things.”

“The secret spy in charge of the Magnet Archipelago will have a hard time. But that isn’t our problem.”

Tan Mo sat in the captain’s room and looked out of the window, where the ocean and sky intersected, his mood cheerful.

Freeing himself from Tu Ji made him carefree.

Although he had a Secret Gate Church cleric still on board and his saint level bodyguard, they were carrying out a more secret mission and to Tan Mo, they were far better to deal with than Tu Ji.

“The entire Blood Light Punishment Institution is full of lunatics. To eliminate the undead, they will do and sacrifice anything.”

“They would rather wrongly kill ten thousand than let one escape. They won’t just do that to others; they will also go through the pains to kill themselves too.”

“The most terrible thing is that the Blood Light Punishment Institution’s background is too strong!”

The backers of the Blood Light Punishment Institution members was tougher than the templar knights.

Because the latter’s only backer was just Heavenly Emperor Sheng Ming, while the Blood Light Punishment Institution had almost all of the empire’s gods backing them in an alliance of benefits.

Other powers had similar organizations to the Blood Light Punishment Institution.

These powers united into the widest divine alliance. It was the gods expressing their ultimate wrath against the necromancers who were encroaching on their core benefits.

“Tu Ji is gone, next is finding a pirate gang to put on a performance.”

“Of course, first I need talk to talk the Secret Gate Church’s people and communicate this in advance.”

With this goal in mind, Tan Mo entered the Treasure Chest’s deepest spatial floor and knocked on a cabin door.

After getting permission, he pushed open the door and entered, seeing the two from the Secret Gate Church.

Tan Mo got right to the point, stating he had a military operation coming up. He gave the reasons why, after all, the Treasure Chest was an imperial warship and didn’t have the right without cause or reason to enter the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, that would provoke quite the trouble. Doing it by force might lead to deportation.

Therefore, before that, Tan Mo would go find pirates to “set up”, he would “lose”, then he would drag his wounded body into an Ice Sculpture Kingdom port and request emergency relief.

By doing this, he could alleviate himself of suspicion and use the dignity of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. They would have no good grounds to refuse him, and he would be able to smoothly enter a kingdom port.

When they reached port, the Secret Gate Church’s two people would naturally sneak out. Then Tan Mo’s escort mission would be complete.

The two Secret Gate Church people glanced at each other, then nodded, indicating that they greenlit it and allowed Tan Mo to do as he saw fit.

Chapter 838: Movement of the Pirates

Tan Mo used information to start choosing a suitable pirate.

First was Da Han.

He was a saint level warlock and a member of the Fire Beard Pirates; he founded the Icebound Pirates and was currently the Fire Beard Pirates’ fourth mate.

This position could change hands.

Tan Mo read the information, “At present, Da Han is leading his Icebound Pirates towards Ice Sculpture Island.”

“Accompanying him are many pirates that joined together to get a piece of the action.”

“It is worthy of a saint level ambience.”

One could say that gold level transcendents were the cornerstone of power and gold level experts could quell a city. For example, Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord and the former lord of Two Eyes Island, Hun Tong.

Then saint levels were the upper echelons of transcendents. They were usually nation level powers. When there were significant operations, they caused wide ranging influence and surged like a gathering storm.

Like Da Han right now, obviously he was acting on his own, but the scene of it caused other pirate groups to flock to him, lifting a particularly vast wave that would batter into the orderly Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s strength was rich and powerful. Especially during the National Ceremony, where the kingdom was more ready to display its strength and would dispatch its navy more often.

Based on past National Ceremonies, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s orderliness would consolidate to unprecedented levels, and safety would substantially increase. The once lively pirates on Ice Sculpture Island would voluntarily retreat ninety li.

But this year’s National Ceremony had Da Han as an unforeseen event.

Da Han’s decision to attack negatively impacted the safety of Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s periphery waters immensely.

If one was simply looking at the numbers, the Icebound Pirates only had one saint level, Da Han, while the Ice Sculpture Kingdom had three saint levels. The contrast in strength was stark.

But in reality, the situation was complicated, and such a simple contrast was unworkable.

Like Zong Ge said, this was the charm of military affairs! Military affairs were never as simple as comparing military power, if that was done, then there was no need for war, and both sides would just fight to the death. Wouldn’t it be simple to judge who would win or lose based on who had more troops?

This was obviously out of the question.

Although the Ice Sculpture Kingdom had more saint levels, they were tied up in other things.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s three saint levels were the king, the royal magician, and the White Dragon King.

Each had a high position and their respective affairs. To say nothing of death, if one went missing or left for some time, it was enough to cause massive waves and even cause their organizations to break down to a degree.

Therefore, when Da Han was coming to invade, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s reply was no simple matter.

The kingdom had many things to be concerned about.

On the face of it, it seemed that they could dispatch a saint level to openly intercept Da Han.

In actuality, it was very difficult to decide who to dispatch.

The king?

He was the kingdom’s ruler, how could he recklessly move like that? No matter what, he would never be the first to enter the battlefield.

The White Dragon King? She controlled the white dragon community and was only an ally of the Ice Sculpture royal family.

The royal magician seemed to be the most appropriate. But he had countless daily affairs and needed to preside over the Warm Snow Cup as the anchor for this grand occasion. He also had important interests that demanded his attention, especially the Ice Lake Grand Formation, which was giving the royal magician unprecedented concerns.

Excluding the problem of who to send, there was also the consideration of security.

Was this the strategy of another enemy? They might be using Da Han as bait to lure in an Ice Sculpture Kingdom saint level, then perhaps ambush them when they had them alone?

This was possible.

Because the Fire Beard Pirates was a legend level force, don’t even mention their leader Fire Beard, they also had other saint levels in their ranks.

For example, it naturally is safer to stay behind in headquarters than to rush into the frontlines alone.

Even if one wanted to go out, they had to at least wait for information to clarify things.

How to get clear information?

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s intelligence apparatus was already working overtime and actively scouting out Da Han’s military situation.

Apart from that, there was the navy.

Once the navy and the Icebound pirates exchanged fire, there would be detailed and reliable information gleaned from the battle.

But if that happened, without a saint level overseeing the fleet, even if there are numerous gold levels and the newest warships, it would be very difficult to withstand Da Han directly.

If a confrontation occurred, there was bound to be losses.

But that in itself was normal procedures.

Through expending some of the navy, they could obtain sufficient time and information for the saint levels to move more correctly.

The standing navy were all elites, but the saint levels were far more important to the kingdom than they were. In the face of saint levels, these elites were mere cannon fodder.

All kinds of factors brought about the current situation.

Da Han was a saint level who led the Icebound Pirates, and he was making things difficult for the entire Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

One of the reasons he faintly held the advantage was that he grasped the initiative.

The pirates roamed beyond the coasts and could fight and retreat as they pleased.
