Of course, Da Han would do his utmost to prevent himself from being outnumbered. Therefore, if two of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s saint levels were dispatched together, he would immediately retreat.

Of course, someone like Da Han also took risks.

If it was in normal times, he wouldn’t be doing this. But right now, times are quite special – the Pirate Throne has appeared. Whichever pirates sat on that seat, became the Pirate King, the Pirate God!

One might as well ask, which pirate wouldn’t be excited by this?

“It’s very obvious Da Han is ambitious. He wants to plunder the Ice Sculpture Kingdom and steal a hefty sum to raise his rank on the Pirate Ship Sacred Monument.”

“The Ice Sculpture Kingdom will never sit back and watch this; they are merely waiting for enough information to move.”

“Da Han doing this while the Ice Sculpture Kingdom is holding the National Ceremony simply is urinating on the kingdom’s head. If the kingdom is unable to maintain stability over its territorial waters and protect the people joining the National Ceremony, then the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s international prestige will undoubtedly take a dive and hit rock bottom.”

“The previous Ice Sculpture King was a legend level and could rely on personal strength to steady popular sentiment. But the current Ice Sculpture King is only a saint level, and compared to his grandfather, he has to maintain international prestige more!”

“This contest will no doubt be a saint level affair.”

“I’ll be a distant spectator to this liveliness. I cannot join in.”

Tan Mo turned over intelligence on Da Han, looking at the second page.

“Qian Xing…”

This pirate was also a saint level that was part of the Fire Beard Pirates.

But unlike the former, he was a loner and had no pirate group.

“He once appeared on Flower Skirt Island and fought against Cai Jing and Magician Da Bei. Ultimately, although he defeated his opponents, he was unable to obtain Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong’s inheritance. He also let Cai Jing, Da Bei, and others escape!”

“And based on the latest intelligence, Cai Jing has already joined the Ice Sculpture kingdom’s Alchemy Guild and became and elder who is currently in charge of unearthing the Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong’s other inheritances.”

“As for Qian Xing, he has been missing for a long time.”

Even if he was still around, Tan Mo had no way of finding and staging a show with him.

Qian Xing was less suitable than Da Han for this “performance”.

Because he was a solitary fighter, and unlike Da Han who looked after his underlings, Qian Xing had no people and was also very strong.

He would be more troublesome than Dan Han.

The third pirate intelligence report describes the pirate merchant Money Pot.

He was a water pigman, a gold level, and the commander of the Dread Firepower Pirates.

He was a famous arms merchant and one of the characteristics of his pirate group was their extreme amount of cannons.

Tan Mo evaluated it and felt that although Money Pot was only a gold level whose fighting strength nearly matched his own, he was not a compatible candidate for his performance.

On the one hand, the Dread Firepower Pirates had many gold levels. On the other hand, their pirate group’s long range firepower was too ferocious.

The fourth report had Feng Lian’s name written on it.

The latest information showed that Feng Lian had sailed his flagship, the Divine Wind, close to Snow Bird Harbor and came to the rescue of the Bayonet Gang remnants. Soon after, along their retreat path, their ships fought the kingdom’s second naval fleet.

Feng Lian’s recent activity was at some ocean beast’s lair. It could be determined that the ocean beast there was a saint level Cockscomb Ration Python.

Tan Mo looked through it and quickly vetoed choosing Feng Lian.

“I cannot treat Feng Lian as a normal gold level.”

Long ago, Feng Lian fled from a saint level while he was a gold level. In the newest intelligence, Feng Lian openly stood off against a saint level Cockscomb Ration Python, showing how bold and powerful he is.

“Although there isn’t information on his bloodline concentration, from his equipment and actual combat performance, one can see that Feng Lian’s bloodline concentration should be very high.”

“This kind of combat strength exceeds normal parameters and should be due to his high bloodline concentration enhancing it.”

“He might be a saint level seed!”

If Tan Mo wanted to utilize Feng Lian for his performance, it would be risky. Secondly, if Feng Lian becomes a saint level in the future, then wouldn’t he be offending a saint level? A saint level pirate at that!

The price was too high.

Tan Mo didn’t want to bear that.

There was another reason, it was the astonishing speed of Feng Lian’s Divine Wind Pirates. Their ships were all magic ships made on Gear Island, and their quality was ensured immensely.

“If we pick a fight with them, the Treasure Chest will be unable to escape, what will be done then?”

Being unable to escape was very embarrassing.

Tan Mo continued to browse the information reports.

He saw the Rope Pirates.

“This is a newly formed pirate group.”

“The commander is Archer Chuan Yun, and the auxiliary commander is Magician Huo Sheng, both of them were once famous bounty hunters.”

“Now they have renounced their old lives and turned to piracy.”

“Only two gold levels…not strong enough for me to ‘lose’ to.”

Tan Mo set them aside.

Next Tan Mo looked at the report on the Heroism Pirates.

This pirate group could work, their commander Ba Qi is very strong, and I am not their match.”

“Unfortunately, since resupplying on Flower Skirt Island, they haven’t appeared again.”

The information reports were finished.

Tan Mo’s brows were also wrinkled.

Don’t be fooled by the number of pirates, he unexpectedly didn’t find a suitable candidate.

Chapter 839: The Two Saints

Thinking about it carefully, it was quite normal.

Because these factors were very difficult to control.

They couldn’t be too strong like Da Han or too weak like the Rope Pirates. Only by combining strength and weakness could Tan Mo remain safe while also appearing genuine enough!

After pondering for a long time, Tan Mo finally fixed his eyes on one report.

“Cong Mang, the Vegetable Pirates’ commander.”

“He is a druid that dual cultivates battle qi, he is proficient with the rod, has an irascible disposition, and once had a close relationship with the Wisteria Merchant Alliance.”

Tan Mo carefully researched Cong Mang’s military achievements, and after contrasting them with himself, he found that Cong Mang’s, he found that his personal combat strength nearly matched his own.

This meant that there was a lot of space for the performance.

Deliberately suffering defeat was also comparatively safe.

“The Vegetable Pirates’ ship is also ordinary and isn’t fast.”

“Although Cong Mang attached himself to Da Han and sails with the Icebound Pirates, there are still opportunities to exploit.”

Tan Mo saw the latest battle report on the Vegetable Pirates.

They had done several operations, looting many merchant ships, while Da Han’s Icebound Pirates, on the contrary, had very few gains. Yet obviously, they were sailing together.

Tan Mo was capable of analyzing information.

He quickly made a judgement: on the one hand, Da Han’s Icebound Pirates might look down on such trivial rubbish. On the other hand, this might indicate that the Vegetable Pirates actively separated themselves from the main fleet, then returned after they got done looting.

Furthermore, according to pirate rules, after Cong Mang returned to the main fleet, he would tithe a part of his plunder.

Therefore, Da Han didn’t need to act and could reap what he hadn’t sown.

“I could first lure Cong Mang away from his post and then have him confront me.”

“Even if we fight to a draw, I can still retreat and have sensible justifications as an outsider – I am worried that Da Han will provide assistance.”

“Hm, it’ll be him.”

As Tan Mo was reminiscing about Cong Mang, the latter was in the middle of combat.

“Charge, faster.”

“Hoodlum battle squad, follow me in!” Cong Mang roared, his voice like a giant drum.

He was a middle aged man who wore a dark green hemp garment, his legs wore leggings, and he had cloth shoes on his feet. He didn’t have hair and was bald, and although he was a druid, he dressed closer to a martial monk.

He took the lead, first to step onto the ship planks and advance bravely forth!

He held a long rod in his hand, slamming it into an enemy ahead of him and giving them a violent death.

Then he threw the corpse he pounded into two other enemies.

Cong Mang jumped down, and like a mountain tiger, he killed his way through the crowded deck.

Cong Mang crouched, and swung his rod around, sweeping the enemies away, felling them in a wide expanse with repeated wails of anguish.

He was the vanguard and the first to get a foothold.

Soon after, large quantities of shaved pirates followed onto the deck and charged in.

They were also bald, held long rods, and wore light green martial clothes.

This was the Vegetable Pirates’ Hoodlum battle squad!

The battle squad was over a hundred people large, and the huge offensive their flying rods created couldn’t be stopped no matter where they went.

From high in the air, the green clothed Vegetable Pirates’ battle squad was rapidly expanding in all directions as the enemy was destroyed like the autumn gale sweeping away fallen leaves.

Very quickly, there was only green on the entire deck.

But the battle still continued.

The first deck was only part of a ship, and there were still enemies gathered on the second deck, stubbornly refusing to admit defeat.

But Cong Mang wasn’t concerned with that.

He had abundant battle experience and knew that charging in now would greatly reduce slaughter efficiency. After all, the terrain below deck favored the defender.

Conversely, it would be better to dispatch ordinary pirates to watch over every ship’s ladder and easily suppress this part of the battlefield.

“Little ones, follow me!” Cong Mang roared, leading the Hoodlum battle squad towards another ship.

They quickly undertook another boarding action.

The other pirates were responsible for taking over the enemy positions they captured.

Cong Mang followed a set pattern and swiftly mopped up most of the merchant ships.

These merchant ships could barely resist, and the strongest person among them was only silver level. During the first battle, Cong Mang had charged into the flagship and killed them.

The battle quickly ended.

The pirates reported the results to Cong Mang.

The plunder was all ordinary goods, there was a lot of them, and it would be quite difficult to dispose of them.

But Cong Mang was satisfied.

“Da Han didn’t bother with this, because he is rich and powerful.”

“We are different, we cannot let any opportunity to strengthen go!”

“This is a good opportunity that is hard to come by little ones.”

“We are now attached to Da Han, and even if we encounter a saint level enemy, we have a chance of withstanding them.”

“This is why we give them a portion of our gains!”

“Our things aren’t easy to seize.”

Cong Man’s voice was loud, and that loud voice inspired morale.

He knew that recently, his sailors had grievances in their hearts. After all, they were the ones who went through the great trouble of looting yet had to transfer some over to Da Han’s Icebound Pirates.

Suddenly, a gentle laughter came from above Cong Mang, as if mocking him.

“Who?!” Cong Mang became furious, instantly gazing at where it was coming from.

A moment later, he saw someone squatting on the summit of the merchant ship’s tallest mast.

He was a dark elf, with dark skin and handsome features.

He had a tall build, a high ponytail, and ten slender fingers. Each of his fingernails was very sharp and glinted with blue radiance.

At that moment, he was looking down at Cong Mang indifferently, the corner of his mouth sneering.

Cong Mang’s look changed dramatically, and he forced his scowl into a smile, “Lord, Lord Qian Xing?!”

“Why…why did you come here?”

Qian Xing sneered, “Why? Am I not welcomed?”

“Your arrival honors our entire pirate group!” Cong Mang gave an exaggerated laugh.

Although Qian Xing was a loner, his cultivation had reached the saint level and Cong Mang was not his equal.

A second later, Qian Xing lightly fell onto the deck, “I have come to take your ship.”

“To return to the Icebound Pirates, I want to look for Da Han.”

Cong Mang showed a difficult expression, “But I haven’t cleaned up the battlefield yet.”

“Eh?” Qian Xing blankly stared at Cong Mang.

Cong Mang suddenly seeped with cold sweat.

But a moment later, Qian Xing clapped his shoulder, “I don’t want to make things difficult for you. Have your flagship set sail, the others can stay behind to clean up the battlefield.”

Cong Mang was overjoyed and repeatedly gave thanks. Then he personally led Qian Xing onto his flagship.

Not long after, he took control of the steering and guided Qian Xing back to the main fleet.

In his mind, Cong Mang was guessing why Qian Xing suddenly appeared and why he wanted to meet Da Han.

“Both Qian Xing and Da Han are saint levels, yet they are actually getting together. Could it be that Fire Beard has plans for the Ice Sculpture Kingdom?”

I wanted to land secretly on Ice Sculpture Island and excavate the Wisteria Cache. If this happens during a war, won’t the destruction be advantageous for me?”

But the truth was not what Cong Mang thought.

Qian Xing’s goal was simple; it was finding Ba Qi!

At the time, he was attracted to Flower Skirt Island by Shuang Tu, the main reason was that Shuang Tu was masquerading under Ba Qi’s name and had asked him for help.

But on Flower Skirt Island, Qian Xing noticed Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong’s inheritance and fought Cai Jing and Da Bei for it.

Although the other two were gold levels, thanks to their sufficient preparations, they were able to teleport out when they lost.

Before leaving, they managed to take away Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong’s inheritance.

Qian Xing was naturally unreconciled, and after the event, he flew alone and looked around the surrounding sea area.

Regretfully, he was a loner and lacked manpower. He also lacked information, and it was like fishing a needle from the sea, and so he failed.

Cai Jing joined the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s Alchemy Guild and became one of their elders. When this information was learned by Qian Xing, he had given up his search for Cai Jing.

At this time, he was pacing back and forth through the ocean looking for Ba Qi.

He had yet to succeed in that.

Qian Xing actually found out that Ba Qi had been taken prisoner in some naval battle.

But because Zhou Zhang had used a divine spell, and disguised things well, he didn’t make any gratifying discoveries.

After a few hours, Cong Mang’s flagship and the Icebound Pirates converged.

“Hahaha, Cong Mang, why did you return with just one ship?”

“Did you meet with a powerful enemy that you could only flee from?”

“Just ask us for help, the price won’t be low though, can you afford it?”

The Icebound Pirates’ experts jeered and made fun of the returning Cong Mang.

Cong Mang was solemn as he appeared on deck and shouted to Qian Xing that they had arrived.

In a split second, the jeers and insults vanished.

The arrogant and obstinate pirates snapped to silence, and they became serious and solemn.

By the time Qian Xing calmly emerged from the captain’s room, and appeared before the masses, the Icebound Pirates’ eyes widened, then they promptly gave their greetings.

Being a saint level was enough to gain such seriousness.

The Icebound Pirates’ auxiliary commander personally greeted, “Lord Qian Xing, our commander is in the middle of prayer, please have some tea first.”

Qian Xing was expressionless, “My time is limited. Where is the prayer room?”

The auxiliary commander looked distressed.

But a moment later, Qian Xing smiled, “Found it.”

He disappeared from where he stood instantly.

The auxiliary commander’s face changed radically, and he turned around and scampered into the ship’s lower decks.

At that moment, in the prayer room, Qian Xing’s figure appeared behind Da Han.

The prayer room was not spacious, and its facilities were very antiquated, including the consecrated divine statue that felt ancient and worn by change.

This wasn’t due to Da Han, rather it was because he believed in the God of Time and Dusk – Inching Time.

Despite Qian Xing’s impatience, when he saw Da Han in the middle of prayer, he stayed his words and chose to remain silent.

“Grand Dusk God, I recite Your divine name and pray under Your divine statue.”

“You are the guardian of time, Your power crosses eternity, Your wisdom sees all things clearly.”

“You guide the steps of the cosmos, and Your finger ignites the dance of Your believers’ lives.”

“You reveal the mysterious meaning of time to the perplexed and foolish. Time is so valuable and can’t be replaced, it is a gift to our existence and reminds us of how weak and short our lives are.”

“Dusk and Time God Inching Time, please continue to show me the way as I continue to sail the River of Time. Help me transcend procrastination and waste, help me embrace each moment of life.”

“Under your guidance, I learn to treasure every feeling and pleasure.”

“I hope your radiance illuminates my spirit eternally…”

Da Han’s prayer finished, and he slowly turned around to look at Qian Xing.

He was a middle aged human male, with ordinary features and there was a hint of anxiety on the tip of his brows.

He wore a white gown and had a strong body that looked sturdy like a door.

His hands were very coarse and had many calluses.

At this moment, he was stroking his fingers and looked at Qian Xing with rapt attention, his eyes seeping with tranquility, “My life still has 89 years, 305 days, 23 minutes, and 51 seconds.”

“Time is so precious and isn’t allowed to be squandered.”

“Qian Xing, tell me your purpose in coming immediately.”

“Please don’t say any superfluous words, thank you.”

Chapter 840: Time Prophecy

Qian Xing was expressionless, and he spoke forthright, “I need your Dusk Church’s Prophecy spell.”

“Help me find the whereabouts of someone.”

“He is one of our members named Ba Qi, you should know of him.”

Da Han slowly nodded, “The Heroism Pirates’ commander.”

He looked deeply at Qian Xing, “It seems you too know how useful underlings are sometimes.”

Da Han’s sentence exposed Qian Xing’s disposition.

Qian Xing was a loner and didn’t have underlings. Before, he didn’t think that was a bad thing, but now, his opinion had changed.

The reason for his change was the Pirate Throne.

So long as one carried out pirate behavior, they could obtain the Pirate Throne’s favor and possibly step into godhood.

Such an enormous enticement caused Qian Xing’s heart to beat.

However, on the sacred monument ranking located in the Pirate Holy City, Qian Xing had fallen to tenth.

He analyzed the reason.

It was very simple; he had no subordinates.

Although he was a saint level and could plunder the vast majority of maritime caravans alone, his efficiency was too low.

With underlings helping, not only would his looting efficiency greatly improve, but the entire pirate group’s plunder would be attributed to his merit in looting activity. As the pirate commander, he would have leadership contribution and natural gain merits. This was uniquely advantageous to the Pirate Throne competition.

Why did Qian Xing want to rescue Ba Qi?

The reason was he wanted to subdue Ba Qi and make the Heroism Pirates into his subordinates.

If that happened, then he could make up for his shortcomings in this competition.

That brief remark revealing his thoughts did not emotionally affect Qian Xing, and he serenely replied, “It’s just as you said, I want to subdue Ba Qi.”

Da Han replied, “Based on Ba Qi’s disposition, he is indeed an excellent candidate, and he is also at death’s door.”

Ba Qi had a forthright character and took their promises seriously.

He was a giant with the Bad Weather bloodline and cultivated the Air Pressure battle qi technique.

He was the only person to successfully break out of the Abyssal Tower in the last 500 years. That alone was legendary enough.

After getting free, he faithfully fulfilled his promise to his teacher to actively spread the Air Pressure battle qi technique.

Lan Zao cultivated the Air Pressure battle qi technique, and the reason he was able to change his cultivation so quickly was because of the former.

To Qian Xing, since Ba Qi asked him for help, then in light of Ba Qi’s temperament, so long as Qian Xing decided to ask, Ba Qi had a high chance of becoming his.

Another thing was that even if Ba Qi died in the future, it wouldn’t matter. At least his manpower would truly fall into his hands without any trouble.

This was why Qing Xing was actively trying to rescue Ba Qi.

Qian Xing had been looking, but not a hair of Ba Qi had been found.

He was unresigned and happened to find Da Han’s Icebound Pirates coming over here, so he thought of Da Han’s Dusk Church and their famous Time Prophecy divine spell.

Therefore, he rushed over to ask for help.

Da Hen calmly gazed at Qian Xing, “In that case, what will you offer in recompense?”

Da Han and Qian Xing were both saint levels, and ordinarily their relationship was ambivalent, so it was impossible for them to help each other for free.

Qian Xing had thought long on his offer, and he raised his right index finger, its long and sharp fingernail was exceptionally noticeable, “My bargaining chip is that I will fight at full strength for one battle.”

Da Han’s eyes slightly hung as he thought deeply.

He was interested.

Qian Xing’s reputation was enough for Da Han to believe his sincerity.

Furthermore, both magic and divine contracts could raise the degree of trust.

Da Han saw through Qian Xing’s plan, and Qian Xing similarly saw Da Han’s current situation.

Da Han was heading for the vicinity of Ice Sculpture Island, and surely he must be planning to conduct a massive pirate looting there.

Under these circumstances, Da Han would unavoidably bump into the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s three saint levels.

At this time, another saint level like Qian Xing’s full support would be an extremely important trump card.

Without pondering on it for long, Da Han answered, “I will do one prophecy for you, and you will support me fully in battle once.”

Qian Xing sneered, “How is that possible?”

“You have to achieve an accurate prophecy that allows me to actually find Ba Qi, then I will help you in battle!”

Da Han realized his haggling was quite improper and he candidly nodded, “Fine.”

He really valued time and signed a contract with Qian Xing on the spot.

Then, he ordered his subordinates to guard the prayer room, and with Qian Xing as witness, began his prophecy.

Da Han faced Inching Time’s divine statue and kneeled on the ground.

A basin was laid out in front of Dan Han. There was also a large wooden spoon in the basin.

Regardless of whether it was the basin or the wooden spoon, both emitted an aura of divine power. It was obvious they were the Dusk Church’s sacred tools!

Divine spell – Retracing Time.

Da Han used a divine spell on the basin before him.

The divine spell’s light was not strong and was quickly saturated by the water in the basin, causing the water in the basin to emit a tranquil white light.

Da Han slowly reached out a hand, grabbed the spoon, and softly ladled out a spoonful of water.

Then he slowly raised the wooden spoon 50 centimeters above the basin’s water surface.

At that height, Da Han began to tilt the wooden spoon, causing a dribble of water to drop back into the basin.

The basin’s water surface turned from tranquil to rippling.

Light and shadow also fluctuated, forming an image.

In the image, Lady Sea Snake excitedly kissed Ruan Tang Ren in the mouth and happily shouted.

Next to her was a gold level magic beast sea serpent they had just captured.

Ba Qi and Lady Sea Snake exchanged some words, then parted ways.

When all of the water in the spoon returned to the basin, the divine light image dissipated into nothingness.

Da Han didn’t turn around, rather he remained gazing at the basin, “Ba Qi seemed to have lent Lady Sea Snake help in catching a gold level sea snake, then after succeeding, he left.”

There was no need for him to explain, as Qian Xing had already seen the light and shadow in the basin.

He deeply pursed his brows, “How can this be? Since he left, why hasn’t there been any news on him?”

Qian Xing looked at Da Han and didn’t say anything else.

According to their deal, Da Han had to prophesize Ba Qi’s precise position.

Thus, Da Han used another divine spell.

Divine spell – Time Rehearsal.

Da Han scooped out another spoonful of water, raised it high, and slowly poured it.

The divine light appeared in the basin, and once again light and shadow interweaved into a picture.

And in that light and shadow, Ba Qi was kneeling on the ground subserviently. A gray gowned magician extended their withered palm over Ba Qi’s head.

The gray gowned magician actually used necromancy!

This made Da Han’s and Qian Xing’s pupils contract simultaneously.

Ordinary necromancers wouldn’t stupefy them, but the one who appeared was of great importance.

He was called Teacher Si Ling, one of You Shen’s twelve saints, and a saint level necromancer!

“Teacher Si Ling…why is it him?”

“What is he doing to Ba Qi?”

“The divine light image shows that Ba Qi isn’t being shackled and can freely move. Yet he is allowing Teacher Si Ling to use necromancy on him.”

“He betrayed the Fire Beard Pirates and now relies on the undead orthodoxy?”

Qian Xing and Da Han looked at each other, both sensing the other’s doubts and suspicions.

Da Han exposed his fear, “You Shen’s twelve saints are almost all saint levels nowadays. Of the twelve saints, Teacher Si Ling is ranked second. But on the wanted list all major powers have, he is ranked behind only You Shen.”

Qian Xing guessed, “Because Ba Qi was relying on the undead force, that is the reason why he vanished without a trace?”

“Thinking about it carefully, it does make sense.”

“Ba Qi’s injury was incurable, and at death’s door, it is possible he turned into an undead to survive.”

Da Han suddenly said, “Wrong.”

Qian Xing looked at him, and Da Han explained, “The two prophecies have contradictions.”

“The divine power inside me told me.”

Qian Xing was more puzzled.

Da Han stared at the basin, his eyes emitting daggers, “Interesting, someone is misleading me, interfering and misleading my divination method!”

“It seems Ba Qi really is missing.”

“Interesting, let me check what the truth is!”

Da Han became interested.

At this moment, he exposed his pirate adventure spirit and banditry anger.

The more difficult and challenging something was, the more it raised his fighting spirit!

Divine spell – Time Intertwined.

Under this divine spell’s effect, the basin’s light and shadow fluctuated ceaselessly, showing Ba Qi’s past, present, and future.

Qian Xing stood as a spectator, his look gradually changing.

He also made out that these time images were off. At first, he didn’t feel it was logical. But as more prophecies were made, more gaps appeared in the light and shadow, and even some logical inconsistencies were discovered!

“What is the truth of it? Let me take a good look!” Dan Han urged all of his divine power, resolutely imbuing it into the basin.

His eyes burst with shooting light, reflecting the light in the basin.

A scene from the past gradually reconstructed into the truth, falling before his eyes.

For a few seconds.

Then the light in both the basin and his eyes went out.

The once lit up prayer room immediately became dark and gloomy.

Da Han’s eyes were shut as of this moment.

Two trickles of blood flowed from his eyes and arced down his cheeks.

It was evident that Da Han had paid the price for this divination!

Da Han didn’t open his eyes, his voice became husky, and his aura also had clearly weakened a lot, “Qian Xing, my divination of Ba Qi in exchange for your full support in a single battle is a wholly fair deal.”

Qian Xing faintly asked, “Where is Ba Qi? Who is covering up the truth?”

“An octopusman, it should be a Charming Blue Church bishop. She captured Ba Qi alive along with some others, including several necromancers.”

“You weren’t looking in the wrong place; they are in the White Hair Trench.”

“They seemed to have stopped there!”

Qian Xing was astonished, The Charming Blue Church? Charming Blue Goddess? She was seriously injured and sank into hibernation; the church’s power has withered utterly. Is there really a bishop moving around in the world?

“So, it turned out Ba Qi has fallen into their hands…Charming Blue Goddess’ main authority is deception, and her disciples are experts at that. No wonder I could never find him!”

Da Han slowly stood up, “How fun.”

“This matter has become amusing.”

“Qian Xing, I can help you again, I can help you deal with this Charming Blue bishop.”

Qian Xing blurted out, “You want to hit a person when they are down, you want to plunder a bishop of a church on the edge of annihilation?”

Da Han calmly nodded, “Maybe we can find the Charming Blue Church’s remnant capital.”

“Even if this church is in a terrible situation, don’t underestimate the capital of a divine power!”

“I will help you save Ba Qi, then afterwards the profit will be split eight for me and two for you.”

Qian Xing coldly snorted, “Six, four.”

“Seven, three.”

“Deal.”

Chapter 841: Earth Elemental Army

Magnet Archipelago.

Tu Ji hid his figure and looked at a distant cave on a mountain top.

Next to him was the imperial secret spy that presided over the Magnet Archipelago.

“Lord Tu Ji, Hong Zhu must be inside that cave.” The imperial secret spy’s expression and tone were both very certain.

This time, he had considered his small life and really did everything he could, regardless of the costs, to scout out this information. He even dipped into his own pocket to subsidize his subordinates and stimulate them into action to a powerful degree.

This explosion of effort indeed had results.

In the end, the imperial secret spy’s cultivated staff and hired personnel had exceptional abilities.

The Imperial Secret Spy Agency quickly found Hong Zhu’s whereabouts.

Tu Ji narrowed his eyes, watching the cave.

To be cautious, he didn’t use an investigation type battle skill. Because he had plenty of experience in the hunt and knew many defensive methods expressly targeted these investigation battle skills and could give out a speedy warning.

Tu Ji also didn’t storm in directly, because he had gotten new information.

From the information, he learned that Hong Zhu was not alone and had two others traveling with her. Their temporary base of operations was that cave.

“Da Dou, a gold level magic chef, hailing from Eruption Island.”

“Sleeping Man, a giant from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Sleeping Man is only his nickname, we don’t know his real name.”

Tu Ji remembered the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Because not long ago, he finished his investigation of Two Eyes Island. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had an impressive showing during the Battle of Two Eyes Island.

“Da Dou had a food eating contest with Ci Hai Shen in Snow Bird Harbor, one he lost.”

“It seemed he took a fancy to Sleeping Man, and after the food eating contest, he immediately kidnapped Sleeping Man.”

“The Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s Alchemy Guild’s Shuang Lian once controlled Honey Snow Tower in pursuit of Sleeping Man. She not only failed, but she also went missing.”

Tu Ji was not afraid of the big guy at all; he was mainly worried about Da Dou.

According to common sense, although Da Dou was a gold level, he was only a magic chef, and his fighting strength was inferior to fighters like Tu Ji.

But Da Dou’s latest battle achievements caused Tu Ji to not discount his fighting strength.

From the information, it seemed that Da Dou caused Shuang Lian’s disappearance.

What did he actually accomplish?

A gold level magician using a magician tower to fight was gone!

It was a little outrageous.

“Da Dou came from Eruption Island, so we should overestimate him more.”

“Furthermore, I have to capture Hong Zhu alive, interrogate her strictly, and obtain the information and clues for the next step.”

Capturing alive and killing were two different things.

Capturing was far more difficult.

Cang Xu and Zi Di at the time used Deceptive Disguise and the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, leading to barely a clue being left behind.

Hong Zhu was the only clue Tu Ji had in his hands.

He didn’t know that Hong Zhu actually knew nothing. She just had bad luck and only wanted to gain a closer relationship with the Wisteria Merchant Alliance by helping their people in battle, so in the future, she could have a convenient way to sell and purchase things.

Even if Tu Ji captured Hong Zhu alive, it would all be in vain and he was destined to gain nothing.

But he was unaware of that.

In fact, he had no other choice. For wasn’t Hong Zhu the only clue he had?

Tu Ji quietly took out an eyeball from his pocket.

The imperial secret spy quickly recognized it, and couldn’t help but stare, “A gold level Spying Truth Eye?”

“That is an expendable item, and when used, it will only last 5 minutes.”

“Is it worth using it here?”

Generally speaking, the Spying Truth Eye was used to survey fortifications and was a strategic device armies used to assault fortified positions.

The cave before them was obviously just a temporary dwelling, and even if it had defenses, it wouldn’t be much.

But Tu Ji used it without the slightest hesitation.

What did this say?

The Blood Light Punishment Institution’s supplies were too grand and unreasonable.

Tu Ji activated the Spying Truth Eye.

The Truth Eye floated into the air, roughly 2 meters above the ground, then pointed at the cave, constantly spying on it.

The entire process did not have the slightest amount of aura, sound, or scent leaking out. If it wasn’t observed by the naked eye, it could not be detected.

Tu Ji and the Truth Eye shared a field of view, and quickly, they detected that inside the cave, there was a lot of flame divine scripts arranged inside the cave.

Then, he saw the big guy and Da Dou.

“No sign of Hong Zhu.” Tu Ji’s face sank, and he glared at the imperial secret spy.

But the imperial secret spy wasn’t panicked, instead he pointed his finger at the foot of the mountain, “Lord Tu Ji, isn’t Hong Zhu over there?”

The Spying Truth Eye had a systemic problem, its narrow field of view. After Tu Ji used it, he was affected by it.

Tu Ji turned his line of sight, following the secret spy’s finger, and sure enough, he saw Hong Zhu.

She was running, carrying a large bag of materials and was halfway up the mountain.

“That’s right, it’s her!” Tu Ji’s eyes flashed sharply.

The imperial secret spy hurriedly asked, “Then, my Lord, can I leave now?”

He didn’t want to get involved with a gold level battle.

As for the information, he naturally had subordinates to collect it.

At that moment, Tu Ji’s body actually quivered slightly, and shortly after, he had a weird expression.

“That’s not urgent.” He said.

“No, it’s very urgent.” The imperial secret spy almost blurted out.

“I remember you said before there were warring clans of earth elementals on Magnet Archipelago?” Tu Ji suddenly asked another question.

Although the imperial secret spy was confused, he still nodded, “Yes.”

Then, he proudly stated, “We actually had a crucial impact on the situation here.”

“In order to prevent the unification of the Magnet Archipelago, whenever a strong figure appears in an earth elemental tribe, we will plot against them, causing their death or crippling.”

“In addition to that, we also fan the flames everywhere and can manufacture conflicts that cause deep blood feuds between the clans.”

“It could be said that the flames of war are incessant on Magnet Archipelago, and we are the main reason for that.”

As for why they are doing this instead of just unifying Magnet Archipelago?

On the one hand, the Imperial Secret Spy Agency was an intelligence agency, and were not a military organization. Their strength and authority did not allow it.

On the other hand, by doing that, it could cause serious diplomatic conflicts. This was not Sheng Ming Empire water, instead it was near the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s vicinity.

As for the Imperial Secret Spies secretly controlling and helping an earth elemental clan unify the island?

That was also out of the question.

Using too much force would leave behind obvious traces and wouldn’t be conducive to remaining concealed.

The imperial secret spies here also had an important assignment, that was transporting supplies to Feng Awakening Island.

IF Magnet Archipelago was unified, there wouldn’t be such a prosperous sight at the port.

Every day, there are large number of outsiders coming in, going on adventures and treasure hunts.

In an environment with many eyes mixed in, transporting army provisions, armaments, and other military supplies was easy to carry out.

These reasons combined, this was the best plan Secret Spy Agency had.

They paid the smallest price to maintain the status quo and constantly performed their duties.

Seeing the secret spy’s faint pride, Tu Ji slowly said, “If that is so, where did that earth elemental army I just detected come from?”

The imperial secret spy blankly stared, feeling he misheard something, “What?”

Tu Ji continued, “More than a dozen gold levels, several thousand earth elementals, eight hundred magnetic earth elementals.”

“I even saw two earth elemental battle squads with a hundred members each.”

“It is right underneath our feet.”

The imperial secret spy replied, “What? My Lord, are…are you playing a joke?”

“They have already set up a divine script formation; it just isn’t activated yet.” Tu JI’s brows creased, telling the imperial secret spy the new situation he just scouted out.

The imperial secret spy laughed and waved his hand, confidently stating, “That is impossible!”

Tu Ji was expressionless, doubtfully gazing at the imperial secret spy.

“Is this the quality of an imperial secret spy?”

Inside the cave.

The big guy opened his mouth and said to Da Dou, “Hungry, want to eat.”

“That meal.”

“Golden.”

“More people, fragrant meal!”

Despite the big guy’s inarticulate speech, Da Dou still heard him clearly.

Da Dou confirmed, “Master, you want to eat the Fluttering Boat Fried Rice? It was the food you ate in the Snow Bird Dueling Arena right?”

The big guy nodded repeatedly, and the corner of his mouth even drooled with disappointment.

“Good, I shall make it for you at once.” Da Dou smiled.

He took out an iron wok and poured out a large amount of cooked rice into it.

He took coconut oil, broke an egg, and mixed both into the iron wok.

The egg quickly solidified, and an iron ladle mashed it, creating small lumps or kernels of yellow, white, and green.

The rice was rapidly sautéed golden.

Da Dou shook the iron wok. Each time, the mixed three colored rice rose, then fell like a beautiful golden sand shower.

The strong aroma quickly overflowed into the cave.

The big guy breathed heavily, staring at the fried rice, his eyes looking at nothing else.

When Da Dou saw the big guy’s infatuated look, the corner of his mouth smiled strongly, “It seems you hope to eat my fried rice. Ah ha ha, I figured you had good taste!”

“This special recipe was invented and innovated by my teacher.”

“Teacher…”

Thinking of his teacher, Da Dou’s mood became worse.

He stared out into space, a memory flashing through his mind.

On his death bed, his teacher said to Da Dou, “Show me again, make that food.”

In front of his teacher, Da Dou successfully made the Fluttering Boat Fried Rice.

His teacher was very grateful, “Da Dou, you are worthy of being my chosen successor. Ha ha ha, I didn’t expect that you would succeed in just a half month of practice.”

“In my old age, I founded a new path for magic chefs, but I am powerless to walk down it.”

“Teacher!” Da Dou kneeled at the bedside, pledging, “I will follow this path and bring it to great heights!”

His teacher smiled, “You know who inspired me? Go to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, there perhaps you can find more opportunities.”

“It is about time for the Ice Sculpture Kingdom to hold its National Ceremony, and you could have received the Ice Sculpture Royal Family’s invitation letter, isn’t that right?”

Da Dou thought of that and couldn’t help but sigh to himself.

Nowadays, he could not return to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

To some level, he failed to live up to his teacher’s expectations.

However, Da Dou did not feel a bit of regret.

He looked at the foolish big guy, thinking, “This is a saint level seed!”

Chapter 842: Encircling the Big Guy

“If I had to choose again, I'd still make the decision to kidnap him.”

“If I could bring him back to Eruption Island, then everything I sacrificed would be worth it!”

“Too bad he caught the gods' attention. To be chosen by the divine, how immense must that favor be?!”

Da Dou still vividly recalled the scenes from the earth elemental tribe's stronghold, where the big guy had unleashed his formidable power.

Playing dumb to catch the wise?

Da Dou had once entertained that theory.

But he soon dismissed the notion.

He had confirmed the big guy wasn't the sharpest tool in the shed, dull and simple-minded. Yet his background was undeniably complex!

“This little guy has been lurking in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Ever since I snatched him away, he's been desperate to return.”

“Perhaps the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps isn't so simple either!”

Da Dou's thoughts raced. Because of Xiao Guai, he now regarded the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps with greater respect.

"What's that smell? So fragrant!" Hong Zhu, carrying a large bundle, entered the cave.

She reported to Da Dou the results of her outing: “Lord Da Dou, I've gathered the dwarf grass and sweet potatoes you requested.”

As she spoke, she opened the bundle.

While stir-frying rice, Da Dou glanced over and nodded approvingly. “Excellent. Most are silver level, with a few even reaching gold level.”

“Didn’t those earth elementals cause you trouble?”

Da Dou was somewhat surprised, not expecting Hong Zhu to exceed his assigned task.

Hong Zhu replied, “Maybe I just got lucky this time. I didn't encounter any decent earth elementals along the way.”

Da Dou continued, " Don't go out anymore."

“Intelligence indicates members of the Blood Light Punishment Institution have arrived nearby.”

“What?” Hong Zhu startled, then nodded quickly in agreement.

She had once fought side by side with Cang Xu on Two Eyes Island. This was a huge suspicion that had become a thorn in Hong Zhu's side. Back then, she had fled Two Eyes Island precisely because she feared she couldn't explain herself clearly.

Hong Zhu still retained her freedom.

Unlike Da Dou, she hadn't become the big guy's slave.

Yet after that battle, she hadn't severed ties with the big guy or Da Dou, she chose to remain attached to him.

“Compared to Da Dou, Lord Sleeping Man is the real powerhouse.”

“I'm a treasure hunter. Isn't he the greatest treasure I've ever encountered?”

The big guy in his Divine Descent state had left an indelible impression on Hong Zhu, sparking countless wonderful fantasies within her.

“My bloodline is what it is, my lifetime rank cap is silver level.”

“Lord Sleeping Man has earned the favor of the gods. If I can rely on him, as he keeps advancing, my life will improve alongside his!”

This was the world of the strong.

Facing the powerful, the weak develop a dependency, a natural survival strategy.

Da Dou finished stir-frying the rice and cast the battle skill [Food Compression], downgrading the gold level fluttering boat fried rice to silver level.

“Eat up. This is what suits you best.” Da Dou scooped the entire pot of fried rice into a giant bowl.

The big guy lifted the enormous bowl and buried his head in it, devouring the meal with relish.

Seeing this, Hong Zhu swallowed hard, filled with envy.

She knew her place: “This isn't something I'm worthy of enjoying.”

“A gold level magic dish compressed down to silver level, what extravagance.”

Hong Zhu had only heard of such things before, never witnessed them.

Lately, however, she'd seen it happen many times with her own eyes.

Da Dou did not disappoint the fishman youngster's expectations, diligently feeding the big guy lavishly at every meal.

He put away the iron wok and began carefully inspecting the ingredients Hong Zhu had brought.

“Not bad. These are all very fresh.”

“For the next meal, I can prepare a uniquely flavored dinner for Master.”

The words barely left his mouth when his expression changed drastically.

“Enemy attack!” he bellowed.

In an instant, he whipped his head around, spun on his heel, and dragged the big guy away.

“Food!” The big guy hadn't finished eating before Da Dou hauled him off.

He hadn't managed to grab the giant bowl. Seeing some fried rice left at the bottom, he instinctively reached out to scoop it up.

He grabbed nothing but air.

The big guy stretched his arms out, watching the fried rice fall to the ground with profound reluctance.

Almost the very next second.

Boom!

A deafening explosion shook the earth.

Dozens of massive conical spikes erupted from the ground, cave walls, and ceiling, violently piercing the open space. In an instant, they nearly blocked off an entire section of the cave.

Had Da Dou been a second later, she would have faced this terrifying assault.

Hong Zhu was utterly horrified.

When Da Dou turned to flee, she followed without hesitation.

Now standing beside him, she trembled with lingering fear. One second later, and she too would have been crushed to dust by those spikes.

“Who in the world is attacking us?!” Hong Zhu was utterly bewildered.

The next moment, the aura of earth elementals surged forth in succession.

One, two, three... over a dozen gold level auras surrounded the cave.

Immediately after, silver level and bronze level earth elementals ceased hiding, surging from the ground to encircle the small mountain where Da Dou stood, leaving no escape.

Hong Zhu's face turned deathly pale.

“I was just picking some wild greens and sweet potatoes,” she murmured in disbelief. “Surely this isn't necessary?”

Da Dou's face turned ashen. “Could it be that the earth elemental tribe we defeated was special?”

The earth elemental army offered no answers, instead roaring as they charged from all directions.

Leading the assault were over a dozen gold level earth elementals.

“We're done for!” Hong Zhu plunged into utter despair at that moment.

“There's still a chance!” Da Dou growled, slamming his fist against his chest.

In the next instant, his fire-patterned tattoos surged, erupting into a blazing inferno.

The flames spread rapidly.

Divine script shimmered within the fire, coalescing into a brilliant, spherical crimson light barrier.

Da Dou gritted his teeth: “I never imagined the divine script formation I painstakingly set up here for safety would actually activate!”

Though lacking combat experience, Da Dou possessed one strength: he excelled at learning from failure.

After being defeated by Shuang Lian and nearly captured, he decisively changed his approach. He now used the Tunnel Staff whenever necessary, no longer hesitating as before.

Last time, while tending to the unconscious the big guy, he failed to detect the underground Earth Elemental Chieftain's ambush. The big guy was snatched away, forcing him to pay a devastating price afterward and nearly costing him his own life.

Thus, upon returning to the cave, he learned from his mistake. He expended considerable effort, arranging numerous fire divine scripts across this small hill.

It was precisely this tactic that allowed Da Dou to detect the enemy's surprise attack, thereby saving the big guy's life.

Now, Da Dou had activated all the divine scripts he had laid out, forming a spherical wall of fire.

The gold level earth elemental bodies charged toward the wall of fire but halted in their tracks.

They sensed a powerful threat emanating from this layer of flames.

These earth elementals were either clan chieftains or the foremost warriors within their tribes. As gold level beings, they naturally held significant status.

It was only through receiving the oracle of the Main Earth Elemental God that they had endured their differences, formed a coalition, and mobilized nearly all their forces to hunt down the big guy.

The hearts of gold level earth elementals were never tightly knit. They hesitated before the fire curtain.

Whoever broke through the fire curtain first would pay a heavy price.

This would undoubtedly forfeit the credit for slaying the big guy.

Without question, whoever personally killed the big guy would be the greatest hero of this operation, most likely to earn the favor of the Main Earth Elemental God!

The earth elementals wavered, while Da Dou raced against time.

He knew his fire barrier could withstand at most one or two opponents of the same level. Moreover, it required constant infusion of divine power to sustain.

With over a dozen gold level foes now present, successful defense was utterly impossible.

"We must leave this place! Everyone, get into my bag!" he shouted to Hong Zhu and the big guy.

Hong Zhu's eyes lit up. Recognizing this as the best strategy, she hurried toward Da Dou.

But Da Dou first grabbed the big guy by the neck and opened the packing bag at his waist.

The big guy crouched on the ground, hands clutching his head, trembling violently.

Terrified by the earth elemental army, he made no move to resist Da Dou.

But Da Dou failed.

He couldn't put the big guy in.

“Space is locked; I can't even open the bag!” Da Dou's face darkened.

Hong Zhu saw it and blurted out, "This must be some kind of spatial anchor spell effect. What do we do now?"

She was utterly tense, pinning her last hope on the two men before her.

Da Dou took a deep breath, half-kneeling on the ground. He quickly murmured a prayer before casting the Flame Flicker divine spell.

This spell allowed him to traverse space, crossing a certain distance.

With the aid of the Flame Totem on his body, though it was difficult, Da Dou succeeded.

He clutched Hong Zhu in one hand while gripping the big guy's throat with the other, expending several times his divine power to transform all three into flames.

“One, two, three, strike!” The gold level earth elementals coordinated their attack, shattering the wall of fire in an instant.

But all they saw was an empty clearing.

Outside the cave, the earth elemental army descended into chaos.

An earth elemental shouted, “They're here!”

The gold level earth elementals spun around and charged out of the cave.

On another mountain peak.

The imperial spy's mouth hung open in disbelief as he watched Da Dou lead the two others, weaving through the earth elemental army.

“Now, do you still think I was joking?” Tu Ji crossed his arms, observing coldly.

The imperial spy slowly shook his head, as if suddenly awakening, finally closing his gaping mouth.

Clasping his forehead, he cried out, "How is this possible? These earth elemental tribes have actually united to form a coalition force?"

“Why are they hunting down Hong Zhu and the others?”

This was precisely the question Tu Ji wanted to ask.

But now was clearly not the time for questions.

With the successive involvement of gold level earth elementals, Hong Zhu and her companions were already in grave peril.

“Hong Zhu... she can't be killed like this,” Tu Ji lamented inwardly.

She was his only lead at present.

Chapter 843: God's Gaze

"And the little giant from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps might hold clues too. Why did Hong Zhu team up with him? Back on Two Eyes Island, Hong Zhu had collaborated with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps."

"Lord Tu Ji, we must leave immediately. If we don't leave now, the earth elementals will spot us, and that won't end well!" the Imperial Spy Chief urged frantically.

Tu Ji, however, sighed deeply and shook his head slowly. "You go create chaos on the other side and escape while you can. I'm going to rescue them!"

"What?!" The spy's jaw dropped in shock once more

He knew Tu Ji from the Blood Light Punishment Institution was no ordinary gold level, but no matter how powerful he was, he was still just one man. There was no way he could stand against this entire earth elemental army!

Tu Ji clearly had the chance to escape, to slip away unnoticed by the earth elementals, yet he chose not to.

"What a lunatic! I'm not risking my life like this!" The imperial spy screamed inwardly, while outwardly hastily obeying orders. He bowed low and swiftly darted off toward the southeast.

Tu Ji's gaze remained fixed on Da Dou, Hong Zhu, and the others.

"Never imagined this operation would demand such a desperate gamble. Sigh!"

Tu Ji pulled out three vials of potion and downed them one after another.

Each vial contained a gold level potion.

The frenzied effects struck instantly after swallowing, forcing Tu Ji to half-kneel on the ground as he spat out a mouthful of blood.

He instinctively covered his mouth with his hand, coughing violently several times before slowly rising to his feet.

His height had increased by at least a meter compared to before taking the potions. His entire physique swelled, muscles bulging with veins snaking across his skin. Bloodshot eyes blazed with terrifying intensity.

His Blood Light Punishment Institution combat uniform stretched to accommodate his new form, remaining perfectly fitted.

This classic potion combination instantly doubled Tu Ji's combat prowess.

But that wasn't enough.

Battle skill - Lifespan Blood Sacrifice!

He clapped his palms together, grunted, and unleashed a torrent of battle qi, radiating a bizarre, blood-red halo.

The swirling light enveloped his entire body.

Ten years of his lifespan were sacrificed in this moment, granting him another explosive surge in combat power.

It doubled once more from the previous level.

This brought his combat strength to four times his base state!

Empowered to this extent, Tu Ji stomped his foot, launching himself like a cannonball.

Boom!

In the next instant, he slammed into the earth elemental army.

Caught off guard, the surrounding earth elementals were engulfed by Tu Ji's follow-up assault, meeting their end instantly.

“They have reinforcements!”

“Divide our forces and take out their support!”

“Wait, this guy's uniform looks kind of...”

"Who cares about that? This is the will of the great Main God! Anyone who dares to intercept us is an enemy of the god! Enemy of the god!"

The earth elementals' morale soared to its peak. Fueled by their faith, they feared death not at all.

Though some shouted to eliminate Tu Ji, the reinforcement, not a single one of the dozen or so gold level earth elementals moved. Their focus remained firmly fixed on the big guy.

Taking out reinforcements versus killing the big guy, the difference in merit is stark.

Da Dou fled with all his might.

His Flame Flicker divine spell proved especially potent amidst the army.

No matter how the earth elementals closed in, they couldn't catch him.

But carrying the big guy and Hong Zhu drained his divine power rapidly.

The three of them noticed Tu Ji's intervention.

"It's... it's someone from the Blood Light Punishment Institution! He... he's here to capture me!" Hong Zhu stammered in terror.

“Isn't that great?” Da Dou's eyes lit up. “Clearly, he's here to save your life.”

Da Dou strained to move closer to Tu Ji.

Gold level earth elementals closed in, surrounding and intercepting him.

The longer their divine script formation operated, the more effectively it solidified the space. Da Dou's Flame Flicker divine spell not only grew shorter in range, but also left behind crimson lines in the air, marking his teleportation points.

This made it even easier for the earth elementals to block his path.

For a moment, Da Dou's attempt to draw closer to Tu Ji seemed like he was walking straight into a trap.

Seeing this, Tu Ji's eye twitched.

He unleashed a flurry of punches and kicks, sending the surrounding earth elementals flying.

Struck by his blows, they fell to the ground, wailing in agony. Soon, cracks spread across their bodies, revealing crimson blood-light seeping through the fissures.

Instantly, the earth elemental bodies shattered, collapsing into piles of earth and blood-soaked corpses.

“It's the Flesh Curse!” A knowledgeable earth elemental elder shouted.

"He really is a member of the Blood Light Punishment Institution!!" Some gold level earth elementals felt a chill run down their spines, growing even more reluctant to confront Tu Ji.

This curse was originally designed to target undead bodies, but it proved remarkably effective against elementals too!

Empowered by the Flesh Curse, even Tu Ji's bare-handed strikes could transform earth elementals into flesh-and-blood forms under its influence.

The elemental bodies' combat advantage vanished completely!

Yet the earth elementals fought with reckless abandon, their sheer numbers overwhelming. After slaughtering hundreds for over two minutes, Tu Ji remained surrounded.

“Hmph!” Tu Ji snorted furiously, drawing a deep breath.

Battle skill - Blood Feast of the Spirits!

His battle qi transformed once more into a crimson glow. The blood light coalesced into arrows, shooting forth by the hundreds in an instant.

Earth elementals struck by the blood arrows let out agonized howls, descending into madness. Some lashed out wildly, others rolled about clutching their heads, while some simply collapsed.

“This is a battle skill specifically targeting souls.”

“Their souls are being annihilated!”

“We must eliminate this terrifying creature! He can't be allowed to attack unchecked any longer!”

The attackers surrounding Tu Ji were, at most, silver level.

Few survived this battle skill.

This battle skill specifically targeted undead lifeforms. Whether zombies, skeletons, or ghosts, they still possessed souls. Now unleashed upon the earth elementals, its effect remained devastating.

Four or five hundred blood arrows swept through, completely clearing the enemies surrounding Tu Ji.

The earth elemental army, though vast with thousands of troops, could not endure such attrition.

If this battle skill were used a few more times, the earth elemental forces would suffer nearly half their numbers.

Even with high morale, the earth elemental army could not withstand such casualties.

30%40%

Under normal circumstances, most armies collapse in spirit and lose all will to fight when losses approach 30%. Earth elementals were dull and stubborn; their armies typically maintained higher morale, reaching up to 40%. But even that was insufficient. After all, they were sentient beings.

The greater their intelligence, the more they pondered, and the deeper their fear of death.

“We can't leave this guy unchecked any longer!” The Blood Feast of the Spirits had forged this consensus among all the earth elementals.

But how?

Not a single one of the dozen or so gold level earth elementals turned to fight Tu Ji.

They all sensed the terror emanating from his aura.

Seeing that still no gold level earth elemental came for him, unable to divert the pressure on Da Dou, Tu Ji couldn't help but laugh bitterly.

“I'll see just how long you can hold out!” Tu Ji prepared to unleash the Blood Feast of the Spirits battle skill once more.

But in the next instant, divine light descended.

The earth elementals were all imbued with defensive divine spells, greatly enhancing their resistance.

Simultaneously, half of the gold level earth elementals turned on their heels and charged toward Tu Ji.

Tu Ji, Da Dou, and the others paled visibly.

“Such colossal divine power!”

“Could it be that a deity is watching over this place?”

Indeed.

Just as the fishman youngster had once focused on the big guy, the Main Earth Elemental God had long since cast a gaze upon them.

Under His command, the gold level earth elementals split into two groups, abandoning their previous foolish tactical choice. One group continued to surround and attack the big guy, while the other engaged Tu Ji in battle.

A look of shock and disbelief washed over Tu Ji's face!

The significance of a god's attention was immense.

At that moment, Tu Ji felt an overwhelming urge to retreat.

“An earth elemental god is watching this battle!” Da Dou instantly turned to the big guy.

In that instant, he was utterly certain: the earth elementals' massive mobilization had only one target, the big guy!

Hong Zhu's eyes widened as she grasped the same truth: "Lord Sleeping Man has been divinely possessed before. Could it be... two deities are now vying for control over him?"

At this thought, Hong Zhu couldn't help but feel a surge of anticipation.

She had witnessed the big guy's divine descent before.

“At such a critical juncture, only another Divine Descent upon Lord Sleeping Man can free us from this predicament!”

Meanwhile, the fishman youngster, the focus of Hong Zhu and Da Dou's intense hopes, was currently studying the memory crystal in his hands.

Since the last teleportation blood sacrifice, after thorough communication with Cang Xu, he had used the time digesting the divine blessing to actively forge memories.

The fishman youngster was already a magician and had performed blasphemous prayers multiple times, making him quite adept at crafting false memories for personal use. This expertise now yielded significant results when crafting memories for others.

Despite smooth progress, the fishman youngster furrowed his brow.

“What's going on?”

“Why am I so unsettled? As if something terrible is happening, or about to happen?”

“Am I worrying too much about Zi Di, Zong Ge, and the others?”

At the same time.

In the White Hair Trench, not far from the Ocean Mother Nest.

Da Han and Qian Xing stealthily approached, their presence concealed.

“A large number of sea beasts have been hunted. The trails converge in this area.”

"We've found the right location. Look at that, and this... There are numerous signs of intelligent activity. It won't be long before we locate the Charming Blue Church's main base."

"Hehehe, even if it's not their main base, it's at least a crucial stronghold. I'm looking forward to the treasures inside!"

Da Han and Qian Xing tracked their way forward, exchanging whispers while channeling their battle qi.

Suddenly, a blinding white light erupted from Da Han's body, while Qian Xing's pendant emitted a sharp, piercing ring.

Both halted instantly.

Da Han thrust both palms forward, striking toward the figure he perceived as “Qian Xing.” Qian Xing, however, struck at a nearby boulder, seemingly oblivious to Da Han's attack.

Chapter 844: Father Save Me

The next moment, the water surged violently, and the light and shadow dissipated.

The scene before Da Han and Qian Xing's eyes changed dramatically. The true form was revealed.

Battle skill and divine spell collided, creating a torrential current.

Da Han and Qian Xing each stumbled back half a step, while Zhou Zhang retreated a full step.

“Heh heh heh, indeed the divine magic of the Charming Blue Goddess!” Da Han sneered coldly.

They had obtained crucial intelligence before the battle and came prepared. However, the divine magic imbued upon them and the equipment they wore were only sufficient for the two of them.

To prevent the Charming Blue Church's deceptive divine magic from inflicting heavy casualties among his forces, Da Han hadn't even brought a single ship!

Zhou Zhang's face was as still as water.

Her Opportunistic Deception had been countered. Against opponents of the same level, it clearly no longer held the same potency. Especially this time, as she had simultaneously deceived two saint levels.

Zhou Zhang stared at Qian Xing and Da Han, declaring: "Human saints, the deep sea is not your territory. We don't welcome you here!"

“Leave this place, or you shall pay a price beyond your imagination.”

Zhou Zhang knew their intentions were hostile. Since they had prepared targeted defenses, they were likely here specifically for her. Still, she made one final attempt.

Da Han's expression remained calm, his tone detached: “This is the Charming Blue Church. I have come precisely for you.”

“I have a proposal to save us both time, hand over all your possessions immediately, and I'll turn tail and leave.”

Qian Xing added from the side, “Oh and Ba Qi. He's a member of our Fire Beard Pirates. Hand him over too.”

“Heh heh heh.” Zhou Zhang laughed in anger, about to speak.

Da Han raised his hand. “Enough talk. I understand.”

“Let's save time. Cut the nonsense.”

“Fight!”

Zhou Zhang's face darkened as she slowly retreated, water ripples swirling around her before fading away.

Da Han and Qian Xing exchanged a glance, then charged forward regardless of Zhou Zhang's location.

They split left and right, a tactical maneuver!

Zhou Zhang's heart sank.

Alone, she could only intercept one of them.

Their tactic clearly aimed to exploit numerical superiority.

Zhou Zhang gritted her teeth, hesitated briefly, then chose to intercept Da Han.

After all, Da Han was a sorcerer, a spellcasting combat class. In terms of slaughter efficiency alone, he surpassed a fighter like Qian Xing.

As for Qian Xing...

“We're going in!” The remaining gold level individuals, led by Zhang Yu Wan and Jiu Hua Bi, charged forward with gritted teeth.

Boom! Boom! Boom!

Zhou Zhang clashed with Da Han. divine spells and magic spells collided, instantly igniting a series of violent explosions.

The blast sent surrounding currents surging wildly, terrifying sonic waves echoing through the ocean trench.

Meanwhile, Zhang Yu Wan, Jiu Hua Bi, and the others launched a formidable assault on Qian Xing.

“I'll play along for now,” Qian Xing remarked with a mocking smile. His form darted like lightning, moving with blinding speed as he weaved freely among the gold level foes.

As the two sides fought, the ripples of the battlefield soon spread and implicated the White Hair Trench.

Thunderous explosions could not be concealed, and from time to time, underwater mountains and seabed shook.

“What's happening?” Cang Xu, mid-ceremony guiding a fishman to faith, halted abruptly, his face etched with shock and disbelief.

The surrounding Charming Blue believers were already in utter chaos.

Every single one was stunned and terrified.

Though they outnumbered their foes, the attackers were saint level!

Before such powerhouses, they were nothing but lambs awaiting slaughter.

No reconnaissance was needed, the aura of a saint level being was so conspicuous that nearly every transcendent could sense it.

“Where is Lord Zhou Zhang?”

“That distinct aura of the saint level, it's clearly Lord Zhou Zhang!”

“And Jiu Hua Bi, Zhang Yu Wan, and the rest! All the gold level experts have gone into battle!”

Cries erupted from the crowd, venting their panic.

A heavy weight pressed down on Cang Xu's heart, especially as he realized one of the battle groups was drawing ever closer.

“Why would two saint level attackers suddenly appear?”

“Could this be why Charming Blue Goddess deliberately cultivated the Commander?”

“No, it makes no sense to provoke two saint level enemies simultaneously.”

Cang Xu gritted his teeth, silencing the relentless questions in his mind, and immediately began secretly contacting the fishman youngster.

The fishman youngster did not respond.

Cang Xu could only wait anxiously.

Their communication tool wasn't real-time messaging; it was more like writing letters or leaving messages.

“Hurry up and see this! Respond to me, Commander!”

“The Ocean Mother Nest is no longer safe.”

Yet the fishman youngster hadn't received Cang Xu's message. He was currently concentrating intensely, channeling divine magic.

Divine spell - Believer Perception!

The fishman youngster's heart-pounding dread grew ever more intense, even interfering with his current tasks.

Confused and alarmed, he could only begin checking everywhere.

First, he inspected the Deep Sea Monster Fish and questioned the tower spirit, finding nothing unusual aboard the ship or within the Ocean Mother Nest.

He then observed the surroundings outside the Ocean Mother Nest. As the battle had not yet begun, everything appeared normal.

The fishman youngster then attempted to use Believer Perception to check on the others.

Lan Zao was thriving in the Dueling Divine Domain, having earned trust.

Dao Hen was carrying out surveillance duties aboard Tan Mo's Treasure Chest, undetected.

But the big guy...

“Oh no! What's happening to Xiao Guai?”

The fishman youngster realized Xiao Guai was in grave peril. Carried by Da Dou, they were desperately weaving through the earth elemental army, yet every attempt to break through failed.

Da Dou's vitality was fading, his body drenched in blood.

Hong Zhu had also sustained significant injuries.

Only the big guy remained well-protected by Da Dou, flailing his limbs and wailing loudly. The perilous situation had pushed him to the brink of madness.

The fishman youngster was stunned by the danger.

He never imagined that the big guy would be surrounded and attacked by the earth elemental army.

What grudge did they have? What enmity was there?

Even if he destroyed an entire earth elemental tribe, this seemed excessive.

Without hesitation, the fishman youngster cast the divine spell [Divine Power Channel]. The moment the channel opened, the big guy sensed it.

“Fa... Father!”

“Bad guys! So many bad guys!”

The big guy screamed for help in his mind.

“Xiao Guai, don't be afraid.”

“Father sees you. You'll be safe.”

The fishman youngster comforted the big guy while focusing his attention, sifting through his memories.

The big guy's memories didn't provide the answer—why they were being subjected to such treatment by the earth elementals.

But the fishman youngster discovered something: the recent divine intervention by the Main Earth Elemental God.

His heart jolted violently: "The Main Earth Elemental God?

"Why would He pay attention to this place? Why intervene personally?"

"And these earth elementals, they're all shouting about a divine war. Is this a divine war?"

This was an utterly devastating piece of news.

Another piece of devastating news was Tu Ji.

The fishman youngster discovered that this member of the Blood Light Punishment Institution had also appeared and joined the battle.

“This guy should have landed on Feng Awakening Island. Why did he show up so quickly in the Magnet Archipelago?”

“He must be after Hong Zhu!”

The fishman youngster had been warned by Cang Xu earlier. Through the big guy's network, he'd already issued orders for the Crimson Orb to go into hiding.

But Tu Ji's arrival at the Magnet Archipelago was too covert, too swift, Hong Zhu hadn't had time to conceal herself.

“What should I do now?”

The fishman youngster hesitated, feeling utterly stumped.

He could now employ various divine spells, even Divine Descent, to aid the big guy in breaking through the siege.

But doing so would inevitably reveal the most critical information.

Even if Tu Ji from the Blood Light Punishment Institution was deceived and failed to recognize the divine power's origin, once he learned of this, he would surely take the big guy seriously and investigate further in the future.

If he or the Blood Light Punishment Institution discovered it was Charming Blue Goddess, it would spell disaster.

Charming Blue Goddess had been gravely wounded and now lay dormant precisely because the Empire's deities held sway.

“I cannot recklessly use the Charming Blue Goddess's magic. For it cannot see the light of day!”

The fishman youngster had harbored these fears before.

Now, this flaw is laid bare.

Though Charming Blue divine spells proved effective, the fishman youngster dared not wield them freely, fearing discovery.

Truthfully, had only Tu Ji been present, he might have acted.

After all, Tu Ji was merely a gold level being.

But the Main Earth Elemental God clearly watched over this place, a deity of a major pantheon. Such a presence left the fishman youngster with no confidence to take risks.

Undoubtedly, the Main Earth Elemental God's power far surpassed that of the Charming Blue Goddess.

“Why is He watching this place?”

“Could it be that I angered Him by deceiving Him and destroying His altar, prompting this retaliation?”

“Or perhaps the Earth Elemental Chieftain I killed was His bloodline descendant?”

“Is he waiting for me to make my move, then follow the trail?”

On the battlefield, Tu Ji charged relentlessly forward.

But he couldn't break through.

His situation now was different from before.

He was under siege by nearly ten gold level opponents!

Several gold level magnetic earth elementals continuously cast magnetizing racial magic, severely restricting Tu Ji's movements.

What Tu Ji truly feared, however, was the stone-tipped mace wielded by one of the earth elementals.

It was a petrifying weapon!

Capable of transforming the flesh of struck enemies into stone.

Time and again, this gold level weapon had intercepted Tu Ji, thwarting his attempts to reach Hong Zhu.

This time was no different.

Just as Tu Ji was about to break through the encirclement, the stone mace swept across.

If he dodged, he would lose his chance to strike and fall back into the trap.

“Screw it!”

A fierce glint flashed in Tu Ji's eyes as he swung his left arm to block.

The next instant, his left arm was shattered clean off by the mace. Not only that, but its surface rapidly changed, transforming into stone.

But Tu Ji had anticipated this, erupting his battle qi directly at the edge of his shoulder.

Bam!

Blood spurted everywhere.

Tu Ji used his battle qi to sever his own arm!

Battle skill - Blood Light Shockwave.

The next instant, the severed arm, not yet fully petrified, erupted in a violent explosion.

Blood spattered everywhere, striking the gold level earth elementals. Every one of them screamed in agony, tumbling to the ground.

At the cost of his left arm, Tu Ji finally broke through, carving a bloody path through the earth elemental army to reach Da Dou and the others.

“Follow me!” Tu Ji bellowed fiercely.

Chapter 845: Five Pointed Star Annihilation Ray

Facing the one-armed blood-soaked figure before her, Hong Zhu felt anything but joy.

She and Tu Ji had fought on the same battlefield, witnessing everything clearly.

Tu Ji could have escaped alone, yet to protect her as a lead, he had sacrificed himself to such an extreme degree!

“No wonder they say the Blood Light Punishment Institution is full of madmen!” Hong Zhu felt this truth acutely at that moment.

What troubled her more was that her relationship with the necromancer had been shallow and simple. If Tu Ji discovered the truth, would he fly into a rage, driven by fury and hatred, and kill Hong Zhu to vent his anger?

Hong Zhu was clearly surrounded by enemies, her life hanging by a thread.

Yet her fear of Tu Ji was even greater.

At this moment, she desperately wanted to shout: “Lord from the Blood Light Punishment Institution, I am innocent! Do not place excessive expectations upon me!”

Compared to Hong Zhu's complex emotions, Da Dou's response was far simpler.

Tu Ji bellowed, “Follow me!”

Da Dou immediately replied, “Understood.”

His answer was crisp and decisive.

He swept his arm, scattering seeds.

Then he used his battle skill again, causing the seeds to grow rapidly.

In an instant, countless green vines swelled like giant serpents, spreading swiftly and engulfing a corner of the battlefield.

The earth elementals that had been closing in were all repelled by these vines.

With this advantage, Da Dou calmly followed Tu Ji's footsteps, carrying both Hong Zhu and the big guy.

Tu Ji's eyes lit up upon witnessing Da Dou's technique.

Da Dou was capable of such feats too.

He couldn't overuse this move; it drained his battle qi too heavily.

But its combat effectiveness was undeniably formidable.

Da Dou employed it both to repel the earth elementals and to demonstrate to the frenzied, ferocious Tu Ji that he wasn't someone to be trifled with!

The two thus joined forces, cooperating to break through the encirclement.

Even the re-gathering gold level earth elementals struggled to withstand them.

Several factors contributed to this:

First, Da Dou and Tu Ji worked in perfect harmony, fully committed to each other. The earth elementals, however, were preoccupied with vying for the first kill, their minds scattered and their actions constrained by mutual rivalry.

Second, their partnership freed up both Da Dou and Tu Ji to unleash their full combat potential.

Previously, they had to juggle defense and rescue, leaving little energy for offense. Da Dou especially focused on escape, only occasionally counterattacking.

Now, with a simple division of offense and defense, they could truly harness their offensive capabilities.

One was a giant from Eruption Island, possessing exceptional physical prowess. The other is a member of the Blood Light Punishment Institution, mastering formidable divine spells and battle skills. Both surpass ordinary gold level combatants in strength.

Moreover, the most troublesome earth elemental has been temporarily neutralized by Tu Ji's battle skill.

Without that pesky petrification weapon, Da Dou and Tu Ji faced fewer constraints, moving with greater freedom.

Their coordinated slaughter carved a bloody path through the enemy lines.

They were on the verge of breaking through the earth elemental army and reaching the edge of the divine script formation.

At that moment, divine light surged skyward once more, vast and majestic, enveloping the entire battlefield.

The next instant, the earth convulsed. Towering walls rose from the ground, forming a thirty-meter-high barrier within seconds.

The surface of the walls rippled with intense divine light.

“A saint level earth-element divine spell!” Da Dou's pupils contracted sharply.

Hong Zhu's face was etched with despair.

They were just one step away from truly escaping.

Yet, in the end, they were still trapped within the walls.

Tu Ji refused to accept defeat, his eyes blazing with fury. He roared, unleashing a crimson battle skill that slammed heavily against the towering wall before him.

The divine light rippled across the wall's surface, leaving it unscathed.

The wall stood like a mountain, sealing off the battlefield, immovable.

Da Dou attempted to charge upward by running along the wall's surface. Yet the higher he ran, the greater the gravitational pull became.

Before he could ascend ten meters, the force became overwhelming, dragging him back down.

The fishman youngster observing this grasped the crucial insight.

“It's a divine script formation deployed by the earth elemental army before the battle began!”

“The Main Earth Elemental God has channeled divine magic through this formation, achieving power on par with the saint level.”

The divine script formation itself was of gold level, but when the Main Earth Elemental God channeled divine magic through it, its power effortlessly transcended its limit, reaching the saint level.

“Deities stand at the pinnacle of transcendence, yet divine support carries inherent limitations.”
