“Take Divine Descent, for instance, its primary constraint lies in the vessel. The superior the vessel, the more fully the god's power manifests.”

“When I use the big guy for channeling, I can only achieve gold level combat strength at best. The primary limitation is that the big guy isn't a fishman, making him incompatible with fishman god authority. The fact that the big guy believes in me instead of the Charming Blue Goddess is also a major flaw."

“The Main Earth Elemental God hasn't actually descended upon any earth elemental believer. Instead, He used the divine script formation as a bridge to cast divine magic.”

"As a vessel, the quality of the divine script formation surpasses even a gold level earth elemental entity. Thus, the Main Earth Elemental God can unleash saint level power.

“In this rescue mission, my true adversary isn't Tu Ji, it's the Earth Elemental God.”

The thought weighed on the fishman youngster's chest like a boulder, constricting his breath.

This was the Main Earth Elemental God! Such a supreme being naturally imposed immense psychological pressure.

An instinctive warning gripped the fishman youngster—any attempt to use the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell before the Main Earth Elemental God would fail spectacularly!

He understood clearly: intervening now would unleash immense trouble later. More likely, even giving his all might not save the big guy!

“No. I must save Xiao Guai.”

"But how exactly can I save him?!" The fishman youngster strained every fiber of his being to think.

Meanwhile, at the White Hair Trench.

After a violent explosion, Da Han and Zhou Zhang both recoiled violently, creating distance between them.

Their clash had been intense from the very start.

Da Han showed no urgency, but Zhou Zhang needed to defeat him first to rush back and confront Qian Xing.

Thus, she fought with full force from the start.

Da Han responded calmly. As a sorcerer, he could only cast innate racial spells. Yet these spells were amplified by his bloodline, making them fierce, swift, and extremely low-cost to cast.

Zhou Zhang countered with a divine spell, a reckless move that left both lightly wounded.

Divine spell - Time Rewind.

Da Han cast the spell on himself, restoring his body to its previous state and erasing the minor injuries entirely.

Divine spell - Self-Deception!

Zhou Zhang also used a divine spell, deceiving herself into believing she was unharmed. The deception reached its peak, and her injuries vanished in an instant.

"It seems I won't be able to take down Da Han anytime soon. This will be a protracted battle!" Realizing this, Zhou Zhang's sense of urgency grew even more intense.

Da Han, however, wore a smile, his demeanor composed. He deliberately flew back, intentionally creating distance.

With two of them, the tactic of splitting forces was clearly advantageous now.

Da Han only needed to hold Zhou Zhang off while allowing Qian Xing to break through elsewhere.

Zhou Zhang had no choice but to follow, charging forward once more to attack Da Han.

Another battle group.

Qian Xing stood proudly, surveying the battlefield. “Can you continue?”

Nearby, several gold level fishmen lay fallen. Their life force was gone, each mortally wounded, their transcendent aura slowly fading.

Unlike Da Han, Qian Xing held clear dominance against the swarm of gold level interceptors, moving with effortless ease.

He toyed with them like a cat with mice, while the gold level believers of Charming Blue wore expressions of tension, panic, and deep, unresolved fury.

“What's there to fear? It's just death!”

“Hold him back! Even if we die, it matters not.” Even if we only delay him for a second, making such a contribution to our Lord is worth it!"

Zhang Yu Wan and Jiu Hua Bi shouted in unison, rallying their comrades.

Their bodies were covered in wounds, limbs mangled and broken, weapons shattered. Though gravely injured, their morale remained unshaken, as if locked at its peak.

Qian Xing sneered coldly: “Impressive fighting spirit.”

“But to think you could delay me? You underestimate me terribly.”

“Did you believe my earlier attacks were merely for fun?”

With that, Qian Xing's form flickered, splitting into over a dozen illusory figures, each indistinguishable from the real one.

The gold level Charming Blue believers scrambled to intercept.

Qian Xing's true form soared dozens of meters upward, rapidly gathering power.

By the time Zhang Yu Wan and the others realized, it was already too late!

Battle skill - Five Pointed Star Annihilation Ray!

Qian Xing extended his right arm, fingers splayed wide. The blue light at his fingertips was blindingly intense.

Saint level battle qi condensed and compressed in his palm, forming a sphere.

The sphere spun rapidly, humming with energy.

In the next instant, the battle qi sphere erupted violently, firing a beam of light.

The outer edge of the beam was defined by the light emanating from the tips of his five fingers, forming five sharp points.

When first fired, the beam was only as thick as his palm.

But as it shot outward, its diameter grew larger. It expanded from his palm to the circumference of his forearm, then to his thigh, growing as thick as a basin, then as massive as a millstone.

Finally, upon striking the gathering place of the Charming Blue Church, its diameter reached two meters.

“No, impossible!”

The gathering place was already cloaked in deception magic. How could Qian Xing possibly perceive it from such a great distance?"

Zhang Yu Wan and Jiu Hua Bi paled in shock.

Seeing their expressions, Qian Xing understood their thoughts.

As the beam in his hand faded and dissipated, he calmly withdrew his hand, looking down at the numerous Gold level cultivators and declared arrogantly, “Battle requires the use of your brain.”

“You underestimate me. I'm no brute.”

Zhang Yu Wan and Jiu Hua Bi instantly understood. The former exclaimed in shock and anger, "I see! You appeared to be toying with us, but you were actually applying external pressure."

“You created the illusion of multiple breakout attempts to test our level of tension.”

After repeated tests, you grasped the direction we were most tense about. That was precisely the route you intended to attack!"

Wisdom was a remarkable power.

Qian Xing's reconnaissance capabilities were insufficient for long-range detection, but through strategic tactics, he exploited the enemy's vulnerabilities and deduced the correct direction of attack!

The Five Pointed Star Annihilation Ray unleashed unparalleled, razor-sharp power. It plowed a straight furrow across the ground, cleaving magic and divine script formations in two.

Then, rising upward, it severed a third of the golden altar.

Finally, it slammed into the Ocean Mother Nest.

The Ocean Mother Nest was shattered clean through!

Chapter 846: Leaving Charming Blue’s Divine Domain

The Star Annihilation Ray plunged deep into the Mother Nest’s core. Its residual force was immense, causing waves to surge and the Deep Sea Monster Fish to rock violently.

The fishman youngster, trapped within the prayer chamber, was severely disrupted. His focus scattered, forcing him to disengage.

“What's happening?!” He snapped his eyes open, only then realizing the cataclysmic shift.

After summoning the tower spirit, he immediately grasped the external situation.

"Two saint level foes have found their way here. Zhou Zhang is being held back by one of them!"

“Her deception divine spell proves ineffective against peers of equal standing.”

Cang Xu's eyes widened as he stared intently at the ravine before him.

The ravine had been carved by the Five Pointed Star Annihilation Ray just moments ago, mere half a step away from him.

Fortunately, though this battle skill possessed immense power, it was highly focused and concentrated, achieving formidable penetrating capability. Thus, despite his proximity, Cang Xu remained unharmed.

His body was unscathed, but his mind reeled from the violent shockwave.

"That's a saint level battle skill! If I'd been grazed, I'd have been vaporized on the spot!”

Just moments ago, Cang Xu had witnessed several unlucky souls getting caught in the beam's edge, instantly vaporized.

Had Cang Xu not been an undead being, he would have been drenched in cold sweat by now.

Even so, the lingering terror caused his soul to tremble and flicker uncontrollably.

“No!” Cang Xu whipped his head around, staring at the Ocean Mother Nest pierced by the beam. His earlier fear was instantly overshadowed by intense worry.

“The Lord Commander is in there.”

“Lord Commander, you absolutely must not come to harm!”

From the day they arrived on Mystifying Monster Island until this very moment, Cang Xu's loyalty to the young commander had undergone a qualitative transformation through countless events, becoming profoundly resilient and deep-rooted.

Though renowned for his composure, Cang Xu now lost his bearings, instinctively dashing toward the Ocean Mother Nest.

But in the next instant, a thunderous roar erupted from within the nest!

Boom!

A colossal sea beast smashed through the mountain wall and burst forth.

It was an octopus, its entire body snow-white, emanating a fierce, surging aura of the saint level.

It was none other than the saint beast, Snow Pear Octopus.

A circular wound gaped in its body, blood gushing freely.

The Five Pointed Star Annihilation Ray had struck it directly, rousing it from its slumber.

This saint level magic beast had been specifically stationed here by the Charming Blue Goddess to serve as a guardian. Now that the Ocean Mother Nest had been destroyed and it had been ambushed, its fury boiled over. It was determined to break out and strangle the culprit on the spot!

Qian Xing's aura was completely unmasked, and the Snow Pear Octopus immediately detected him. Stirring up tens of thousands of tons of seawater, it charged forward, causing the earth to shake and mountains to tremble wherever it passed.

Seeing a saint level sea beast hurtling toward him, Qian Xing naturally didn't want to clash head-on and quickly dodged.

But in the next instant, he was enveloped by divine magic. His senses were deceived: long distances shrank, short ones stretched; straight lines became curves, curves turned into straight lines.

Outwardly, the nearby valley walls seemed distant, while distant mountain ranges appeared close at hand.

He swam through the water, convinced he was moving in a straight line, yet in reality, he was navigating a series of intricate curves.

He was like a headless fly, crashing into things left and right.

Divine spell - Chaotic Deception: Distance.

Divine spell - Chaotic Deception: Direction.

“Damn it!” Qian Xing realized he'd been hit, but it was already too late.

The Snow Pear Octopus' tentacles lashed out like giant flesh whips, slamming him like a soccer ball into the mountain wall. He was driven five or six meters deep!

A saint beast entrusted with important duties by the Charming Blue Goddess naturally possessed power far surpassing ordinary saint beasts.

Cang Xu was swept far back by the massive wave created by the Snow Pear Octopus' advance, tumbling and rolling several times on the ground before finally coming to a halt.

He was covered in dust and dirt, looking utterly disheveled.

The churning water stirred up silt from the seabed, obscuring visibility. The vast majority of the believers screamed in terror, swimming wildly in all directions.

Taking advantage of the chaos, Cang Xu charged directly into the Ocean Mother Nest.

Several clever believers made the same choice as Cang Xu.

But they couldn't see the Deep Sea Monster Fish, which was cloaked by the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell. Upon entering the nest, Cang Xu immediately spotted the Deep Sea Monster Fish..

Seeing the vessel intact, he breathed a huge sigh of relief.

Meanwhile, the fishman youngster also snapped into action.

Witnessing the nest breached and the saint beast charging in to smash an even larger hole through its walls, he immediately dispatched the alchemy puppet to retrieve Cang Xu.

Cang Xu's own entry saved the fishman youngster considerable effort.

The alchemy puppet, naturally imbued with the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, smoothly retrieved Cang Xu in plain sight.

Upon rushing back to the Deep Sea Monster Fish, Cang Xu found no trace of the fishman youngster.

The tower spirit relayed the fishman youngster's message to Cang Xu.

Only then did Cang Xu realize that drastic changes weren't confined to this location, the big guy was also under siege by an earth elemental army, facing imminent peril!

The fishman youngster's monitoring had been interrupted. Now, Cang Xu must resume surveillance, ready to intervene at any moment and rescue the big guy with all his might.

“Of all times!” Cang Xu sighed deeply.

The fishman youngster had also entrusted him with a crucial task: to pilot the Deep Sea Monster Fish and depart this place swiftly.

"Sigh, I wanted to stay here for a bit longer."

"To think such an unexpected situation would occur..."

"We've been exposed. It's no longer safe here. The Charming Blue Church lacks the strength to hold this place, and the Mother Nest has been destroyed. We truly cannot linger here!"

“Then let's go.”

Cang Xu entered the cockpit, took command, and steered the ship away.

Other matters, like Jian Gui, Ba Qi, and so on, were cast aside.

“Xiao Guai, you absolutely must not come to harm!”

"Be alive... you must still be alive!

The fishman youngster's heart burned with anxiety. He cast the divine spell [Believer Perception], then activated [Divine Power Channel] to reconnect with the big guy.

Meanwhile, on the Magnet Archipelago battlefield, the situation for Da Dou and his group had grown even more perilous.

Suppressed by the earthen divine light, their movements slowed to a crawl. They could only rely on defensive battle skill to withstand the siege of the earth elementals.

The fishman youngster had a grim expression: "Even if I use Divine Descent, the big guy will have gold level combat strength at most."

"Earlier, he was able to kill the gold level Earth Elemental Chieftain because of the Deceptive Disguise spell."

“But now, with Tu Ji and the Main Earth Elemental God watching, how effective would that spell be?”

“Using it would be like confessing our weakness.”

With Charming Blue Goddess gravely wounded and in deep slumber, the fishman youngster had gained profound insight into the Charming Blue Church's situation through the divine spell [Believer Perception].

He knew that rashly using the Charming Blue divine spell under these circumstances wouldn't make the enemy fear and retreat but would instead provoke them into even stronger offensive desires.

“But even so, I must save Xiao Guai's life!”

“No matter how troublesome the aftermath, we must get through this immediate crisis first.”

Despite this resolve, the fishman youngster still faced an unsolvable problem.

His hands tightened slightly as he gripped the Charming Blue divine artifact, Mermaid Fairy Tale.

Earlier, within the earth elemental lair, the statue of the Main Earth Elemental God had served as the most obvious target for casting spells. But now, with the Main Earth Elemental God's gaze fixed upon him, the fishman youngster didn't know where to cast his Deceptive Disguise spell, even if he wanted to use it.

Just as the fishman youngster stood bewildered and at a loss, a Charming Blue divine revelation suddenly descended, landing upon him.

The revelation offered him an unconventional choice, causing the fishman youngster's eyes to light up.

After a moment's hesitation, the fishman youngster sprang into action!

Divine spell - Divine Artifact Mastery.

This was an active divine art; its effects only manifested upon casting.

Its effect was powerful yet unadorned: the mastery of divine artifacts!

And at that moment, the fishman youngster held in his hands the very artifact: [Mermaid Fairy Tale].

Divine spell - Deceptive Scheming.

Divine spell - Distortion Filter.

The overlapping use of these two divine spells not only swiftly covered the battlefield but also began to corrode the divine script formation beneath the ground.

Chk, chk, chk…

A series of faint, light sounds continuously emanated from the divine artifact [Mermaid Fairy Tale].

With each soft crack, a pearl-like bubble within the shell-shaped artifact shattered, vanishing into nothingness.

A powerful force of deception erupted in silence.

In that instant, the fishman youngster realized: “That's it. For a god to focus their gaze, they need a focal point of faith.”

“These focal points can be believers or divine statues.”

“And right now, the Main Earth Elemental God relies on that vast divine script formation laid out beneath the ground!”

“By using my deception divine spell to corrode the formation now, I might indirectly deceive the Main Earth Elemental God.”

As for the likelihood of this success, the fishman youngster had no idea.

After all, his cultivation was far too low, merely gold level.

The Main Earth Elemental God, however, was the sovereign of an entire pantheon. Even among all the gods, he was undeniably first-rate.

Even if Charming Blue Goddess recovered to Her peak state, She would still be merely a minor god, incomparable to the Main Earth Elemental God.

The rapid depletion of pearl bubbles within the divine artifact [Mermaid Fairy Tale] clearly demonstrated how difficult it was to deceive a Main God!

“But even if there's only a sliver of hope, I'll do everything to save Xiao Guai...”

The fishman youngster was lost in thought when suddenly, his body and mind shook violently, and darkness engulfed his vision.

When he opened his eyes again, he had broken free from his previous state, severing his connection with the big guy once more.

“What's going on?”

“What happened this time?”

The fishman youngster hurriedly scanned his surroundings.

Cang Xu was piloting the Deep Sea Monster Fish, having just departed the Ocean Mother Nest and entered the White Hair Trench.

Under the combined effects of the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, the Deep Sea Monster Fish sailed through the gaping hole in plain sight, yet no one noticed.

Contrary to the fishman youngster's fears, it faced no fierce assault.

Yet in the next instant, a bolt of lightning struck his mind as the fishman youngster grasped the reason.

“It's the Charming Blue Divine Domain!”

“By leaving the divine domain, I severed the connection.”

“Within the divine domain, the Charming Blue divine spells receive unimaginable amplification.” Once I leave the divine domain, casting divine spells on my own yields nowhere near the same effect."

The fishman youngster tried it immediately, his heart sinking: “On my own, I can barely sense Xiao Guai, I can't even establish the Divine Power Channel!”

“I must return. I have to stay within the Charming Blue Divine Domain to save Xiao Guai!”

Chapter 847: Tu Ji Dies

“No, Lord Commander, this is far too dangerous!” Cang Xu blurted out his objection without hesitation upon hearing the fishman youngster still wanted to linger.

But the youngster's resolve was far more unwavering than his own.

“Cang Xu, you know me.”

“I will never abandon Xiao Guai!”

"He calls me Father. He's still waiting for me there."

"What father could ever abandon his child so easily?"

Cang Xu knew the fishman youngster well. He didn't doubt the boy's unwavering resolve at this moment.

But he couldn't back down now!

So, he pleaded earnestly: “Commander, staying here means facing certain death.”

"Two saint level foes are launching a surprise attack. Zhou Zhang alone can hardly hold them off. Even with the Snow Pear Octopus, the tide won't turn."

“The reason is simple: this is the heartland of the Charming Blue Church. Defending here will inevitably cost them the initiative.”

"Even if those two saint attackers don't breach our lines, merely maintaining constant activity on the periphery would cause Zhou Zhang immense trouble, firmly tying down her attention."

"Furthermore, with the Ocean Mother Nest now destroyed, the arrangements of Charming Blue Goddess no longer hold. Remaining here would likely subject you to a saint level investigation."

“Even if those two hostile saints cannot breach this location, they can still launch remote attacks.”

“That single strike already speaks volumes about how dire the situation is!”

“The Ocean Mother Nest has become the most perilous place of all!”

The fishman youngster shook his head: “Xiao Guai...”

Cang Xu cut in: “My Lord, sometimes we must make necessary choices and sacrifices.”

"You are our commander. If you were to die trying to rescue Xiao Guai, what would become of the rest of us?"

Cang Xu had struck at the heart of the fishman youngster's hesitation.

The youngster was indeed deeply troubled by this prospect.

Yet after a moment's thought, he shook his head. Fixing Cang Xu with a piercing gaze, he said, "Cang Xu, I recall you have a son too. If you were standing in my place today, and your son faced the same predicament as Xiao Guai, what would you do?"

Cang Xu: “...”

The fishman youngster's words left him speechless.

The fishman youngster smiled and patted his shoulder, his eyes brimming with satisfaction, but his tone turned resolute: “Now I command you, Cang Xu!”

“Continue piloting the Deep Sea Monster Fish. Escape first, and stand ready to pick me up.”

“Once I handle Xiao Guai's situation, I'll leave this place.”

Cang Xu's face darkened as he refused outright: "My Lord, if you don't leave, how can I possibly escape? Let me go with you..."

"No. If I fall, someone must clean up the mess. You are the only one."

At this, the fishman youngster sighed deeply: “I know this isn't a rational decision.”

“But I can't do it!”

“I'd rather risk myself than abandon Xiao Guai without even trying!”

"You've already done so much for him. Without you, he wouldn't have escaped Mystifying Monster Island.” Cang Xu lamented, vowing, “I absolutely will not sail the ship away again."

“Though the Deep Sea Monster Fish is only gold level, it can still withstand some of the saint level attacks.”

“It can increase your chances of survival, Commander.”

“I'd rather kill myself now than sail the Deep Sea Monster Fish away.”

The fishman youngster opened his mouth to speak, but Cang Xu cut him off: “Lord Commander!”

“Just as you're determined to save Xiao Guai now, my resolve is the same as yours.”

“I obey the orders you gave me. I'm leaving now.”

"Time is critical. Every second counts for Xiao Guai. Lord, you mustn't delay any longer!"

Their exchange wasn't spoken words, but mental waves.

The principle resembled the duelists' long-distance communication. Except duelists required the Dueling God, relied on Tranquil Hill, and drained their own divine favor and power to achieve it.

The fishman youngster and Cang Xu stood face-to-face now, needing none of that.

Mental communication was incredibly swift. Though their exchange felt lengthy, in reality, only an instant passed.

Cang Xu swiftly disembarked alone, while the fishman youngster commanded the tower spirit to steer the Deep Sea Monster Fish further into the depths. He himself immediately entered the prayer chamber, his heart burning with anxiety and tension as he initiated another long-distance connection.

Divine spell - Perceive Believer.

Divine spell - Divine Power Channel.

Divine spell - Divine Artifact Mastery.

Divine spell - Deceptive Scheming.

Divine spell - Distortion Filter.

Using all five divine spells simultaneously placed an immense strain on his mind, causing both his divine power and mental energy to deplete at an alarming rate.

The fishman youngster had no idea how long he could hold on. he was simply fighting with every ounce of strength he possessed!

Da Dou and the others were in dire peril!

The big guy, unaware of the fishman youngster's gaze, wept silently within.

The Main Earth Elemental God's divine spell formed a colossal earthen cone mid-air, crashing down toward the big guy!

Yet in Tu Ji's perception, Hong Zhu had become the target of the massive earthen cone's assault.

"No! That's my only lead!!" Tu Ji roared madly.

He had paid such a heavy price to capture Hong Zhu, only to fail in the end?

He couldn't accept it!

He was a member of the Blood Light Punishment Institution. His faith was unwavering. From the day he joined, he had pursued necromancers with his whole heart, risking his life without a moment's slack or hesitation.

Just now, he had detected hesitation in the Main Earth Elemental God's gaze. But that hesitation stemmed solely from this calculation: Would a timely retreat better serve my pursuit of the necromancer?

After weighing it, he concluded it would not.

Thus, he remained in the fray, sacrificing his arm and detonating himself to join forces with Da Dou.

Now, facing the Main Earth Elemental God's direct assault, his emotions surged. Death held no fear for him. He leapt upward without hesitation.

Soaring into the air, his body stretched wide like a cross, he stood tall and proud, positioning himself directly above “Hong Zhu” (the big guy).

Boom!

The next instant, the earth spike struck the ground, but missed Tu Ji, landing beside him instead.

The earth shook violently, a massive shockwave sending the ground rolling like a tidal wave. Da Dou, the big guy, and others nearby were all violently hurled backward.

The Main Earth Elemental God unleashed divine power once more, conjuring a barrage of earth spikes that surged toward the big guy.

Tu Ji plunged forward, blocking the path before “Hong Zhu” (the big guy).

The sharp spikes halted abruptly, the closest spike's tip mere inches from Tu Ji's nose.

The entire crowd gasped in shock.

Drenched in sweat, blood smeared across his face, Tu Ji's expression grew even more frenzied. “Hahahaha!”

“I am from the Blood Light Punishment Institution! This operation is to hunt down necromancers!”

"To eradicate necromancers, nearly all deities have signed a covenant. Cooperation is a fundamental clause. Even for an earth elemental deity, if you kill me, it counts as breaking the covenant!"

Tu Ji shouted, explaining while rallying himself.

After finishing his speech, he glared fiercely at the gold level earth elementals surrounding him.

These elementals had closed in once more.

Their expressions were complex.

This madman, Tu Ji, had risked everything to block the line of fire for a single clue.

And yet, he succeeded!

The Main Earth Elemental God had truly ceased attacking.

Heaven forbid, this world was utterly insane!

But the next moment.

Pop.

The earth spike closest to Tu Ji suddenly shot forward.

Tu Ji opened his mouth in laughter, but before he could react, the spike was already thrust into his mouth.

Sharp spikes protruded from the back of his skull.

As he died, Tu Ji's battle qi erupted violently, but it was useless.

A flash of golden battle qi shone briefly before being effortlessly suppressed by the Main Earth Elemental God's overwhelming divine power!

Tu Ji was dead!

Silence fell over the battlefield as everyone stared in stunned silence once more.

The Main Earth Elemental God's power slowly dissipated. The earth elementals snapped out of their trance, erupting in deafening cheers.

To them: this madman Tu Ji had been effortlessly crushed by their own deity.

To Da Dou and the others: Tu Ji was utterly insane, daring to openly defy a god! Even with a covenant, what did breaking it once matter to a god?

Therefore, Tu Ji died!

Like a mere ant, he had no chance to resist.

Yet to the Main Earth Elemental God, it was not Tu Ji he had slain, but the big guy!

With a sense of satisfaction, the Main Earth Elemental God gradually withdrew his divine power.

Inside the Deep Sea Monster Fish, the fishman youngster was exhausted to the point of collapse.

He knelt on the floor, hands braced against the deck, the only way to keep himself from collapsing.

To sustain the five divine spells, the fishman youngster had pushed beyond his limits, draining himself completely.

His entire body ached, darkness swirled before his eyes, and his temples throbbed painfully!

The divine artifact, The Mermaid Fairy Tale, lay discarded nearby. Only five or six pearl bubbles remained inside the shell!

To deceive an entity like the Main Earth Elemental God, even the Mermaid Fairy Tale had been squeezed dry. Nearly all the pearl bubbles collected earlier had been consumed!

“I, I did it!”

“I succeeded...” An unparalleled sense of accomplishment washed over the fishman youngster, filling his body and mind with a soaring, exhilarating sensation.

“But I must hold on, Xiao Guai is still surrounded by the earth elemental army!”

The fishman youngster refocused his attention, casting a concerned glance.

After Tu Ji's death, the earth elemental army's morale soared, continuing their relentless pursuit of Da Dou and the others.

“What should I do?”

“Maintaining the Believer Perception and the Divine Power Channel is already pushing my limits. I can't cast a third divine spell!”

A chill gripped the fishman youngster's heart.

The big guy was trapped in mortal danger, with no outside help!

Meanwhile, after leaving the Deep Sea Monster Fish, Cang Xu fled for his life.

“Jian Gui?!” Along the way, he unexpectedly discovered Jian Gui.

Sealed by Zhou Zhang, Jian Gui appeared as an ordinary person.

But a sea beast, sacrificed as an offering, had broken free from its bonds and entered a frenzied state, about to crush Jian Gui.

A thought struck Cang Xu, and he immediately cast an undead spell to save Jian Gui.

“Come with me, Lord Jian Gui.” Cang Xu grabbed Jian Gui's arm, ready to flee further.

Jian Gui seemed slightly surprised, halting him with a smile: “A bit unexpected, yet not entirely surprising.”

“In any case, I'm pleased.”

Cang Xu sighed. “We can talk about that later, Lord Jian Gui.”

“We need to get out of here fast.”

“No need,” Jian Gui replied calmly. "I'm rather intrigued by the Ocean Mother Nest now. If you dare, escort me there."

Cang Xu: “?!”

He stared intently at Jian Gui, suddenly feeling a violent tremor throughout his body and mind.

Chapter 848: Teacher Si Ling

Cang Xu's encounter with Jian Gui was accidental, he hadn't sought him out intentionally.

Thus, the location where they met was no longer Jian Gui's captivity quarters.

The reason for his rescue lay in the immense value of Jian Gui's background, which offered Cang Xu vast room for speculation.

True to Cang Xu's expectations, Jian Gui behaved like an ordinary person, displaying no combat prowess whatsoever.

This was because Zhou Zhang had personally sealed him, rendering his powers unusable.

Yet after being saved, Jian Gui's subsequent actions deeply puzzled Cang Xu.

Weakened to the level of an ordinary man, why wouldn't he attempt to escape? Instead, he sought to enter the Ocean Mother Nest, wasn't that courting death?

Judging by his past actions, he was certainly not one to take reckless risks!

“Then what's going on here?” Cang Xu silently questioned himself.

He had once been a noble steward. He had held that position for many years. Interacting with all social strata, he had witnessed the scheming of the upper classes and recognized the cunning of the lower classes.

This experience gradually ingrained in him the habit of thinking in terms of conspiracy.

Why would Jian Gui head toward the Ocean Mother Nest at this perilous moment?

He feared death, yet now he was actively courting it.

He must have his own plan and purpose!

The likelihood of death, as he saw it, was far lower than what an outsider like me perceived!

Could it be that the two saint levels who came to ambush them were lured here by him?

No, that's unlikely.

These two saints were human, not necromancers, they carried not a trace of undead aura.

Cang Xu had just been piloting the Deep Sea Monster Fish and, through the tower spirit, had uncovered Qian Xing and Da Han’s identities.

Thanks to the War Merchant, though his database was incomplete, it was more than sufficient for the survivors!

It was incredibly useful!

His database cataloged transcendents across the entire world.

Naturally, only those who were renowned or possessed unique qualities made the cut.

Qian Xing and Da Han were among them.

Both were saint level, that alone qualified them for inclusion in the War Merchant's intelligence network.

Cang Xu couldn't see the specifics of the two squads, but the Deep Sea Monster Fish's reconnaissance capabilities far surpassed his own.

“They are Qian Xing and Da Han, both from the Fire Beard Pirates!”

“The probability of them collaborating with the necromancer is very low.”

“Could it be that, just like in the battle on the Underworld River, the necromancer faction has quietly received reinforcements?”

“Did Jian Gui make contact?”

This was Cang Xu's natural conjecture.

He knew it was entirely possible.

The bonds between souls were profoundly mysterious. Zhou Zhang had sealed Jian Gui, yet failed to sever this connection. Certainly, the bond had weakened.

But it could not be fully cut.

Thus, even under Zhou Zhang's watchful eye, Jian Gui and other necromancers could pull off underhanded maneuvers, engaging in large-scale memory forgery operations.

By extension, it was entirely plausible that Jian Gui had utilized this soul-to-soul link to locate powerful reinforcements.

“Perhaps the undead orthodoxy has even developed specialized methods...”

Another testament to the power of soul connections was the divine spell [Believer Perception].

Cang Xu understood: Though his commander benefited from the divine domain's amplification, even without it, the young commander could still sense figures like the big guy and Lan Zao.

Such vast perceptual range utterly transcended conventional spells!

As a magician, Cang Xu had his own interpretation: "The range of the divine spell [Believer Perception] isn't exaggerated because the deity itself possesses an exaggerated perception range. The commander isn't a deity; he's only at the gold level, yet he still possesses such an extraordinary perception range."

“The connection between souls cannot be described by conventional distance.”

“Or rather, the distance between souls differs from conventional concepts—it is a mystical concept.”

“Why is the range of Believer Perception so exaggerated?”

“Because faith and divine power have transformed the believers. Their soul essences continually converge and align, making the distance between souls grow exceedingly close."

By the time Cang Xu reached this conclusion, only an instant had passed in the outside world.

He immediately addressed Jian Gui: “How can we possibly enter the Ocean Mother Nest now?”

"Lord Jian Gui, curiosity is indeed one of a magician's finest qualities. You have praised me for it before, but we must also assess the situation."

"The risks of proceeding now are too great. You have not yet broken free from the seal! If Lord Zhou Zhang is defeated and the enemy's saint level attacks, they will undoubtedly slaughter us in the Mother Nest without mercy. If Lord Zhou Zhang succeeds and returns to find you in the Mother Nest, she will never forgive you."

“After all, you are not me. You are not yet a Charming Blue believer.”

Jian Gui laughed heartily and waved his hand. “Then go.”

Cang Xu hesitated, then slowly shook his head, his expression grave. “I cannot.”

“You saved me. Having encountered this, I cannot stand by and watch you die.”

With that, Cang Xu forcibly intervened, using only the spell, mage hand, to hoist Jian Gui into the air.

Jian Gui's eyes widened in fury as he shouted, “Stop! Stop! Stop!”

“Xu Jia, this is my personal choice.”

“Leave me be.”

Cang Xu shook his head. "Don't forget, I'm also a Charming Blue believer. I won't stand by and watch an outsider enter the Ocean Mother Nest."

“Believer?”

Jian Gui sneered. "Give me a break. You saw us forging memories and never reported it. Your faith is nothing outstanding!"

Cang Xu sighed and spoke honestly, "Even if you're right, I can't let you go to your death. You're a legitimate heir of the undead orthodoxy, closely tied to the Twelve Saints. In the future, I'm counting on you to guide me and help me connect with the orthodox factions."

"Qi Ling and Xiao Wang Zi are already dead. If you perish here too, when the undead orthodoxy investigates afterward, I'll be the sole survivor. How could I possibly explain that? If your faction misunderstands me, there'd be no way to clear my name."

“So, to be safe, I'll take you with me.”

Jian Gui was speechless.

Under normal circumstances, he would have been furious, enraged, even filled with hatred.

But his relationship with Cang Xu was anything but normal.

Yet one crucial point must not be forgotten.

Jian Gui controlled Cang Xu. He had already tampered with Cang Xu earlier, leaving the latter's life and death entirely at Jian Gui's whim.

In truth, Cang Xu had already successfully neutralized these arrangements through the fishman youngster's divine spells.

Not only that, the youngster had further employed divine spells to imbue Cang Xu with specialized deception effects.

For a time, the youngster lacked confidence in resolving the hidden mechanisms within Cang Xu, unsure of his ability.

But after becoming a divine emissary and acquiring numerous advanced divine spells, he practiced several times, his confidence soaring.

Ultimately, he successfully eliminated the hidden threat within Cang Xu.

Jian Gui remained unaware of this development. In his perception, the hidden mechanism within Cang Xu still existed.

This belief granted him a significant psychological advantage.

He persistently believed Cang Xu was merely his pawn, under his control.

Moreover, Cang Xu's actions did not stem from malice. He gave Jian Gui the impression—Cang Xu genuinely and sincerely wanted to save my life!

Whether it was repaying a debt, returning a favor, or seeking future benefits by aligning with the undead orthodoxy, all these motives seemed perfectly reasonable. Jian Gui understood and even accepted them.

In fact, Jian Gui found Cang Xu's straightforwardness refreshing, perceiving him as someone who acted with integrity.

Anger, rage, affection, and a sense of control, these emotions tangled together, coalescing into a helpless, powerless feeling that was both laughable and exasperating.

Just as Jian Gui wrestled with how to persuade Cang Xu, a figure materialized beside them.

Jian Gui was utterly astonished.

He hadn't expected him to appear so effortlessly!

Cang Xu also stared at the mysterious arrival, his expression shaken.

This transcendent resembled an elderly man, radiating only bronze level energy, vibrant and alive, without a trace of undead aura.

Yet Cang Xu recognized him instantly: “You... You're... Teacher Si Ling?”

Teacher Si Ling smiled at Cang Xu.

His features were ordinary, human in appearance, with a gentle demeanor, like an old scholar secluded in a mage tower.

He nodded slightly, studying Cang Xu with interest. “You recognize me?”

'Of course!'

“Your wanted posters are plastered all over the Main Plane, practically everywhere,” Cang Xu thought, stifling his inner rant.

Chapter 849: It Really Mutated into a Saint Level Bloodline?!

In fact, Cang Xu possessed considerable knowledge of the undead orthodoxy factions.

Ever since becoming a necromancer, he had kept a close watch on them.

Previously, however, his own level and the need for disguise had limited his access to high-level intelligence.

But after the incident on Mystifying Monster Island, he gained access to War Merchant's database, which drastically expanded his knowledge of the upper echelons. War Merchant's database contained detailed descriptions of the orthodox undead faction, the undead Founder You Shen, and his Twelve Saints.

Then, Cang Xu encountered Jian Gui and even journeyed with him into the Underworld River. Though the process was perilous, it indeed deepened Cang Xu's understanding of the undead orthodoxy. He gained not only high-level knowledge but also a tangible grasp of the current realities of a necromancer's existence.

Therefore, Cang Xu understood all too well the formidable power of the smiling Teacher Si Ling before him, who appeared like a gentle scholar.

Within the entire orthodox undead faction, his importance and value were second only to the founder. This was reflected in the wanted list, where his bounty was second only to You Shen's!

"After becoming a necromancer, I began paying close attention to undead orthodoxy. Your appearance has long been etched in my memory. For you are a senior figure, a sage who walks ahead yet still mentors those who follow. My admiration for you knows no bounds! Simply because you have continuously pioneered new necromancy magic, constantly expanding the boundaries of necromancy!"

“We pursue new knowledge, while you create it!” Cang Xu spoke with genuine sincerity, offering Teacher Si Ling a solemn salute.

Jian Gui couldn't help but glance at Cang Xu, seemingly surprised by his eloquence.

Based on Jian Gui's understanding of Teacher Si Ling, Cang Xu's words had struck precisely at the core of the other's pride.

Sure enough, Teacher Si Ling laughed heartily, his face lighting up with genuine delight. “The pursuit of knowledge, that is the hallmark of an exceptional magician. I can see you are a talented magician, destined for even greater heights!”

Then, Teacher Si Ling studied Cang Xu with keen interest. “If I'm not mistaken, you possess a saint level undead bloodline, correct?”

Jian Gui froze.

Cang Xu was stunned too.

After a moment of silence, Jian Gui couldn't help but exclaim: “What?”

This gold level orthodox necromancer now wore a look of utter shock.

Cang Xu also revealed astonishment.

But it was half genuine, half feigned.

The genuine part was that his bloodline had been detected without him sensing any magic fluctuations.

The feigned part was his performance—as if his painstaking disguise had been exposed with a single remark.

In truth, this was precisely the moment he’d been waiting for to reveal his “saint aptitude” to Jian Gui. On one hand, it demonstrated his value, compelling Jian Gui to take notice and extend goodwill. If circumstances allowed, he could even use Jian Gui as a conduit to join the orthodox undead faction directly.

This had always been Cang Xu's plan.

He'd even discussed it with the fishman youngster.

After all, he'd already put it into practice. Enhancing his Hanging Corpse Tree bloodline proved exceedingly difficult. The main obstacle was the lack of sufficient, suitable undead life for the Bloodcore to absorb.

Only the Ghost World, or rather, only the orthodox undead factions on the Main Plane, possessed such stable channels, allowing Cang Xu to indirectly obtain these cultivation resources.

Plans could not outpace change.

Cang Xu hadn't anticipated the revelation of his bloodline secret occurring so swiftly!

He continued his performance.

Cang Xu gave a wry smile and turned to explain to Jian Gui: "Please forgive me, Lord Jian Gui. I deliberately kept this from you."

Jian Gui: “...”

'Your apology, delivered with such righteous conviction, actually sounds quite sincere!'

Cang Xu continued, "After my bloodline mutation, I gradually sensed subtle changes. But I forced myself to endure, trying not to show it outwardly."

“At the time, I was curious, hopeful, and terrified, my fear of loss was overwhelming.”

“That's why I previously schemed to obtain the Undead Bloodline Detection from you.”

"After using that spell, I discovered that through mutation, I had acquired a saint level Hanging Corpse Tree bloodline. I was so thrilled I nearly wanted to announce it to the entire world, but after calming down, many new worries arose."

"After all, those who share hardship may not share prosperity. Moreover, this isn't about sharing prosperity. So, out of caution, I kept this secret to myself."

“Later, I reflected, perhaps my Lord, Charming Blue Goddess, recognized my innate talent and acknowledged my devotion to Her, then bestowed those unique Charming Blue divine spells upon me!”

These words were truly heartfelt. Cang Xu expressed his concern and admitted his mistake with complete sincerity.

Not only that, but he was also reasonable and well-grounded in his arguments.

He further bolstered his persuasiveness by invoking Charming Blue Goddess and the fact that he had become Her believer.

“You... you... you...” Jian Gui opened his mouth several times to speak but couldn't finish a single sentence.

He was utterly shocked!

The Hanging Corpse Tree possessed a saint level bloodline.

Saint level!

While saint level was merely one step above gold level, within the transcendent hierarchy, they represented entirely different standings.

Gold level was considered mid-tier, while saint level was already high tier.

Jian Gui himself was only gold level.

Moreover, he knew full well that his bloodline's limit was precisely there. Meaning he'd be stuck at gold level for life.

But what about Xu Jia (Cang Xu)?

His bloodline mutation had actually granted him a saint level undead bloodline!

Could someone truly ascend to a higher bloodline through mutation?

This was incredibly rare!

Incredibly rare!

“His aptitude was clearly mediocre before, yet after one round of bloodline mutation, he possesses a saint level undead bloodline?”

“Why him? Why not me?”

The bitterness was palpable.

Envy and jealousy filled Jian Gui's heart.

Once, he had looked down on Cang Xu, dismissing him as insignificant. So what if he became a Charming Blue believer? He'd never reach gold level in his lifetime!

But now, it was Jian Gui looking up to Cang Xu.

A saint level Hanging Corpse Tree bloodline... If the blood concentration was strong enough, Cang Xu could very well become a saint in the future.

Saint level!

Wasn't that the transcendent level of the Twelve Saints?

Of course, the Twelve Saints held a unique status within the orthodox undead hierarchy. Not every saint level necromancer could be called one of the Twelve Saints.

But in truth, not all twelve were even saint level themselves. And Jian Gui had never once laid eyes on a saint level necromancer outside the orthodox factions.

Witnessing a nobody suddenly turn the tables and secure a future brighter and grander than his own. Jian Gui's pride shattered.

Yet he quickly adjusted.

“Hahahaha,” he laughed.

“Xu Jia, you underestimate me terribly. I can only rejoice for you!”

"But you're right. As a rogue necromancer, the slightest misstep could get you hunted down. Your caution is absolutely necessary."

"Oh, right. You may no longer be a rogue necromancer. With this bloodline, our orthodox faction will offer you ample opportunity to join us."

At this point, Jian Gui cast an imperceptible glance toward Teacher Si Ling.

In that instant, he understood: Teacher Si Ling had been hidden and elusive, yet suddenly revealed himself. It must be that Teacher Si Ling was curious about Cang Xu. After secretly casting spells, he sensed Cang Xu's potential, prompting his enthusiastic appearance.

Teacher Si Ling was indeed famously fond of mentoring juniors, but there was a prerequisite—the junior had to at least be worthy of mentorship!

While Jian Gui observed Teacher Si Ling, the latter was observing Jian Gui.

Seeing Jian Gui's respectful congratulations, Teacher Si Ling sensed his broad perspective and magnanimity, which deepened his favorable impression.

His scrutiny of Jian Gui, however, served primarily to understand Cang Xu from this angle.

From Jian Gui's initial shock, swift comprehension, and subsequent congratulations to Cang Xu, Teacher Si Ling's estimation of Cang Xu also rose.

On one hand, Jian Gui's magnanimous demeanor not only demonstrated his own broad perspective but also indicated that Cang Xu had cultivated a good rapport with Jian Gui, capable of gaining the support of other necromancers.

On the other hand, this was Teacher Si Ling's first meeting with Cang Xu.

To rapidly deepen his understanding, gleaning information through Jian Gui became a convenient, efficient, and crucial avenue.

After all, Jian Gui was already one of their own within the undead orthodoxy lineage.

Witnessing Jian Gui's trust in Cang Xu, Teacher Si Ling inevitably found his own trust in Cang Xu growing.

Despite this being their first meeting, Cang Xu's recent explanation made Teacher Si Ling far more inclined to believe it was genuine.

Through this brief encounter, Teacher Si Ling had already sketched out Cang Xu's profile in his mind—the latter was likely an exceptional wild necromancer, possessing a magician's heart. His passion for pursuing knowledge earned Teacher Si Ling's admiration and respect for such a young talent.

More crucially, he hadn't been corrupted by negative energy. Interacting with one of their own like Jian Gui was a pleasant experience. Upon discovering the saint level bloodline, he received the heartfelt blessings from Jian Gui. That's fantastic!

Though rare, the mutation of a bloodline into a saint level lineage was indeed possible.

Cang Xu's explanation was sound and logically consistent; Teacher Si Ling detected no flaws.

Teacher Si Ling's smile grew even warmer: “Come, let us visit the Mother Nest and tour the Charming Blue Divine Domain.”

Only then did Cang Xu appear to “suddenly realize,” exclaiming in surprise, “So... it was your idea, Teacher Si Ling, for Lord Jian Gui to go to the Mother Nest!”

Jian Gui rolled his eyes: "You just figured that out? As if I'd ever willingly seek death."

He was delighted.

Genuinely pleased.

Amid his envy and admiration, he suddenly realized: "Huh? I've placed a trap on Xu Jia's body. Yet he still chose to save me, proof our bond is strong."

“The better his potential, the stronger he'll be when he reaches the saint level. Then I'll have a saint level ally!”

“Thinking about it this way, it's absolutely brilliant!”

“Everything is under my control.”

Chapter 850: Even Among Saints, They Are Not The Same Level

Among all the survivors, Cang Xu was arguably the most adept at concealing his true abilities.

His rich life experience had taught him well: feigning ignorance and playing dumb were highly practical survival skills.

By pretending not to have seen through Jian Gui and avoiding displays of intelligence, he avoided arousing his suspicion. This allowed Jian Gui to cling to his old impression of Cang Xu and maintain its mistaken sense of intellectual superiority.

To a certain extent, this also significantly mitigated Jian Gui's jealousy over Cang Xu's transformation into a saint level bloodline.

Cang Xu's most important goal was achieved. By pretending to be stupid, he forced Jian Gui to reveal his secret and made Teacher Si Ling reveal himself.

Teacher Si Ling's appearance caught not only Jian Gui off guard but also exceeded Cang Xu's own expectations.

Yet in terms of outcome, this was a monumental victory!

Though seemingly weak, Cang Xu had, through his unique diplomatic skills, defused Jian Gui's jealousy in a remarkably short time and secured an excellent first impression with Teacher Si Ling.

Among the survivors, none could have performed better than Cang Xu.

Such diplomatic achievements, though seemingly minor, concealed immense potential benefits!

Merchants like Zi Di and Fei She undoubtedly grasped this profoundly.

Yet they still fell short of Cang Xu's exceptional skill. This wasn't merely due to his rich life experience, but also his dark, calculating mind, his ability to penetrate hearts, and his masterful acting.

Zi Di possessed natural talent in this regard, yet compared to Cang Xu, she was still too green.

“Both of you come with me,” Teacher Si Ling instructed. “I'll broaden your horizons.”

He was a necromancer who took great care of his juniors, evident from his title “Teacher.”

While Jian Gui was a true-blooded member of the undead orthodoxy, Cang Xu was not. Yet Teacher Si Ling recognized the latter's potential and had been favorably impressed at first glance, wishing to cultivate him.

Thus, he resolved to bring both Jian Gui and Cang Xu into the divine domain.

“T-Thank you, Teacher Si Ling!” Cang Xu exclaimed, his excitement palpable. Without hesitation, he followed closely behind.

Even if Teacher Si Ling hadn't invited him, he would have followed anyway.

He couldn't shake his unease about the fishman youngster.

After all, Teacher Si Ling was renowned far and wide. His reputation far surpassed that of the fishman youngster.

The fishman youngster might be well-known within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, but Teacher Si Ling's fame encompassed the entire Main Plane!

A person's reputation was like a tree's shadow, great renown was proof of power.

Cang Xu trusted the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, yet he still feared Teacher Si Ling might see through them.

He knew much about Teacher Si Ling and understood that he possessed a special divine artifact—the Truth Slate!

“Teacher Si Ling wields the divine artifact, the Truth Slate. He is the most likely saint level being we've encountered so far to see through the Deceptive Disguise spell!”

"If something goes wrong later, I'll have to step in. As a member of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance, I'll vouch for the commander and reveal that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is actually the merchant alliance's pawn."

Jian Gui was well aware of Cang Xu's background with the Wisteria Merchant Alliance.

In fact, back on Weeping Pine Island, Cang Xu had approached Xiao Wang Zi and others precisely under this guise.

Therefore, the contingency plan Cang Xu devised contained no logical flaws.

However, when Teacher Si Ling effortlessly led Jian Gui and Cang Xu through the breach in the Ocean Mother Nest, formally entering the Charming Blue Divine Domain, Teacher Si Ling showed no expression of surprise.

His gaze swept the surroundings, glancing over the Deep Sea Monster Fish without lingering.

Cang Xu scrutinized the area, finding nothing amiss.

“It seems he hasn't detected the Deep Sea Monster Fish!” Cang Xu breathed a sigh of relief.

The excuse he'd devised still had numerous flaws.

The most glaring one was the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

This gold level magic ship was crafted by War Merchant. It had made an appearance during the Battle of Two Eyes Island, where the fishmen had mistaken it for a Charming Blue saint beast.

Survivors speculated wildly about its true nature.

But one thing was certain: many outside knew of the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

If Teacher Si Ling knew anything—especially the strong connection between the Deep Sea Monster Fish and War Merchant, it would spell trouble!

Back on Weeping Pine Island, Jian Gui presented three demands for exchange.

The first was the undead inheritance of the corpse aspect and bone aspect. The second was any hidden intelligence concerning Charming Blue Goddess. The third was a valid means of contacting the legendary magician, War Merchant.

Jian Gui, a legitimate member of the undead orthodoxy, demanded information on Charming Blue Goddess and War Merchant. With only gold level qualifications, he was far from qualified.

Especially since his trading partners were merely figures like Xiao Wang Zi and Ku Feng.

Therefore, it was highly probable he was merely tasked with gathering intelligence, expanding the scope of his search as much as possible, without harboring much hope.

Who else could command him?

Cang Xu easily deduced: the undead orthodoxy was searching for War Merchant and Charming Blue Goddess!

Under these circumstances, if Teacher Si Ling discovered the Deep Sea Monster Fish and happened to know it belonged to War Merchant, then Cang Xu and the fishman youngster would inevitably face rigorous investigation. The consequences were unimaginable!

Fortunately, such a dire scenario did not materialize.

Teacher Si Ling lifted his head, gazing intently at the Charming Blue Divine Domain before sighing deeply. “Charming Blue Divine Domain... After so many years, I find myself here once more.”

His expression held a complex mix of emotions.

Cang Xu: ?!

In an instant, he keenly sensed the hidden meaning behind Teacher Si Ling's intricate expression.

“What's going on?”

“It seems... Teacher Si Ling doesn't harbor strong hostility toward the Charming Blue Church?”

“Didn't he join the battle after hearing Jian Gui's plea for help?”

Earlier, Cang Xu had speculated that Teacher Si Ling's appearance was likely reinforcements Jian Gui had accidentally summoned through their soul bond.

But now, it seemed things weren't quite as he'd assumed.

Cang Xu's gaze involuntarily shifted to Jian Gui.

Jian Gui wore only a hint of puzzlement, more akin to the relief of a conjecture confirmed.

The next moment, he spoke: “Teacher Si Ling, could it be... the Charming Blue Church truly is our ally?”

Teacher Si Ling glanced at him, smiled faintly, and nodded slowly.

Cang Xu: “?!”

Jian Gui grew agitated: “Zhou Zhang is a Charming Blue Archbishop. Could she not have known about this connection?”

“No,” Teacher Si Ling replied. “This time, her blood sacrifice was also meant to await my arrival.”

Jian Gui's eyes widened: “Then why did she capture me? To sacrifice me? To kill me!?”

Teacher Si Ling replied meaningfully: “You are already dead.”

Jian Gui: “No, I mean, she wanted to completely exterminate me!”

Teacher Si Ling smiled: “Did she?”

Jian Gui: “Uh...”

Just as he was about to speak, a surge of saint aura abruptly closed in.

It was Qian Xing!

The Snow Pear Octopus was tough as nails, a formidable opponent. But Qian Xing's mobility far surpassed its own, effortlessly shaking off the Snow Pear Octopus for the moment.

“No need to waste time tangling with a saint beast. I'm not stupid.”

“Let me see what treasures lie here!”

Qian Xing, a member of the Fire Beard Pirates, was a pirate through and through.

Plunder was in a pirate's blood, and Qian Xing was no exception.

With Da Han now occupied by Zhou Zhang, Qian Xing shook off the Snow Pear Octopus and charged toward the settlement of the Charming Blue believers.

He saw the divine script and magic formation he'd shattered, the gold level altar he'd slashed down to a third of its size, and the breached Ocean Mother Nest.

His excitement soared, his expectations skyrocketing, then he crashed headlong into the Charming Blue Divine Domain.

Like Teacher Si Ling, he too failed to spot the Deep Sea Monster Fish. Instead, he saw the trio of undead.

He swept past Jian Gui and Cang Xu, his gaze instantly locking onto the seemingly “ordinary” Teacher Si Ling.

So familiar!

“You... are Teacher Si Ling?” Qian Xing recognized him instantly, his pupils contracting violently.

Without Da Han's prophecy, his identification wouldn't have been so swift. But before they acted, Da Han had foreseen the future, witnessing Teacher Si Ling modifying Ba Qi.

Thus, Qian Xing recognized him instantly.

Then, in a flash, he changed direction and fled outward!

As a fighter skilled in close combat, his first instinct when facing a magician of the same level would be to engage in melee.

But Qian Xing didn't do that. Instead, he chose to run away immediately.

For one reason alone—his opponent was Teacher Si Ling!

Cang Xu stared in stunned silence.

Teacher Si Ling smiled faintly, his attitude serene.

Noticing Cang Xu's expression, Jian Gui lifted his chin slightly, a hint of pride in his voice. “The gap between saints is unthinkable.”

“The higher the transcendent level, the greater the disparity between those of the same level.”

Cang Xu's mouth opened slightly as he looked at Teacher Si Ling, his eyes brimming with curiosity, admiration, and awe.

Teacher Si Ling, with hands clasped behind his back, sensed Cang Xu's gaze. His smile grew even more serene.

Without exchanging a single blow, Qian Xing fled.

He shot out from the Mother Nest, closing in on Da Han, and whispered telepathically: “Teacher Si Ling! I saw Teacher Si Ling!”

Da Han, locked in combat with Zhou Zhang, paled visibly and snapped back, "Are you certain? You must know the Charming Blue Church excels at deception."

Necromancy magic - Soul Capture!

In the next instant, Teacher Si Ling unleashed a saint level undead spell.

Though he remained hidden within the divine domain, both Da Han and Qian Xing fell victim simultaneously.

Instantly, they felt their souls seized, their bodies stiffening.

Though they recovered moments later, their expressions darkened.

“It's Soul Capture!”

“Teacher Si Ling has established his domain here, and we didn't notice a thing.”

“Let's go! It really is him!”

As Jian Gui had said, there was a difference between saint level individuals.

The greatest disparity lay in their domains.

Saint level transcendents could command domain power, but the scope, quality, and other aspects of their domains varied vastly.

Qian Xing's domain, for instance, was confined to a mere ten meters around his body. Da Han's extended slightly further, reaching over a hundred meters.

Yet now, Teacher Si Ling stood over a thousand meters away from them.

More terrifyingly, though his domain was spread out, it revealed not a single trace of abnormality. Only when Teacher Si Ling made his move did Qian Xing and Da Han sense it.

Such profound and subtle mastery over domain power was rare among the saint level individuals.

Who else could it be but Teacher Si Ling?

Even if it weren't him, but someone else impersonating him, this level of mastery alone was enough to make Da Han and Qian Xing feel an intense urge to retreat.

So, in the next instant, these two saint level pirates didn't hesitate. They turned around simultaneously and fled.

Of course, it wasn't that they lacked the strength to fight. It was simply that it wasn't worth fighting.

They were pirates. They came here to get rich, not to risk their lives!
